Lisp 的演化

The Evolution of Lisp

Richard P. Gabriel Lucid, Inc. 707 Laurel Street Menlo Park, California 94025 Phone: (415) 329-8400 FAX: (415) 329-8480 E-mail: rpg@lucid.com

摘要

Abstract

Lisp 是世界上最伟大的编程语言，至少它的拥护者是这样认为的。Lisp 的结构使人们很容易扩展这门语言，甚至无需从头开始，就能实现全新的方言。总体而言，推动 Lisp 演化的，与其说是对技术需求的冷静评估，不如说是机构之间的竞争、彼此压过一头的心态，以及“黑客文化”特有的、因技术巧思而产生的喜悦。不过，这一过程最终既造就了一门杂乱却强大的工业级编程语言，也造就了一种小巧而强大、技术上纯粹、适合编程语言理论研究者使用的方言。本文接续 McCarthy 在第一届 HOPL 会议论文中结束的地方，按时间顺序追溯 Lisp 的发展：从 PDP-6 时代，经由 Interlisp 和 MacLisp 的鼎盛时期、专用 Lisp 机器的兴衰，一直讲到当前的标准化活动。随后，我们考察若干有代表性的语言特性的技术演变，其中既有显著的成功，也有显著的失败，以此说明 Lisp 区别于其他编程语言的设计问题。我们还讨论把 Lisp 用作设计其他编程语言的实验室。最后，我们反思推动 Lisp 演化的各种力量。

Lisp is the world’s greatest programming language—or so its proponents think. The structure of Lisp makes it easy to extend the language or even to implement entirely new dialects without starting from scratch. Overall, the evolution of Lisp has been guided more by institutional rivalry, one-upsmanship, and the glee born of technical cleverness that is characteristic of the “hacker culture” than by sober assessments of technical requirements. Nevertheless this process has eventually produced both an industrialstrength programming language, messy but powerful, and a technically pure dialect, small but powerful, that is suitable for use by programming-language theoreticians. We pick up where McCarthy’s paper in the first HOPL conference left off. We trace the development chronologically from the era of the PDP-6, through the heyday of Interlisp and MacLisp, past the ascension and decline of special purpose Lisp machines, to the present era of standardization activities. We then examine the technical evolution of a few representative language features, including both some notable successes and some notable failures, that illuminate design issues that distinguish Lisp from other programming languages. We also discuss the use of Lisp as a laboratory for designing other programming languages. We conclude with some reflections on the forces that have driven the evolution of Lisp.

1 引言

1 Introduction

1.1 本文的组织方式

1.1 Organization of This Paper

过去三十年间，Lisp 经历了许多变化。我们发现，无论按时间顺序还是采用其他方式，都无法用一次线性叙述，连贯地涵盖所有值得关注的内容。项目和方言以复杂的方式出现、分裂、合并和消亡；个人的职业生涯穿行其间，有时与这些联系平行，但更多时候彼此交叉。思想从一个项目跳到另一个项目，从一个人传到另一个人。因此，我们选择从多个切面来呈现这个主题。这种组织方式不可避免地会带来一些重复。

A great deal has happened to Lisp over the last thirty years. We have found it impossible to treat everything of interest coherently in a single linear pass through the subject, chronologically or otherwise. Projects and dialects emerge, split, join, and die in complicated ways; the careers of individual people are woven through these connections in ways that are sometimes parallel but more often orthogonal. Ideas leap from project to project, from person to person. We have chosen to present a series of slices through the subject matter. This organization inevitably leads to some redundancy in the presentation.

第 2 节从项目和人物的角度讨论 Lisp 的历史，接续 McCarthy [McCarthy, 1981] 的叙述，直到 IEEE、ANSI 和 ISO 为 Lisp 方言制定正式标准的工作。第 3 节分别追踪若干技术主题随时间的演变，重点是各个主题中思想的流动。第 4 节追溯 Lisp 作为语言实验室和实现工具的用途，尤其是人工智能语言的开发，并特别关注这些人工智能语言如何反过来影响 Lisp 本身。第 5 节总结 Lisp 为何会以这样的方式演化。

Section 2 discusses the history of Lisp in terms of projects and people, from where McCarthy left off[McCarthy, 1981] up through the efforts to produce official standards for Lisp dialects within IEEE, ANSI, and ISO. Section 3 examines a number of technical themes and traces separately their chronological evolution; here the emphasis is on the flow of ideas for each topic. Section 4 traces the use of Lisp as a language laboratory and implementation tool, especially for the development of AI languages; particular attention is paid to ways in which feedback from the AI languages influenced the development of Lisp itself. Section 5 draws some conclusions about why Lisp evolved as it did.

1.2 语言演化的模式

1.2 The Pattern of Language Evolution

从 Lisp 1.5 开始，Lisp 的演化呈现出“多样化、接受、整合”的循环。在多样化阶段，人们试验新的语言构造、编程风格、实现策略和编程范式，并把它们引入已有 Lisp 方言，或者设计新的 Lisp 方言。无论哪一种情况，实际上都创造了一种新的 Lisp 方言。在接受阶段，这些新方言要么被接受，要么被拒绝。新方言的设计者或支持者会设定接受条件，这一阶段是否成功，由这些条件决定。在整合阶段，多种方言被合并为一种主流方言。通常会选择一种方言作为根基，再从同一棵树或其他方言中取来枝条。整合就是一种正式或非正式的标准化过程。最成功的整合会产生稳定的开发平台。

The evolution of Lisp since Lisp 1.5 is characterized by a cycle of diversification, acceptance, and consolidation. During diversification, new language constructs, new styles of programming, new implementation strategies, and new programming paradigms are experimented with and introduced to existing Lisp dialects, or new Lisp dialects are designed. In either case, a new Lisp dialect is, in effect, created. During acceptance, these new Lisp dialects are either accepted or rejected. The designers or backers of the new dialect will set conditions for acceptance, and the success of the acceptance phase will be determined by those conditions. During consolidation, a variety of dialects are merged into a mainstream dialect. Typically one dialect will be chosen as the root and the branches will be taken from the same tree as the root or from other dialects. Consolidation is a process of standardization, either formal or informal. Consolidation, when most successful, results in a stable development platform.

这一循环并非不可改变：过程可能中断、停停走走，也可能跳过某些阶段。不过，我们可以把任何时点的演化过程看作处于其中某个阶段，并以进入下一阶段为目标。

The cycle is not inexorable: The process can break down, stop and start, and cycles can be skipped. But it is possible to view the process of evolution at any point as being within one of these stages of the cycle, with the goal of moving to the next stage.

每个阶段都可以通过进入该阶段所需的条件来刻画。

Each stage can be characterized by the conditions that allow it to be entered.

1.2.1 接受的条件

1.2.1 Conditions for Acceptance

最关键的阶段是接受。它决定哪些语言特性和范式能够进入下一轮稳定发展。接受的条件包括：运行在合适的机器上、适应本地用户的工作模式、解决迫切的新问题，以及具有适当的吸引力。下面逐一讨论。

The most critical stage is acceptance. This stage determines which language features and paradigms will be part of the next stage of stable development. The conditions for acceptance are being on the right machines, fitting into local user models, solving a pressing new problem, having the right cachet. Each will be explored in detail.

接受阶段取决于某个特定的接受群体是否选择以新方言为工作基础。商业人工智能公司就是这样一个群体。例如，某种 Lisp 方言可能通过与主流语言集成，来解决这一群体的问题。该方言能否进入循环的下一阶段，即整合，取决于这个群体是否愿意开始接受它，以及它是否确实提供了解决方案，从而通过接受阶段。

The acceptance stage depends on a particular acceptance group choosing to base their work on the new dialect. An example acceptance group comprises the commercial artificial intelligence companies. A particular dialect of Lisp might be targeted to solve the problems of this community through, for example, integration with mainstream languages. Whether this dialect moves on to the next stage in the cycle—consolidation—depends on whether this acceptance group chooses to enter a period of acceptance with the dialect and in fact the dialect passes the acceptance stage by actually providing solutions.

方言应当运行在合适的机器上，其中包括合适厂商的机器。当那些决定是否接受它的人能够立即使用它，并获得适当的性能时，Lisp 方言才会被接受。这也意味着其规模和性能必须适合这些机器。如果关键用户群体使用的是某种容量和速度的计算机，那么该方言就应当在这种配置上表现合格。问题在于，方言的设计者或推广者有时选择最初目标机器时，只看已安装计算机的总量，而没有关注目标用户群体中已经安装或即将安装的、范围更小的那部分机器。后文将看到，Interlisp 和 NIL 都因推广者选错机器而逐渐被弃用。

The dialect should run on the right machines. This includes being on the right manufacturers’ machines. A Lisp dialect will be accepted when the people who will determine acceptability can use the dialect right away with proper performance. This also includes having acceptable size and performance for the machines. If the key user groups are using computers of a certain size and speed, the Lisp dialect should run acceptably on that configuration. This is complicated by the fact that the designers or promoters of a dialect will sometimes choose the initial target computer with an eye only towards the total number of installed computers rather than the more restrictive number of computers installed or soon-to-be installed in the target user base. As we will see, InterLisp and NIL fell into disuse when their promoters chose the wrong computers.

其次，要适应本地用户的工作模式。每个用户群体都有自己的工作风格或方法体系，新方言必须融入其中。新方言往往是已有方言的扩展；如果接受群体不能及时拥抱它，它就无法进入接受阶段，也不大可能成为整合的一部分。有时接受群体刚刚形成，还没有既定工作风格或方法，此时新方言就应提供一种该群体能够接受的工作模式。

Next is fitting into local user models. Each user group will have a style of working or set of methodologies within which the new dialect must fit. Oftentimes a new dialect will be an extension of an existing one, and unless the acceptance group is able to embrace the dialect in a timely fashion, the dialect will not enter the acceptance stage and will unlikely be part of a consolidation. Sometimes the acceptance group is newly formed and has no existing working styles or methodologies, and in this case the new dialect should present a working model acceptable to the acceptance group.

接下来是及时解决问题。所谓接受群体，是指如果它需要某种方言，那么它的成功就会决定该方言能否被接受的群体。在新方言即将进入这一阶段时，这个群体必须有尚未得到满足的需求；否则，它没有必要改用新方言，甚至可能认为切换风险太大。如果这个群体从事商业活动，或者其成功标准由外部决定，它就会倾向于规避风险，新方言必须在实现这些标准方面提供显著的、可感知的价值。例如，一个正要开展大型项目、且对生产率或复用有严格要求的群体，可能很容易接受具有强大面向对象功能的 Lisp 方言。只有边际性或渐进性改进的方言，往往不会被接受。

Next is solving timely problems. An acceptance group is one whose success will determine the acceptance of a dialect if that dialect is required by the acceptance group. The acceptance group must have a set of needs not addressed at the time the new dialect would enter this stage; otherwise the acceptance group would have no need to switch to the new dialect, and perhaps such a switch would be too risky for the group. If the group is commercial or has a set of outside-determined success criteria, that group will be risk averse, and the new dialect must offer significant perceived value for achieving these criteria. For example, a group that is embarking in a large project with strict productivity or reuse requirements, a Lisp dialect with a strong object-oriented component might be readily embraced. A dialect with marginal or incremental improvements will often not be accepted.

最后是吸引力（cachet）。我们使用这个词时，取的是广告业和时尚界的含义。一条具有这种吸引力的围巾，会被最高端、最令人向往的上流社会人士佩戴，因此受到追捧。一款具有这种吸引力的香水令人心动、备受珍视；人们不只是想拥有它，而是想把它据为己有。

Finally is cachet. We use the word ‘cachet’ as it is used in the advertising and fashion industries. A scarf with cachet is worn by people in the most exclusive and desired echelons of high society, and so that scarf is highly desired and sought after. A perfume with cachet is charged, prized, and people don’t merely want to own it—they want to possess it.

计算机也是如此。最好的例子，尽管有人可能不同意，是 Macintosh 与 IBM PC 的对比。客观来看，PC 上的软件多得多，在商业领域的使用量大概也远超 Macintosh。然而，Macintosh 有某种特质，能吸引追求前沿的计算机爱好者。看看那些总是滔滔不绝地赞美计算机、认为人人都该有一台的发烧友：他们不是已经拥有 Macintosh，就是希望拥有一台。PC 与 Macintosh 的差别就在于这种吸引力。

So it is with computers. The best example—though some might argue with it—is the Macintosh as compared with the IBM PC. When you look at them objectively, there is considerably more software on the PC, and it is probably used much more heavily in business than the Macintosh. But there is something about the Macintosh that appeals to the leading edge computerist. If you look at most hobbyists—the ones who rave on and on about how great computers are and how everyone should have one—they either own a Macintosh or wish they did. The difference between a PC and a Macintosh is cachet.

这种吸引力不一定意味着同类最佳、最昂贵或最独享。1967 年的 Ford Mustang 比 1992 年的 Mercedes 300 SE 更有这种吸引力，尽管后者昂贵得多。

Cachet does not always mean best of breed or most expensive or most exclusive. A 1967 Ford Mustang has more cachet than an 1992 Mercedes 300 SE, though the latter is far more expensive.

接受群体由一个个必须亲自愿意采用新方言的人组成。Lisp 用户通常把自己看作走在前沿的人。因此，新方言必须至少具有某些前沿技术。例如，CLOS 为 Common Lisp 增添了这种吸引力，几乎使带有 CLOS 的 Common Lisp 被视为一种新方言，因为它被认为比主流面向对象编程更进一步。

Acceptance groups comprise people who must individually wish to adopt a new dialect. The people who would use Lisp generally characterize themselves as leading edge. Therefore, there must be at least some aspects of leading edge technology in the new dialect. For example, CLOS lends cachet to Common Lisp—almost causing Common Lisp with CLOS to be regarded as a new dialect—because it is perceived as an advance over mainstream object-oriented programming.

1.2.2 整合的条件

1.2.2 Conditions for Consolidation

整合是确立正式或非正式标准的阶段。有时，多种方言会通过标准化过程与设计合理化过程的结合而合并。发生这种情况时，通常会选择一种方言作为基础，再融入其他已获接受的方言的特性。

Consolidation is the stage during which standards—formal or informal—are set. Sometimes several dialects are merged through a combination of a standardization process and a design rationalization process. When this happens one dialect will usually be chosen as the base and features from other accepted dialects will be blended into it.

进入整合阶段的条件是：一种或多种方言已经被接受，接受群体正处于上升期，而且人们认为这些方言已经帮助、并将继续帮助这个群体。

The conditions for entering consolidation are that the dialect or dialects have been accepted, that the acceptance group is in ascendency, and that the dialect is perceived to have helped the acceptance group and will continue to help.

首先，方言必须已经被接受。这不仅意味着它满足了群体的功能需求，即其特性确实有用，也意味着它很好地融入了这个群体，并满足其性能和规模目标。

First, the dialect must be accepted. This implies not only that the dialect met the functional needs of the group—its features were useful—but that the dialect fit well into the group and met its performance and size goals.

其次，接受群体必须准备好迎接增长。这是 Lisp 方言成功的重要外部因素，对其他语言可能也是如此。将要使用该语言的用户群体，本身必须成功，或被认为即将成功。因此，一门语言要跨越双重门槛：既要在某个群体中成功，也要吸引一个自身会成功的群体。结合进入接受阶段的条件，这意味着语言还必须成功选对目标平台。

Next, the acceptance group must be poised for growth. This is an important external factor for the success of a dialect of Lisp, and possibly for other languages. The user group that would use the language must itself be successful or be perceived to about to be successful. Thus, a language faces a double hurdle: To succeed with a particular group and to have appealed to a group that will itself succeed. When coupled with the conditions for entering the acceptance stage, this implies that the language must also have succeeded in choosing the right target platform.

此外，当接受群体正在增长或即将增长时，其人数和地域范围通常都会扩大，因此交换软件就变得重要。商业群体和研究群体都会出现这种情况。

Furthermore, when an acceptance group is growing or about to grow, that group will typically be growing in size and in geographical extent, and so exchanging software becomes important. This happens both when the acceptance group is commercial and when it is research-oriented.

最后，人们必须认为方言对接受群体的成功，或成功条件，作出了贡献。也就是说，群体必须已经成功或被认为即将成功，而方言必须显得对这一局面有所贡献。仅仅是群体成功，或者方言只是对使群体达到当前地位的工作有所帮助，通常还不够：方言必须被视为这项工作不可或缺的一部分。否则，群体就会倾向于重新评估语言选择；而对 Lisp 来说，一旦进入这种重新评估，它就很难被保留下来。

Finally is the perceived contribution of the dialect to the success or success conditions of the acceptance group. That is, the acceptance group must either be successful or be perceived to about to be successful, and the dialect must appear to have contributed to that situation. It is usually not sufficient that the acceptance group succeed or that the dialect have been merely useful to the effort that put the acceptance group where it is: The dialect must be perceived as a necessary part of the effort. Otherwise the acceptance group will be tempted to reevaluate the language decision, and in the case of Lisp, once this reevaluation is entered upon, it is difficult for Lisp to be retained.

1.2.3 多样化的条件

1.2.3 Conditions for Diversification

当旧方案不够用，或者没有别的事情可做时，多样化就会发生。进入多样化阶段的条件是：外部驱动力趋于衰弱，活动退回较小的研究或开发群体，以及先前的方言在某些方面失败了。

Diversification happens when old solutions are inadequate or when there is nothing else to do. The conditions for entering diversification are that external driving factors are languishing, that there is a retreat to smaller research or development groups, and that the previous dialects have failed in some way.

首先，当接受群体走下坡路时，能够投入语言实现改进和渐进式设计的资源就会减少。语言经历整合阶段后，通常通过实现和设计上的改进来渐进完善：可以改善性能或缩小规模，也可以增加一些次要的新特性；但范式转变需要新一轮多样化、接受和整合。

First, when the acceptance group is in descendency, there are fewer resources to allocate for language implementation improvement and incremental design. When a language has been through the consolidation stage, it is subject to incremental improvement through implementation improvements and design improvements: performance or size can be improved, and some new, minor language features can be added, but paradigm shifts require a new cycle of diversification, acceptance, and consolidation.

语言专家不再有义务进行这些小修小补，而会转向解决诸如导致接受群体衰退的问题。也许他们会瞄准一个新的接受群体及其问题；也许另一种或几种语言具有足够的吸引力或成功，促使人们从中挖掘内容，融入新的方言。

The language experts will no longer be obligated to make these small improvements and will instead turn their attention to solving such problems as those which caused the acceptance group to decline. Perhaps a new acceptance group will be targeted along with its problems. Or perhaps another language or languages will have such cachet or success that that language will be mined for incorporation into a new dialect.

其次，这种退缩会形成较小的群体，一个个语言研究小圈子。由于创新通常涉及小群体，这几乎是新设计的必要条件。真正重要的是，这些语言设计群体，虽然它们可能不这样称呼自己，能够不受打扰地追求新设计。

Next, such retreat creates smaller groups, pockets of language groups. Because innovation typically involves small groups, this is virtually a necessary condition for new design. The real importance is that the language design groups—though they might not be called such—will be free from interruption to pursue their new designs.

最后，先前的解决方案必须曾让接受群体遭遇失败。例如，如果群体只是因为外部经济因素而衰退，那么就没有必要追求变化。当然，这从来阻止不了那些为变化而变化的人。有些人称之为“我就是要按自己的方式来”综合征。

Finally, the previous solutions must have had failures for the acceptance group. If the acceptance group declines because of extraneous economic factors, for example, there would be no need to pursue change. Though this has never stopped those intent on change for change’s sake. Some call this the I-Did-It-My-Way syndrome.

多样化有许多来源。一门语言失败时，人们可能回归早期原则并重新设计，以修复问题；也可能有另一种采用不同范式的语言，看起来适合解决原方言未能解决的问题，或者那门语言的吸引力令人无法抗拒；还可能是纯粹的智识或科学好奇心，引导设计者走上一条产生新语言特性或新范式的道路。

Diversification comes from many sources. When a language fails, there may be attempts to fix the problems by retreating to earlier principles and redesigning; there may be another language with a different paradigm that appears suitable for the solution to problems the dialect failed to solve, or perhaps the cachet of that language will be irresistible; or pure intellectual or scientific curiosity will lead a designer down a path that results in new language features or paradigms.

1.2.4 思想的传播与交融

1.2.4 Pollenation

语言设计和演化由人推动，而人的职业生涯会把他们从一组关注点带到另一组关注点。就像雪球在薄雪上滚动一样，人会不断吸收自己所处理问题的影响。因此，在我们关于 Lisp 多样化的故事中，某些人会出现又离开；当他们离开一段时间后重新出现时，已经积累了新经验，并会把这些经验运用起来。新参与者会出现，与其他人展开人与人之间的互动；这些互动的产物，也就是语言，会在思想上体现出与各种其他语言、语言概念、特性和范式的亲缘关系。

Language design and evolution is driven by people, whose careers carry them from one set of concerns to another. And like a snowball rolled along a thin layer of snow, people pick up influences from the problems they work on. And so we observe that particular individuals enter and leave our story of the diversification of Lisp, and when they reappear after an absence, they have new experiences under their belts and will apply those experiences. New players will appear and interact as people with others, and the results of those interactions—the languages—will reflect intellectual affinities to a wide variety of other languages, language concepts and features, and language paradigms.

接受群体由人组成，整个语言演化和多样化的故事，都发生在人类关切和制度的背景之下。尽管人们会诉诸语言设计的客观标准，不可避免的人性仍然清晰可见。

Acceptance groups are people, and the whole story of language evolution and diversification is against a background of human concerns and institutions. Despite the fact that appeal is made to objective criteria for language design, the inevitable humanness shines through.

2 实现项目的时间脉络

2 Implementation Projects Chronology

以下各节描述从 1960 年到本文写作时 Lisp 演化中的事件。对于每个时代或局面，我们先给出纯粹的时间顺序叙述，再把事件放进“多样化、接受、整合”的循环背景中，并说明重要的技术概念。

The following sections describe the events in the evolution of Lisp from 1960 until the present. Each era or situation is first described as a pure chronology and then the events of the era or situation are placed within the context of the cycle of diversification-acceptance-consolidation, and the important technical concepts are described.

最终发展成 Lisp 的语言，其早期构想始于 1956 年，当时 John McCarthy 参加了达特茅斯人工智能夏季研究项目。实际实现工作于 1958 年秋开始。1978 年，McCarthy 讲述了这门语言的早期历史 [McCarthy, 1981]，大致讲到 Lisp 1.5 之后不久。另见 [McCarthy, 1980]。我们首先简述从 Lisp 1.5 到 MacLisp 和 Interlisp 的实现，随后深入追踪 MacLisp 和 Interlisp 的发展及其不同理念。接着介绍同期的其他 Lisp 方言，包括 Scheme、Portable Standard Lisp、Zetalisp 和 Common Lisp 的其他前身。我们将详细描述 Common Lisp，包括其标准化和实现，并考察面向对象技术融入 Lisp 的过程，尤其关注 Common Lisp Object System（CLOS）。我们也会考察欧洲和日本的 Lisp 演化，但细节较少；EuLisp 是例外，因为它是作为 Common Lisp 的对立方案而定义的一种 Lisp 方言。

Early thoughts about a language that eventually became Lisp started in 1956 when John Mc- Carthy attended the Dartmouth Summer Research Project on Artificial Intelligence. Actual implementation began in the fall of 1958. In 1978 McCarthy related the early history of the language [McCarthy, 1981], taking it approximately to just after Lisp 1.5. See also [McCarthy, 1980]. We start with a brief description of the implementations of Lisp starting with Lisp 1.5 and ending with Maclisp and Interlisp. At that point we will delve into MacLisp and Interlisp more deeply, tracing their development and the differing philosophies they embraced. Other Lisp dialects of the same period will be presented next, including Scheme, Portable Standard Lisp, Zetalisp, and the other precursors to Common Lisp. Common Lisp will be described in detail, including its standardization and implementations. The blending of object-oriented techniques into Lisp will be examined, especially with an eye towards the Common Lisp Object System (CLOS). European and Japanese Lisp evolution will be examined, but in less detail, except for EuLisp, which is a dialect of Lisp defined in opposition to Common Lisp.

2.1 从 Lisp 1.5 到 PDP-6 Lisp：1960—1965 年

2.1 From Lisp 1.5 to PDP-6 Lisp: 1960–1965

在这一时期，Lisp 迅速传播到多种计算机上：有的从另一台计算机上已有的 Lisp 自举而来，有的则重新实现。几乎所有情况下，Lisp 方言都小巧简单，实现方式直接，对原始语言的改动很少。

During this period, Lisp spread rapidly to a variety of computers, either by bootstrapping from an existing Lisp on another computer or by a new implementation. In almost all cases, the Lisp dialect was small and simple; the implementation straightforward. There were very few changes made to the original language.

20 世纪 60 年代初，Timothy Hart 和 Thomas Evans 在 Univac M 460 上实现了 Lisp 1.5；M 460 是 Univac 490 的军用版本。该实现借助交叉编译器，以及用于 Lisp 实现最底层的少量机器语言代码，从 IBM 7090 上的 Lisp 1.5 自举而来 [Hart, 1985]。

In the early 1960’s, Timothy Hart and Thomas Evans implemented Lisp 1.5 on the Univac M 460, a military version of the Univac 490. It was bootstrapped from Lisp 1.5 on the IBM 7090 using a cross-compiler and a small amount of machine language code for the lowest levels of the Lisp implementation [Hart, 1985].

Robert Saunders 及其在 System Development Corporation 的同事，在 IBM 制造的 AN/FSQ-32/V，也就是 Q-32 计算机上实现了 Lisp 1.5 [Saunders, 1985b]。这一实现从斯坦福大学的 IBM 7090 和 PDP-1 计算机自举而来。

Robert Saunders and his colleagues at System Development Corporation implemented Lisp 1.5 on the IBM-built AN/FSQ-32/V computer—the Q-32 [Saunders, 1985b]. The implementation was bootstrapped from the IBM 7090 and PDP-1 computers at Stanford University.

斯坦福的 PDP-1 Lisp 由 John McCarthy 和 Steve Russell 实现。

The PDP-1 Lisp at Stanford was implemented by John McCarthy and Steve Russell.

1963 年，L. Peter Deutsch 当时还是高中生，在 Bolt Beranek and Newman（BBN）的 PDP-1 上实现了一种类似 Lisp 1.5 的 Lisp [Deutsch, 1985]，称为 Basic PDP-1 Lisp。

In 1963, L. Peter Deutsch (at that time a high school student) implemented a Lisp similar to Lisp 1.5 on the PDP-1 at Bolt Beranek and Newman (BBN) [Deutsch, 1985]. This Lisp was called Basic PDP-1 Lisp.

到 1964 年，MIT 电气工程系的一台 IBM 7094 上已经运行着 Lisp 1.5 的一个版本，其操作系统为兼容分时系统 CTSS。[?] 这一 Lisp 和 Basic PDP-1 Lisp，是 DEC 与 MIT 技术模型铁路俱乐部的一些成员于 1964 年春实现的 PDP-6 Lisp [PDP-6 Lisp, 1967] 的主要影响来源。这个 Lisp 是 PDP-6 上编写的第一个程序。它也是 MacLisp 的祖先；MacLisp 在 MIT 的 PDP-6 以及后来的 PDP-10 上，运行于不兼容分时系统 ITS [Eastlake, 1968; Eastlake, 1972]。

By 1964 a version of Lisp 1.5 was running in the Electrical Engineering Department at MIT on an IBM 7094 computer, running the Compatible Time Sharing System (CTSS). [?]. This Lisp and Basic PDP-1 Lisp were the main influences on the PDP-6 Lisp [PDP-6 Lisp, 1967] implemented by DEC and some members of MIT’s Tech Model Railroad Club in the spring of 1964. This Lisp was the first program written on the PDP-6. Also, this Lisp was the ancestor of MacLisp, the Lisp written to run under the Incompatible Time Sharing System (ITS) [Eastlake, 1968; Eastlake, 1972] at MIT on the PDP-6 and later on the PDP-10.

BBN 在 PDP-1 上实现了 Basic PDP-1 Lisp 的后继版本，Daniel Bobrow 和 D. L. Murphy 又在 Scientific Data Systems 940（SDS 940）上实现了一个向上兼容版本，参照的是 MIT CTSS 系统上的 Lisp 1.5。Alice Hartley 和 Murphy 随后为 PDP-10 编写了另一个向上兼容版本，称为 BBN Lisp [Teitelman, 1971]。1973 年，在 SDS 被 Xerox 收购并更名为 Xerox Data Systems 后不久，BBN 与 Xerox 帕洛阿尔托研究中心共同维护 BBN Lisp，并将其更名为 Interlisp [Teitelman, 1974]。

At BBN a successor to Basic PDP-1 Lisp was implemented on the PDP-1 and an upwardcompatible version, patterned after Lisp 1.5 on the MIT CTSS system, was implemented on the Scientific Data Systems 940 (SDS 940) by Daniel Bobrow and D. L. Murphy. A further upwardcompatible version was written for the PDP-10 by Alice Hartley and Murphy, and this Lisp was called BBN Lisp [Teitelman, 1971]. In 1973, not long after the time that SDS was acquired by Xerox and renamed Xerox Data Systems, the maintenance of BBN Lisp was shared by BBN and Xerox Palo Alto Research Center and the name of the Lisp was changed to Interlisp [Teitelman, 1974].

PDP-6 [DEC, 1964] 和 PDP-10 [DEC, 1969] 在设计上特别适合 Lisp：字长为 36 位，地址为 18 位。因此，一个 CONS 单元，也就是一对指针或地址，可以高效地存放在一个字中。半字指令使 CONS 单元的 CAR 和 CDR 操作非常快。这两种机器还具有快速而强大的栈指令，从而使 Lisp 的函数调用很快。

The PDP-6 [DEC, 1964] and PDP-10 [DEC, 1969] computers were, by design, especially suited for Lisp, with 36-bit words and 18-bit addresses. This allowed a CONS cell—a pair of pointers or addresses—to be stored efficiently in a single word. There were half-word instructions that made manipulating the CAR and CDR of CONS cells very fast. The PDP-6 and PDP-10 also had fast, powerful stack instructions, which enabled fast function calling for Lisp.

这些实现几乎都有一个小型手写汇编核心和一个编译器；其余部分则用 Lisp 编写并编译。

Almost all of these implementations had a small hand-coded (assembly) core and a compiler; the rest of the Lisp was written in Lisp and compiled.

1965 年，几乎所有已有的 Lisp 都相同，或只有微不足道的差别。1965 年以后，更准确地说，在 MacLisp 与 BBN Lisp 分道扬镳之后，Lisp 方言便大量涌现。

In 1965, virtually all of the Lisps in existence were identical or differed only in trivial ways. After 1965—or more precisely, after MacLisp and BBN Lisp diverged from each other—there came a plethora of Lisp dialects.

分析

Analysis

这些早期 Lisp 方言符合多样化阶段的典型模式：

These early Lisp dialects fit into the pattern typical of the diversification stage:

这一时期，语言工作的经费很少，各群体彼此孤立，主要服务于本地接受群体的需求，而该群体通常只有少数研究者。典型情形可以概括为：“一个人工智能实验室，走廊那头有位 Lisp 高手。”当时，人们对实现策略进行了大量试验。很少有人考虑整合，尤其不会想到正式标准化流程，部分原因在于各实验室都抱有开拓者的心态。

During this period there was little funding for language work, the groups were isolated from each other, and each group was directed primarily towards serving the needs of the local acceptance group, which was limited to a handful of researchers. The typical situation is characterized by the description “an AI lab with a Lisp wizard down the hall”. During this period there was a good deal of experimentation with implementation strategies. There was little thought of consolidation, particularly in the form of a formal standards process, partly because of the pioneering feeling that each lab embodied.

最早真正成为标准的 Lisp 是 MacLisp 和 Interlisp，因此值得特别介绍。

The first real standard Lisps were MacLisp and Interlisp, and as such they deserve some attention.

2.2 MacLisp

2.2 MacLisp

从 20 世纪 60 年代末到 80 年代初，MacLisp 是 MIT 人工智能实验室的主要 Lisp 方言。同一时期，该实验室的其他重要 Lisp 工作包括 Lisp-Machine Lisp，后来称为 Zetalisp，以及 Scheme。MacLisp 通常与 PDP-10 联系在一起，但它也运行于另一种机器，即 Honeywell 6180，使用 Multics 操作系统 [Organick, 1972]。

MacLisp was the primary Lisp dialect at the MIT AI Lab from the late 1960’s until the early 1980’s. Other important Lisp work at the Lab during this period included Lisp-Machine Lisp (later named Zetalisp) and Scheme. MacLisp is usually identified with the PDP-10 computer, but MacLisp also ran on another machine, the Honeywell 6180, under the Multics operating system [Organick, 1972].

2.2.1 早期 MacLisp

2.2.1 Early MacLisp

MacLisp/Interlisp 时代的显著特点，是重视达到或接近生产质量的实现。这一时期整合了各种实现技术，并对细节给予高度关注。

The distinguishing feature of the MacLisp/Interlisp era is the attention to production quality or near production quality implementations. This period saw a consolidation of implementation techniques, with great attention to detail.

例如，在 Lisp 1.5 中，特殊变量（special variable）把标识符绑定到一个存储位置；指向该位置的指针，放在与该标识符同名的符号的属性表上。程序员声明 x 为 special 时，编译器和加载器会协同创建名为 x 的符号。x 的属性表包含一个以 special 为指示符的属性，其值是一个存储单元。这个单元就是 x 所指的位置，对 x 的赋值会把值写入这里。对 x 进行 let 绑定，也就是重新绑定名字 x 时，该单元的旧值会连同足以恢复它的信息一起压入控制栈；当控制流返回并越过 LET 绑定点时，旧值就会被恢复。

For example, in Lisp 1.5 a special variable is a binding of an identifier to a location, a pointer to which is placed on the property list of the symbol whose name is the identifier. When a programmer declares x to be special, the compiler and loader conspire to create a symbol named x. The property list for x has a property with the indicator special, and its value is a cell. That cell is the location to which x refers and where assignments to x place values. When x is let-bound (the name x is newly bound), the old value stored in this cell is placed on the control stack along with enough information to restore the old value to this cell when control returns past the LET-binding point.

普通变量（common variable）使用值单元保存值，而绑定普通变量需要显式的关联表 a-list。运行时求普通变量的值，需要调用 EVAL。

A common variable is one in which the value cell is used to hold the value, and binding a common variable requires the use of an explicit a-list. Evaluating a common variable at run time requires a call to EVAL.

特殊变量可以由不同的已编译函数共享，但不能与解释执行的函数共享；普通变量可以由已编译函数和解释执行的函数共享，但相对而言比特殊变量慢得多。

Special variables can be shared among different compiled functions, but cannot be shared with interpreted functions; common variables can be shared by compiled functions and interpreted functions, but are relatively much slower than special variables.

从实现角度看，特殊变量是今天所谓浅绑定的前身，而普通变量是深绑定的前身。

In terms of implementation, special variables are the precursors of what is now known as shallow binding, while common variables are the precursors of deep binding.

在浅绑定中，特殊变量的值始终保存在符号的值单元里。对符号进行 let 绑定时，旧值压入控制栈，新值写入值单元。在 LET 的动态作用域内，对符号值的引用会看到新值。控制流退出 LET 时，恢复旧值。

In shallow binding, the value of a special variable is always kept in the value cell of a symbol. let-binding the symbol causes the old value to be placed on the control stack and the new value to be placed in the value cell. References to the value of the symbol within the dynamic scope of the LET will see this new value. When control exits the LET, the old value is restored.

在深绑定中，发生 let 绑定时，标识符与值组成的对被放入控制栈。引用符号的值时，先在栈中查找绑定；找不到才引用值单元。为了相对高效地搜索栈，可以把特殊绑定块，也就是栈上保存标识符和值对的位置，链接起来；可以在符号中放一个标志，表示它是否已被绑定，因此是否应在栈中查找；也可以在频繁引用特殊变量的栈帧中缓存它们。这些技术可以单独使用，也可以组合使用。

In deep binding, the identifier-value pair is placed on the control stack when let-binding occurs. Reference to the value of a symbol first looks at the stack for any bindings there, and if none are found, the value cell is referenced. Searching the stack can be done relatively efficiently by linking together special-binding blocks (the places where identifier-value pairs are stored on the stack), by putting a flag in the symbol stating whether the symbol has been bound (and therefore should be looked-up on the stack), or by caching special variables in stack frames in which they will be frequently referenced. These techniques can be used alone or in combinations.

在早期 MIT PDP-6 Lisp [PDP-6 Lisp, 1967] 中，Richard Greenblatt 认为 Lisp 1.5 同时保留 common 和 special 两类变量不够优雅，于是从语言中删除 COMMON 变量，却让 SPECIAL 变量改用值单元。这是 Lisp 首次采用后来称为浅绑定的实现。

In the early MIT PDP-6 Lisp [PDP-6 Lisp, 1967], Richard Greenblatt decided that having both common and special variables, as in Lisp 1.5, was inelegant; he removed COMMON variables from the language, but made SPECIAL variables work using the value cell. This was the first implementation of Lisp to use what is called shallow binding.

MacLisp 与 Interlisp 的一个关键差别是对语法的态度。MacLisp 偏好纯粹的列表风格，使用 EVAL 作为顶层；Interlisp 则与 Lisp 1.5 一样使用 EVALQUOTE。要在 MacLisp 中创建由原子 a 和 b 组成的点对，可以向 EVAL 输入：

A key difference between MacLisp and Interlisp was the approach to syntax. MacLisp favored the pure list style, using EVAL as the top level. Interlisp, along with Lisp 1.5, used EVALQUOTE. To create a dotted pair of the atoms a and b in MacLisp, one would type this expression to EVAL:

(cons (quote a) (quote b))

或者使用语法缩写，以 'x 表示 (quote x)：

or, using the syntactic abbreviation ’x for (quote x),

(cons 'a 'b)

在 Lisp 1.5 中，可以向 EVALQUOTE 输入这样的表达式，实际上是两个表达式：

In Lisp 1.5, one could type an expression (actually two expressions) like this to EVALQUOTE:

cons(a b)

EVALQUOTE 名称中的“quote”意指对被调用函数的“参数进行隐式引用”。MacLisp 分离出去，完全使用 EVAL 作为顶层接口；BBN-Lisp 及其后继 Interlisp 则兼顾两者：输入只有一个形式时使用 EVAL，输入行有两个或更多形式时使用 APPLY。

The “quote” in the name EVALQUOTE signifies the “implicit quoting of the arguments” to the function applied. MacLisp forked offand used EVAL exclusively as a top level interface, while BBN- Lisp (and thus Interlisp) accommodated both, using EVAL if the input was one form and APPLY if the input line was two or more forms.

“引用参数”这个说法其实既误导又不精确。它指的是一个假想预处理器所做的事：把 cons(a b) 这样的输入转换为 (cons 'a 'b)。因此，EVALQUOTE 这个术语源于对原始模型的误解。类似的误解也延续到了所谓 FEXPR 或“特殊形式”的描述中。有些 Lisp 文献说特殊形式会“引用其参数”，但实际上，特殊形式有一条决定其含义的特殊规则，而这条规则涉及不对某些形式求值 [Pitman, 1980]。

The phrase “quoting arguments” actually is misleading and imprecise. It refers to the actions of a hypothetical preprocessor that transforms the input from a form like cons(a b) to one like (cons ’a ’b). The term EVALQUOTE thus arose through an incorrect understanding of the original model. A similar misunderstanding carried over into the description of the so-called FEXPR or “special form”. In some texts on Lisp one will find descriptions of special forms that speak of a special form “quoting its arguments” when in fact a special form has a special rule for determining its meaning and that rule involves not evaluating some forms [Pitman, 1980].

McCarthy [McCarthy, 1981] 指出，最初的 Lisp 解释器被视为一台通用图灵机：给定一组指令，也就是函数，以及磁带上的初始输入，也就是参数，它就能执行任意计算。因此，cons(a b) 原本应被理解为一个函数，以及与之分开的一个字面参数列表，而不是 (cons (quote a) (quote b)) 的变形。回头看，EVALQUOTE 顶层若叫 APPLY 顶层会更恰当，也与 EVAL 顶层形成漂亮的对称；BBN-Lisp 文档明确指出了这种对称性。事实上，若不是以下两点差别，EVALQUOTE 就与 Lisp 1.5 的 APPLY 相同：(a) Lisp 1.5 的 APPLY 接受第三个参数，即环境；今天通常认为这是一个错误，因为它导致动态绑定，而不是忠实反映 lambda 演算所需的词法作用域；(b) “EVALQUOTE 能以某种例外方式处理特殊形式”[McCarthy, 1962]。今天，这种例外被称为 kluge，即权宜拼凑 [Raymond, 1991]。（MacLisp 的 APPLY 也支持这种拼凑。）

McCarthy [McCarthy, 1981] noted that the original Lisp interpreter was regarded as a universal Turing machine: It could perform any computation given a set of instructions (a function) and the initial input on its tape (arguments). Thus it was intended that cons(a b) be regarded not as a mutated version of (cons (quote a) (quote b)), but as a function and (separately) a literal list of arguments. In hindsight we see that the EVALQUOTE top level might better have been called the APPLY top level, making it pleasantly symmetrical to the EVAL top level; the BBN-Lisp documentation brought out this symmetry explicitly. (Indeed, EVALQUOTE would have been identical to the function APPLY in Lisp 1.5 if not for these two differences: (a) in Lisp 1.5, APPLY took a third argument, an environment (regarded nowadays as something of a mistake that resulted in dynamic binding rather than the lexical scoping needed for a faithful reflection of the lambda calculus); and (b) “EVALQUOTE is capable of handling special forms as a sort of exception” [McCarthy, 1962]. Nowadays such an exception is referred to as a kluge [Raymond, 1991]. (Note that MacLisp’s APPLY function supported this kluge.)

特殊形式是一种 Lisp 表达式，其含义由特殊规则决定，而不是遵循默认规则：先对所有子形式求值，得到函数和一系列参数，再调用该函数。例如，表达式 (+ a b) 的含义或操作是先对 a、b 求值，再把函数 + 应用于结果。表达式 (if a b c) 则先对 a 求值；如果结果不是 NIL，就对 b 求值并作为 if 的值，否则对 c 求值并作为其值。就这个意义而言，if 被称为特殊形式。（原文后一条件写作“and if so”，此处按上下文译为“否则”。）

A special form is a Lisp expression whose meaning is determined by a special rule rather than by the default rule of evaluating all subforms to produce a function and a sequence of arguments on which to invoke it. For example, the meaning or operation of the expression (+ a b) is determined by evaluating a and b, and applying the function + to the results. The meaning or operation of the expression (if a b c) is determined by evaluating the expression a, and if the value is not NIL, the expression b is evaluated as the value of the if, and if so the expression c is evaluated as its value. In this sense if is called a special form.

严格说来，把 if 称为特殊形式会引起误解，因为 if 实际上是用于标识某个形式应按特殊形式处理的操作符名称。有些人因此把 if 称为特殊操作符。

Actually, the term special form in reference to if is misleading because if is actually the name of the operator used to distinguish a form that is to be taken as a special form. Some refer to if as a special operator.

这是我们将在 Lisp 社群中多次看到的一种倾向：术语被误用，或以令人混淆的方式使用。

This is an example of a trend we will see several times in the Lisp community—the misuse or confusing use of terminology.

在 Lisp 1.5 和 MacLisp 中，实现特殊形式的方法，是把一个函数放在某个符号的属性表上，该符号的打印名称与特殊形式的名称相同。例如，MacLisp 的 COND 有一个 FSUBR 属性，其中 f 表示特殊形式，subr 表示已编译的子程序。参数如何求值则交给程序员决定。下面是在 MacLisp 中把 IF 定义为特殊形式的一种方式，FEXPR 是 FSUBR 的解释执行版本：

In Lisp 1.5 and MacLisp special forms are implemented by placing a function on the property list of the symbol whose print name corresponds to the special form’s name. For example, in MacLisp, COND has an FSUBR property, where the “f” in FSUBR signifies a special form, and the “subr” signifies a compiled subroutine. The evaluation process for arguments is then left up to the programmer. Here is how IF could be defined as a special form in MacLisp (an FEXPR is the interpreted version of an FSUBR):

(defun IF fexpr (form)
 (let ((predicate (car form))
      (then (cadr form))
      (else (caddr form)))
  (cond ((eval predicate) (eval then))
       (t (eval else)))))

这段代码并未尽可能优化，但它说明，用户编写的代码可以扩展特殊形式。

This code is not as efficient as it could be, but it illustrates that special forms can be extended by user-written code.

MacLisp 引入了 LEXPR：这种函数可以接受任意数量的参数，把它们放在栈上，并把函数唯一的形参绑定为传入参数的数量。形参表使用一个符号而不是列表，表示这是 LEXPR。下面演示如何定义 LIST：它接受可变数量的参数，并返回由这些参数组成的列表：

MacLisp introduced the LEXPR, which is a type of function that takes any number of arguments and puts them on the stack; the single parameter of the function is bound to the number of arguments passed. The form of the lambda-list for this argument—a symbol and not a list—signals the LEXPR case. Here is an example of how to define LIST, a function of a variable number of arguments that returns the list of those arguments:

(defun LIST n
 (do ((i n (1- i))
     (answer () (cons (arg i) answer)))
     ((zerop i) answer)))

形参 n 绑定为传入参数的数量。表达式 (arg i) 引用传入的第 i 个参数。

Parameter n is bound to the number of arguments passed. The expression (arg i) refers to the ith argument passed.

对 LEXPR 及其已编译对应形式 LSUBR 的需求，来自人们希望拥有像 + 这样参数数量可变的函数。尽管语义上并不需要 n 元 +，但对程序员而言，写 (+ a b c d) 比等价却更繁琐的 (+ a (+ b (+ c d))) 方便得多。后面至少还会看到一个例子：在多样化阶段，LEXPR 概念有助于实现新的语言特性。

The need for the LEXPR (and its compiled counterpart, the LSUBR) arose from a desire to have variable arity functions such as +. Though there is no semantic need for an n-ary +, it is convenient for programmers to be able to write (+ a b c d) rather than the equivalent but more cumbersome (+ a (+ b (+ c d))). Later we will see at least one other place where the notion of a LEXPR will be useful for implementing new language features during diversification.

DEFMACRO 所依赖的简单而强大的宏机制，于 20 世纪 60 年代中期在 MacLisp 中引入。见第 3.3 节。

The simple but powerful macro facility on which DEFMACRO is based was introduced in MacLisp in the mid-1960’s. See section 3.3.

相对于 Lisp 1.5，其他主要改进包括数组；把 MEMBER 等简单谓词改成返回有用值的函数；PROG2；以及引入 ERR 函数，使用户代码能够发出错误信号。

Other major improvements over Lisp 1.5 were arrays; the modification of simple predicates— such as MEMBER—to be functions that return useful values; PROG2; and the introduction of the function ERR, which allowed user code to signal an error.

在 Lisp 1.5 中，某些内置函数可能在参数不正确等情况下发出错误信号。通常，这会导致程序终止或调用调试器。Lisp 1.5 还有 ERRSET，可用于受控执行可能出错的代码。特殊形式

In Lisp 1.5, certain built-in functions might signal errors, when given incorrect arguments, for example. Signaling an error normally resulted in program termination or invocation of a debugger. Lisp 1.5 also had the function ERRSET, which was useful for controlled execution of code that might cause an error. The special form

(errset form)

会在错误不会终止程序或进入调试器的上下文中，对 form 求值。如果 form 没有出错，ERRSET 返回一个只包含结果值的单元素列表；如果 form 执行出错，ERRSET 则静默返回 NIL。

evaluates form in a context in which errors do not terminate the program or enter the debugger. If form does not cause an error, ERRSET returns a singleton list of the value. If execution of form does cause an error, the ERRSET form quietly returns NIL.

MacLisp 增加了发出错误信号的 ERR 函数。如果在 ERRSET 形式的动态上下文中调用 ERR，那么传给 ERR 的参数会成为该 ERRSET 形式的返回值。

MacLisp added the function ERR, which signals an error. If ERR is invoked within the dynamic context of an ERRSET form, then the argument to ERR is returned as the value of the ERRSET form.

程序员很快就开始把 ERRSET 和 ERR 用于更一般的控制目的，即动态非局部退出，而不只是捕获和发出错误。不幸的是，这样使用 ERRSET 也会静默捕获意外错误，使程序更难调试。1972 年 6 月，MacLisp 引入了一对新的原语 CATCH 和 THROW，使 ERRSET 可以保留用于其原本的错误捕获用途。

Programmers soon began to use ERRSET and ERR not to trap and signal errors but for more general control purposes (dynamic non-local exits). Unfortunately, this use of ERRSET also quietly trapped unexpected errors, making programs harder to debug. A new pair of primitives, CATCH and THROW, was introduced into MacLisp in June 1972 so that ERRSET could be reserved for its intended use of error trapping.

分析

Analysis

ERRSET 和 CATCH 的教训很重要。ERRSET 和 ERR 的设计者原本针对某种特定情形，定义了一对原语来处理它。然而，这些原语的构造包含两部分：一部分捕获并忽略错误，另一部分把控制转移到动态调用链上更早的位置。由于没有这样的非局部控制转移原语，程序员便开始以非预期方式使用已有机制。于是，设计者不得不回头把人们需要的功能拆分出来。“设计，无论仔细与否、非预期使用、随后重新设计”的模式十分常见。

The lesson of ERRSET and CATCH is important. The designers of ERRSET and ERR had in mind a particular situation and defined a pair of primitives to address that situation. However, the construction of these primitives is in two parts: one part that traps and ignores errors, and another part that transferred control to a dynamically earlier point. Because there were no such non-local control transfer primitives, programmers began to use the existing facilities in unintended ways. Then the designers had to go back and split offthe desired functionality. The pattern of design (careful or otherwise), unintended use, and later redesign is common.

MacLisp 由一个庞大的汇编语言核心、一个解释器和一个编译器构成。前文已经看到，开发者一方面整合其他语言的思想，通常通过合理化处理，另一方面又通过新数据结构使语言多样化。

MacLisp was written as a large assembly language core, an interpreter, and a compiler. Above we saw that the developers of MacLisp were both consolidating some of the ideas from other languages, typically by rationalization, and were diversifying the language with new data structures.

当 MacLisp 开发者看到一个庞大且有影响力的接受群体开始形成，即 Project MAC 和 Mathlab/Macsyma 团队时，MacLisp 进入下一阶段。工作重点转向满足用户社群的需求，而不再只是语言设计和实现本身。

The next phase of MacLisp development began when the developers of MacLisp started to see a large and influential acceptance group emerge—Project MAC and the Mathlab/Macsyma group. The emphasis turned to satisfying the needs of their user community rather than doing language design and implementation as such.

2.2.2 后期 MacLisp

2.2.2 Later MacLisp

在生命周期后期，MacLisp 从其他 Lisp 方言和其他语言中吸收了语言特性，也发明了一些新东西。

During the latter part of its lifecycle, MacLisp adopted language features from other Lisp dialects and from other languages, and some novel things were invented.

MacLisp 最重要的发展发生在 20 世纪 70 年代初：快速算术编译器 LISCOM [Golden, 1970] 的技术被纳入 MacLisp 编译器。这个新编译器 NCOMPLR [Moon, April 1974; White, 1969–1982; Pitman, 1983]，后来成为衡量其他 Lisp 编译器生成代码运行速度的标杆。MIT 人工智能实验室的需求涵盖视觉和机器人中的数值计算，受其推动，人们提出了几种表示和编译数值代码的新方法，使编译后的 MacLisp 数值性能接近 FORTRAN 编译器 [Fateman, 1973]。

The most significant development for MacLisp occurred in the early 1970’s when the techniques in the “fast arithmetic compiler” LISCOM [Golden, 1970] were incorporated into the MacLisp compiler. This new compiler, NCOMPLR [Moon, April 1974; White, 1969–1982; Pitman, 1983], would become a standard against which all other Lisp compilers were measured in terms of the speed of running code. Inspired by the needs of the MIT Artificial Intelligence Laboratory (AI Lab), whose needs covered the numeric computations done in vision and robotics, several new ways of representing and compiling numeric code resulted in numeric performance of compiled MacLisp on a near par with FORTRAN compilers [Fateman, 1973].

LISCOM 主要出自 Jeff Golden 之手。PDP-10 上已编译 Lisp 数值代码与 FORTRAN 数值代码的相对性能，刺激了 Digital 的编译器开发者改进 DEC FORTRAN 编译器，直到 Lisp 编译器难以与之竞争。后来的 Lisp 编译器在数值代码上的相对性能，再也没有达到 1972 年的高峰，尽管有些表现相当不错。

LISCOM was largely the work of JeffGolden. The relative performances of compiled numeric Lisp code and FORTRAN numeric code on the PDP-10 stirred the Digital compiler writers to improve the DEC FORTRAN compiler to the point where it was difficult for the Lisp compilers to compete. The relative performance of subsequent Lisp compilers on numeric code has never reached the high-water mark achieved in 1972, but some Lisp compilers have done very well.

为了满足 Macsyma 用户的需求，MacLisp 在 1970 年或 1971 年加入了大整数，即任意精度整数运算。代码大体忠实地转写了 [Knuth, 1969] 中的算法。后来 Bill Gosper 提出了一些改进，尤其是一种 GCD 版本，结合了二进制最大公约数算法的优点和 Lehmer 加速大整数除法的方法 [Knuth, 1981, ex. 4.5.2-34]。

Bignums—arbitrary precision integer arithmetic—were added in 1970 or 1971 to meet the needs of Macsyma users. The code was a more or less faithful transcription of the algorithms in [Knuth, 1969]. Later Bill Gosper suggested some improvements, notably a version of GCD that combined the good features of the binary GCD algorithm with Lehmer’s method for speeding up integer bignum division [Knuth, 1981, ex. 4.5.2-34].

1973 年和 1974 年，David Moon 领导了在 Multics 下的 Honeywell 6180 上实现 MacLisp 的工作。作为项目的一部分，他编写了第一部真正全面的 MacLisp 参考手册，人们亲切地称之为“Moonual”[Moon, April 1974]。

In 1973 and 1974, David Moon led an effort to implement MacLisp on the Honeywell 6180 under Multics. As a part of this project he wrote the first truly comprehensive reference manual for Maclisp, which became familiarly known as the “Moonual” [Moon, April 1974].

Richard Greenblatt 于 1974 年启动 MIT Lisp Machine 项目；David Moon、Richard Stallman 和许多 MIT 人工智能实验室的 Lisp 黑客最终加入。随着项目推进，一些语言特性被选择性地移植回 PDP-10 MacLisp，两个项目相互启发。

Richard Greenblatt started the MIT Lisp Machine project in 1974; David Moon, Richard Stallman, and many other MIT AI Lab Lisp hackers eventually joined this project. As this project progressed, language features were selectively retrofitted into PDP-10 MacLisp as the two projects cross-fertilized.

复杂 lambda 形参表的出现，部分受到 MDL [Galley, 1975] 的影响。MDL 是 MIT 动态建模组使用的语言，运行于一台 PDP-10，该机器与人工智能和 Mathlab 的机器放在同一机房。Lisp 1.5 过于简朴的语法，不足以清晰表达函数参数的不同角色。复杂 lambda 形参表作为解决方案出现并被广泛接受，最终却使原本优雅的 Common Lisp Object System 变得极其复杂。

Complex lambda lists partly arose by influence from MDL [Galley, 1975], which was a language for the Dynamic Modeling Group at MIT. It ran on a PDP-10 located in the same machine room as the AI and Mathlab machines. The austere syntax of Lisp 1.5 was not quite powerful enough to express clearly the different roles of arguments to a function. Complex lambda-lists appeared as a solution to this problem and became widely accepted; eventually they terribly complicated the otherwise elegant Common Lisp Object System.

MDL 也是 MIT 开发的一门与 Lisp 相关的语言，早先名为“Muddle”，这个名字不太讨资助者喜欢。它对主流 Lisp 的演化产生了强烈影响。它引入了 lambda 形参表选项，例如用 "OPTIONAL" 表示可选参数，用 "AUX" 定义辅助变量，用 "ARGS" 表示 &rest 变量，即绑定到剩余参数列表的变量，还有一些从未进入主流 Lisp 方言的选项。MDL 有 locative，可描述为 C 意义上的左值，但它是一等对象：本质上是指向存储位置的指针，可以像变量一样读写这些位置，在 C++ 中这一概念称为引用。MDL 还有拼接标记，使一串值，称为 segment，可以展开为参数；Common Lisp 反引号语法中的 ,@ 就源于此。MDL 还通过示例引入了在名称末尾加 ? 表示谓词的惯例，与主流 Lisp 使用尾缀 P 不同。Scheme 现在采用“合理的” ? 惯例，Common Lisp 则采用“传统的” P 惯例，因此只对数字返回真的谓词，在 Scheme 中叫 NUMBER?，在 Common Lisp 中叫 NUMBERP。

MDL—its earlier, less-palatable-to-funders name was “Muddle”—was a Lisp-related language, also developed at MIT, that had a strong influence on mainstream Lisp evolution. It introduced lambda-list options such as "OPTIONAL" to denote optional arguments, "AUX" for defining auxiliary variables, "ARGS" for a &rest variable (one that is bound to a list of the remaining arguments), and some others that never made it into mainstream Lisp dialects. MDL had locatives, which might be described as “lvals” (in the C sense), only first-class; essentially they are pointers to memory locations and can be treated like variables to read and write those locations (in C++ this concept is known as references). MDL had a splicing marker to enable a sequence of values (called a segment) to be spread as arguments; the splicing marker ,@ used in Common Lisp’s backquote syntax is descended from this. MDL also introduced through example the use of a final ? in a name to connote a predicate, in contrast to mainstream Lisp dialects, which used a trailing P. (Scheme now uses the “sensible” ? convention and Common Lisp the “traditional” P convention, so the predicate that is true of numbers and only numbers is called NUMBER? in Scheme and NUMBERP in Common Lisp.)

Lisp-Machine Lisp 吸收了 lambda 形参表和 locative，并把求值与拼接标记改用于反引号。不过，这一概念的演化经过了 Conniver [?]，在那里被改进成一种在外观和效果上与 Lisp-Machine Lisp 很相似、但实现截然不同的形式。Scheme 吸收了用尾缀 ? 表示谓词的惯例。MDL 的多任务机制也以各种形式重新出现在 Lisp-Machine Lisp、Conniver 和多个 Common Lisp 实现中。

Lisp-Machine Lisp would pick up the lambda lists and the locatives, and would adapt the evaluation and splicing markers in backquote, though the evolution of the concept passes through Conniver [?] where it is refined into a form very similar in appearance and effect as in Lisp-Machine Lisp, but in fact the implementation is quite different. Scheme would pick up the use of a trailing “?” for predicates. MDL had a multitasking facility that would reappear in various forms in Lisp-Machine Lisp, Conniver, and various implementations of Common Lisp.

MacLisp 引入了读取表（read table）的概念。读取表为程序和数据提供可编程的输入语法。输入字符时，系统查表以确定该字符的语法属性，并据此组成词法单元，例如判断哪些字符表示空白。还可以把函数与字符关联，使读取某个字符时调用相应函数；该函数可以继续读取输入，再返回一个值，纳入正在读取的数据结构。这样，用户就能重新编程内置解析器。这一强大机制使试验 Lisp 的替代输入语法变得容易，从以 'x 表示 (quote x) 的简单缩写，到反引号机制和复杂的 Algol 风格解析器。有关这些试验的更多讨论，见第 3.7.1 节。

MacLisp introduced the notion of read tables. A read table provides programmable input syntax for programs and data. When a character is input, the table is consulted to determine the syntactic characteristics of the character for use in putting together tokens. For example, the table is used to determine which characters denote whitespace. In addition, functions can be associated with characters, so that a function is invoked whenever a given character is read; the function can read further input before returning a value to be incorporated into the data structure being read. In this way the built-in parser can be reprogrammed by the user. This powerful facility made it easy to experiment with alternative input syntaxes for Lisp, ranging from such simple abbreviations as ’x for (quote x) to the backquote facility and elaborate Algol-style parsers. See section 3.7.1 for further discussion of some of these experiments.

借助读取表，MacLisp 曾使用多种不同的表面语法，例如 CGOL [Pratt, 1976]。

With the read table facility, several different surface syntaxes for MacLisp have been used, for example CGOL [Pratt, 1976].

Lisp 一直面临一个问题：有些人觉得它的语法陌生而令人却步，因此犹豫是否采用。许多程序员更喜欢类似 Algol 的语法。1991 年，CAD Framework Initiative 小组投票决定采用 Scheme 作为扩展语言，却希望使用类似 C 的表面语法（原文此处参考文献待补，指向近期一条 Scheme Digest 消息）。类似地，1989 年 Common Prototyping Language 研究组 [?] 建议采用一种具有许多 Lisp 特性、但表面语法更熟悉的语言。

A continuing problem with Lisp has been the hesitancy of people to adopt it because its syntax is unfamiliar and forbidding to some. Many programmers prefer an Algol-like syntax. In 1991, the CAD Framework Initiative group (??? reference is to a recent Scheme Digest message) voted to use Scheme as its extension language, but expressed a desire to use a C-like surface syntax. Similarly, the 1989 Common Prototyping Language study group [?] recommended using a language with many Lisp features but with a familiar surface syntax.

1971 年，Jon L White（原文如此）把用于将名称，即字符串，映射到符号的数据结构，从列表改成哈希表 [White, 1969–1982]。因此，Lisp 1.5 传统的 OBLIST，即对象列表，也就是具名原子列表，被更名为 OBARRAY。这标志着实现者开始更谨慎地选择数据结构，以优化规模或速度。后来的 Lisp 实现在内部更加依赖哈希表。OBARRAY 的引入，是 Lisp 实现者技术成熟度提高的重要一步。

In 1971, Jon L White (sic) changed the representation of the data structure that maps names (strings) to symbols from a list to a hash table [White, 1969–1982]; thus the traditional OBLIST (list of objects, that is, named atoms) of Lisp 1.5 was renamed the OBARRAY. This signaled a move to a more careful choice of data structures to optimize size or speed. Later implementations of Lisp relied even more heavily on hash tables for parts of their implementations. The introduction of the OBARRAY marked an important step in the increase of sophistication of Lisp implementors.

Jon L White 通常被称为“Jonl”，可以像 O’Donnell 那样读作“j´onnell”，也可以读作“john-ell”。因此，他的名字通常写成“Jon L White”，而不是正确的“John L. White”。

Jon L White was commonly known as “Jonl”, which can be pronounced as either “j´onnell” like “O’Donnell” or “john-ell”. Because of this, his name is usually written “Jon L White” rather than the correct “John L. White.”

1974 年，MacLisp 的输入输出系统得到重大改进。在此之前，一个 MacLisp 程序同一时间只能打开一个输入文件和一个输出文件，另有与键盘、终端屏幕的独立连接。在所谓 NEWIO 系统中，打开文件的数量只受操作系统限制。ITS 的上限为 16，在当时被认为已经很多！在 NEWIO 中，文件，更准确地说是打开的文件连接，成为一等对象，可以作为参数传递、作为值返回，并被垃圾回收。实现中最棘手的问题之一，是确保回收文件对象时能干净地关闭文件。

Big improvements to the MacLisp I/O system were implemented in 1974. Prior to that point, a MacLisp program could have only one input file and one out file open at a time, plus separate connections to the keyboard and terminal screen. Under the so-called NEWIO system, the number of open files was limited only by the operating system. (ITS afforded an upper limit of 16, which in that day was considered a very large number!) Under NEWIO files, or rather open file connections, became first-class objects that could be passed as arguments, returned as values, and garbagecollected. (One of the trickiest parts of the implementation was ensuring that files were closed cleanly as file objects were reclaimed.)

在 Unix 尚未普及的年代，操作系统设计与编程语言设计一样，充满试验。正如每个实验室可能有自己的 Lisp 方言，各实验室运行不同的操作系统也不稀奇：即便不是完全自行开发，也往往是从别处取得后本地修改的版本。1978 年，人们希望把 MIT 用 MacLisp 编写的软件分享给其他机构。虽然大多数主要实验室也有一台甚至两台 PDP-10，却没有一家运行 ITS。因此，MacLisp 必须移植到另外五种 PDP-10 操作系统上：

In the days before Unix became widespread, there was as much experimentation in the design of operating systems as in the design of programming languages. Just as each laboratory might have its own dialect of Lisp, it was not unusual for each laboratory to run a different operating system—if not completely home-grown, then a local mutation of one obtained elsewhere. In 1978, there was a desire to share MIT software written in MacLisp with other institutions. While most major laboratories also had a PDP-10, if not two, none of them ran ITS. MacLisp therefore had to be ported to five other PDP-10 operating systems:

• TOPS-10：DEC 自己的 PDP-10 操作系统，以两个用十进制表示的 18 位整数来标识用户和文件目录，例如 483,11823。

• TOPS-10, DEC’s own operating system for the PDP-10, in which users and file directories were identified by two 18-bit integers expressed in decimal, such as 483,11823

• CMU TOPS-10：卡内基梅隆大学本地修改的版本，用 N920JW51 这样的助记标识符，而不是整数对，来标识用户和文件目录。这里 JW 代表 Jon White，5 表示账户于 1978 年分配，1 表示他是当年第二位首字母为 JW 的用户。注意，两个字母和两个数字可以编码进 18 位。

• CMU TOPS-10, which had been locally modified at Carnegie-Mellon University to identify users and file directories by mnemonic identifiers such as N920JW51 rather than pairs of integers (in this example, JW stands for Jon White, 5 means the account was assigned in 1978, and 1 means he was the second user with initials JW that year—note that two letters and two digits are encodable in 18 bits)

• WAITS [?]：斯坦福对 TOPS-10 大幅修改的版本，用两个最多三个字符的单词标识用户和文件目录，例如 LSP,DOC 表示 Lisp 文档，1,RPG 表示 Richard P. Gabriel。注意，三个不含小写字母的 6 位字符，恰好可以放进 18 位！

• WAITS [?], a much more extensively mutated version of TOPS-10 at Stanford, which identified users and file directories by two words of up to three characters each (such as LSP,DOC for Lisp documentation or 1,RPG for Richard P. Gabriel—note that three 6-bit characters (no lower case) fit into 18 bits!)

• TENEX：由 BBN 开发，与 TOPS-10 截然不同，用户名、目录名和文件名最长可达 31 个字符。

• TENEX, developed at BBN and radically different from TOPS-10, with user, directory, and file names of up to 31 characters

• TOPS-20：DEC 认真吸收 TENEX 并加以修饰后的产物。

• TOPS-20, the result of DEC’s taking TENEX to heart and gussying it up

这项移植工作直接却繁琐，需要在 MacLisp 的主汇编源文件中插入数百条条件汇编命令，大部分位于输入输出例程中。

This porting effort was straightforward but tedious, requiring hundreds of conditional-assembly commands to be inserted into the master assembly-language source files for MacLisp, mostly in the I/O routines.

分析

Analysis

MacLisp 可以看作对 20 世纪 60 年代初纷纷出现的 Lisp 实现的一次整合。有一个特定的接受群体，即 Project MAC，推动整合，形成了一个源自 Lisp 1.5、稳定而高性能的实现。因此，我们看到了这一循环的第一个实例：Lisp 1.5 时代的多样化，MIT Project MAC 初期的接受，Project MAC 鼎盛时期的整合，以及后文将看到的、发生在新一轮多样化之前的 MIT Lisp 经费衰减。

MacLisp can be seen as one consolidation of the flurry of Lisp implementations in the early 1960’s. There was a particular acceptance group—Project MAC—that drove consolidation into a stable, high performance implementation of a derivative of Lisp 1.5. Therefore, we can see the first example of the cycle: diversification during the Lisp 1.5 era, acceptance at the start of the MIT Project MAC era, consolidation during the heyday of Project MAC, and, as we’ll see, a decline of funding for Lisp at MIT preceding a period of diversification.

1969 至 1981 年间，MacLisp 先后得到几个接受群体的支持：1969 年至约 1973 年是人工智能实验室，尤其是视觉组；1972 年至约 1981 年则是 Mathlab/Macsyma 团队，尽管更早由 William Martin 领导的 Mathlab 团队也有很大影响。

During the period from 1969 until 1981, MacLisp enjoyed several acceptance groups: from 1969 until around 1973 it was the AI Lab, in particular the vision group. From at 1972 until around 1981 it was the Mathlab/Macsyma group, though the earlier Mathlab group under William Martin was a strong influence.

人工智能实验室开展一般性的人工智能研究，但重点是视觉、机器人、自然语言、规划和表示。此外，也有人对面向人工智能的语言设计感兴趣，例如 Carl Hewitt、Terry Winograd、Gerry Sussman 和 Guy Steele。

The AI Lab conducted research into artificial intelligence generally, but it focussed on vision, robotics, natural language, planning, and representation. In addition there was some interest in language design for AI, as exemplified by Carl Hewitt, Terry Winograd, Gerry Sussman, and Guy Steele.

Mathlab 和 Macsyma 团队关注符号数学。这门学科开发算法、数据结构和程序，以符号方式操作数学的结构与概念。例如，符号微分，与数值微分相对，是最早的符号数学程序之一。

The Mathlab and Macsyma groups were interested in symbolic mathematics, which is a discipline that develops algorithms, data structures, and programs to symbolically manipulate the structures and concepts of mathematics. For example, symbolic differentiation (as opposed to numeric differentiation) is one of the first symbolic mathematics programs.

从 1972 年到 1983 年（原文此处有“???”待核标记），MacLisp 的支持由 Macsyma 团队提供；该团队得到能源部的经费，用于 Macsyma 联盟和一些新开发工作。Macsyma 联盟由一组在工作中使用 Macsyma 的机构组成，例如劳伦斯利弗莫尔国家实验室（原文此处亦有“???”）。这些成员通常也得到能源部资助。

From 1972 to 1983 ??? the support for MacLisp was provided by the Macsyma group which had Department of Energy funding for the Macsyma Consortium and some new development. The Macsyma Consortium was a group of institutions, for example Lawrence Livermore National Laboratories ???, that used Macsyma for their work. Generally these members were also funded by DOE.

因此，MacLisp 拥有一个把它接受为标准的群体。不过，由于 MacLisp 只运行于 PDP-10，几乎没有必要通过与其他语言整合来标准化这门语言。然而，

Thus MacLisp had an acceptance group, which had accepted MacLisp as its standard. Nevertheless, because MacLisp ran only on PDP-10’s, there was little need to standardize the language through consolidation with other languages. However,

MacLisp 只从其他 Lisp 方言吸收了少量特性。1974 年，约十几人在 MIT 参加了 MacLisp 与 Interlisp 实现者之间的一次会议，其中包括 Warren Teitelman、Alice Hartley、Jon L White、Jeff Golden 和 Guy Steele。人们原本希望找到实质性的共同基础，但会议实际上揭示了两组人之间从实现细节到整体设计理念的巨大鸿沟。（双方都不愿放弃既定策略，很可能部分源于 PDP-10 已经十分严峻的资源限制：这是一台内存约一兆字节、速度约一 MIPS 的机器。后来 MIT Lisp 机器出现，速度更快、地址空间大得多，那些曾主张“小巧强大的执行环境，加上独立编程工具”的人，也转而拥抱用 Lisp 编写工具、把 Lisp 环境变成完整编程环境的策略。）最终，这次“MacLisp/Interlisp 大峰会”只促成了微不足道的特性交换：MacLisp 从 Interlisp 接受了 (CAR NIL) → NIL 和 (CDR NIL) → NIL 的行为，而 Interlisp 接受了读取表的概念。

MacLisp adopted only a small number of features from other Lisp dialects. In 1974, about a dozen persons attended a meeting at MIT between the MacLisp and Interlisp implementors, including Warren Teitelman, Alice Hartley, Jon L White, JeffGolden, and Guy Steele. There was some hope of finding substantial common ground, but the meeting actually served to illustrate the great chasm separating the two groups, in everything from implementation details to overall design philosophy. (Much of the unwillingness of each side to depart from its chosen strategy probably stemmed from the already severe resource constraints on the PDP-10, a one-megabyte, one-MIPS machine. With the advent of the MIT Lisp Machines, with their greater speed and much greater address space, the crowd that had once advocated a small, powerful execution environment with separate programming tools embraced the strategy of writing programming tools in Lisp and turning the Lisp environment into a complete programming environment.) In the end only a trivial exchange of features resulted from “the great MacLisp/Interlisp summit”: MacLisp adopted from Interlisp the behavior (CAR NIL) →NIL and (CDR NIL) →NIL, and Interlisp adopted the concept of a read table.

采用 Interlisp 对 NIL 的处理方式，并未得到所有人的热情欢迎。下面引用 Jon L White 的公开公告 [White, 1969–1982，1974 年 7 月 9 日，第 2 项]：

The adoption of the Interlisp treatment of NIL was not received with universal warmth. We quote the public announcement by Jon L White [White, 1969–1982, July 9, 1974, item 2]:

为了与 Interlisp 兼容（哼），NIL 的 CAR 和 CDR 永远、永远都是 NIL。NIL 仍然有属性表，GET 和 PUTPROP 也仍然可用于它，但 NIL 的属性表不再是它的 CDR（烂招，烂招）。据说这样就能写出如下代码：

For compatibility with Interlisp (foo), the CAR and CDR of NIL are always but always NIL. NIL still has a property list, and GET and PUTPROP still work on it, but NIL’s property list is not its CDR (crock, crock). The claim is that one can write code such as

(CADDR X)

而不必使用更耗时、也更占空间的写法：

instead of the more timeand space-consuming

(AND (CDR X) (CDDR X) (CADDR X))

诸如此类。请把抱怨发给 GLS，不过我怀疑这对你不会有什么用。

and so on. Send complaints to GLS, but I doubt it will do you any good.

这里需要稍作解释。问题的部分根源在于，在 MacLisp 中，对原子符号应用 CDR 会返回该符号的属性表。经过这一改动，符号 NIL 就与所有其他符号不同：再也不能通过这种方式取得它的属性表。CDR 恰好能这样处理符号，本来只是实现上的偶然，却被用户大量依赖。这又是一个特性被用于非预期用途的例子，与前面 ERRSET 和 ERR 的情形类似。因此，CDR 的约定并不统一：用于点对时做一件事，用于符号时却做另一件在抽象层面毫不相关、只是碰巧由同一条机器指令实现的事。解决办法是补全属性表的抽象操作集合；1975 年，即为兼容 Interlisp 而改动约九个月后，MacLisp 引入了 PLIST 和 SETPLIST，从此不必再对符号使用 CDR 和 RPLACD。

A few words of explanation are in order. Part of the source of the problem is that, in MacLisp, CDR applied to an atomic symbol returned the symbol’s property list. With this change, the symbol NIL becomes unlike all other symbols in there being no way to get its property list. That CDR happened to work this way on symbols was an accident of implementation that users began to rely on heavily—this is another example of the unintended use of a feature that we saw with ERRSET and ERR. The contract for CDR, then, was not uniform: when applied to dotted pairs it did one thing, and when applied to symbols it did an abstractly unrelated thing that happened to be implemented by the same machine instruction. The solution was to complete the set of abstract operations for property lists; PLIST and SETPLIST were introduced to MacLisp in 1975, about nine months after the Interlisp compatibility change, so that CDR and RPLACD need not be used on symbols.

用户可以参考的 MacLisp 信息来源如下：

These were the sources of information about MacLisp that users could reference:

1. 最初的 PDP-6 Lisp 手册 [PDP-6 Lisp, 1967] 及一份更新 [?]。

1. The original PDP-6 Lisp manual [PDP-6 Lisp, 1967] and an update [?].)

2. LISP ARCHIV [White, 1969–1982]：一份完整的在线日志，列出语言中新增加和删除的每项特性。每次 MacLisp 发布新版，发布说明就加在日志开头。该文件从 1969 年一直维护到 1981 年 Jon L White 离开 MIT、前往 Xerox 时为止。（这种奇怪拼写源于 ITS 的设计：文件名最多由两个部分组成，每部分最多 6 个字符。在采用 6 加 3 字符限制、且标点规则不同的 TOPS-10 上，该文件名为 LISP.ARC。）

2. LISP ARCHIV [White, 1969–1982], a complete on-line log listing each newly added and deleted feature of the language. With each new release of MacLisp, release notes were prepended to the log. This file was maintained from 1969 until 1981, when Jon L White left MIT for Xerox. (The strange spelling is a consequence of the design of ITS, which limited file names to two components of at most 6 character each. On TOPS-10, with its 6-and-3 limits and different punctuation, the file was called LISP.ARC.)

3. 1974 年版《MacLisp 手册》[Moon, April 1974]，由 David A. Moon 在把 MacLisp 移植到 Multics 的过程中编写。这份宝贵文档通常被口头称为“Moonual”。

3. The 1974 edition of the MacLisp Manual [Moon, April 1974], written by David A. Moon as part of the effort to put MacLisp up on Multics. This valuable document was usually referred to orally as the “Moonual.”

4. Jon L White 发表的一篇 MacLisp 简史 [?]。

4. Jon L White published a short history of MacLisp [?].

5. Kent Pitman 于 1983 年编写的《MacLisp 修订手册》。

5. The Revised MacLisp Manual by Kent Pitman, written in 1983.

6. “走廊那头的高手”：MIT 的实现者 Jon L White 和 Guy Steele，以及各地的 MacLisp 专家，例如斯坦福大学的 Richard P. Gabriel、澳大利亚某大学（原文为 ??? University）的 Rodney A. Brooks、CMU 的 David Touretzky，以及伊利诺伊大学的 Timothy Finin。就像 Ray Bradbury 的《华氏 451 度》中的流亡者，这些或许有些狂热的黑客本身就是文档，只要有人询问，就能背出所需信息。从今天的角度看，这种情况也许令人遗憾，但对一个围绕快速变化的软件开展工作的小社群来说，却行得通。

6. “The wizard down the hall”: implementors Jon L White and Guy Steele at MIT, and local MacLisp gurus such as Richard P. Gabriel at Stanford University, Rodney A. Brooks at ??? University in Australia, David Touretzky at CMU, and Timothy Finin at the University of Illinois. Like the exiles in Ray Bradbury’s Fahrenheit 451, these perhaps fanatical hackers were the documentation and would recite necessary information on request—a deplorable situation, perhaps, from today’s perspective, but workable in a small community working with a rapidly changing piece of software.

7. 源代码。MacLisp 社群精神的一部分，就是大家知道：只要对语言的行为有任何疑问，都可以去读代码。

7. The source code. Part of the spirit of the MacLisp community was the knowledge that when there was any doubt about what the language did, one could read the code.

请注意，MacLisp 主要运行于 PDP-10，且只有一套集中维护的源代码文件，因此，这个规模较小、联系紧密的社群几乎不觉得有必要另立一份完整的语言规范。MacLisp 开始跨越两种机器架构时产生的 Moonual，恰好是印证规则的例外；但原有规则仍占上风，因为 PDP-10 圈子把 Multics 社群视为外人，欢迎他们在 LISP ARCHIV 公布新变化时努力跟上。Moonual 此后九年都没有修订，直到 Kent Pitman 编写了后来被称为“Pitmanual”的手册 [Pitman, 1983]。

Note that MacLisp ran primarily on PDP-10’s, and there was a single, central set of source code files, so its relatively small and tightly-knit community felt little need for a separate, complete language specification. (The Moonual, produced when MacLisp began to straddle two machine architectures, is the exception that proves the rule; but the rule stood, for the PDP-10 enclave regarded the Multics community as outsiders, welcome to try to keep up as new changes were announced in LISP ARCHIV. The Moonual was not revised for another nine years, when Kent Pitman produced what became known as the “Pitmanual” [Pitman, 1983].

相比之下，Interlisp 团队为分布更广的用户社群编写了全面的参考手册，并另行制定了虚拟机规范，以帮助把 Interlisp 移植到不同计算机架构。随着 MacLisp 社群演变为 MIT Lisp 机器社群，更好的文档成为必需品，并最终问世，这再次要归功于 David Moon，这一次还有 Daniel Weinreb [Weinreb, November 1978]。这本手册被称为“chine nual”，读作 sheenual，因为大号粗体书名 Lisp Machine Manual 环绕整个平装书封面，正面只露出这些字母。

The Interlisp effort, by contrast, produced a comprehensive reference manual for its more farflung user community and a separate virtual machine specification that aided in porting Interlisp to different computer architectures. As the MacLisp community metamorphosed into the MIT Lisp Machine community, better documentation became a necessity and eventually appeared, thanks again to David Moon, this time with Daniel Weinreb [Weinreb, November 1978]. (This one was called the “chine nual” (pronounced sheenual) because the title Lisp Machine Manual in big block letters was wrapped around the entire paperback cover so that only those letters showed on the front.)

还有若干其他编程语言是在 MacLisp 内部开发的，例如 Micro-Planner、Conniver 和 Scheme。人们也试验了多种面向对象扩展，包括 Extend 和 Flavors。Extend [Burke, 1983] 是一种通过继承扩展 Lisp 内置类型系统的机制；Flavors 将在后文详细讨论。

Several other programming languages were developed within MacLisp: Micro-Planner, Conniver, and Scheme to name a few. Several object-oriented extensions to MacLisp were tried out, including Extend and Flavors. Extend [Burke, 1983] is a mechanism to extend the builtin Lisp type system using inheritance, and Flavors will be discussed in detail below.

到 20 世纪 70 年代中期，随着 Lisp 程序不断变大，PDP-10 的地址空间限制，即 256K 个 36 位字，约一兆字节，越来越明显地成为严重制约。此时，MacLisp 已在规模虽小但影响力很大的用户社群中得到近十年的广泛使用与接受。事实将证明，以大型汇编语言核心为基础的实现策略，使这一方言难以保持原状继续发展；其他方言、其他语言带来的思想压力，以及实现者自身的语言设计抱负，将推动 Lisp 走向新的方向。

By the mid-1970’s it was becoming increasingly apparent that the address space limitation of the PDP-10—256k 36-bit words, or about one megabyte—was becoming a severe constraint as the size of Lisp programs grew. MacLisp by this time had enjoyed nearly 10 years of strong use and acceptance within its somewhat small but very influential user community. Its implementation strategy of a large assembly language core would prove to be too much to stay with the dialect as it stood, and intellectual pressures from other dialects, other languages, and the language design aspirations of its implementors would result in new directions for Lisp.

对许多人来说，MacLisp 稳定使用的时期是一个黄金时代，Lisp 世界里一切都恰到好处。（今天，许多怀旧者也把同一时期视为人工智能的黄金时代。）到 1980 年，MacLisp 的接受群体开始衰退，Macsyma 的经费也维持不了太久。人工智能领域接连发生的经费危机，削弱了人工智能实验室的 Lisp 高手队伍；来自 MIT 及其周边的核心高手转往其他机构。20 世纪 70 年代末和 80 年代初，是一个多样化时期。

To many, the period of stable MacLisp use was a golden era in which all was right with the world of Lisp. (This same period is also regarded today by many nostalgics as the golden era of Artificial Intelligence.) By 1980 the acceptance group for MacLisp was on the decline—the funding for Macsyma would not last too long. Various funding crises in AI had depleted the ranks of the AI Lab Lisp wizards, and the core group of wizards from MIT and MIT hangers-on moved to new institutions. The late part of the 1970’s and early part of the 1980’s was a period of diversification.

前面出现过的一些名字还会再次出现：White、Steele、Moon、Greenblatt、McCarthy、Deutsch、Bobrow 和 Gabriel；其余人则会淡出这段叙述。

Several names we have seen will reappear—White, Steele, Moon, Greenblatt, McCarthy, Deutsch, Bobrow, and Gabriel—and the rest will disappear.

2.3 Interlisp

2.3 Interlisp

Interlisp 及其前身 BBN-Lisp 为 Lisp 的编程风格和方法论引入了许多激进思想。其中最显眼的体现在编程工具中，例如拼写纠正器、文件包、DWIM、CLISP、结构编辑器和 MASTERSCOPE。

Interlisp (and BBN-Lisp before it) introduced many radical ideas into Lisp programming style and methodology. The most visible of these ideas are embodied in programming tools, such as the spelling corrector, the file package, DWIM, CLISP, the structure editor, and MASTERSCOPE.

这些思想的起源，可以在 Warren Teitelman 关于人机共生的博士论文 [Teitelman, 1966] 中找到。尤其是，结构编辑，相对于“文本”或“纸带”编辑 [Rudloe, 1962]，以及断点、advice 和 CLISP 的根源都在其中。（William Henneman 在 1964 年描述过 A-language 的翻译器 [Henneman, 1985]，它为 Lisp 提供类似英语或 Algol 的表面语法，见第 3.7.1 节，但其复杂程度和灵活性远不及 CLISP。Henneman 的工作似乎并未直接影响 Teitelman；至少 Teitelman 没有引用它，尽管他引用了收录 Henneman 论文的同一论文集中的其他论文 [Berkeley, 1985]。）

The origin of these ideas can be found in Warren Teitelman’s PhD dissertation on man-computer symbiosis [Teitelman, 1966]. In particular, it contains the roots of structure editing (as opposed to “text” or “tape” editing [Rudloe, 1962]), breakpointing, advice, and CLISP. (William Henneman in 1964 described a translator for the A-language [Henneman, 1985], an English-like or Algollike surface syntax for Lisp (see section 3.7.1), but it was not nearly as elaborate or as flexible as CLISP. Henneman’s work does not appear to have directly influenced Teitelman; at least, Teitelman does not cite it, though he cites other papers in the same collection containing Henneman’s paper [Berkeley, 1985].)

拼写纠正器和 DWIM 的设计，是为了弥补人的小失误。当一个符号没有值，或没有函数定义时，Interlisp 拼写纠正器 [Teitelman, 1974] 就会被调用，因为该符号可能拼错了。纠正器把可能拼错的符号与已知词语列表比较。用户可以选择控制系统的拼写纠正行为，系统会采取三种做法之一：(a) 自动纠正；(b) 暂停并询问建议的纠正是否可以接受；(c) 仅仅报告错误。

The spelling corrector and DWIM were designed to compensate for human foibles. When a symbol had no value (or no function definition), the Interlisp spelling corrector [Teitelman, 1974] was invoked, because the symbol might have been misspelled. The spelling corrector compared a possibly misspelled symbol with a list of known words. The user had options for controlling the behavior of the system with respect to spelling correction. The system would do one of three things: (a) correct automatically; (b) pause and ask whether a proposed correction were acceptable; or (c) simply signal an error.

拼写纠正器受一个大得多的程序统一控制，这个程序叫 DWIM，即“Do What I Mean”，按我的意思做。Interlisp 检测到任何类型的错误时，都会调用 DWIM 来决定适当的行动。DWIM 能纠正某些括号错误；这类错误与标识符拼写错误一起，构成了用户最常见的输入错误。

The spelling corrector was under the general control of a much larger program, called DWIM, for “Do What I Mean”. Whenever an error of any sort was detected by the Interlisp system, DWIM was invoked to determine the appropriate action. DWIM was able to correct some forms of parenthesis errors, which, along with the misspelling of identifiers, comprised the most common typographical errors by users.

如果纠正不能永久保留，DWIM 就不会特别有用。谁愿意日复一日地确认同一个纠正呢？Interlisp 维护函数定义文件的方式，与 MacLisp 等系统不同。对 MacLisp 用户而言，ASCII 字符文件是程序的主要表示形式；这些文件随后被读入运行中的系统，或预先编译成二进制代码以供日后加载。Interlisp 也把文件用作运行中 Lisp 系统之外的非易失存储介质，保存用户源代码，但用户很少直接查看文件，也不在意其格式。用户代码的所有修改都在 Interlisp 编程环境内进行。用户不是用 ASCII 编辑器修改文件，而是使用常驻的 Lisp 结构编辑器，编辑程序的 S 表达式表示。

DWIM would not have been especially useful if correcting errors were not done permanently. (Who wants to acknowledge the same correction day after day?) Interlisp did not maintain a file of function definitions in the same way as, say, MacLisp. To a MacLisp user, a file of ASCII characters was the primary representation of a program; these files were then read into a running system or precompiled into binary code for later loading. In Interlisp, files were also used as a nonvolatile storage medium, external to the running Lisp system, for storing user source code; but the user seldom examined his file directly, nor cared about its format. All modifications to user code were done within the Interlisp programming environment. An Interlisp user did not modify files using an ASCII editor, but instead used a resident Lisp structure editor to edit the S-expression representation of the program.

Interlisp 借助文件所逼近的理想状态，是用户始终与一个永不终止的 Lisp 系统交互；重要的是正在使用中的程序，而不是它在文件中的文本表示。DWIM 纠错时，会修改内存中的程序表示以体现纠正；从用户角度看，作为几乎附带的副作用，充当程序表示非易失备份介质的文件也会更新。因此，一旦错误被纠正，就永久纠正了。

The ideal situation that was approximated by Interlisp, using files, was that the user interacted with a Lisp system that never terminated, and it was the program as it was in use that was important, not its representation as text in a file. When errors were corrected by DWIM, the inmemory program representation was altered to reflect the correction and, as an almost incidental side effect from the user’s point of view, the file that served as the nonvolatile backup medium for the program representation also was updated. Thus, once a bug was corrected, it was permanently corrected.

CLISP，即 Conversational LISP，是嵌入普通 Interlisp 语法中的一种混合了 Algol 风格和英语风格的语法。下面是用 Interlisp CLISP 语法编写的一个有效 FACTORIAL 定义：

CLISP (Conversational LISP) was a mixed Algol-like and English-like syntax embedded within normal Interlisp syntax. Here is a valid definition of FACTORIAL written in Interlisp CLISP syntax:

DEFINEQ((FACTORIAL
(LAMBDA (N) (IF N=0 THEN 1 ELSE N*(FACTORIAL N-1)))))

CLISP 也依赖通用的 DWIM 机制。注意，它实际上不仅必须重新排列词法单元、插入括号，还必须把 N=0 和 N* 这样的原子拆分成若干适当的词法单元。因此，用户无需在中缀运算符两侧加空格。

CLISP also depended on the generic DWIM mechanism. Note that it not only must, in effect, rearrange tokens and insert parentheses, but also must split atoms such as N=0 and N* into several appropriate tokens. Thus the user need not put spaces around infix operators.

CLISP 定义了一组实用的迭代构造。下面这个简单程序打印 m ≤ p ≤ n 范围内的所有素数 p：

CLISP defined a useful set of iteration constructs. Here is a simple program to print all the prime numbers p in the range m ≤p ≤n:

(FOR P FROM M TO N DO (PRINT P) WHILE (PRIMEP P))

CLISP、DWIM 和拼写纠正器可以协同工作，把下面的内容识别为一个有效的 FACTORIAL 定义 [Teitelman, 1973]：

CLISP, DWIM, and the spelling corrector could work together to recognize the following as a valid definition of FACTORIAL [Teitelman, 1973]:

DEFINEQ((FACTORIAL
(LAMBDA (N) (IFFN=0 THENN 1 ESLE N*8FACTTORIALNN-1))))

Interlisp 最终会把这个错乱的定义“纠正”为前面展示的有效形式。注意，在采用位配对键盘的 Model 33 电传打字机上，Shift-8 是左括号。后来打字机式配对键盘普及，左括号改为 Shift-9，而 Shift-8 是星号，DWIM 也不得不随之修改。

Interlisp eventually “corrects” this mangled definition into the valid form shown previously. Note that shift-8 is left parenthesis on the Model 33 teletype, which had a bit-paired keyboard. DWIM had to be changed when typewriter-paired keyboards (on which left parenthesis was shift-9, and shift-8 was the asterisk) became common.

Interlisp 的批评者，尤其是 MacLisp 的拥护者，常常评论说，不论这种说法是否公允，DWIM 似乎只针对 Teitelman 特有的输入错误调过参数，其他人的错误则不管。这些批评者喜欢把 DWIM 戏称为“Do What Teitelman Means”，按 Teitelman 的意思做，或“Do What Interlisp Means”，按 Interlisp 的意思做。“让系统自动‘纠正’程序？先生，我对此可是抱着十分 DWIM，也就是黯淡的看法！”MacLisp 的拥护者则说，MacLisp 使用 DDWIDM：“Don’t Do What I Didn’t Mean”，别做我没打算让你做的事。

(Critics of Interlisp, particularly those who were proponents of MacLisp, often commented, rightly or wrongly, that DWIM seemed to be tuned to the particular typing mistakes to which Teitelman was prone, and no others. These critics were given to lampooning DWIM with such expansions as “Do What Teitelman Means” or “Do What Interlisp Means.” “Let the system automatically ‘correct’ programs? I take a very DWIM view of that, sir!” MacLisp proponents would remark that MacLisp used DDWIDM: “Don’t Do What I Didn’t Mean.”)

Interlisp 有一个结构编辑器。Lisp 代码在 Interlisp 系统内显示和修改。编辑器的操作作用于用户选定的当前 S 表达式，或当前表达式周围的一小段上下文。这个编辑器开发于电传打字机和其他慢速打印终端普遍使用的时代，使程序员能在这类终端上非常高效地编辑 Lisp 源代码。对几乎只用 Lisp 编程的人来说，这种编辑方式很自然，尽管也有人始终偏好编辑 ASCII 文本。

Interlisp had a structure editor. Lisp code was displayed and altered within the Interlisp system. Operations in the editor applied to the current S-expression—as selected by the user—or to a small surrounding context of the current expression. This editor was developed when teletypes and other slow, printing terminals were standard; the Interlisp structure editor enables a programmer to edit Lisp source code very efficiently on such terminals. This style of editing is natural to people who are programming almost exclusively in Lisp, though some people prefer always to edit ASCII text.

MASTERSCOPE 是一种用于了解大型系统中函数信息的设施。它能够分析一组代码，建立数据库，并以交互方式回答问题。它记录的关系包括：哪些函数直接或间接调用哪些其他函数，哪些变量在哪里被绑定，哪些函数会破坏性地修改某些数据结构，等等。（MacLisp 有一个对应工具 INDEX，但其通用性和灵活性远远不及 MASTERSCOPE，而且只能以批处理方式运行，生成包含完整交叉索引报告的文件。）

MASTERSCOPE was a facility for finding out information about the functions in a large system. MASTERSCOPE could analyze a body of code, build up a data base, and answer questions interactively. MASTERSCOPE kept track of such relationships as which functions called which others (directly or indirectly), which variables were bound where, which functions destructively altered certain data structures, and so on. (MacLisp had a corresponding utility called INDEX, but it was not nearly as general or flexible, and it ran only in batch mode, producing a file containing a completely cross-indexed report.)

Interlisp 引入了块编译的概念，即把多个函数作为一个块来编译；这使函数调用速度超过了 Interlisp 在不采用这种方式时所能达到的速度。

Interlisp introduced the concept of block compilation, in which multiple functions are compiled as a single block; this resulted in faster function calling than would otherwise have been possible in Interlisp.
