Interlisp 运行于 PDP-10、Vaxen，即 Vax 的复数形式 [Raymond, 1991]，以及施乐和 BBN 开发的各种专用 Lisp 机器上。最常见的 Interlisp 机器是 Dolphin、Dorado 和 Dandelion，合称 D-machines。Dorado 是三者中最快的，Dandelion 则最常用。有趣的是，不同 Interlisp 实现使用不同技术处理特殊变量：PDP-10 上的 Interlisp-10 使用浅绑定，而 D-machines 上的 Interlisp-D 使用深绑定。这两种实现技术具有不同的性能特征：某个程序在一种机制下的运行时间，换到另一种机制下可能会增加到十倍。

Interlisp ran on PDP-10’s, Vaxen (plural of Vax [Raymond, 1991]), and a variety of specialpurpose Lisp machines developed by Xerox and BBN. The most commonly available Interlisp machines were the Dolphin, the Dorado, and the Dandelion (collectively known as D-machines). The Dorado was the fastest of the three, and the Dandelion the most commonly used. It is interesting that different Interlisp implementations used different techniques for handling special variables: Interlisp-10 (for the PDP-10) used shallow binding, while Interlisp-D (for D-machines) used deep binding. These two implementation techniques exhibit different performance profiles—a program with a certain run time under one regime could take 10 times longer under the other.

这种性能出乎预料的情况，在 Lisp 中很普遍。可以认为，程序员只有理解一种语言的实现，才能用它写出高效代码。C 的实现很直接，因为 C 操作与冯·诺依曼体系结构计算机的机器操作紧密对应。Lisp 的实现则并不直接，而是取决于一组复杂的实现技术和选择。程序员不仅需要熟悉所选技术，还要了解使用这些技术对性能的影响。难怪优秀的 Lisp 程序员比优秀的 C 程序员更难找。

This situation of unexpected performance is prevalent with Lisp. One can argue that programmers produce efficient code in a language only when they understand the implementation. With C, the implementation is straightforward because C operations are in close correspondence to the machine operations on a Von Neumann architecture computer. With Lisp, the implementation is not straightforward, but depends on a complex set of implementation techniques and choices. A programmer would need to be familiar not only with the techniques selected, but the performance ramifications of using those techniques. It is little wonder that good Lisp programmers are harder to find than good C programmers.

正如 MacLisp 的 I/O 系统围绕 ITS 提供的设施设计，Interlisp 的 I/O 系统也围绕 TENEX 的设施设计。综合来看，TENEX 的设施在许多方面比 ITS 更先进，尽管在中断处理等领域更难用，而且提供了更友好的用户界面。在 Interlisp 中，文件在所有地方都用名称表示；每个 I/O 函数，包括读写函数，而不只是打开和关闭函数，都接受一个指明文件名的字符串。因此，可以同时打开许多文件，但文件并不是以可被自动回收的第一类 Lisp 对象表示的。

Just as MacLisp’s I/O system was designed around the facilities provided by ITS, Interlisp’s I/O system was designed around those of TENEX, which, all things considered, were in many ways more advanced than those of ITS (though more awkward in such areas as interrupt handling) and offered a friendlier user interface. Files in Interlisp were represented everywhere by name; every I/O function (read and write, not just open and close accepted a character string naming the file. Thus one could have many files open simultaneously, but files were not represented by first-class Lisp objects subject to automatic reclamation.

与 MacLisp 一样，Interlisp 也扩展了 Lisp 1.5 的函数调用机制，涉及参数如何传给函数。Interlisp 函数定义通过两种属性的交叉组合来规定参数：LAMBDA 与 NLAMBDA，以及 spread 与 nospread。

Like MacLisp, Interlisp extended the function calling mechanisms in Lisp 1.5 with respect to how arguments can be passed to a function. Interlisp function definitions specified arguments as the cross product of two attributes: LAMBDA versus NLAMBDA, and spread versus nospread.

LAMBDA 函数对每个参数求值；NLAMBDA 函数不对任何参数求值，也就是说，调用中未经求值的参数子形式直接作为参数传入。spread 函数要求固定数量的参数；nospread 函数接受可变数量的参数。这两种属性并非完全正交：nospread NLAMBDA 的形参绑定到一个由未经求值的参数形式组成的列表，而 nospread LAMBDA 的形参则绑定到传入参数的数量，并使用 ARG 函数取得实际参数值。因此，Interlisp 与 MacLisp 的机制之间有着密切的对应关系：

LAMBDA functions evaluate each of their arguments; NLAMBDA functions evaluate none of their arguments (that is, the unevaluated argument subforms of the call are passed as the arguments). Spread functions require a fixed number of arguments; nospread functions accept a variable number. These two attributes were not quite orthogonal, because the parameter of a nospread NLAMBDA was bound to a list of the unevaluated argument forms, whereas the parameter of a nospread LAMBDA was bound to the number of arguments passed and the ARG function was used to retrieve actual argument values. There was thus a close correspondence between the mechanisms of Interlisp and MacLisp:

Interlisp 与 MacLisp 的对应关系

Interlisp MacLisp

LAMBDA spread：对应 EXPR；LAMBDA nospread：对应 LEXPR；NLAMBDA spread：无对应形式；NLAMBDA nospread：对应 FEXPR。

LAMBDA spread EXPR LAMBDA nospread LEXPR NLAMBDA spread no equivalent NLAMBDA nospread FEXPR

不过，MacLisp 与 Interlisp 在这里还有一个重要区别。在 MacLisp 中，“固定数量的参数”具有相当严格的含义：接受三个参数的函数，调用时必须恰好提供三个参数，不能多也不能少。在 Interlisp 中，任何函数都可以合法地用任意数量的参数调用；多余的参数形式会被求值，但其值会被丢弃，缺少的参数值则默认为 NIL。这是双方在 1974 年峰会上无法调和的主要分歧之一。此后，MacLisp 的拥护者嘲笑 Interlisp 缺乏约束、容易出错，而 Interlisp 的爱好者则认为 MacLisp 笨拙、不灵活，因为他们享有可选参数的便利；MacLisp 直到很晚，才从 Lisp-Machine Lisp 移植加入 &optional 和其他复杂的 lambda 列表语法，获得这种便利。

There was another important difference here between MacLisp and Interlisp, however. In MacLisp, “fixed number of arguments” had a quite rigid meaning; a function accepting three arguments must be called with exactly three arguments, neither more nor less. In Interlisp, any function could legitimately be called with any number of arguments; excess argument forms were evaluated and their values discarded, and missing argument values were defaulted to NIL. This was one of the principal irreconcilable differences separating the two sides at their 1974 summit. Thereafter MacLisp partisans derided Interlisp as undisciplined and error-prone, while Interlisp fans thought MacLisp awkward and inflexible, for they had the convenience of optional arguments, which did not come to MacLisp until &optional and other complex lambda-list syntax was retrofitted, late in the game, from Lisp-Machine Lisp.

Interlisp 引入的最具创新性的语言扩展之一，是意大利面条栈 [Bobrow, 1973]。在存在特殊变量的情况下，由闭包保留动态函数定义环境的问题，直到意大利面条栈发明后才得到完整解决。

One of the most innovative of the language extensions introduced by Interlisp was the spaghetti stack [Bobrow, 1973]. The problem of retention (by closures) of the dynamic function-definition environment in the presence of special variables was never completely solved until spaghetti stacks were invented.

意大利面条栈的思想，是把栈的结构推广得更像一棵树：只要指向某个分支的指针仍被保留，树的相应分支也会保留。也就是说，栈的各个部分与其他 Lisp 对象一样，受相同的垃圾回收策略管理。与闭包不同，这里保留的环境同时捕获控制环境和绑定环境。

The idea behind spaghetti stacks is to generalize the structure of stacks to be more like a tree, with various branches of the tree subject to retention whenever a pointer to that branch is retained. That is, parts of the stack are subject to the same garbage collection policies as are other Lisp objects. Unlike closures, the retained environment captures both the control environment and the binding environment.

严格说来，意大利面条栈是利用线性栈来高效实现树形栈的一种技术；当没有栈的部分被保留时，几乎不会产生性能损失。

Spaghetti stacks, per se, are an efficient implementation of tree-structured stacks using a linear stack; in the situation where parts of the stack are not retained, there is almost no performance penalty incurred.

Interlisp 比 MacLisp 更大程度地保留了 Lisp 1.5 的风格。MacLisp 改变了一些函数的某些参数顺序，例如 MAPCAR 和其他 MAP 函数，而 Interlisp 保留了原来的顺序。Interlisp 编程风格深受编程环境、CLISP 和 DWIM 影响，因此其 Lisp 部分仍与早期 Lisp 十分相似；改善编程风格的努力集中在 CLISP 和 DWIM 上。像 MacLisp 这样保留“Lisp 味”编程风格的方言，则推动了语言本身的 Lisp 部分发展。

Interlisp retained the Lisp 1.5 flavor to a greater extent than did MacLisp. MacLisp changed the order of some arguments to some functions (such as MAPCAR and the other MAP functions) while Interlisp retained the original order. Interlisp programming style is heavily influenced by the programming environment, CLISP, and DWIM; therefore the Lisp portion of Interlisp remains very similar to early Lisps, the effort in improving programming style focusing on CLISP and DWIM. In dialects like MacLisp, in which a “Lispy” style was retained, the Lisp part of the language itself was advanced.

Interlisp 所作的一项较小但有趣的语法扩展，是引入超级括号，即 superparenthesis 或 superbracket。如果读取操作遇到右方括号 ]，它会配平所有尚未闭合的左圆括号，或者一直配平到最近一个尚未闭合的左方括号 [ 为止。下面是这种语法的一个简单例子：

One of the minor, but interesting, syntactic extensions that Interlisp made was the introduction of the superparenthesis, or superbracket. If a right square bracket ] is encountered during a read operation, it balances all outstanding open left parentheses, or back to the last outstanding left square bracket [. Here is a simple example of this syntax:

DEFINEQ((FACTORIAL
(LAMBDA (N)
(COND [(ZEROP N) 1]
(T (TIMES N (FACTORIAL (SUB1 N]

分析

Analysis

MacLisp 与 Interlisp 大约同时诞生，存续时间也差不多。它们的接受群体不同，尽管对这两个群体作一般性描述时，并不能将它们区分开来：两者都是主要由 ARPA，后来称 DARPA，资助的人工智能实验室中的研究人员，而且都受过 MIT、CMU 和斯坦福大学的教育。它们的主要实现运行于相同机器；一个以环境出色的 Lisp 享有声望，另一个则是精简、强劲、高性能的 Lisp。主要差异来自于对编程问题采取的不同哲学立场。用户群体带来的压力也有所不同：MacLisp 用户，尤其是 Mathlab 团队，愿意使用集成程度较低的编程环境，以换取优秀的优化编译器，并让 PDP-10 地址空间中的很大一部分保持空闲，供自己使用。Interlisp 用户则更愿意通过使用完整的集成开发环境，专注于编码任务。

MacLisp and Interlisp came into existence about the same time and lasted about as long as each other. They differed in their acceptance groups, though any generic description of the two groups would not distinguish them: both groups were researchers at AI labs funded primarily by ARPA (later DARPA), and these researchers were educated by MIT, CMU, and Stanford. The principal implementations ran on the same machines, and one had cachet as the Lisp with the nice environment while the other was the lean, mean, high-powered Lisp. The primary differences came from different philosophical approaches to the problem of programming. There were also different pressures from their user groups; MacLisp users, particularly the Mathlab group, were willing to use a less integrated programming environment in exchange for a good optimizing compiler and having a large fraction of the PDP-10 address space left free for their own use. Interlisp users preferred to concentrate on the task of coding by using a full, integrated development environment.

MacLisp 的理念围绕高质量实现、性能、文本编辑器和语言表达能力展开。Interlisp 的理念则围绕编程环境和编码任务展开；在当时，他们会把后者称为编程任务。

The MacLisp philosophy centered on a high quality implementation, performance, text editors, and language expressiveness. The Interlisp philosophy centers on the programming environment and the task of coding—back then they would call it the task of programming.

MacLisp 是基于文件、面向文本编辑器的环境，而 Interlisp 是常驻式环境，带有内存中的 Lisp 结构编辑器。MacLisp 环境使用松散连接的外部工具，Interlisp 环境则与语言实现紧密耦合；事实上，环境与语言共享同一地址空间，实际属于同一个程序。

MacLisp was a filed-based, text-editor-oriented environment, while Interlisp was residential with an in-memory (in-core) Lisp structure editor. The MacLisp environment used external tools linked loosely together, while the Interlisp environment was tightly coupled with the language implementation—in fact, the environment and the language shared the same address space, and actually were part of the same program.

Interlisp 的理念主要由 Teitelman 在 20 世纪 60 年代中期形成。当时大屏幕终端很少，最常见的是打印式电传打字机终端，主要的文本编辑器则是“纸带编辑器”。纸带编辑器把文本当作一条很长的线性纸带来操作。命令可以按一定距离，或通过搜索，在纸带上前后移动；文本可以插入和删除。当时一种流行的编辑器 TECO，最初叫 Tape Editor and Corrector，后来改为 Text Editor and Corrector。简言之，在 Interlisp 环境之外，输入和纠正代码的过程十分困难，因此 Interlisp 开发环境的目标就是简化并缩短这一过程。连键入代码都很困难，所以人们寻求各种捷径。

The Interlisp philosophy was developed largely by Teitelman in the middle 1960’s, at which time there were very few large screen terminals—the printing teletype-type terminal was most common. And the primary text editors were “tape editors.” A tape editor is a text editor that operates on the text as if it were a long linear tape. Commands moved backwards and forwards on the tape, either by certain distances or by searching. Text could be inserted and deleted. One popular editor at the time, TECO, was originally called the Tape Editor and Corrector (later Text Editor and Corrector). In short, the process of entering and correcting code was very difficult outside the Interlisp environment, and so the goal of the Interlisp development environment was to simplify and shorten that process—even typing code was difficult and so shortcuts were sought.

Teitelman 的方法新颖而令人振奋，而且确实提高了编码效率。

Teitelman’s approaches were new and exciting, and certainly they improved coding productivity.

另一种选择 MacLisp 也有某些优势。由于语言实现基于文件和文本，开发环境由独立的外部工具承担；这些工具可以沿各自的演化路径发展，MacLisp 程序员则可以直接继承这些好处。不过，在 Emacs [?] 出现之前，可获得的好处并不多。

The alternative, MacLisp, had certain advantages as well. Because the language implementation was file-based and text-based, the development environment was captured by independent external tools, which could follow their own evolutionary process, and MacLisp programmers could simply inherit the benefits. Until Emacs [?] appeared, though, there were few benefits to be had.

工具分离时，用户无需在接受语言的同时接受一整套工具。如果必须选择一个整体软件包，并承担环境工具可能不合用的风险，那么对某些人来说，选择语言加环境的代价就可能过高。

When the tools are separate, the user does not need to accept a set of tools along with the language. With a package to choose, and risk that the environmental tools might be unsuitable, the cost of choosing a language plus environment might be too high for some.

不过，MacLisp 程序员也希望提高效率。实际上，他们大概并不在乎效率，只是想让工作更简单、更有趣。结果是，MacLisp 开发者把更多注意力放在语言本身，加入宏和新的数据结构，并改善性能。

However, MacLisp programmers wished to have their productivity improved—actually, they probably didn’t care about productivity, they simply wanted their job simplified and made more fun. The result was that MacLisp developers focussed more on the language itself, adding macros and new data structures, and improving performance.

另一组理念差异体现在美化打印上。下面是 Lisp 1.5 中 MEMBER 函数的定义示例，完全按照《Lisp 1.5 程序员手册》[McCarthy, 1962] 中的样子重现：

Another set of differences in philosophy lies in pretty-printing. The following is an example of the definition of the function, MEMBER, in Lisp 1.5. It is reproduced exactly as it appeared in the Lisp 1.5 Programmer’s Manual [McCarthy, 1962]:

DEFINE((
(MEMBER (LAMBDA (A X) (COND ((NULL X) F)
((EQ A (CAR X) ) T) (T (MEMBER A (CDR X))) )))
))

同一程序若在今天用 Common Lisp 编写，会是下面这样：

The same program written in Common Lisp today would look like this:

(defun member (element list)
(cond ((null list) ())
((eq element (first list)) t)
(t (member element (rest list)))))

请注意，现代缩进旨在澄清代码中各个重要部分之间的关系。旧风格看起来则没有什么设计。

Notice the modern indentation is designed to clarify the relations between significant portions of the code. The old style appears to have no design.

下面这个中间阶段的示例源于 1966 年的编码风格：

This intermediate example is derived from a 1966 coding style:

DEFINE((
(MEMBER (LAMBDA (A X) (COND
((NULL X) F)
((EQ A (CAR X) ) T)
(T (MEMBER A (CDR X))) )))
))

这种风格似乎把函数名、参数和 COND 的开头视为一个惯用整体，因此把它们放在同一行。COND 子句的各个分支对齐，从而显示所考虑的各个情形的结构。

The design of this style appears to take the name of the function, the arguments, and the very beginning of the COND as an idiom, and hence they are on the same line together. The branches of the COND clause line up, which shows the structure of the cases considered.

在 MacLisp 中，缩进风格是有意识地演化出来的，因为程序员自己负责缩进，至少在 Emacs [?] 这样的文本编辑器加入半自动缩进设施之前如此。程序代码通过文本编辑器和打印稿查看，而在 Interlisp 中，代码主要在屏幕上查看，但并非完全如此。

With MacLisp, the style of indentation was consciously evolved, because programmers themselves were responsible for doing the indentation, at least until text editors like Emacs [?] added semi-automatic indentation facilities. Program code was viewed by text editors and on printout, while in Interlisp program code was primarily, but not exclusively, viewed on the screen.

Interlisp 提供了美化打印器，可以把内存中的程序输出到文件或终端。结构编辑器使用它来显示程序代码。尽管这个美化打印器具有粗略的可编程能力（原文此处为 ???），但很少有人定制它。（原文另有“Fonts???”待核注。）由于 MacLisp 也有美化打印器，而程序员已经采用各自的美化打印风格，因此其美化打印器必须可编程，事实也确实如此。[White, 1969–1982; Moon, April 1974; Pitman, 1983; ?; Steele, 1990c]

Interlisp provided a pretty-printer that would take the in-memory program and dump it to a file or to the terminal. The structure editor used the pretty-printer to show program code. Though the pretty-printer was crudely programmable (???) few people customized it. (Fonts???) Because MacLisp had a pretty-printer and programmers had adopted their own pretty-printing style, the pretty-printer had to be programmable, and it was. [White, 1969–1982; Moon, April 1974; Pitman, 1983; ?; Steele, 1990c]

当然，Interlisp 开发者也改进了 Interlisp，但主要的语言改进是意大利面条栈，因此也带来某种动态闭包、记录包，以及基于字段的结构。在性能方面，开发者加入了块编译，并使用不止一个 PDP-10 地址空间。

Of course, Interlisp developers improved Interlisp, but the primary language improvements were spaghetti stacks (and hence a sort of dynamic closure), the record package, and field-based structure. In terms of performance, Interlisp developers added block compilation and using more than one PDP-10 address space.

不过，语言扩展的主要机制是通过环境实现的。DWIM、拼写纠正器和 CLISP 通过改变表面语法，并容忍某些类型的错误，起到了定义一种新语言的作用。

However, the primary mechanisms for language extensions were through the environment. DWIM, the spelling corrector, and CLISP served to define a new language by changing the surface syntax and tolerating certain types of errors.

结果是，Interlisp 中的语言实验较少，环境实验较多；MacLisp 则正好相反。这两种方言在整个 20 世纪 70 年代直到 80 年代都是主要方言，但在 80 年代初的大整合发生时，MacLisp 及其衍生方言所产生的影响远远最强。这是由于 Interlisp 内部的核心 Lisp 语言停滞不前，而这种停滞正是开发者专注环境所付出的代价。

As a result, there were fewer language experiments and more environment experiments in Interlisp, while the opposite was true in MacLisp. These two dialects were the primary dialects throughout the 1970’s and into the 1980’s, yet when the big consolidation of the early 1980’s took place, MacLisp and MacLisp derivatives provided by far the strongest influences. This is due to the stagnation of the core Lisp language within Interlisp. This stagnation was the price of environmental attention by the Interlisp developers.

当然，Interlisp 的衰亡还有其他因素，其中一个重要因素是：直到最后，它的拥护者仍相信 Interlisp 状况极佳，且拥有新的前景。

Of course, there were other factors in Interlisp’s demise, not the least of which was the belief by its champions that Interlisp was enjoying excellent health and new prospects up to the very end.

Interlisp 同样代表了多样化和接受之后的一次整合，而它后来的多样化活动围绕环境，而不是核心语言展开。与 MacLisp 不同，Interlisp 开发者试图通过把它移植到其他机器上来推广它；MacLisp 开发者则各自前往新机构，尝试新的语言思想，通过新的设计和新的 Lisp 变体实现多样化。

Interlisp also represented a consolidation after diversification and acceptance, and its later diversification activities centered around the environment and not the core language. Unlike MacLisp, the developers of Interlisp tried to spread Interlisp through porting it to other machines, while the MacLisp developers—each offat new institutions and trying out new language ideas—were diversifying by making new designs and new variants of Lisp.

2.4 20世纪70年代初

2.4 The Early 1970’s

尽管 MacLisp 和 Interlisp 主导了20世纪70年代，这一时期仍有其他几种重要的 Lisp 方言在使用。它们大多更接近 MacLisp，而非 Interlisp。其中使用最广的两种是 Standard Lisp [?][Marti, 1979] 和 Portable Standard Lisp [Group, 1982]。Standard Lisp 由 Anthony Hearn、Martin Griss 及其学生和同事于1966年定义 [Griss, 1982]，目的是定义 Lisp 1.5 及其他 Lisp 方言的一个子集，作为移植 Lisp 程序的媒介，尤其是符号代数系统 REDUCE。

Though MacLisp and Interlisp dominated the 1970’s, there were several other major Lisp dialects in use during this period. Most were more similar to MacLisp than to Interlisp. The two most widely used dialects were Standard Lisp [?][Marti, 1979] and Portable Standard Lisp [Group, 1982]. Standard Lisp was defined in 1966 [Griss, 1982]) by Anthony Hearn and Martin Griss, along with their students and colleagues. The motivation was to define a subset of Lisp 1.5 and other Lisp dialects that could serve as a medium for porting Lisp programs, most particularly the symbolic algebra system REDUCE.

Standard Lisp 的设计原则是：只要某个已有的 Lisp 实现，也就是“目标 Lisp”，能够实现 Standard Lisp 的构造，Standard Lisp 和 REDUCE 就能运行在该方言之上。

Standard Lisp was designed so that if an existing Lisp implementation (called the “target Lisp”) could implement the Standard Lisp constructs, then Standard Lisp and REDUCE could run on top of that existing dialect.

后来，Hearn 及其同事发现，要获得良好性能，就必须加强对环境和编译器的控制，于是 Portable Standard Lisp，简称 PSL，诞生了。Standard Lisp 试图搭载已有的 Lisp，而 PSL 则是一套全新的完整实现，配有可重定向目标平台的编译器 [Griss, 1981]。这是 Lisp 编译技术演化中一项重要的开创性工作。到70年代末，PSL 已能运行于十多种不同的计算机上。

Later Hearn and his colleagues discovered that for good performance they needed more control over the environment and the compiler, and Portable Standard Lisp (PSL) was born. Standard Lisp attempted to piggyback on existing Lisps, while PSL was a complete, new Lisp implementation with a retargetable compiler [Griss, 1981], an important pioneering effort in the evolution of Lisp compilation technology. By the end of the 1970’s, PSL ran on more than a dozen different types of computers.

PSL 的实现采用了两项技术。第一，使用名为 SYSLISP 的系统实现语言，编写对原始、无类型表示的操作。第二，使用一组参数化的汇编级翻译宏，称为 c-macros。Portable Lisp Compiler（PLC）先把 Lisp 代码编译为抽象汇编语言，再结合上下文，将 c-macro 描述与抽象指令进行模式匹配，转换为与机器相关的 LAP（Lisp Assembly Program）格式。例如，根据操作数的来源和运算结果的去向，可以选择不同的机器指令。

PSL was implemented using two techniques. First, it used a system implementation language called SYSLISP, which was used to code operations on raw, untyped representations. Second, it used a parameterized set of assembly-level translation macros called c-macros. The Portable Lisp Compiler (PLC) compiled Lisp code into an abstract assembly language. This language was then converted to a machine-dependent LAP (Lisp Assembly Program) format by pattern-matching the c-macro descriptions with the abstract instructions in context. For example, different machine instructions might be selected depending on the sources of the operands and the destination of the result of the operation.

从70年代后半期到80年代中期，PSL 通过引入编辑器和其他工具不断改善环境。其中尤其值得一提的是 Emode：这是一款出色的、类似 Emacs 的多窗口编辑器，支持相当智能的编辑，并能在 Lisp 与编辑器之间双向传递信息。后来，Martin Griss 及其在加利福尼亚州帕洛阿尔托惠普公司的同事，开发了功能更丰富的版本 Nmode。惠普于80年代中期将这一版本的 PSL 和 Nmode 商业化。

In the latter half of the 1970’s and on into the mid-1980’s, the PSL environment was improved by adapting editors and other tools. In particular, a good multiwindow Emacs-like editor called Emode was developed that allowed fairly intelligent editing and the passing of information back and forth between the Lisp and the editor. Later, a more extensive version of Emode called Nmode was developed by Martin Griss and his colleagues at Hewlett-Packard in Palo Alto, California. This version of PSL and Nmode was commercialized by HP in the mid-1980’s.

60年代，斯坦福大学把 MacLisp 的一个早期版本移植到 PDP-6 上，称为 Lisp 1.6 [Quam, 1972]。最初的移植版后来由 John Allen 和 Lynn Quam 重写；Whit Diffie 随后改进了编译器。

At Stanford in the 1960’s, an early version of MacLisp was adapted to the PDP-6; this Lisp was called Lisp 1.6 [Quam, 1972]. The early adaptation was rewritten by John Allen and Lynn Quam; later compiler improvements were made by Whit Diffie.

UCI Lisp [Bobrow, 1972] 是 Lisp 1.6 的扩展版，加入了 Interlisp 风格的编辑器，并对编程环境作了其他改进。70年代初至中期，斯坦福及其他机构的一些人使用过 UCI Lisp。

UCI Lisp [Bobrow, 1972] was an extended version of Lisp 1.6 in which an Interlisp style editor and other programming environment improvements were made. UCI Lisp was used by some folks at Stanford during the early to mid-1970’s, as well as at other institutions.

1976年，Richard Gabriel 在当时由 John McCarthy 领导的斯坦福人工智能实验室 SAIL，把 MIT 版本的 MacLisp 移植到 WAITS 操作系统。这一方言取代了包括 UCI Lisp 在内的 Lisp 1.6 衍生版本。在 Edward Feigenbaum 领导的启发式编程项目中，Interlisp 是主要方言。Cordell Green 的自动编程小组 PSI，则通过远程登录南加利福尼亚州信息科学研究所 ISI 的一台 PDP-10 来使用 Interlisp。

In 1976 the MIT version of MacLisp was ported to the WAITS operating system by Richard Gabriel at the Stanford AI Laboratory (SAIL), which was directed at that time by John Mc- Carthy. This dialect supplanted the Lisp 1.6 derivatives, including UCI Lisp. At the Heuristic Programming Project, under the direction of Edward Feigenbaum, Interlisp was the primary dialect. Cordell Green’s automatic programming group (PSI) used Interlisp via remote login to a PDP-10 at Information Science Institute (ISI) in southern California.

分析

Analysis

Standard Lisp 和 PSL 代表了思想的一次整合，主要目标是把某个特定程序 Reduce，提供给使用各种 Lisp 方言的用户。Reduce 的用户群体正在壮大，并且迫切希望使用它。人们认为，这两种方言是推广这一实用系统的必要条件。Standard Lisp 试图搭载已有 Lisp，而 PSL 则试图更好地控制性能。

Standard Lisp and PSL represented a consolidation of ideas with the primary focus of delivering a particular program, Reduce, to a range of users who used among them a variety of Lisp dialects. The users of Reduce were on the rise and eager to use it. These two dialects were seen as necessary for the spread of the useful Reduce system. Standard Lisp was an attempt to piggyback on existing Lisps, while PSL represented an attempt to control performance a little better.

UCI Lisp 是把 Interlisp 的思想移植到简单的 Lisp 1.6 基础上的结果，因此可以把它看作 Interlisp 整合过程的一部分。

UCI Lisp was an adaptation of Interlisp to a simple Lisp 1.6 base, and so we can regard this as part of the consolidation of Interlisp.

Lisp 1.6 本身于70年代中期消失，是 Lisp 1.5 时代最后留下的几个残余之一。

Lisp 1.6 itself disappeared during the mod-1970’s, it being one of the last remnants of the Lisp 1.5 era.

2.5 PDP-10 的消亡

2.5 The Demise of the PDP-10

到70年代中期，PDP-10 的18位地址空间显然已无法为人工智能程序提供足够的工作空间。PDP-10 系列计算机，包括 KL-10 和 DEC-20，经过修改，支持一种扩展寻址机制：通过相对于30位基址寄存器的索引，访问多个18位地址空间。

By the middle of the 1970’s it became apparent that the 18-bit address space of the PDP-10 would not provide enough working space for AI programs. The PDP-10 line of computers (KL-10’s and DEC-20’s) was altered to permit an extended addressing scheme, in which multiple 18-bit address spaces could be addressed by indexing relative to 30-bit base registers.

然而，对 Lisp 实现者来说，这并不是体系结构的一次平滑扩展。从每个字容纳两个指针，改为每个字只能容纳一个指针，意味着几乎所有内部数据结构都必须彻底重新设计。只有两种 Lisp 实现了扩展寻址：罗格斯大学 Charles Hedrick 的 ELISP [?]，以及 PSL。

However, this addition was not a smooth expansion to the architecture as far as the Lisp implementor was concerned; the change from two pointers per word to only one pointer per word required a complete redesign of nearly all internal data structures. Only two Lisps were implemented for extended addressing: ELISP by Charles Hedrick at Rutgers [?]and PSL.

解决地址空间问题的一种办法是制造专用 Lisp 机器，见第2.6节；另一种办法是使用地址空间更大的商用计算机，最早的是 VAX [DEC, 1981]。

One response to the address space problem was to construct special-purpose Lisp machines (see section 2.6). The other response was to use commercial computers with larger address spaces; the first of these was the VAX [DEC, 1981].

VAX 给 Lisp 实现者同时带来了机会和问题。其指令集为高效实现底层 Lisp 原语提供了一些好机会，但需要巧妙，甚至可能过于巧妙的数据结构设计。然而，VAX 的函数调用指令无法准确匹配 Lisp 的函数调用。此外，尽管理论地址空间很大，VAX 显然是为许多小程序，而不是几个大程序设计的。页表占用了过高比例的内存，大型 Lisp 程序的分页开销问题在 VAX 上始终未得到彻底解决。最后还有既有投入的问题：当时不止一个重要 Lisp 实现拥有大量难以移植的汇编代码。

Vaxen presented both opportunities and problems for Lisp implementors. The VAX instruction set provided some good opportunities for implementing the low level Lisp primitives efficiently, though it required clever—perhaps too clever—design of the data structures. However, Lisp function calls could not be accurately modeled with the VAX function-call instructions. Moreover, the VAX, despite its theoretically large address space, was apparently designed for use by many small programs, not several large ones. Page tables occupied too large a fraction of memory, and paging overhead for large Lisp programs was a problem never fully solved on the VAX. Finally, there was the problem of prior investment; more than one major Lisp implementation at the time had a large assembly-language base that was difficult to port.

最后一击是 DEC 放弃整个 PDP-10 体系结构，转而专注 VAX。回头看，这无疑是 DEC 正确的战略决策，但在当时，只让那些热爱 PDP-10 的 Lisp 实现者多了一个怨恨 VAX 的理由。少数幸运者有资源设计并制造自己的硬件；对其余人而言，VAX 是唯一选择。在 PDP-10 爱好者眼中，它缓慢、昂贵，而且毫不可爱。

The final blow came when DEC abandoned the entire PDP-10 architecture to concentrate on the VAX. In retrospect this was undoubtedly a good strategic decision on DEC’s part, but at the time it only gave those Lisp implementors who had loved the PDP-10 another reason to resent the VAX. A lucky few had the resources to design and build their own hardware; for the rest, the VAX was the only game in town. To the PDP-10 aficionados it seemed slow, expensive, and unlovable.

70年代末开发的主要 VAX Lisp 方言是 VAX Interlisp、移植到 VAX 的 PSL、Franz Lisp 和 NIL。

The primary Vax Lisp dialects developed in the late 1970’s were VAX Interlisp, PSL (ported to the VAX), Franz Lisp, and NIL.

Franz Lisp [Foderaro, 1982] 是为使加州大学伯克利分校的符号代数研究得以继续而编写的，负责人 Richard J. Fateman 曾是 MIT Macsyma 的主要实现者之一。Fateman 和学生们以哈佛大学编写的 PDP-11 Lisp 为起点，把它扩展为一种类似 MacLisp 的 Lisp。由于 Franz Lisp 几乎完全用 C 编写，最终能够运行在几乎所有基于 Unix 的计算机上。后来成立了 Franz, Inc. 公司以进一步发展 Franz Lisp；截至原文写作时，它仍在开发和销售。

Franz Lisp [Foderaro, 1982] was written to enable research on symbolic algebra to continue at the University of California at Berkeley, under the supervision of Richard J. Fateman, who was one of the principal implementors of Macsyma at MIT. Fateman and his students started with a PDP- 11 version of Lisp written at Harvard, and extended it into a MacLisp-like Lisp that eventually ran on virtually all Unix-based computers, thanks to the fact that Franz Lisp is written almost entirely in C. Later Franz, Inc., was formed to further Franz Lisp; development and sales of Franz Lisp continue to this day.

由于 Franz 主要被定位为符号代数研究工具，它从未像 MacLisp 那样成为一套扎实成熟的 Lisp。另一方面，它运行于人工智能社群最普及的计算机之一，是一种很容易获得的 Lisp 方言。

Because Franz was intended to be primarily a vehicle for research in symbolic algebra, it never became a solid Lisp in the same sense that MacLisp did. On the other hand, it was a widely available Lisp dialect on one of the most widely available computers in the AI community.

NIL [Burke, 1983] 被定位为 MacLisp 的继任者，由 MIT 的 Jon L White、Guy L. Steele Jr. 等人设计，受到同在 MIT 开发的 Lisp-Machine Lisp 的影响。它的名字是“New Implementation of Lisp”的一个过分俏皮的缩写，引起了不少混淆。NIL 是一门大型 Lisp，其偏爱 MacLisp 的实现者把效率放在首位；很快，它的实现就围绕大量 VAX 汇编代码展开。

NIL [Burke, 1983], intended to be the successor to MacLisp, was designed by Jon L White, Guy L. Steele Jr., and others at MIT, under the influence of Lisp-Machine Lisp, also developed at MIT. Its name was a too-cute acronym for “New Implementation of Lisp” and caused a certain amount of confusion. NIL was a large Lisp, and efficiency concerns were paramount in the minds of its MacLisp-oriented implementors; soon its implementation was centered around a large VAX assembly-language base.

1978年，Gabriel 和 Guy Steele 着手在 S-1 Mark IIA 上实现 NIL [Brooks, 1982a]。这是一台由劳伦斯利弗莫尔国家实验室设计制造的超级计算机 [Correll, 1979; Hailpern, 1979]。这套 Lisp 从未完全投入使用，却成为把先进编译技术应用于 Lisp 实现的试验平台。尤其是，这项工作推广了 MacLisp 编译器的数值计算技术，并将其与主流寄存器分配策略统一起来 [Brooks, 1982b]。随着 S-1 Lisp 编译器的开发，用 Lisp 实现 Lisp，并获得与最佳手工调优的汇编实现相近的性能，再次变得可行。

In 1978, Gabriel and Guy Steele set out to implement NIL [Brooks, 1982a] on the S-1 Mark IIA, a supercomputer being designed and built by the Lawrence Livermore National Laboratory [Correll, 1979; Hailpern, 1979]. This Lisp was never completely functional, but served as a testbed for adapting advanced compiler techniques to Lisp implementation. In particular, the work generalized the numerical computation techniques of the MacLisp compiler and unified them with mainstream register allocation strategies [Brooks, 1982b]. With the development of the S-1 Lisp compiler, it once again became feasible to implement Lisp in Lisp and to expect similar performance to the best hand-tuned, assembly-language-based Lisp systems.

1975年，Gerald J. Sussman 和 Guy L. Steele Jr. 开始试验一些解释器，试图理解 Actor 计算模型的影响，详见第2.8节。这促成了1976至1978年间一系列论文的发表，描述一种名为 Scheme 的新 Lisp 方言。

In 1975 Gerald J. Sussman and Guy L. Steele Jr. began experimenting with some interpreters in an attempt to understand the consequences of the Actor model of computation. (This experimentation is explained in section 2.8.) This led to the publication during 1976–1978 of a series of papers describing a new dialect of Lisp called Scheme.

Scheme 是最早认真对待词法作用域和一等函数之含义的语言之一；这里的词法作用域不同于 Lisp 编译器已经使用近十年的局部作用域。具体而言，Scheme 正确处理了闭包：闭包由函数及其定义时的局部环境组成。此外，它接受了 Actor 计算模型的思想，把函数调用看作跳转到一个过程，而不一定是调用一个随后会返回的过程。

Scheme was one of the first languages to have taken seriously the implications of lexical scoping (as opposed to local scoping, which had been in use in Lisp compilers for almost a decade) and first-class functions. Namely, Scheme correctly treated closures—a closure is a function along with the local environment within which it was defined. Furthermore, it embraced the Actor model of computation in the sense that function calling could be treated as a jump to a procedure rather than as a call to a procedure that would return.

这一理论认为，剩余计算被封装在另一个函数中，按 Scheme 术语称为过程，名叫续延。续延接受一个参数，即要传递或返回的值。这就形成了续延传递式编程。例如，把 x 与 y 相乘的结果加7，再把结果交给续延 cont，可以写成：

The theory stated that the remainder of the computation was encapsulated in another function (or procedure as Scheme terminology has it) called a continuation. A continuation takes one argument, which is the value to be passed on (returned). This leads to a style of programming called continuation-passing. For example, adding 7 to the result of multiplying x and y, giving the result to the continuation cont, would be written like this:

(* x y (lambda (temp) (+ temp 7 cont)))

因此，每个原始函数都接受一个额外参数，即它的续延；按照约定，用户定义的函数也是如此。

Thus every primitive function takes an additional argument, which is its continuation, and so, by convention, do user-defined functions.

在解释器中，这个参数可以是隐式的。如果显式表示，* 的定义如下：

In an interpreter setting, this argument can be implicit. When explicit, the definition of * would look like this:

(define * (lambda (m1 m2 cont)
(cont (*$ m1 m2))))

其中 *$ 是底层乘法原语。

where *$ is the low-level multiplication primitive.

Scheme 逐渐吸引了一批注重理论、优雅和简洁的追随者，他们根据三篇开创性的 Scheme 论文编写解释器、编译器和系统。其他 Lisp 开发者也被语言及其阐述方式的清晰性打动，此后多年都宣称，相比其接受群体要求使用的、不那么干净的语言，他们更喜欢 Scheme。

Scheme slowly gathered a following of theoretically and elegance and simplicity minded individuals who wrote interpreters, compilers, and systems based on the three seminal Scheme papers. Other Lisp developers, taken with the clarity of the language and its presentation, would for years proclaim their preference for Scheme over the less clean language their acceptance group required.

到这个十年结束时，Scheme 至少已经有一个优秀的编译器 [Steele, 1978a]。

At the end of the decade, Scheme had had at least one good compiler written for it [Steele, 1978a].

70年代中期，法国的 Greussay 开发了基于解释器的 Vlisp [Greussay, 1977]。其基础方言大致处于 Interlisp 的水平，但引入了一些有趣概念，例如 chronology：一种动态环境，通过创建求值器的不同实例，实现中断以及 trace、step 等环境功能。Vlisp 强调快速解释器，其思路是提供一个用于移植求值器的虚拟机。这个虚拟机处于汇编语言层级，旨在便于移植并高效执行。解释器的速度主要来自两点：通过区分不同参数个数函数的函数类型空间，实现快速函数分派；以及消除尾递归。Vlisp 可能是首个支持通用尾递归消除的生产级 Lisp。当时包括 MacLisp 在内的其他方言，只在某些情况下消除尾递归，且不保证其行为可预测。Vlisp 是 Le Lisp 的前身；后者是80年代法国和欧洲的重要 Lisp 方言之一。虽然两者方言不同，但共享了一些实现技术。

In France in the mid-1970’s, Greussay [Greussay, 1977] developed an interpreter-based Lisp called Vlisp. At the level of the base dialect of Interlisp, it introduced a couple of interesting concepts, such as the chronology, which is a sort of dynamic environment for implementing interrupts and environmental functions like trace and step, by creating different incarnations of the evaluator. Vlisp’s emphasis was on having a fast interpreter. The concept was to provide a virtual machine that was used to transport the evaluator. This virtual machine was at the level of assembly language and was designed for easy porting and efficient execution. The interpreter got a significant part of its speed from two things: a fast function dispatch using a function type space that distinguished a number of functions of different arity, and tail recursion removal. (Vlisp was probably the first production quality Lisp to support general tail recursion removal. Other dialects of the time, including MacLisp, did tail recursion removal in certain situations only, in a manner not guaranteed predictable.) Vlisp was the precursor to Le Lisp, one of the important Lisp dialects in France and Europe during the 1980’s; though the dialects were different, they shared some implementation techniques.

分析

Analysis

PDP-10 的设计非常适合实现早期 Lisp 方言：地址为18位，字长36位，所以一个 CONS 单元恰好放进一个字；它有方便的半字操作指令，函数调用机制也适合 Lisp。

The PDP-10 was designed in such a way that the early dialects of Lisp could be implemented quite well on them: CONS cells fit into one word because memory addresses were 18 bits and words were 36 bits, there were convenient halfword manipulation instructions, and the function calling mechanism was amenable to Lisp.

VAX 的出现改变了这一切。Lisp 数据结构与 VAX 的体系结构设施之间，没有容易建立的对应关系。此外，VAX 推荐使用的过程调用指令，也不适合新一代类似 MacLisp 的方言：这些方言都支持可变数量的参数，而 VAX 指令集只对固定参数数量的函数调用提供了良好支持，尽管可以用几种拼凑方法实现有效的 Lisp 函数调用。当 C 和 FORTRAN 等其他语言正学习相互调用时，这又给 Lisp 融入其中增加了一道障碍。

With the advent of the Vax this changed. The had no easy matches between Lisp data structures and Vax architectural facilities. Furthermore, the preferred Vax procedure call instructions did not fit well with the new style of MacLisp-like Lisp dialects: Each of these dialects supported a variable number of arguments, and the Vax instruction set supported well only function calls with a fixed number of arguments, though there were several ways to cobble together an effective function call implementation for Lisp. At a time when other languages such as C and FORTRAN were learning to talk to each other, however, this provided yet one more impediment to Lisp’s joining the crowd.

到70年代末，仍看不到适合 Lisp 的新商用机器，似乎只有 VAX。尽管 Glenn Burke 多年奋力支持，VAX NIL 从未被广泛接受。Interlisp/VAX 的性能更是一场灾难。“通用”工作站，也就是旨在运行 Lisp 以外语言的工作站，以及个人计算机，都还没有真正出现。对多数 Lisp 实现者和用户而言，商用硬件的前景相当黯淡。

At the end of the 1970’s, no new commercial machines suitable for Lisp were on the horizon; it appeared that the Vax was all there was. Despite years of valiant support by Glenn Burke, Vax NIL never achieved widespread acceptance. Interlisp/VAX was a performance disaster. “Generalpurpose” workstations (i.e., those intended or designed to run languages other than Lisp) and personal computers hadn’t quite appeared yet. To most Lisp implementors and users, the commercial hardware situation looked quite bleak.

不过，自1974年起，Lisp 机器的研究和原型项目就已展开；到70年代末，Lisp 机器似乎代表着未来。

But from 1974 onward there had been research and prototyping projects for Lisp machines, and at the end of the decade it appeared that Lisp machines were the wave of the future.

此时的局面是：MacLisp 的自然接受群体正在衰退，Interlisp 的接受群体却在上升；后者是那些即将成立公司的、具有商业意识的人工智能团队。Interlisp 运行于商业接受群体认可的一组机器上，也符合他们的工作风格。用于 Interlisp 的新 Lisp 机器，另节讨论，颇有吸引力。Interlisp 似乎已经具备取得整合主导权的条件，甚至可能吸引 MacLisp 用户。

At this point we have the situation where the natural acceptance groups for MacLisp are on the decline, but the acceptance groups of Interlisp are on the ascendency; these groups are the commercially minded AI groups that are about to be incorporated. Interlisp runs on a set of machines that are accepted by the commercial acceptance groups, and its fits their style of work. The new Lisp machines (discussed in another section) for Interlisp have a decided cachet. Things are ripe for Interlisp to claim the right of consolidation, perhaps even winning over MacLisp users.

MacLisp 则随着各种 Lisp 机器方言进入多样化阶段。这些机器似乎太新，尚难以立即被接受；它们的新开发环境相对于旧式 MacLisp 开发方式的变化，也许太大，难以进入接受阶段。不过，它们确实很有吸引力。

MacLisp is entering a phase of diversification with the various Lisp-Machine dialects. It appears that the Lisp machines are too new to be readily acceptable; and their new development environments are maybe too much of a change from the old-style MacLisp development to move into an acceptance phase. But they do have cachet.

接下来的竞争，将看谁能选择具有合适特性的机器，帮助自己的 Lisp 取得成功。眼前的问题是：合适的机器究竟是 VAX、某种 Lisp 机器，以及其中哪一种，还是一台当时尚不为人所知的机器？结果将是后一种。

The race will be to see who can choose the machine or machines with the right characteristics to help the Lisp succeed. The immediate question is whether the Vax will be the right machine, the Lisp machines (and which one), or a machine then unknown. It will turn out to be a then-unknown machine.

请注意，Franz Lisp 和 PSL 除了可移植性之外，没有什么能提供给新的接受群体；作为 Lisp，它们都退回到了更早的 MacLisp 时代。尽管可移植性似乎是一项重要的生存特征，但如果没有某种声望，一种方言仍然希望渺茫。

Notice that Franz Lisp and PSL have nothing to offer the new acceptance groups except for portability, and each as a Lisp is a throwback to earlier MacLisp times. Though portability appears to be an important survival characteristic, without some cachet there is little hope for a dialect.

Scheme 为我们的循环主题提供了一个有趣变体。Gerry Sussman 曾参与多个语言小组：MDL，也叫 Muddle，小组、Micro-Planner 小组和 Conniver 小组。在 Scheme 之后，他又将研究约束语言。他的目标受众是他自己，以及从事与其工作相关研究的人，或希望使用他所设计语言的研究人员。

Scheme represents an interesting variation on our cycle theme. Gerry Sussman has been involved with a variety of language groups: the MDL (or Muddle) group, the Micro-Planner group, and the Conniver group. After Scheme he would work on constraint languages. His target audience is himself along with those researchers who are involved in work related to Sussman’s work or researchers who wish to use one of Sussman’s languages.

从 Micro-Planner 到 Conniver，再到 Scheme，Sussman 的工作始终具有声望，因此几乎总能获得接受；但这些接受群体的影响力不强，所以相关思想并不总能流行起来。

Sussman’s work always has cachet—from Micro-Planner to Conniver to Scheme—and so his work almost always gains acceptance, but the acceptance groups do not have strong presence, so these ideas do not always catch on.

令人意外的例外是 MDL：Sussman 属于这个小组，但 MDL 本身没有流行起来，在 MIT 也不受推崇。然而，MDL 的大量思想在 Conniver 中流行开来，随后又进入 Lisp-Machine Lisp。我们至今仍能看到这些思想在 Common Lisp，以及一定程度上的 EuLisp 中发挥作用。但使用这些语言的人中，有多少知道 MDL，或其主要设计者 Chris Reeves 呢？

Surprising is the exception of MDL: Sussman belonged to this group but MDL itself did not catch on, and at MIT it was not highly regarded. Nevertheless, a great many ideas from MDL caught on in Conniver and then Lisp-Machine Lisp. We still see these ideas hard at work in Common Lisp and to some extend in EuLisp. Yet how many people who use these languages know of MDL, or its prime designer Chris Reeves?

因此，在70年代，Sussman 是一个多样化的推动者；由于接受受众很小，他的接受和整合阶段都相当短。

Thus, Sussman was during the 1970’s a diversification generator whose acceptance/consolidation phases were quite short, given the small acceptance audience.

从全局来看，Scheme 将提供一些概念，供其他方言在整合阶段采用。考察80年代的 Scheme 时，我们会看到，它有一个主要接受群体，同时也有其他一些潜在群体。其主要接受群体，就是历次 Scheme 修订报告的作者们。

In the global context, Scheme would provide concepts that would be used during the consolidation phases of other dialects. When we look at Scheme in the 1980’s we will see that there is one primary acceptance group for Scheme, along with the potential of some others. The primary acceptance group for Scheme is the set of authors of the various revised reports on Scheme.

2.6 Lisp 机器

2.6 Lisp Machines

按照 Lisp 所能运行的硬件类型，可以把讨论分为两类：商用通用硬件和 Lisp 机器。所谓商用通用硬件，是为通用计算而商业化设计的机器。在本文的讨论中，可以把它理解为并非专为支持 Lisp 而设计的计算机。例如，Digital Equipment Corporation 的 VAX 11/780 属于商用通用硬件，而 Symbolics 3600 则是专为支持 Lisp 而设计的计算机。一些 Lisp 机器设计者把商用通用硬件称为“FORTRAN 机器”。

The discussion of Lisp can be broken down into two categories according to the type of hardware on which the Lisp can run. The two important categories are stock hardware and Lisp machines. A stock hardware machine is one that is commercially designed for general purpose computing. For the purposes of our discussion a stock hardware computer can be taken to be a computer that is not designed specifically to support Lisp. For example, a Digital Equipment Corporation Vax 11/780 is stock hardware, and a Symbolics 3600 is an example of a computer designed specifically to support Lisp. Some Lisp machine designers refer to stock hardware computers as FORTRAN machines.

我们使用“商用通用硬件”一词，而今天其他人可能会说“通用计算机”。后一个说法似乎更恰当，因为它与“专用硬件”相对。然而，真正应作的对比，是具备标准能力的通用硬件，与具备额外能力的通用硬件。执行 Lisp 程序时经常进行的几种操作，可以借助专用硬件得到更好的执行，或显著加速。Lisp 机器中最常获得专用硬件辅助的操作，是标签处理、函数调用和垃圾回收。但 Lisp 机器通常仍是一台通用计算机，至少原则上也能运行 Fortran 或 C 代码。

We use the term “stock hardware” where other people today might use the term “generalpurpose computer.” The latter term might seem a little more appropriate because it is in contrast to “special-purpose hardware.” However, the proper contrast is between general-purpose hardware with stock capabilities and general-purpose hardware with additional capabilities. Several of the operations frequently performed while executing Lisp programs can be better performed or significantly sped up by special hardware. (The most frequently hardware-assisted operations performed by special hardware on Lisp machines are tagging, function calling, and garbage collection.) But a Lisp machine typically is a general-purpose computer that can also be used to run Fortran or C code, at least in principle.

Lisp 机器的存储字可以设计得足够宽，以直接支持指针：地址和标签位都能放入一个存储字，而不损失任何寻址位。通过计算机中的专门数据通路，检查指针标签的指令可以执行得很快。加法等操作可以在执行运算的同时，并行检查操作数类型；如果类型不合适，就标记一个异常条件，然后采取更通用的处理方式。

The words of storage in a Lisp machine can be designed to have enough bits to support pointers directly—the address and the tag bits can both fit in a word of storage without losing any addressing bits. Instructions to check the tag of a pointer can be made fast with special data paths in the computer. Operations, such as addition, can check the types of their operands in parallel with the operation itself, and if the types are not suitable, an exceptional condition can be flagged, and a more general course of action can then be taken.

函数调用是 Lisp 程序中最频繁的操作之一。栈帧的构造可以由硬件完成。缓存栈的一部分，可以显著影响 Lisp 实现的性能。

Function calling is one of the most frequent operations in Lisp programs. The construction of stack frames can be performed by hardware. Caching parts of the stack can make a major performance difference to a Lisp implementation.

垃圾回收通常按需执行：动态堆的空闲空间耗尽时，可以启动垃圾回收以获得空闲空间。垃圾回收可能耗时较长；让它与其他 Lisp 操作并发执行，就能消除相关的长时间停顿。已知的大多数增量垃圾回收技术，都最适合借助专用硬件实现。

Garbage collection is often performed by need—when the dynamic heap runs out of free space a garbage collection can be initiated to gain some free space. Garbage collection can take a relatively long time, and performing it concurrently with other Lisp operations can eliminate the long pause associated with garbage collection. Most of the known techniques for incremental garbage collection are best implemented with special hardware.

许多 Lisp 机器本质上是通用计算机，只是为 Lisp 专门编写了微码。

Many Lisp machines are essentially general purpose computers which have been specially microcoded for Lisp.

尽管此前已有关于 Lisp 机器的非正式讨论，Peter Deutsch 似乎发表了第一份具体提案 [Deutsch, 1973]。Deutsch 勾勒的基本愿景是一台单用户、小型计算机级别的机器，通过专门微码运行 Lisp，并支持 Lisp 开发环境。该论文中对 Lisp 产生持久影响的两个关键思想是：（1）基于函数的读访问与写访问的对偶性；（2）通过 CDR 编码紧凑表示线性列表。

Though ideas for a Lisp machine had been informally discussed before, Peter Deutsch seems to have published the first concrete proposal [Deutsch, 1973]. Deutsch outlined the basic vision of a single-user minicomputer-class machine that would be specially microcoded to run Lisp and support a Lisp development environment. The two key ideas from Deutsch’s paper that have had a lasting impact on Lisp are (1) the duality of load and store access based on functions and (2) the compact representation of linear lists through CDR-coding.

此前所有 Lisp 方言都用 CAR 读取点对的第一个分量，而用一个名义上无关的函数 RPLACA 写入同一分量。Deutsch 建议，CAR 这样的函数应同时具有“加载”和“存储”模式。如果以加载模式调用 (f a1 ... an)，就应返回一个值；如果以存储模式调用，例如 (f a1 ... an v)，就应把新值 v 存入加载版本会访问的位置。Deutsch 指出，每个访问函数都应关联两个内部函数，一个负责加载，一个负责存储；当该函数出现在某组特定的特殊形式中时，应调用存储函数。他提出的语法很能说明问题：下面就是建议的 RPLACA 定义。

All Lisp dialects up to that time had one function CAR to read the first component of a dotted pair and a nominally unrelated function RPLACA to write that same component. Deutsch proposed that functions like CAR should have both a “load” mode and a “store” mode. If (f a1 ... an) is called in load mode, it should return a value; if called in store mode, as in (f a1 ... an v), the new value v should be stored in the location that would be accessed by the load version. Deutsch indicated that there should be two internal functions associated with every accessor function, one for loading and one for storing, and that the store function should be called when the function is mentioned in a particular set of special forms. However, his syntax is suggestive; here is the proposed definition of RPLACA:

(lambda (x y) (setfq (car x) y))

Deutsch 解释，这里的特殊形式叫 SETFQ，是因为“它引用函数，而对其他一切求值”。在 Lisp-Machine Lisp 中，这个名称被缩写为 SETF。Deutsch 把对偶函数的思想归功于 Alan Kay。

Deutsch commented that the special form used here is called SETFQ because “it quotes the function and evaluates everything else.” This name was abbreviated to SETF in Lisp-Machine Lisp. Deutsch attributed the idea of dual functions to Alan Kay.

2.6.1 MIT Lisp 机器：1974—1978

2.6.1 MIT Lisp Machines: 1974–1978

Richard Greenblatt 于1974年启动 MIT Lisp 机器项目；他的提案 [Greenblatt, 1974] 引用了 Deutsch 的论文。项目成员还包括 Thomas Knight、Jack Holloway 和 Pitts Jarvis。他们设计的机器名为 CONS，其设计借鉴了 Xerox PARC ALTO 微处理器、DEC PDP-11/40、CMU 对 PDP-11/40 的扩展，以及 DEC 的 Sam Fuller 提出的一些指令修改思想。

Richard Greenblatt started the MIT Lisp Machine project in 1974; his proposal [Greenblatt, 1974] cites the Deutsch paper. The project also included Thomas Knight, Jack Holloway, and Pitts Jarvis. The machine they designed was called CONS, and its design was based on ideas from the Xerox PARC ALTO microprocessor, the DEC PDP-11/40, the PDP-11/40 extensions done by CMU, and some ideas on instruction modification suggested by Sam Fuller at DEC.

这台机器旨在以良好性能支持一种向上兼容 MacLisp、同时增加“Muddle-Conniver”参数声明语法的 Lisp。其他目标包括：成本不过高，每台低于70,000美元；单用户操作；提供通用目标语言并标准化过程调用；使编译程序的存储效率达到 PDP-10 的三倍；硬件支持类型检查和垃圾回收；主要用 Lisp 实现，减少对汇编或其他底层实现语言的依赖；面向显示屏的交互；较长的连续运行时间，无须频繁重启机器来启动 Lisp；以及采用下文多种技术的面向帧的存储。

This machine was designed to have good performance while supporting a version of Lisp upwards-compatible with MacLisp but augmented with “Muddle-Conniver” argument declaration syntax. Its other goals included non-prohibitive cost (less than $70,000 per machine), single user operation, common target language along with standardization of procedure calls, a factor of three better storage efficiency than the PDP-10 for compiled programs, hardware support for type checking and garbage collection, a largely Lisp-coded implementation (less reliance on assembly language or other low-level implementation language), and display-oriented interaction. , lengthy uptimes (infrequent need to reboot the machine with to start Lisp), frame-oriented storage using a variety of techniques described below.

主要的存储管理思想，是“不可见指针”。这是一个指向存储位置的指针；除存储管理代码外，任何东西都看不见它。不可见指针有一个特殊标签。任何试图访问包含不可见指针的存储位置的操作，都会触发一种特殊的间接寻址操作：实际访问的是不可见指针所指向的存储位置。对不可见指针的操作，会由微码自动、透明地处理；或者通过陷阱处理程序完成。不可见指针可用于多种场合：

The CONS machine was to support invisible pointers, which are a generalization of the indirect pointer in the PDP-10. An indirect pointer was a memory storage format that had the following effect: If M is a memory location containing an indirect pointer to location T, then a special instruction that fetches from M will invisibly fetch from T, and a special instruction that stores into M will store into T. Normal memory instructions would store into and fetch from M.

另一个重要特性是硬件支持增量垃圾回收。垃圾回收器可以在程序执行期间逐步工作，避免传统 Lisp 系统中长时间的回收停顿。

Invisible pointers differ from this mechanism by switching the specification of such indirection from the instruction to the data, so that a special tag on the location M, for example, would signal that indirection to T would be used, not a special instruction.

这些机器还使用了称为“转发指针”的机制。当对象被移动时，可以在原位置留下转发指针。硬件会识别它并自动跟随到对象的新位置。这有助于实现垃圾回收以及其他存储管理功能。

Invisible pointers were used for the following things:

• 增量垃圾回收：垃圾回收器的操作与 Lisp 系统的操作交错进行。在复制式回收器中，对象被复制后，会在原处留下一个属于不可见指针的转发指针，使对旧位置的引用转发到新位置。

• Incremental garbage collection in which the operation of the garbage collector is interspersed with the operation of the Lisp system. In a copying collector, when an object is copied, a forwarding pointer that is an invisible pointer is left behind so that references to the old location would forward to the new one.

• CDR 编码：列表采用线性的、类似向量的表示，而不是纯粹的 CONS 单元表示。向量的每个元素包含逻辑上存放在 CAR 中的对象，并带有标签，说明下一个连续单元就是 CDR。当对这种列表使用 RPLACD 时，原来的向量元素会被一个不可见指针取代，该指针指向一个真正的 CONS 单元，而不再使用更优化的表示。这样就能最大限度保留 CDR 编码节省的存储空间。

• CDR-coding, in which a linear, vector-like representation is used for lists rather than the pure CONS cell representations. In this representation each element of the vector contains the object logically stored in the CAR, and the element is tagged indicating that the next sequential cell is the CDR. When RPLACD is used on such a list, the old vector element is replaced with an invisible pointer that points to a real CONS cell instead of the more optimal representation. Thus the storage savings of CDR-coding can be maximally retained.

• 统一处理特殊变量与局部变量：可以用相同的寻址模式引用两者，因为特殊变量可以表示为一个存储位置，其中包含指向值单元的不可见指针。

• Uniform treatment of special and local variables. The same addressing mode can be used to refer to special and local variables because the special variable can be represented as a location with an invisible pointer to the value cell.

• 可以实现类似 MDL 所定义的 locative，提供一个用于引用的对象；该对象是指向实际分量的不可见指针。

• Locatives similar to those defined in MDL can be implemented providing an object to use for reference, where that object is an invisible pointer to the actual component.

CONS 机器制造出来后，又设计了改进的后继版本 CADR，并制造了几十台。CADR 是一款使用专门微码的32位处理器，具有一些对 Lisp 特别有用的数据通路。这些机器成为 MIT 人工智能实验室的计算主力，因此，成立一家衍生公司将其商业化，似乎顺理成章。然而，由于主要参与者之间存在分歧，最终成立了两家公司：Lisp Machines Incorporated（LMI）和 Symbolics。最初，两家公司都制造 CADR 的克隆机。不久后，Symbolics 推出3600系列，在随后的5年中成为 Lisp 机器性能的行业领军者。

The CONS machine was built; then a subsequent improved version named the CADR was designed and some dozens of them were built. The CADR was a specially microcoded, 32-bit processor, with some data paths of particular usefulness to Lisp. These became the computational mainstay within the MIT AI Lab, and it seemed sensible to spin offa company to commercialize this machine. Because of disagreements among the principals, however, two companies were formed: Lisp Machines Incorporated (LMI) and Symbolics. Initially each manufactured CADR clones. Soon thereafter Symbolics introduced its 3600 line, which became the industry leader in Lisp machine performance for the next 5 years.

尽管 Greenblatt 特别注意提供支持快速垃圾回收的硬件机制，早期 MIT Lisp 机器实际上有好几年都没有实现垃圾回收器；更确切地说，即使回收器出现后，用户也宁愿禁用它。多数编程工具，尤其是编译器和程序文本编辑器，都被设计为尽量避免分配 CONS 单元，并在可能时显式回收临时数据结构。在此基础上，Lisp 机器的地址空间足够大，虚拟内存系统也足够好，用户可以连续运行数天，甚至几周，才需要把正在运行的“世界”保存到磁盘，再重新启动。这样来回复制到磁盘，相当于一种缓慢、手工触发的复制式垃圾回收器。虽然70年代有大量关于交错式和并发垃圾回收的理论研究 [?; ?; Baker, 1978; ?]，但直到 David Moon 发明短生期垃圾回收，并在 Lisp 机器上实现后，持续式垃圾回收才得到普遍接受 [Moon, 1984]。短生期垃圾回收后来也被改造用于通用硬件。

While Greenblatt had paid particular care to providing hardware mechanisms to support fast garbage collection, the early MIT Lisp Machines in fact did not implement a garbage collector for quite some years; or rather, even when the garbage collector appeared, users preferred to disable it. Most of the programming tools (notably the compiler and program text editor) were designed to avoid consing and to explicitly reclaim temporary data structures whenever possible; given this, the Lisp Machine address spaces were large enough, and the virtual memory system good enough, that a user could run for several days or even a few weeks before having to save out the running “world” to disk and restart it. Such copying back and forth to disk was equivalent to a slow, manually triggered copying garbage collector. (While there was a great deal of theoretical work on interleaved and concurrent garbage collection during the 1970’s [?; ?; Baker, 1978; ?], continuous garbage collection was not universally accepted until David Moon’s invention of ephemeral garbage collection and its implementation on Lisp Machines [Moon, 1984]. Ephemeral garbage collection was subsequently adapted for use on stock hardware.)

早期 MIT Lisp-Machine Lisp 方言 [Weinreb, November 1978] 与 MacLisp 非常相似。它实现了既定目标：只需极少的移植工作，就能支持 MacLisp 程序。相对于 MacLisp，最重要的扩展包括：

The early MIT Lisp-Machine Lisp dialect [Weinreb, November 1978] was very similar to MacLisp. It lived up to its stated goal of supporting MacLisp programs with only minimal porting effort. The most important extensions beyond MacLisp included:

• 改进的编程环境，主要包括常驻编译器、调试设施和文本编辑器。虽然这使 Lisp-Machine Lisp 更接近 Interlisp 那种完全基于 Lisp 的编程环境理想，但它仍坚定地以文件为中心。文本编辑器是 EMACS 的仿制品，先叫 EINE，即 EINE Is Not EMACS，后来叫 ZWEI，即 ZWEI Was EINE Initially。这两个递归缩写同时又是德语中的版本数字，因而格外有趣。

• An improved programming environment, consisting primarily of a resident compiler, debugging facilities, and a text editor. While this brought Lisp-Machine Lisp closer to the Interlisp ideal of a completely Lisp-based programming environment, it was still firmly file-oriented. The text editor was an EMACS clone, first called EINE (EINE Is Not EMACS) and then ZWEI (ZWEI Was EINE Initially), the recursive acronyms of course being doubly delicious as version numbers in German.

• 复杂的 lambda 参数表，包括 &optional、&key、&rest 和 &aux。

• Complex lambda lists, including &optional, &key, &rest, and &aux

• Locative，提供类似 C 的能力，可以指向结构内部的位置。

• Locatives, which provided a C-like ability to point into the middle of a structure

• DEFMACRO，更方便的宏定义设施。它提供一种机制，用来引用将传给宏函数的形式中的各个部分。见第3.3节。

• DEFMACRO, a much more convenient macro definition facility This is a macro definition facility that provides a mechanism for referring to parts of the form which will be passed to the macro function. See section 3.3.

• 反引号，一种通过填充模板来构造数据结构的语法。这项列表构造功能源自 MDL 的求值模型和 Conniver 的列表构造设施。

• Backquote, a syntax for constructing data structures by filling in a template . This is a feature for constructing lists, and it derives from the MDL evaluation model and the Conniver list building facility.

• 栈组，提供协程设施。

• Stack groups, which provided a coroutine facility

• 多值返回：函数调用可以返回多个值，而无须构造列表。此前人们采用过各种临时性技巧；Lisp-Machine Lisp 是首个为此提供原语的 Lisp 方言。其他语言，例如 POP-2，也提供过多值返回。

• Multiple values, the ability to pass more than one value back from a function invocation without having to construct a list. Prior to this various ad hoc techniques had been used; Lisp-Machine Lisp was the first dialect of Lisp to provide primitives for it. (Other languages such as POP-2 have also provided for multiple values.)

• DEFSTRUCT，记录结构定义设施，可与 Interlisp 的记录包比较。

• DEFSTRUCT, a record structure definition facility (compare the Interlisp record package)

• 针对特殊变量的闭包。这些闭包不同于 Scheme 的闭包：程序员必须显式列出环境捕获的变量；调用闭包时，需要把 SPECIAL 变量绑定到保存的值。

• Closures over special variables. These closures were not like the ones in Scheme; the variables captured by the environment must be explicitly listed by the programmer and invocation of the closure required the binding of SPECIAL variables to the saved values.

• Flavors，一种支持多重继承的、非层次式的面向对象编程系统，由 Howard Cannon 和 David A. Moon 设计，并集成到 Lisp 机器编程环境的部分组件中。尤其是窗口系统，就是用 Flavors 编写的 [Weinreb, March 1981]。

• Flavors, an object-oriented, non-hierarchical programming system with multiple inheritance, was designed by Howard Cannon and David A. Moon and integrated into parts of the Lisp Machine programming environment (the window system, in particular, was written using Flavors [Weinreb, March 1981]).

• SETF，广义变量设施。

• SETF, a facility for generalized variables

考虑向 Lisp 机器 Lisp 解释器输入以下一系列形式。最后一个形式返回3，而不是7，因为调用 fun 中的闭包，会把特殊变量 y 重新设为原来的值。以下代码使用 Lisp 机器 Lisp 编写：

Consider the following sequence of forms passed to a Lisp machine Lisp interpreter. The last form returns 3 instead of 7, because the special variable y is reset to its old value by invoking the closure in fun, written in Lisp machine Lisp:

(defun foo (x)
(+ (bar) x))
(defun bar () y)
(setq fun ((lambda (y)
(closure ’(x) (function foo)))
3))
(setq y 7)
(funcall fun 0) => 3 ;not 7

Common Lisp 是后来最流行的 Lisp 方言，其中 SETF 的广泛使用，可以沿着 Symbolics Zetalisp 和 MacLisp，追溯到 MIT Lisp-Machine Lisp 的影响，再经 Greenblatt 的提案追溯到 Peter Deutsch，最终追溯到 Alan Kay。

The use of SETF throughout Common Lisp—a later and the most popular dialect of Lisp—can be traced through Symbolics Zetalisp and MacLisp to the influence of MIT Lisp-Machine Lisp and then back through Greenblatt’s proposal to Peter Deutsch and thence to Alan Kay.

统一处理访问，也就是状态的读取和写入，使 Common Lisp 比原本可能的样子更一致。程序员不再需要同时记住读取函数，例如 CAR，以及单独的写入或更新函数，例如 RPLACA；也无须记住参数顺序，例如 RPLACA 究竟先放点对，还是先放要写入其 car 的新值。若读取操作的一般形式是 (f ...)，写入形式就是 (setf (f ...) newvalue)。关于数据读写，程序员只需知道这一点。

The uniform treatment of access—reading and writing of state—has made Common Lisp more uniform that it might otherwise be. It is no longer necessary to remember both a reader function (such as CAR) and also a separate writer or update function (such as RPLACA), nor to remember the order of arguments (for RPLACA, which comes first, the dotted pair or the new value for its car?). If the general form of a read operation is (f ...), then the form of the write is (setf (f ...) newvalue), and that is all the programmer needs to know about reading and writing data.

在 CLOS 中，这一思想被扩展到方法。如果某个方法 M 被指定为读取器，调用形式是 (M object)，就可以定义一个写入方法，其调用形式为 (setf (M object) newvalue)。

In CLOS, this idea was extended to methods. If there is a method M that is specified to act as a reader and is invoked as (M object), then it is possible to define a writer method that is invoked as (setf (M object) newvalue).

CLOS 如此契合这一惯用法，并不令人意外。如果 Alan Kay 在1973年前后启发了这一思想，那么当时他正处于早期参与 Smalltalk 的阶段。读取和写入都是对象可以支持的方法；CLOS 对 Kay 愿景的 Lisp 版本所作的适配，不过是重新发现了这一思想最初的面向对象根源。

That CLOS fits this idiom so well is no surprise. If Alan Kay was the inspiration for the idea around 1973, he was in the midst of his early Smalltalk involvement. Reading and writing are both methods that an object can support, and the CLOS adaptation of the Lisp version of Kay’s vision was a simple reinvention of the object-oriented genesis of the idea.

2.6.2 Xerox Lisp 机器：1973—1980

2.6.2 Xerox Lisp Machines: 1973–1980

Alto 是1973年开发的一台可编写微码的机器 [Thacker, 1982]，在 Xerox 用于个人计算实验，使用 Interlisp 以及 Mesa 等其他语言 [Geschke, 1977]。Alto 版 Interlisp 环境大约于1975年开始在 PARC 和斯坦福大学使用。

The Alto was a microcodable machine developed in 1973 [Thacker, 1982] and used for personal computing experimentation at Xerox, using Interlisp and other languages such as Mesa [Geschke, 1977]. The Alto version of the Interlisp environment first went into use at PARC and at Stanford University around 1975.

Alto 标配64K个16位字的内存，可扩展至256K字。对单用户计算机而言，这已相当大，但仍只有 PDP-10 内存的一半。即使采用 Deutsch 在 [Deutsch, 1973] 中讨论的全部代码密度技巧，这台机器仍不足以支撑庞大的 Interlisp 环境，因此没有得到用户的广泛接受。

The Alto was standardly equipped with 64K 16-bit words of memory, expandable up to 256K words, which was quite large for a single-user computer but still only half the memory of a PDP-10. The machine proved to be underpowered for the large Interlisp environment, even with all the code density tricks discussed by Deutsch in [Deutsch, 1973], so it was not widely accepted by users.

Alto 也用于构建第一个 Smalltalk 环境，即“临时 Dynabook”，在这方面它相对成功。

The Alto was also used to build the first Smalltalk environment—the “interim Dynabook”—and here it was relatively successful.

1976年，Xerox PARC 开始设计名为 Dorado，或 Xerox 1132，的机器。它采用 ECL，即发射极耦合逻辑，这是当时一种高速数字逻辑实现技术，旨在取代 Alto。1978年已有一台原型机，可以运行全部 Alto 软件。1979年完成了重新设计，并制造了一批，供 Xerox 内部以及斯坦福大学等实验地点使用。Dorado 专为解释编译器生成的字节码而设计，也正是这样运行 Alto 软件的。它本质上是一台仿真机器。

In 1976, Xerox Parc began the design of a machine called the Dorado (or Xerox 1132), which was an ECL (Emitter Coupled Logic, an at-the-time fast digital logic implementation technology) machine designed to replace the Alto. A prototype available in 1978 ran all Alto software. A redesign was completed in 1979 and a number of them were built for use within Xerox and at certain experimental sites such as Stanford University. The Dorado was specifically designed to interpret byte codes produced by compilers, and this is how the Dorado ran Alto software. The Dorado was basically an emulation machine.

Interlisp 利用 Interlisp 虚拟机模型 [Moore, 1976] 被移植到这台机器上。Dorado 运行 Interlisp 的速度，超过 KL-10 以单用户方式运行 Interlisp 的速度。如果能够广泛商业供应，它本会是一台很出色的 Lisp 机器。但 Dorado 是按原型机设计的，始终未能通过 FCC 的射频发射要求〔原稿注：??? 请核实此说是否属实〕，因此仅限于 Xerox 的场所以及斯坦福大学等特殊实验环境使用。

Interlisp was ported to this machine using the Interlisp virtual machine model [Moore, 1976]. The Dorado running Interlisp was faster than a KL-10 running single-user Interlisp, and it would have proved a very nice Lisp machine if it had been made widely available commercially. the Dorado was designed as a prototype and was never able to pass the FCC RF emission requirements (??? check this is true), and its use was limited to Xerox sites and some special experimental settings, such as at Stanford University.

Interlisp 也以类似方式移植到一台更小、更便宜的机器 Dolphin（1100）上；该机于70年代末作为 Lisp 机器商业销售。Dolphin 的性能优于 Alto，但作为 Lisp 执行平台仍然太差，因此从未真正成功。

Interlisp was similarly ported to a smaller, cheaper machine called the Dolphin (1100), which was made commercially available as a Lisp machine in the late 1970’s. The performance of the Dolphin was better than that of the Alto, but bad enough that the machine was never truly successful as a Lisp engine.

80年代初，Xerox 又制造了 Dandelion（1108）。它比 Dolphin 快得多，但仍不及 Dorado。由于这三种机器的名称都以字母 D 开头，它们被统称为“D 机器”。

In the early 1980’s, Xerox built another machine called the Dandelion (1108), which was considerably faster than the Dolphin but still not as fast as the Dorado. Because the names of these three machines all began with the letter “D”, they became collectively known as the “D-machines.”

所有 Xerox Lisp 机器都使用增量式引用计数垃圾回收器 [Deutsch, 1976]：每次分配存储时，执行垃圾回收过程中的几个步骤。因此，每单位时间用于垃圾回收的工作量很小，而且有界。

All the Xerox Lisp machines used a reference-count garbage collector [Deutsch, 1976] that was incremental: a few steps of the garbage collection process would execute each time storage was allocated. Therefore there was a short, bounded amount of work done for garbage collection per unit time.

70年代末，BBN 也制造了一台名为 Jericho 的机器，用作 Interlisp 执行平台。它始终仅供 BBN 内部使用。[?]

In the late 1970’s BBN also built a machine, the Jericho, that was used as an Interlisp engine. It remained internal to BBN. [?]

早期 Lisp 机器历史分析

Analysis of Early Lisp Machine History

MIT Lisp 机器项目，整合了 MacLisp、MDL 和 Conniver 所体现的多样化成果，同时加入了一些新扩展，以及从其他来源改造而来的思想。

The MIT Lisp machine project represented a consolidation of the diversification demonstrated by MacLisp, MDL, and Conniver. Some new extensions and some adaptations from other sources were made.

接受群体仍是本地研究人员，但到80年代，这一点将发生变化：新的接受群体将是商业开发者和企业研究实验室。争取接受的竞争，将围绕“可接受的计算机”和“可接受的工作流程”展开。工作流程还包括 Lisp 程序员的特点、成本和可获得性。

The acceptance group was still the local researchers, but during the 1980’s that would change: the new acceptance group would become the commercial developer and corporate research lab. The acceptance battle would center around the factors of acceptable computer and acceptable work process. Included in the work process is the nature, cost, and availability of Lisp programmers.

Xerox Lisp 机器体现的是移植，也就是语言基本停滞时会发生的事情。Interlisp 能否被接受，如今取决于它选择哪种机器。真正有竞争力的可接受机器只有两类：VAX 和 D 机器。任何成功依赖 VAX 的 Lisp，都没能挺过80年代。80年代初的竞争，将在 D 机器与源自 MIT 的 Lisp 机器之间展开；而同一时期开始的大整合，将彻底改变竞争的性质。MacLisp 和 Interlisp 都将很快消亡，纯粹形式的 Lisp 机器 Lisp 也将随之消亡。

The Xerox Lisp machines represented porting, which is what happens when the language is essentially stagnant. Acceptance of Interlisp would now hinge on its choice of machine. There would be two real contenders for the acceptable machine—the Vax and the D-machines. No Lisp whose success depended on the Vax would survive the 1980’s. It would be a race between the D-machines, and the MIT-derived Lisp machines in the early 1980’s, and the great consolidation started in the early 1980’s would completely change the nature of the game. MacLisp and Interlisp would both be soon dead, and Lisp machine Lisp in its pure form along with them.

必须认识到，随着 Lisp 机器的出现，Lisp 的声望正在上升。其接受群体也即将扩大；当时人们认为，在 Lisp 机器设计者和开发者的悉心引导下，技术将取得胜利。公司将纷纷成立，乘上人工智能和 Lisp 技术的浪潮，消耗巨额风险资本。这是对“FORTRAN 机器堡垒”发动的第一次，也是截至原文写作时唯一一次看似可信的进攻。我们知道，这次进攻最终失败了；找出原因，也许能让我们更了解自己，既作为计算专业人士，也作为普通人。

It is important to realize that the cachet of Lisp through the existence of Lisp machines was on the rise. The acceptance group would soon also be on the rise, and the feeling was that technology would triumph under the careful guidance of the Lisp machine designers and developers. Companies would form to ride the wave of AI and Lisp technology, consuming immense quantities of venture capital. This was the first—and until today the only—plausible attack mounted on fortress FORTRAN-machine. As we know that attack would fail, and discovering the reasons may teach us a lot about ourselves as computing professionals, and as people.

摆脱了旧体系结构的地址空间限制后，各家 Lisp 机器公司都大幅扩充 Lisp 实现，在编程环境中加入图形、窗口和鼠标交互能力。Lisp 语言本身，尤其是 MIT Lisp 机器上的 Lisp，其功能数量和复杂性也不断增长。虽然其中一些思想源自别处，但它们在整个 Lisp 社群中的普及，既受到思想本身声望的推动，也同样受到 Lisp 机器的成功和声望的推动。

Freed from the address-space constraints of previous architectures, all the Lisp machine companies produced greatly expanded Lisp implementations, adding graphics, windowing capabilities, and mouse interaction capabilities to their programming environments. The Lisp language itself, particularly on the MIT Lisp Machines, also grew in the number and complexity of features. Though some of these ideas originated elsewhere, their adoption throughout the Lisp community was driven as much by the success and cachet of the Lisp machines as by the cachet of the ideas themselves.

然而，对多数用户来说，价值最终在于软件，而不在于使软件成为可能的硬件技术。Lisp 机器公司在80年代末陷入困境，或许正是因为没有充分理解这一事实的后果。通用硬件最终再次强大到足以支持 Lisp，运行于这些机器上的 Lisp 实现开始具备有效竞争力。

Nevertheless, for most users the value lay ultimately in the software and not in its enabling hardware technology. The Lisp machine companies ran into difficulty in the late 1980’s, perhaps because they didn’t fully understand the consequences of this fact. General-purpose hardware eventually became good enough to support Lisp once again, and Lisp implementations on such machines began to compete effectively.

2.7 IBM 的 Lisp：Lisp360 与 Lisp370

2.7 IBM Lisps: Lisp360 and Lisp370

尽管最早的 Lisp 实现在 IBM 计算机上，IBM 却在60年代末逐渐淡出 Lisp 领域，原因有二：MIT 与 DEC 的合作更好，以及 MIT 与 IBM 之间的专利纠纷。

Although the first Lisps were implemented on IBM computers, IBM faded from the Lisp scene during the late 1960’s, for two reasons: better cooperation between MIT and DEC and a patent dispute between MIT and IBM.

60年代初，Digital Equipment Corporation 与 MIT 讨论其计算机需求，并加入了一些有利于 Lisp 实现的功能。与7094一样，每个36位字可以容纳两个地址，构成一个点对；但 PDP-10 的地址为18位，而非15位。PDP-10 的半字指令使 CAR、CDR、RPLACA 和 RPLACD 特别容易实现，且速度很快。栈指令和基于栈的函数调用指令，也提高了 Lisp 函数调用的速度。MIT 人工智能实验室获得了第一台，或第二台，PDP-6；它一直是实验室的主要计算平台，直到被后继机 PDP-10 取代。

In the early 1960’s, Digital Equipment Corporation discussed with MIT the needs MIT had for computers, and features were added to help Lisp implementations. As on the 7094, each 36- bit word could hold two addresses to form a dotted pair, but on the PDP-10 each address was 18 bits instead of 15. The PDP-10 halfword instructions made CAR, CDR, RPLACA, and RPLACD particularly fast and easy to implement. The stack instructions and the stack-based function calling instructions improved the speed of of Lisp function calls. (The MIT AI Laboratory received the first—or second—PDP-6, and it was the lab’s mainstay computing engine until it was replaced by its successor, the PDP-10.)

此外，60年代初，IBM 和 MIT 就谁发明了磁芯存储器发生争执，IBM 坚持对 MIT 执行其专利权。MIT 的回应是不再像过去那样广泛使用 IBM 设备。这进一步推动了对 DEC 设备的采用，尤其是在 Lisp 和人工智能领域。

Moreover, in the early 1960’s, IBM and MIT disputed who had invented core memory, and IBM insisted on enforcing its patents against MIT. MIT responded by declining to use IBM equipment as extensively as it had in the past. This provided further impetus to use DEC equipment instead, particularly for Lisp and AI.

尽管如此，IBM 仍为 IBM 360 实现了 Lisp，名为 Lisp360。IBM 370 推出后，又开始实现 Lisp370，后来改称 Lisp/VM。

Nevertheless, Lisp was implemented at IBM for the IBM 360 and called Lisp360. When the IBM 370 came out, Lisp370 implementation began. Lisp370 was later called Lisp/VM.

Lisp360 基本上是一种批处理 Lisp，在大学教学中使用得相当广泛。

Lisp360 was basically a batch Lisp, and it was used fairly extensively for teaching in universities.

Lisp370 的起点，是根据用 SECD 模型 [Landin, 1964] 表达的形式语义，定义一个核心 Lisp。这一定义只占一两页。项目由纽约州约克敦高地 IBM Thomas J. Watson 研究中心的 Fred Blair 领导，他也制定了 SECD 定义。团队其他成员包括 Richard W. Ryniker II、Cyril Alberga、Mark Wegman 和 Martin Mikelsons；他们主要服务于自己以及少数研究小组，例如约克敦的 SCRATCHPAD 小组和一些人工智能小组。

Lisp370 began with the definition of a core Lisp based on a formal semantics expressed in the SECD model [Landin, 1964]. This definition fit on one or two pages. The Lisp370 project was under the direction of Fred Blair (who developed the SECD definition) at the IBM Thomas J. Watson Research Center in Yorktown Heights, New York. Other members of the group included Richard W. Ryniker II, Cyril Alberga, Mark Wegman, and Martin Mikelsons; they served primarily themselves and a few research groups, such as the SCRATCHPAD group and some AI groups at Yorktown.

Lisp370 同时支持特殊绑定和词法绑定，也支持针对词法变量和特殊变量的闭包，采用的技术类似意大利面栈。特殊值单元被实现为实际值的缓存。每个值单元配有一个有效性单元，指明该绑定在哪个控制树分支上有效。如果无效，就从当前栈帧开始搜索，以设置正确的值和有效性信息。该算法由 Allen Brown 于1976年夏天开发，Richard Gabriel 和 William Scherlis 提供了一些帮助；Brown 此前曾是 MIT 的 Sussman 的学生。Jon L White 在1977整个日历年于约克敦高地从事 Lisp370 工作，随后返回 MIT；他在 IBM 的经历，对后续 MacLisp 的发展以及 NIL 方言产生了一些影响。

Lisp370 supported both special binding and lexical binding, as well as closures over both lexical and special variables, using a technique similar to spaghetti stacks. The implementation of special value cells took the form of a cache for the actual values. The value cell was paired with a validation cell that specified a branch of the control tree for which the binding was valid. When invalid, a search from the current stack frame was made to set the correct value and the validation. The algorithm for this was developed by Allen Brown (who had previously been a student of Sussman’s at MIT) with some assistance from Richard Gabriel and William Scherlis in the summer of 1976. Jon L White spent calendar year 1977 at Yorktown Heights working on Lisp370 and then returned to MIT; his experience at IBM had some influence on subsequent MacLisp development and on the NIL dialect.

Lisp370 拥有类似 Interlisp 的编程环境，可在硬拷贝终端和随处可见的3270字符显示终端上工作。Interlisp-10 模型很合适，因为它本来就是考虑慢速终端而开发的，而多数 IBM 大型机交互的半双工特性也有类似感觉。1976年夏天，Gabriel 复制自己为斯坦福人工智能实验室 MacLisp 编写的环境，写出了这一环境的首个版本；后者本身又是他1974年为伊利诺伊大学协调科学实验室的 MacLisp 所写环境的修订版。后来，Mark Wegman 及其约克敦同事将环境扩展为基于屏幕，而非基于行，的交互和编辑。不过，它仍保留了基于 S 表达式的编辑器，以及 Gabriel 最初拼装的较原始系统的一些其他基本特征。

Lisp370 had an Interlisp-like programming environment written to operate on both hardcopy terminals and the ubiquitous 3270 character-display terminal. The Interlisp-10 model was ideal because it was developed with slow terminals in mind, and the half-duplex nature of most IBM mainframe interactions had a similar feel. During the summer of 1976, Gabriel wrote the first version of this environment as a duplicate of the environment he had written for MacLisp at the Stanford AI Lab. (this environment itself being a revision of one he had written for MacLisp at the Co-ordinated Science Lab at the University of Illinois in 1974). Later, Mark Wegman and his colleagues at Yorktown extended this environment to include screen-based—rather than linebased—interaction and editing. , though it still retained the s-expression based editor and some of the other essential characteristics of the more primitive system Gabriel put together.

底层 Lisp 系统也得到改进，例如提高性能，并将编译环境与运行时环境分离；后者通过使用独立的 Lisp 映像实现。

Improvements were made to the underlying Lisp system, such as good performance and the separation of the compilation and runtime environments (which was accomplished through the use of separate Lisp images).

后来，360/370 系列计算机上又出现了其他 Lisp，包括几种 Common Lisp。IBM 370 上第一个 Common Lisp 由 Intermetrics 编写，具有良好的编译技术；但该 Lisp 的整体构造不佳，在 Lisp 世界中从未产生多大影响。几年后，Lucid 根据与 IBM 的合同，把自己的 Common Lisp 移植到370上。80年代末，IBM 撤回对 Lisp370 的支持，转而支持 Common Lisp。

Other Lisps later appeared on the 360/370 line of computers, including several Common Lisps. The first Common Lisp to appear on the IBM 370 was written by Intermetrics, featuring good compilation technology. However, the Lisp was not well-constructed and never made much of an impact in the Lisp world. Several years later, Lucid ported its Common Lisp to the 370 under contract to IBM. IBM withdrew its support of Lisp370 in the late 1980’s in favor of Common Lisp.

分析

Analysis

约克敦高地的这些 Lisp，是围绕 T. J. Watson 研究实验室本地接受群体展开的多样化成果。具体而言，Fred Blair、Richard W. Ryniker II、Cyril Alberga、Mark Wegman 和 Martin Mikelsons 组成的团队，主要服务于自己和少数研究小组，例如 Scratchpad 小组及一些人工智能小组。

The Yorktown Heights Lisps were based on a diversification surrounding the local acceptance group at the T. J. Watson Research Laboratory. In particular, this group consisting of Fred Blair, Richard W. Ryniker II, Cyril Alberga, Mark Wegman, and Martin Mikelsons served primarily themselves and a few research groups, such as the Scratchpad group and some AI groups.

访问人员也影响了多样化的方向。例如，来自 MIT 人工智能实验室的 Allen Brown 于1975至1978年在约克敦工作，Jon L White 在1977整个日历年在那里工作。Richard Gabriel 则在1976年夏天开发 Lisp370 编程环境，不过他实际位于加州圣何塞的 IBM 研究中心。

This group had visitors that influenced the direction of diversification. For example, Allen Brown who was at the MIT AI Lab worked at Yorktown from 1975 to 1978, Jon L White worked there during the 1977 calendar year. Richard Gabriel worked during the summer of 1976 on the Lisp370 programming environment (though he was physically at the IBM research center in San Jose, California).

接受是局部的，也没有争议；但整合与多样化的方向，取决于来自其他环境的员工所带来的影响。除上述人员外，Mark Wegman 加入团队的时间比其他人晚；他的到来，恰逢团队对编程环境的愿景超越了 Gabriel 最初引入的范围。

Acceptance was local and noncontroversial, but consolidation along with diversification was determined by influence from employees who came from other environments. In addition to the ones listed, Mark Wegman was a more recent addition to the team than the others, and his arrival coincided with a broader vision of the programming environment than Gabriel introduced.

除了作为商业化的自然目标外，370 从未具有任何声望。事实证明，即使有一度极为流行的 Common Lisp，370 也从未在 Lisp 的成功中扮演多么重要的角色。Lisp 的自然接受群体中，没有人把370视为一台合适且具有可用环境的机器，尽管 Lisp370 确实提供了不错的开发环境。

The 370 never had any cachet except as the natural target of commercialization. It would turn out that even with the immensely popular (for a time) Common Lisp, the 370 never figured as much of a player in the success of Lisp. No one from the natural acceptance group for a Lisp considered the 370 a proper machine with a usable environment, even though Lisp370 certainly provided a nice development environment.

这引出一个有趣的问题。Intellicorp、Inference 和 Teknowledge 等人工智能公司，都把财富500强企业作为目标，尤其是那些以370作为主力机的企业。每家公司都预测，只要能把产品放到370上，自己的命运就会好转。然而，除 Aion 外，这对人工智能公司从未产生多大影响。Aion 提供的产品规模较小，也稍微更直接地针对客户需求。

This brings up an interesting point. The AI companies like Intellicorp, Inference, and Teknowledge all targeted the Fortune 500 companies, particularly those that used 370’s as their workhorses. Each AI company predicted that its own fortunes would rise if they could only get their products on the 370. Yet, except for Aion, this never made much difference to the AI companies. Aion provided a more modest set of products, aimed slightly more directly at the needs of the customers.

其他多数公司构建的是大型专家系统外壳，随后可以通过编程来解决特定问题。这些解决方案通常需要人工智能公司协助创建。

Most of the other companies built large expert system shells, which could then be programmed to solve particular problems. These solutions usually required the aid of the AI companies to create.

有人可能认为，这些公司提供的、基于 Lisp 的人工智能解决方案之所以没有产生重大影响，是因为它们用 Lisp 编写。但如果解决方案足够好，底层语言就不会有区别。也许从人工智能的角度看，人工智能本身并没有这些公司所相信的那种声望，因此这些解决方案未获接受。

One could argue that the Lisp-based AI solutions produced by the AI companies failed to have a major impact because the solutions were written in Lisp. But, had the solutions been adequate, the underlying language would have made no difference. Perhaps from the perspective of AI, AI itself did not have the cachet that the AI companies believed it should have, and so these sorts of solutions were not accepted.

或者，声望也许确实存在，只是无关紧要。声望意味着走在前沿，而某些组织对前沿并不感兴趣，它们关心的是稳妥。这些公司购买 IBM PC，也购买 IBM 370。Macintosh 的声望不会影响它们，人工智能的声望也一样。

Or, perhaps, the cachet was there, but is irrelevant. Cachet is leading edge, and some organizations are not interested in leading edge, but in the safe course. These companies buy IBM PC’s, and IBM 370’s as well. The cachet of the Macintosh does not affect them, and neither does the cachet of AI.

请记住：声望是一种关系，存在于拥有它的事物与渴望它的人之间。

Remember, cachet is a relationship between a thing that has it, and a person who wants it.

2.8 Scheme：1975—1980年

2.8 Scheme: 1975–1980

名为 Scheme 的 Lisp 方言，最初源于 Gerald Jay Sussman 和 Guy Steele 在1975年秋的一次尝试：他们想弄清 Carl Hewitt 把参与者理论作为计算模型时的若干问题。Hewitt 的模型是面向对象的，并受到 Smalltalk 的影响；每个对象都是能够接收消息并作出响应的、具有计算活动能力的实体。这些对象称为参与者，消息本身也是参与者。一个参与者可以有任意多个“熟人”，也就是说，用 Hewitt 的说法，它可以“知道”其他参与者，向它们发送消息，或者把熟人作为消息或消息的一部分发送出去。消息传递是唯一的交互手段。函数式交互通过续延来建模：例如，可以向名为 factorial 的参与者发送数字5，以及另一个参与者，要求把最终算出的值（想必是120）发送给后者。

The dialect of Lisp known as Scheme was originally an attempt by Gerald Jay Sussman and Guy Steele during Autumn 1975 to explicate for themselves some aspects of Carl Hewitt’s theory of actors as a model of computation. Hewitt’s model was object-oriented (and influenced by Smalltalk); every object was a computationally active entity capable of receiving and reacting to messages. The objects were called actors, and the messages themselves were also actors.
