An actor could have arbitrarily many acquaintances; that is, it could “know about” (in Hewitt’s language) other actors and send them messages or send acquaintances as (parts of) messages. Message-passing was the only means of interaction. Functional interactions were modeled with the use of continuations; one might send the actor named “factorial” the number 5 and another actor to which to send the eventually computed value (presumably 120).

Sussman 和 Steele 从 Hewitt 的论文和语言设计中理解这一模型的某些推论时遇到了困难，于是决定构造一个参与者语言的玩具实现来做实验。他们以 MacLisp 为工作环境，先构造一个微型 Lisp 解释器，再加入创建参与者和发送消息所需的机制。这个玩具 Lisp 将提供实现基本参与者内部行为所需的原语。

Sussman and Steele had some trouble understanding some of the consequences of the model from Hewitt’s papers and language design, so they decided to construct a toy implementation of an actor language in order to experiment with it. Using MacLisp as a working environment, they decided to construct a tiny Lisp interpreter and then add the necessary mechanisms for creating actors and sending messages. The toy Lisp would provide the necessary primitives for implementing the internal behavior of primitive actors.

由于 Sussman 刚刚研究过 Algol [Naur, 1963]，他建议从一种采用词法作用域的 Lisp 方言入手。看来，无论如何都需要这样的机制来跟踪参与者的熟人。这使参与者和函数几乎可以用相同的机制创建。求值一个以 lambda 开头的形式，会捕获当前的变量查找环境并创建闭包；求值一个以 alpha 开头的形式，同样会捕获当前环境，但创建的是参与者。消息传递在语法上可以与函数调用采用同样的写法。解释器中传统上称为 apply 的部分将区分参与者和函数。函数会返回一个值，而参与者永不返回；相反，它通常会调用一个续延，即它所知道的另一个参与者。因此，可以定义如下函数：

Because Sussman had just been studying Algol [Naur, 1963], he suggested starting with a lexically scoped dialect of Lisp. It appeared that such a mechanism would be needed anyway for keeping track of acquaintances for actors. This allowed actors and functions to be created by almost identical mechanisms. Evaluating a form beginning with the word lambda would capture the current variable-lookup environment and create a closure; evaluating a form with the word alpha would also capture the current environment but create an actor. Message passing could be expressed syntactically in the same way as function invocation. The difference between an actor and a function would be detected in the part of the interpreter traditionally known as apply. A function would return a value, but an actor would never return; instead, it would typically invoke a continuation, another actor that it knows about. Thus one might define the function

(define factorial
  (lambda (n)
    (if (= n 0) 1 (* n (factorial (- n 1))))))

或者定义与之等价的参与者：

or the equivalent actor

(define actorial
  (alpha (n c)
    (if (= n 0)
        (c 1)
        (actorial (- n 1)
                  (alpha (f) (c (* f n)))))))

注意，在这个例子中，调用外层 alpha 表达式的消息所传入的 c 和 n 的值，成为内层 alpha 表达式创建的续延参与者的熟人。必须显式传递这个续延，因为不指望对 actorial 的递归调用返回一个值。

Note in this example that the values of c and n, passed in the message that invokes the outer alpha expression, become acquaintances of the continuation actor created by the inner alpha expression. This continuation must be passed explicitly because the recursive invocation of actorial is not expected to return a value.

Sussman 和 Steele 对这个玩具参与者实现非常满意，把它命名为 Schemer，希望它能沿着 Planner 和 Conniver 的传统发展成另一种人工智能语言。然而，ITS 操作系统把文件名限制为6个字符，因此名字被截短为 Scheme，此后就沿用了下来。

Sussman and Steele were very pleased with this toy actor implementation and named it “Schemer” in the expectation that it might develop into another AI language in the tradition of Planner and Conniver. However, the ITS operating system had a 6-character limitation on file names and so the name was truncated to simply “Scheme” and that name stuck.

随后出现了一个关键发现。在我们看来，它说明了语言设计中实验的价值。Sussman 和 Steele 在使 apply 正确运行后检查其代码，惊讶地发现：apply 中处理函数应用和参与者调用的代码完全相同！进一步检查解释器的其他部分，例如创建函数和参与者的代码，也证实了这一认识：函数被设计为返回值，而参与者被设计为不返回，这种意图上的差别在实现中任何地方都没有造成区别。区别完全在于编写它们的主体时所使用的原语。如果底层原语返回值，用户就能编写返回值的函数；如果所有原语都要求续延，用户就能编写参与者。但 lambda 和 alpha 机制本身完全相同，因此 Sussman 和 Steele 得出结论：参与者与闭包是同一个概念。Hewitt 后来同意这一判断，不过指出，他的理论中有两种基本参与者，即具有可修改状态的单元和强制互斥访问的同步器；如果不加入等价的基本扩展，就无法在采用词法作用域的纯 Lisp 中把它们表示为闭包。

Then came a crucial discovery—one that, to us, illustrates the value of experimentation in language design. On inspecting the code for apply, once they got it working correctly, Sussman and Steele were astonished to discover that the codes in apply for function application and for actor invocation were identical! Further inspection of other parts of the interpreter, such as the code for creating functions and actors, confirmed this insight: the fact that functions were intended to return values and actors were not made no difference anywhere in their implementation. The difference lay purely in the primitives used to code their bodies. If the underlying primitives return values, then the user can write functions that return values; if all primitives expect continuations, then the user can write actors. But the lambda and alpha mechanisms were themselves identical, and from this Sussman and Steele concluded that actors and closures were the same concept. Hewitt later agreed with this assessment, noting, however, that two types of primitive actor in his theory, namely cells (which have modifiable state) and synchronizers (which enforce exclusive access), cannot be expressed as closures in a lexically scoped pure Lisp without adding equivalent primitive extensions.

这一发现并没有使 Sussman 和 Steele 降低对参与者模型或 Lisp 的评价；相反，他们认为 Scheme 仍很可能成为下一种人工智能语言，在一个简单得多的框架中容纳当时关于数据结构和控制结构的许多想法。最初的 Scheme 报告 [Sussman, 1975b] 描述了一种极为精简的语言，基本构造少到不能再少，每个概念对应一个构造。（一个够用，何必用两个？）其中有函数构造器 lambda、不动点运算符 labels、条件构造 if、副作用构造 aset、续延访问器 catch、函数应用、变量引用，此外几乎没有别的东西。它也提供符号、列表和数字等若干基本数据结构，但这些结构及其操作被视为实际使用上的便利，而非理论上的必需。

Sussman and Steele did not think any less of the actors model—or of Lisp—for having made this discovery; indeed, it seemed to them that Scheme still might well be the next AI language, capturing many of the ideas then floating around about data and control structure but in a much simpler framework. The initial report on Scheme [Sussman, 1975b] describes a very spare language, with a minimum of primitive constructs, one per concept. (Why take two when one will do?) There was a function constructor lambda, a fixpoint operator labels, a condition if, a side effect aset, a continuation accessor catch, function application, variable references, and not too much else. There was an assortment of primitive data structures such as symbols, lists, and numbers, but these and their associated operations were regarded as practical conveniences rather than theoretical requirements.

1976年，Sussman 和 Steele 又写了两篇以 Scheme 为框架探索程序设计语言语义的论文。《Lambda：终极命令式》[Steele, 1976b] 展示了如何在 Scheme 中为各种各样的控制结构思想建模。其中一些模型借鉴了 Peter Landin、John Reynolds 等人的早期工作 [Landin, 1965; Reynolds, 1972; Friedman, November]。这篇论文一方面带有教程性质，另一方面也是控制结构的汇编目录。（它还是后来一长串“Lambda：终极 X”论文中的第一篇；这一连续笑话在 Lisp 社区中的知名度，不亚于“X 被认为有害”这类标题在更广泛的程序设计语言社区中的知名度。）《Lambda：终极声明式》[Steele, 1976a] 集中讨论 lambda 作为重命名构造的本质，也更广泛地比较了 Scheme 与 Hewitt 的 PLASMA（见第4节），把一般的面向对象编程以及具体的参与者模型与闭包联系起来。这又启发了一组构造实用 Scheme 编译器的技术，论文对此作了相当详细的概述。这篇论文是一份学位论文计划书；最终的学位论文讨论了名为 RABBIT 的 Scheme 编译器 [Steele, 1978a]。大约同一时期，印第安纳大学的 Mitchell Wand 和 Daniel Friedman 也在从事类似工作 [Wand, 1977]，并在此期间与 Sussman 和 Steele 交换论文。

In 1976 Sussman and Steele wrote two more papers that explored programming language semantics using Scheme as a framework. Lambda: The Ultimate Imperative [Steele, 1976b] demonstrated how a wide variety of control structure ideas could be modeled in Scheme. Some of the models drew on earlier work by Peter Landin, John Reynolds, and others [Landin, 1965; Reynolds, 1972; Friedman, November] The paper was partly tutorial in intent and partly a consolidated catalog of control structures. (This paper was also notable as the first in a long series of “Lambda: The Ultimate X ” papers, a running gag that is as well-known in the Lisp community as the “X Considered Harmful” titles are in the broader programming-languages community.) Lambda: The Ultimate Declarative [Steele, 1976a] concentrated on the nature of lambda as a renaming construct; it also provided a more extensive comparison of Scheme and Hewitt’s PLASMA (see section 4), relating object-oriented programming generally and actors specifically to closures. This in turn suggested a set of techniques for constructing a practical compiler for Scheme, which this paper outlined in some detail. This paper was a thesis proposal; the resulting dissertation discussed a Scheme compiler called RABBIT [Steele, 1978a]. Mitchell Wand and Daniel Friedman were doing similar work at Indiana University at about this time [Wand, 1977], and they exchanged papers with Sussman and Steele during this period.

Steele 还在 ACM 全国会议上发表论文，讨论 lambda 演算对尾递归和过程调用优化的意义 [Steele, 1977d]。事后看来，这篇论文对 Lisp 社区以外语言实现的总体影响似乎完全可以忽略。不过，一位听众对 Steele 说，他很喜欢投影片示例中选用的过程名。那一刻，Steele 突然意识到，并不是全世界都把 FOO 和 BAR 当作标准的临时示例名称。

Steele also presented a paper for the ACM National Conference that discussed the implications of lambda calculus for tail-recursion and optimization of procedure calls [Steele, 1977d]. Its net effect on the implementations of languages outside the Lisp community appears in hindsight to have been completely negligible. However, one listener remarked to Steele how much he had enjoyed the choice of procedure names in the examples shown on the overhead transparencies. At that moment Steele was struck by the realization that not all the world uses FOO and BAR as standard nonce terminology.

随后，Steele 和 Sussman 撰写了 Scheme 的修订报告 [Steele, 1978c]；报告标题原本是向 Algol 致敬，却又引发了另一串越来越滑稽的标题 [Clinger, 1985a; Clinger, 1985b; Rees, 1986; ?]。不久之后，他们又写了一部长篇专论，标题借用了《赋格的艺术》的双关，展示许多带有不同变化的小型 Lisp 解释器。这部专论主要是在周六于 Sussman 家中的书房里写成的。Steele 早晨骑自行车过去，躲避波士顿的车流；然后两人泡上一大壶茶，整个下午交替进行争论和通过拨号线路连接 MIT 打字。这部专论最终没有写完，只发表了第零、第一和第二部分 [Steele, 1978b]。第零部分介绍一种以递归方程为模型的微型一阶 Lisp 方言。第一部分讨论作为数据的过程，探索词法绑定和动态绑定。第二部分研究状态的分解及副作用的含义。第三部分原计划讨论求值顺序，即传值调用与传名调用；第四部分原计划讨论元语言、宏处理器和编译器。最后两部分没有写成并不算重大损失，因为其他研究者很快就充分讨论了这些主题。虽然《解释器的艺术》在 Scheme 的小圈子里颇有名气，却被一家 ACM 期刊拒稿。

Subsequently Steele and Sussman wrote a revised report on Scheme [Steele, 1978c]; the title of the report was intended as a tribute to Algol but in turn inspired another increasingly silly series of titles [Clinger, 1985a; Clinger, 1985b; Rees, 1986; ?]. Shortly thereafter they wrote an extended monograph, whose title was a play on The Art of the Fugue, illustrating numerous small Lisp interpreters with variations. This was written mostly in Sussman’s study at home on Saturdays. Steele would bicycle over in the morning, dodging the Boston traffic; then they would make a huge pot of tea and spend the afternoon alternately debating and and then typing on a dial-up line to MIT. The monograph was never finished; only parts Zero, One, and Two were published [Steele, 1978b]. Part Zero introduced a tiny first-order dialect of Lisp modeled on recursion equations. Part One discussed procedures as data and explored lexical and dynamic binding. Part Two addressed the decomposition of state and the meaning of side effects. Part Three was to have covered order of evaluation (call-by-value versus call-by-name), and Part Four was intended to cover metalanguage, macro processors, and compilers. That these last two parts were never written is no great loss, since these topics were soon treated adequately by other researchers. While The Art of the Interpreter achieved some notoriety in the Scheme underground, it was rejected by an ACM journal.

这些论文中的许多内容并非全新；它们的主要贡献，是在理论研究者所用的模型（参与者和 lambda 演算）与实践者所用的模型（Lisp 实现者和用户）之间架起桥梁。Scheme 使指称语义等领域的理论成果更容易被 Lisp 黑客理解，也为理论研究者提供了一个可用的实验操作平台。无需一个集中式实现团队，去支持许多地点或各种机器上的 Scheme。与 Standard Lisp 一样，甚至比它更小、更简单，Scheme 可以在极短时间内建立在另一个 Lisp 系统之上。许多其他地点都出现了本地实现和方言，一个很好的例子是印第安纳大学的 Scheme 311 [Fessenden, 1983; Clinger, 1984]；直到数年之后，才有人认真尝试制作可移植的独立 Scheme 系统。[?]

A great deal in all these papers was not new; their main contribution was to bridge the gap between the models used by theoreticians (studying actors and the lambda calculus) and practicians (Lisp implementors and users). Scheme made theoretical contributions in such areas as denotational semantics much more accessible to Lisp hackers; it also provided a usable operational platform for experimentation by theoreticians. There was no need for a centralized implementation group to support Scheme at a large number of sites or on a wide variety of machines. Like Standard Lisp but even smaller and simpler, Scheme could be put up on top of some other Lisp system in a very short time. Local implementations and dialects sprang up at many other sites (one good example is Scheme 311 at Indiana University [Fessenden, 1983; Clinger, 1984]); it was several years before anyone made a serious attempt to produce a portable stand-alone Scheme system. [?]

Jonathan Rees、Norman Adams 等人先在耶鲁、后在 MIT 对 Scheme 实现开展了大量工作，产生了名为 T 的 Scheme 方言。这个名字在各方面都是个好笑话，因为 T 之于 Scheme，大致相当于 NIL 方言之于 MacLisp。其目标是提供一种简单而实现特别高效的方言 [Rees, 1982]：

Extensive work on Scheme implementations was carried on at Yale and later at MIT by Jonathan Rees, Norman Adams, and others. This resulted in the dialect of Scheme known as T; this name was a good joke all around, since T was to Scheme approximately what the NIL dialect was to MacLisp. The goal was to be a simple dialect with an especially efficient implementation [Rees, 1982]:

T 围绕一种小型核心语言构建，没有复杂特性，因此容易学习……我们没有支持那些自己还不能完全认同的特性。T 所省略的东西很重要：我们避开了 Common Lisp 的复杂参数列表语法、关键字选项，以及把多种功能重载到同一个函数上的做法。以后再把某样东西推广，远比现在实现一种日后可能后悔的东西容易。所有特性都经过了关于风格纯粹性和通用性的仔细考虑。

T centers around a small core language, free of complicated features, thus easy to learn. . . . we have refrained from supporting features that we didn’t feel completely right about. T’s omissions are important: we have avoided the complicated argument list syntax of Common Lisp, keyword options, and multiple functionality overloaded on single functions. It’s far easier to generalize on something later than to implement something now that one might later regret. All features have been carefully considered for stylistic purity and generality.

因此，T 的设计有意识地背离了 Lisp 传统；对于 Steele 和 Sussman 依赖传统而没有加以清理的地方，它也背离了早期 Scheme。内置函数的命名被统一起来。T 把传统的 -P 后缀全面替换为问号，因此 numberp 变成 number?，null 变成 null?。同样，每个破坏性操作的名字都以感叹号结尾，因此 MacLisp 中 append 的破坏性版本 nconc 变成了 append!。（Muddle [Galley, 1975] 已经引入用问号表示谓词的做法，Sussman 多年来也在部分文章中采用这一约定。Interlisp 对破坏性操作的标记比 MacLisp 更一致，使用字母 d 作为前缀。）

The design of T therefore represented a conscious break not only from the Lisp tradition but from earlier versions of Scheme in places where Steele and Sussman had relied on tradition rather than cleaning things up. Names of built-in functions were regularized. T replaced the traditional -P suffix with universal use of the question mark; thus numberp became number? and null became null?. Similarly, every destructive operation had a name ending with an exclamation point; thus nconc, the MacLisp name for the destructive version of append, became append!. (Muddle [Galley, 1975] had introduced the use of question mark to indicate predicates and Sussman had used this convention over the years in some of his writing. Interlisp had had a more consistent system of labeling destructive operations than MacLisp, using the letter d as a prefix.)

T 最初面向 VAX（同时支持 Unix 和 VMS）以及 Apollo 工作站。系统的大部分用 T 编写，并通过 T 编译器 TC 自举。尤其是求值器和垃圾回收器，都用 T 而非机器语言编写。T 项目从 S-1 Lisp 编译器的一个版本 [Brooks, 1982b] 出发，进行了大量改进；在此过程中，他们发现了 S-1 编译器和原始 RABBIT 报告 [Steele, 1978a] 中的若干错误。与 S-1 Lisp 编译器一样，它大量依赖主流编译器文献中的优化策略，尤其是 Wulf 等人关于 BLISS-11 编译器的工作 [Wulf, 1975]。

T was initially targeted to the VAX (under both Unix and VMS) and to Apollo workstations. Most of the system was written in T and bootstrapped by the T compiler, TC. The evaluator and garbage collector, in particular, were written in T and not in machine language. The T project started with a version of the S-1 Lisp compiler [Brooks, 1982b] and made substantial improvements; in the course of their work they identified several bugs in the S-1 compiler and in the original report on RABBIT [Steele, 1978a]. Like the S-1 Lisp compiler, it relied heavily on optimization strategies from the mainstream compiler literature, most notably the work by Wulf and others on the BLISS- 11 compiler [Wulf, 1975].

第二代 T 编译器 ORBIT [Kranz, 1986] 集成了许多主流优化策略和 Lisp 专用优化策略，形成了真正达到生产质量的 Scheme 环境。RABBIT 的组织原则，是通过源到源转换把 Lisp 代码转换成“续延传递风格”（CPS）；ORBIT 推广并扩展了这一策略，使之能够处理变量赋值。其寄存器分配器采用踪迹调度，优化跨越分支、汇合和过程调用的寄存器使用。ORBIT 还支持调用用 Lisp 以外语言编写的过程。（这与 CMU 等地为 Common Lisp 开发通用“外部函数调用”机制的工作处于同一时期。）

A second generation of T compiler, called ORBIT [Kranz, 1986], integrated a host of mainstream and Lisp-specific optimization strategies, resulting in a truly production-quality Scheme environment. RABBIT was organized around a principle of translating Lisp code by performing a source-to-source conversion into “continuation-passing style” (CPS); ORBIT generalized and extended this strategy to handle assignments to variables. The register allocator used trace scheduling to optimize register usage across forks, joins, and procedure calls. ORBIT also supported calls to procedures written in languages other than Lisp. (This was contemporaneous with efforts at CMU and elsewhere to develop general “foreign function call” mechanisms for Common Lisp.)

2.9 Common Lisp 的前奏：1980—1984年

2.9 Prelude to Common Lisp: 1980–1984

1981年春，情况如下。MIT Lisp 机器项目衍生出两家 Lisp 机器公司：Lisp Machines Inc.（LMI）和 Symbolics, Inc.。前者主要由 Richard Greenblatt 创立，后者由包括 David A. Moon 在内的一个更大团队创立。两家公司最初都把第二代 MIT Lisp 机器 CADR 产品化，并从 MIT 获得 Lisp 机器软件许可；协议要求把公司所作的任何改进反馈给 MIT。Symbolics 很快开始设计和制造后继 Lisp 机器，命名为3600。自1978年首次公布定义以来，Lisp-Machine Lisp 已有很大发展，获得了许多新特性，其中最显著的是名为 Flavors 的面向对象扩展。

In the Spring of 1981, the situation was as follows. Two Lisp machine companies had sprung up from the MIT Lisp machine project: Lisp Machines Inc. (LMI) and Symbolics, Inc. The former was founded principally by Richard Greenblatt and the latter by a larger group including David A. Moon. Both initially productized the CADR, the second MIT Lisp machine, and each licensed the Lisp machine software from MIT under an arrangement that included passing back any improvements made by the companies. Symbolics soon embarked on designing and building a follow-on Lisp machine, which it would call the 3600. The language Lisp-Machine Lisp had evolved greatly since the first definition published in 1978, acquiring a variety of new features, most notably an object-oriented extension called Flavors.

Xerox 的 Lisp 机器 Dolphin 和 Dorado 运行 Interlisp，主要在美国西海岸的研究实验室使用。BBN 正在制造名为 Jericho 的 Interlisp 机器，同时也在把 Interlisp 移植到 VAX。

The Xerox Lisp machines Dolphin and Dorado were running Interlisp and were in use in research Labs mostly located on the West Coast. BBN was constructing its Interlisp machine called the Jericho, and a port of Interlisp to the Vax was under way.

MIT 启动了一个项目，在 VAX 和 S1 计算机上定义并实现 MacLisp 的后继语言 NIL。

At MIT a project had started to define and implement a descendant of MacLisp called NIL on the Vax and S1 computers.

在 CMU，Scott Fahlman 及其同事和学生正在定义并实现一种类似 MacLisp 的 Lisp 方言，名为 Spice Lisp，计划在 Spice 机器上实现；Spice 是“科学个人集成计算环境”的缩写。

At CMU, Scott Fahlman and his colleagues and students were defining and implementing a MacLisp-like dialect of Lisp called Spice Lisp, which was to be implemented on the Spice machine— Scientific Personal Integrated Computing Environment.

2.10 早期 Common Lisp

2.10 Early Common Lisp

如果此时 Lisp 社区没有进行整合，Lisp 可能已经消亡。ARPA 不愿资助一批无谓竞争、刻意相异的 Lisp 项目，而商业舞台当时尚未出现。

If there were no consolidation in the Lisp community at this point, Lisp might have died. ARPA was not interested in funding a variety of needlessly competing and gratuitously different Lisp projects. And there was no commercial arena—yet.

2.10.1 走出 MacLisp 的混乱

2.10.1 Out of the Chaos of MacLisp

1981年4月，ARPA 召集了一次“Lisp 社区会议”，各实现团队聚在一起讨论 Lisp 的未来。ARPA 资助了大量人工智能研究，其目标是看看如何遏制其研究社区中 Lisp 方言日益分化的趋势。

In April 1981, ARPA called a “Lisp Community Meeting”, in which the implementation groups got together to discuss the future of Lisp. ARPA sponsored a lot of AI research, and their goal was to see what could be done to stem the tide of an increasingly diverse set of Lisp dialects in its research community.

ARPA 会议前一天，Interlisp 社区的一部分成员先行聚会，讨论如何展示一个在多种机器上蓬勃发展的 Interlisp 社区。他们打算强调：一种标准语言 Interlisp 及一种标准环境，正在越来越多种计算机上运行。

The day before the ARPA meeting, part of the Interlisp community got together to discuss how to present a situation of a healthy Interlisp community on a variety of machines. The idea was to push the view of a standard language (Interlisp) and a standard environment existing on an ever-increasing number of different types of computers.

会议当天，Interlisp 社区成功地把自己展示为一个目标和使命一致的团体。

The day of the meeting, the Interlisp community successfully presented themselves as a coherent group with one goal and mission.

MacLisp 衍生团队的表现，则最好用一则轶事说明。各团队依次起身介绍自己的发展方向和理由。有人提出，与 Interlisp 社区相比，MacLisp 社区的方向似乎不够明确。Scott Fahlman 说：“MacLisp 社区并不处于混乱状态。它由四个定义明确的团队组成，朝着四个定义明确的方向前进。”大家停顿片刻，等笑声平息 [Steele, 1982]。

The MacLisp-descended groups came offin a way that can be best demonstrated with an anecdote. Each group stood up and presented where they were heading and why. Some questions arose about the ill-defined direction of the MacLisp community in contrast to the Interlisp community. Scott Fahlman said, “the MacLisp community is not in a state of chaos. It consists of four welldefined groups going in four well-defined directions.” There was a moment’s pause for the laughter to subside [Steele, 1982].

Gabriel 参加了 ARPA 会议前一天 Interlisp 的碰头会，也目睹了 MacLisp 社区在正式会议上的场面。他不认为 MacLisp 各团队之间的分歧不可克服，于是开始推销团队间某种合作的想法。

Gabriel attended the Interlisp pow-wow the day before the ARPA meeting, and he also witnessed the spectacle of the MacLisp community at the meeting. He didn’t believe that the differences between the MacLisp groups were insurmountable, so he began to try to sell the idea of some sort of cooperation among the groups.

他首先找了 Jon L White。他们讨论的想法有两层：一是尝试统一 MacLisp 社区对未来 Lisp 的设想，二是通过合同为 ARPA 完成此事。当时 Gabriel 参与的 S1 Lisp 项目正在实现 NIL，因此他与 White 自然有共同立场。他们与 SRI 人工智能实验室负责人 Nils Nilsson 讨论了这一想法，并同意争取更多支持。

First he approached Jon L White. The idea they discussed was two-fold: to try to unify the MacLisp community’s Lisp aspirations, and to do so for ARPA under contract. At this time Gabriel was in the S1 Lisp project, which was implementing NIL, and thus there was a natural affinity with White. They discussed the idea with Nils Nilsson who was head of the AI Lab at SRI, and they agreed to try to pull together more support for the idea.

接着，Gabriel 和 White 找到当时在 CMU、参与 SPICE Lisp 项目的 Guy Steele。他们三人都以某种方式与 S-1 NIL 项目有关，并计划在 ARPA 会议次日前往劳伦斯利弗莫尔国家实验室。在前往实验室那条炎热、尘土飞扬而漫长的道路上，他们坐在 Gabriel 没有空调的大众甲壳虫里，摇下车窗，讨论合作事宜，并同意去找 Fahlman。

Second, Gabriel and White approached Guy Steele, then at CMU and affiliated with the SPICE Lisp project. The three of them—Steele, White, and Gabriel—were all associated one way or another with the S-1 NIL project, and they were on their way to Lawrence Livermore National Laboratory the day after the ARPA meeting. They discussed the collaboration in Gabriel’s un-airconditioned VW bug while on the hot, dusty, long road to LLNL, with the windows down. They agreed to approach Fahlman.

几个月后，Gabriel、Steele、White、Fahlman、William Scherlis（Gabriel 的同事，当时在 CMU）和 Rod Brooks（S1 Lisp 项目成员）在 CMU 会面，讨论新 Lisp 的一些技术细节。特别是，新方言应具有以下基本特性：

A few months later, Gabriel, Steele, White, Fahlman, William Scherlis (a colleague of Gabriel’s then at CMU), and Rod Brooks (part of the S1 Lisp project) met at CMU, and some of the technical details of the new Lisp were discussed. In particular, the new dialect was to have the following basic features:

• 词法作用域，包括完整闭包。

• Lexical scoping, including full closures

• 多值，形式类似 Lisp-Machine Lisp，但对于强制单值的情形可能作一些修改。

• Multiple values, of a form like Lisp-Machine Lisp, but perhaps with some modifications for single-value-forcing situations

• 采用 Lisp-2，即值单元与函数单元分离 [Gabriel, 1988]（见第??节）。

• A Lisp-2, in the sense of separate value and function cells [Gabriel, 1988] (see section ??)

• DEFSTRUCT。

• Defstruct

• SETF。

• SETF

• 丰富的浮点数支持，包括复数和有理数（这是 S-1 Lisp 的主要影响）。

• Fancy floating point numbers, including complex and rational numbers (this was the primary influence of S-1 Lisp)

• 类似 Lisp-Machine Lisp 的复杂 lambda 列表声明。

• Complex lambda-list declarations, similar to Lisp-Machine Lisp

• 不提供动态闭包（在 NIL 中称为 flexures）。

• No dynamic closures (called “flexures” in NIL)

经过一天半的技术讨论，大家前往离 CMU 不远、供应油腻潜艇三明治的 Oakland Original 餐馆。午餐期间和饭后，他们讨论起新 Lisp 的名字。NIL、SPICE Lisp 等显而易见的名字被提出又否决，因为会过分抬高某一团队而忽视其他团队；Yu-Hsiang Lisp（鱼香 Lisp）等不那么显然的名字也被提出，并被不情愿地否决。

After a day and a half of technical discussion, this group went offto the Oakland Original, a greasy submarine sandwich place not far from CMU. During and after lunch the topic of the name for the Lisp came up, and such obvious names as NIL and SPICE Lisp were proposed and rejected—as giving too much credit to one group and not enough to others—and such non-obvious names as Yu-Hsiang Lisp were also proposed and reluctantly rejected.

大家觉得最好的名字是 Standard Lisp，但已有另一种方言使用这个名字。在寻找近义词时，有人提出 Common Lisp。Gabriel 说这不是个好名字，因为他们想定义的是一种“精英 Lisp”，而 Common Lisp 听起来太像“普通人的 Lisp”。

The name felt to be best was “Standard Lisp,” but another dialect was known by that name already. In searching for similar words, the name “Common Lisp” came up. Gabriel remarked that this wasn’t a good name because we were trying to define an Elitist Lisp, and “Common Lisp” sounded too much like “Common Man Lisp.”

晚餐时，大家在匹兹堡另一个街区的意大利餐馆 Pleasure Bar 继续讨论命名，但仍一无所获。

The naming discussion resumed at dinner at the Pleasure Bar, an Italian restaurant in another Pittsburgh district, but no luck was had by all.

后来，Moon 在电子邮件中提到“不管我们把这个共同的 Lisp 叫什么”，这一次，大家虽然为找不到更好的名字而深感遗憾和不安，还是选定了 Common Lisp。

Later in E-mail, Moon referred to “whatever we call this common Lisp,” and this time, amongst great sadness and consternation that a better name could not be had, it was selected.

下一步是联系更多团队。关键是 Lisp 机器公司，计划最后再接触。此外，Gabriel 自告奋勇去拜访伯克利的 Franz Lisp 团队和盐湖城的 PSL 团队。

The next step was to contact more groups. The key was the Lisp machine companies, which would be approached last. In addition, Gabriel volunteered to visit the Franz Lisp group in Berkeley and the PSL group in Salt Lake City.

Gabriel 与 PSL 团队的会面友好而富有信息。该团队同意参加讨论，也可能考虑采用结果，但显然认为 Common Lisp 的工作很难成功；由于 PSL 已在如此多的机器上运行，他们认为它作为下一代 Lisp 已明显领先。不过，PSL 团队的 Don Morrison 后来成为这一过程的积极参与者。Martin Griss 另作如下评论（1982年1月21日给 Guy Steele 的私人通信，附有 PSL 手册草稿，因此很可能是带个性化称呼的通函）：

Gabriel’s meeting with the PSL group was cordial and informative, and the group agreed to participate in the discussions and to possibly consider the result, but it was clear they felt that the Common Lisp effort would be difficult to pull off, and that PSL had a clear head start as the next Lisp, given that it ran on so many machines.Nevertheless Don Morrison of the PSL group would become an active participant in the process. Martin Griss separately commented (personal communication to Guy Steele, January 21, 1982, likely a form letter with personalized salutation, as it accompanied a draft of the PSL manual):

我们正在两个极端之间犹豫：一端是让 PSL 仅仅成为 Standard LISP 的简单扩展，另一端是实现一种全新的、更现代的 LISP。与新 Common LISP 方言开发者（来自 LISP Machine、Spice LISP、NIL 和 S-1 社区）的互动，以及可能参与这一工作，或许会使这种平衡有所改变。目前，我们主要依据 LISP Machine 和 Common LISP 手册，以及 Franz LISP 和 UCI LISP，为 PSL 改造或开发模块。

We are torn between keeping PSL to be just a simple extension of Standard LISP, and the other extreme of implementation of an entirely new, more modern LISP. Interaction with the developers of the new Common LISP dialect (from the LISP Machine, Spice LISP, NIL, and S-1 community), and possible participation with this effort may change to balance somewhat. For the moment, we have been adapting or developing modules for PSL primarily based on the LISP Machine and Common LISP manuals, Franz LISP, and UCI LISP.

与 Franz 团队的会面就不那么友好了，显然他们没有很大兴趣，不过团队中有一位成员参加了下文将介绍的投票过程。

The meeting with the Franz group was less cordial, and it was clear that there was no strong interest, though a member of the group joined the balloting process to be described.

Gabriel 一反常态地乐观，去接触 Xerox 的 Interlisp 团队，得到的欢迎比在伯克利更热情，但离开时只获得一个表示：会有人，大概是 Bill VanMelle，主要以观察员身份参加会议。

In a fit of optimism that usually does not characterize him, Gabriel approached the Xerox Interlisp group, and received a warmer welcome than he did in Berkeley, but he left with only an indication that someone, probably Bill VanMelle, would attend the meetings, mostly as an observer.

不知为何，Lisp370 团队始终没有被邀请加入后来所谓的 Common Lisp 小组。由于目标似乎明显是美国国内事务，即 ARPA 社区内部的软件交换，因此完全没有联系欧洲团队；在后来几年，这被视为不可原谅的侮辱。

For no good reason, the Lisp370 group was never invited to join the Common Lisp Group, as it became known. Because the purpose seemed so clearly domestic—the interchange of software within the ARPA community—no European group would be contacted at all, and this would be seen as an unforgivable insult in later years.

Gabriel 花了一些时间向 ARPA 介绍 Common Lisp 的计划，并争取到支持。ARPA 允许把用于 Spice 项目的一部分经费用于 Common Lisp，也允许使用 ARPANet 进行讨论，甚至为原本无法参与的人提供账户和网络访问。

Gabriel spent some time talking to ARPA about the plans for Common Lisp, and enlisted their support. ARPA allowed some of the funds that it was spending on the Spice project to be spent on Common Lisp, and they allowed the ARPANet to be used for the discussions, even providing accounts and net access to people who would not otherwise have been able to participate.

参与这项草根工作的人和团队，大多因为隶属或关联人工智能实验室而接入 ARPANET，因此很自然地决定通过网络电子邮件完成大部分工作，邮件自动归档。事实上，这是第一次几乎完全通过电子邮件开展的大型语言标准化工作。

The people and groups engaged in this grassroots effort were, by and large, on the ARPANET— because they were affiliated or associated with AI labs—so it was natural to decide to do most of the work over the network through electronic mail, which was automatically archived. In fact this was the first major language standardization effort carried out nearly entirely by E-mail.

1981年夏，Brooks 和 Gabriel 正在 CMU 与 Steele 一起从事 S-1 Lisp 工作。由于这一巧合，Common Lisp 的许多会议和大量工作得以进行。

During the summer of 1981, Brooks and Gabriel were working at CMU with Steele on S-1 Lisp. Because of this coincidence, many meetings and a lot of work on Common Lisp could take place.

与 Symbolics 和 LMI 的会面于1981年6月在 Symbolics 举行。Steele 和 Gabriel 从匹兹堡驱车前往剑桥。会议交替进行两类讨论：深入讨论方言应包含哪些内容的技术讨论，以及解释新方言为何有益的政治讨论。从 Lisp 机器公司的角度看，真正的舞台是 Lisp 机器，让同一种方言在更多地方运行似乎只是学术兴趣。当然，让同一种方言在多处运行也有商业理由，只是有商业头脑的人没有参加会议。

The meeting with Symbolics and LMI took place at Symbolics in June 1981. Steele and Gabriel drove from Pittsburgh to Cambridge for the meeting. The meeting alternated between a deep technical discussion of what should be in the dialect and a political discussion about why the new dialect was a good thing. From the point of view of the Lisp machine companies, the action was with Lisp machines, and the interest in the same dialect running more places seemed academic. Of course, there were business reasons for getting the same dialect running in many places, but people with business sense did not attend the meeting.

最后，两家 Lisp 机器公司都决定加入，Common Lisp 小组由此成立：

At the end, both Lisp machine companies decided to join the effort, and the Common Lisp Group was formed:

Alan BawdenRichard P. GabrielWilliam L. ScherlisRodney A. BrooksJoseph GinderRichard M. StallmanRichard L. BryanRichard GreenblattBarbara K. SteeleGlenn S. BurkeMartin L. GrissGuy L. Steele Jr.Howard I. CannonCharles L. HedrickWilliam vanMelleGeorge J. CarretteEarl A. KillianWalter van RoggenDavid DillJohn L. KulpAllan C. WeschlerScott E. FahlmanLarry M. MasinterDaniel L. WeinrebRichard J. FatemanJohn McCarthyJon L WhiteNeal FeinbergDon MorrisonRichard ZippelJohn FoderaroDavid A. MoonLeonard Zubkoff
Alan Bawden Richard P. Gabriel William L. Scherlis Rodney A. Brooks Joseph Ginder Richard M. Stallman Richard L. Bryan Richard Greenblatt Barbara K. Steele Glenn S. Burke Martin L. Griss Guy L. Steele Jr. Howard I. Cannon Charles L. Hedrick William vanMelle George J. Carrette Earl A. Killian Walter van Roggen David Dill John L. Kulp Allan C. Weschler Scott E. Fahlman Larry M. Masinter Daniel L. Weinreb Richard J. Fateman John McCarthy Jon L White Neal Feinberg Don Morrison Richard Zippel John Foderaro David A. Moon Leonard Zubkoff

作为妥协，大家同意值得定义一个语言家族，使按所定义语言编写的任何程序，都能在家族中的任何一种语言上运行。因此，准备定义某种子集，虽然当时还不清楚是否有人会直接实现这个子集。

As a compromise, it was agreed that it was worth defining a family of languages in such a way that any program written in the language defined would run in any language in the family. Thus, a sort of subset was to be defined, though it wasn’t clear anyone would implement the subset directly.

被删去的 Lisp 机器特性包括 Flavors、窗口系统、多重处理（包括多任务）、图形和 locatives。

Some of the Lisp machine features that were dropped were Flavors, window systems, multiprocessing (including multitasking), graphics, and locatives.

那个夏天，Steele 以 Spice Lisp 手册为基础编写 Common Lisp 初始手册。Brooks、Scherlis 和 Gabriel 协助了最初工作。Scherlis 主要以非正式建议的方式，在类型系统方面提供具体帮助。由于 Gabriel 住在 Steele 家中，两人经常讨论问题。

During the summer, Steele worked on an initial Common Lisp manual based on the Spice Lisp manual. His initial work was assisted by Brooks, Scherlis, and Gabriel. Scherlis provided specific assistance with the type system, mostly in the form of informal advice to Steele. Gabriel and Steele regularly discussed issues because Gabriel was living at Steele’s home.

这份草稿因到处都是大洞而被称为“瑞士奶酪版”，其中一部分是一张选票，提出各种备选方案和是非问题。通过基于电子邮件的讨论和表决，完成了第一轮投票。随后在1981年11月召开面对面会议，最终解决所提出的问题。

The draft, called the Swiss Cheese Edition—because it was full of large holes—was partly a ballot in which various alternatives and yes-no questions were proposed. Through a process of E-mail-based discussion and voting, the first pass of voting took place. This was followed by a face-to-face meeting in November of 1981, where the final decisions on the questions posed were settled.

接着又进行一轮完善，形成了其他几份类似的草稿兼选票。

This led to another round of refinement with several other similar drafts/ballots.

大部分工作通过电子邮件讨论完成，讨论保存在文件档案中。MIT 斯隆学院的一位研究者曾利用这些档案研究基于计算机邮件的决策和互动。

Most of the work was carried out by E-mail discussion. These discussions were captured in file archives. One research at the Sloan School at MIT has used these archives to study computer-mailbased decision-making and interaction.

这种工作方式产生了一些有趣结果。人们在电子邮件中比当面更容易出言不逊、直言不讳。网络上的许多讨论十分激烈，但好处是会自动记录下来，无需依靠模糊的记忆或褪色的笔迹。所有邮件都自动集中保存，因此不会丢失或放错地方。

This style of working had some interesting results. People are more prone to be insulting and forthright in E-mail than in person. Many of the discussions carried on over the network were quite spirited, but they had the advantage of being written down automatically. There was no need to rely on dim memory or pale ink. All the mail was automatically stored in a centralized place, so there was no chance of losing or misplacing it.

讨论通常以提案、讨论、反提案的形式展开，经常交换现有软件的代码示例或新语法建议。想赶上进度或回顾记录的人，都可以迅速查阅全部内容。

The discussions were often in the form of proposals, discussions, and counterproposals. Code examples from existing software or proposed new syntax were often exchanged. And all was subject to quick review by people wishing to come up to speed or to go back to the record.

这种方式也有缺点。最主要的是无法观察别人的反应，看不出某一点是否激怒了对方，而这通常意味着该点对其很重要。也无法看出某个论点是否过头或缺乏支持。这意味着会浪费时间，而且必须精心组织书面论证才能推动工作。

This style also had some drawbacks. Foremost was that it was not possible to observe the reactions of other people, to see whether some point angered them, which would mean the point was important to them. There was no way to see that an argument went too far or had little support. This meant that time was wasted and that carefully crafted written arguments were required to get anything done.

一旦过程启动，处理问题的方式便从单纯整合现有方言这一显然的方向，转变为试图设计出“正确的东西”。一些人认为，这是重新思考某些问题、放弃严格兼容 MacLisp 目标的好时机，而这一兼容目标对早期 Lisp-Machine Lisp 的设计曾十分重要。某些问题，例如 NIL 是否既是符号又是 CONS 单元，并未重新考虑，尽管大家普遍认为应该如此。

Once the process began, the approach to the problem changed from just a consolidation of existing dialects, which was the obvious direction to take, to trying to design the right thing. Some people took the view that this was a good time to rethink some issues and to abandon the goal of strict MacLisp compatibility, which was so important to the early Lisp-Machine Lisp designs. Some issues, like whether NIL is both a symbol and a CONS cell, were not rethought, though it was generally agreed that they should be.

有一个早期提出的问题值得一提，因为它是后来 ISO 的 Lisp 工作期间针对 Common Lisp 的一项主要批评的核心（见第2.12节）。这就是模块化问题，它包含两方面：（1）Common Lisp 是否应划分为核心语言加模块；（2）是否应划分为所谓白页、黄页和红页。这两个话题似乎在讨论中混在了一起。

One issue that came up early on is worth mentioning, because it is at the heart of one of the major attacks on Common Lisp, which was mounted during the ISO work on Lisp (see section 2.12). This is the issue of modularization, which had two aspects: (1) whether Common Lisp should be divided into a core language plus modules and (2) whether there should be a division into the so-called white, yellow, and red pages. These topics appear to have been blended in the discussion.

“白页”指手册正文，凡列入白页的内容，任何声称自己是 Common Lisp 的实现都必须以某种方式实现。“黄页”指可加载的、与实现无关的软件包，例如 TRACE 和科学子程序包。“红页”则打算描述依赖具体实现的例程，例如设备驱动程序。

“White pages” refers to the manual proper, and anything that is in the white pages must be implemented somehow by a Lisp whose developers claim it is a Common Lisp. “Yellow pages” refers to implementation-independent packages that can be loaded in, for example, TRACE and scientific subroutine packages. The “red pages” were intended to describe implementation-dependent routines, such as device drivers.

不过，直接阅读议题本身就会看到第一个问题：把 Common Lisp 划分为核心加模块。

Nevertheless, the first question is brought up by a direct reading of the issue: Division of Common Lisp into a core plus modules.

如果把它理解为一种分层方案，设有中心层以及依赖内层的外层，那么 Common Lisp 就更容易裁剪为子集，从而自然产生面向小型机器的实现。这会满足对廉价、普及型实现的需求，也更容易提供用于教学的语言版本，并且能够避免后来 ISO 会议上欧洲以及程度较轻的日本所发起的强烈攻击。

If this were taken to mean a proposal that would have partitioned the language into layers with a central layer and outer layers that depend on the inner ones, then Common Lisp could have been more easily subsetted, which would have led to obvious implementations on smaller machines. This would have satisfied the need to cheap, prolific implementations. This would also have made providing educational versions of the language more readily available. It also would have prevented the strong attack during the ISO meetings by Europe and by, to a lesser degree, Japan.

有影响力成员的回应很能说明问题：“这似乎很古怪。请说明动机。也许这些模块是否可选应由实现决定？”“保持模块化是个好目标，但别指望完全成功。”“这种划分只有一点点道理。”[?; ?]

The response from influential members is revealing: “This seems weird. Motivate it. Maybe these modules are optional at the implementation’s choice?” “Keeping things modular is a good goal, but don’t expect to succeed completely.” “The division only makes a little sense.” [?; ?]

小组过于关注古怪的白、黄、红之分，而没有关注核心语言与扩展语言之分。如果当时走向不同，Common Lisp 的未来也会不同。

The group focussed too much on the funny white-yellow-red distinction and not on the corelanguage/extended-language distinction. Had this gone differently, so would have the future of Common Lisp.

关于 () 是否是符号的决定被小心地推迟。尽管这一决定一直拖到决策过程接近尾声，已经让人们在情感上接受 Common Lisp，并把一部分自我认同寄托其上，但真正讨论时仍造成了分裂。Symbolics 威胁说，除非接受其立场，否则就退出小组，于是其立场被接受。其邮件中最关键的一段如下：

Carefully deferred was the decision regarding whether () was a symbol. Even though this decision was left until nearly the end of the decision process—causing people to emotionally accept Common Lisp and attach part of their egos to it—when the discussion came up, it was divisive. Symbolics threatened to withdraw from the group unless their position was accepted, and so it was. The salient paragraph from their message is as follows:

我们在内部讨论过 T 和 NIL 的问题。如果我们是在设计一种全新语言，当然会重新考虑这些问题，以及 Lisp 的许多其他瑕疵（或者美人痣）。（不一定会改变，但肯定会重新考虑。）然而，与转换成本相比，现在改变 T 和 NIL 所能带来的好处很小。Symbolics 唯一能够接受的解决办法，就是维持现状。

We have had some internal discussions about the T and NIL issues. If we were designing a completely new language, we would certainly rethink these, as well as the many other warts (or beauty marks) in Lisp. (We might not necessarily change them, but we would certainly rethink them.) However, the advantages to be gained by changing T and NIL now are quite small compared to the costs of conversion. The only resolution to these issues that Symbolics can accept is to retain the status quo.

这说明，有些看似微不足道的问题，会对人们产生深远影响。

This shows that there are some issues, apparently trivial, that can have a profound influence on people.

随后又形成了三份草稿：漏勺版（1982年7月29日）、激光版（1982年11月16日）和欢乐满人间版（???）。这些俏皮名字的含义可由副标题解释：

版本副标题
漏勺版洞比以前更多，不过更小了！
激光版据说应当完全相干一致。
欢乐满人间版几乎在每个方面都完美无缺。

Three more drafts were made—the Colander Edition (July 29, 1982), the Laser Edition (November 16, 1982), and the Mary Poppins Edition (???). The cute names are explained by their subtitles: Colander: Even More Holes Than Before—But They’re Smaller! Laser Edition: Supposed to be Completely Coherent Mary Poppins Edition: Practically Perfect in Every Way

到1983年初，几乎所有决定都已完成，但即使 Digital Press 加快出版，《Common Lisp：语言》仍要将近一年后才能面世。

Virtually all decisions were completed by early 1983, but it was almost a year before the book Common Lisp: The Language would be available, even with a fast publishing job by Digital Press.

在 Common Lisp 设计期间，Guy Steele 从 CMU 休假，加入了 Tartan Laboratories。

During the period of Common Lisp design, Guy Steele took a leave of absence from CMU and joined Tartan Laboratories.

Common Lisp 小组公开声明的目标如下，转述自 CLtL1 [Steele, 1984]：

The declared goals of the Common Lisp Group were as follows, paraphrased from CLtl1 [Steele, 1984]:

• 共同性：Common Lisp 最初是为了集中几个实现团队的工作，这些团队各自在不同计算机上构建 MacLisp 的后继实现。虽然各实现之间的差异仍会造成一些不兼容，Common Lisp 应成为这些实现共同采用的方言。

• Commonality: Common Lisp originated in an attempt to focus the work of several implementation groups, each of which was constructing successor implementations of MacLisp for different computers. While the differences among the several implementations will continue to force some incompatibilities, Common Lisp should serve as a common dialect for these implementations.

• 可移植性：Common Lisp 应排除无法在广泛类别的计算机上轻易实现的特性。这一方面应排除需要微码或特殊硬件的特性，另一方面也应排除通常为通用硬件所需的特性，例如声明。（我们用“通用现成硬件”指商业销售、通用、可直接购得的计算机，与专为支持 Lisp 而设计的计算机相对。）

• Portability: Common Lisp should exclude features that cannot be easily implemented on a broad class of computers. This should serve to exclude features requiring microcode or hardware on one hand as well as features generally required for stock hardware, for example declarations. (We use the term stock hardware to describe commercially available, generalpurpose, “off-the-shelf” computers as opposed to computers specifically designed to support Lisp.)

• 一致性：解释器与编译器应表现出相同的语义。

• Consistency: The interpreter and compiler should exhibit the same semantics.

• 表达力：Common Lisp 应从多种方言中吸收最好的经验，不仅包括 MacLisp，也包括 Interlisp。

• Expressiveness: Common Lisp should cull the best experience from a variety of dialects, including not only MacLisp but Interlisp.

• 兼容性：Common Lisp 应努力依次与 Zetalisp、MacLisp、Interlisp 兼容，优先级按此顺序排列。

• Compatibility: Common Lisp should strive to be compatible with Zetalisp, MacLisp, and Interlisp, in that order.

• 效率：应当能够为 Common Lisp 编写优化编译器。

• Efficiency: It should be possible to write an optimizing compiler for Common Lisp.

• 能力：Common Lisp 应成为优秀的系统构建语言，适合编写类似 Interlisp 的用户级软件包，但本身不提供这些软件包。

• Power: Common Lisp should be a good system-building language, suitable for writing Interlisp-like user-level packages, but it will not provide those packages.

• 稳定性：Common Lisp 应缓慢、审慎地演进。

• Stability: Common Lisp should evolve slowly and with deliberation.

Spice Lisp 对 Common Lisp 工作作出了两项重要贡献。一是 Common Lisp 手册草稿，它源于 Spice Lisp 手册，也大量借鉴 MIT Lisp 机器手册；二是参考实现。CLtL I 出版后的几年里，Spice Lisp 实实在在地成为若干 Common Lisp 实现的源码来源。计算机公司会取得免费源码并移植到自己的机器上，从而提供基础水平的 Common Lisp。

Spice Lisp provided two significant contributions to the Common Lisp effort. One is the draft Common Lisp manual, which was derived from the Spice Lisp manual (and also borrowed heavily from MIT Lisp Machine Manuals) and the other is a reference implementation. For several years after the appearance of CLtL I, Spice Lisp served as the source, literally, for a number of Common Lisp implementations. Computer companies would take the free source code and port it to their computers, providing a base level Common Lisp.

随着时间推移，公司更倾向于从头编写自己的 Common Lisp，或者与商业 Common Lisp 公司签约：前者是为了掌握专业能力，后者是为了获得维护。

With time companies would prefer to either write their own Common Lisps from scratch or contract with a commercial Common Lisp company to gain expertise in the former case and maintenance in the latter.

后文将看到，CLOS 的开发也受益于一个可以自由获取的参考实现。

Later will see that the development of CLOS benefited from the existence of a reference implementation that was freely available.

2.10.2 早期的不满之声

2.10.2 Early Rumblings

Common Lisp 的定义过程并非一帆风顺。始终有些人觉得自己被强行裹挟，或者事情进展不妙。Interlisp 团队参与了投票，但有一次他们写道：

The Common Lisp definition process was not all rosy. Throughout there was a feeling among some that they were being railroaded or that things were not going well. The Interlisp group had input in the balloting process, but at one point they wrote:

Interlisp 社区有些困惑，不知道应该为这项工作作出什么贡献。显然，Common Lisp 在语言特性上不会有多少决定倾向 Interlisp。我们该怎么办？

The Interlisp community is in a bit of a quandary about what our contribution to this endeavor should be. It is clear that Common Lisp is not going to settle very many languages features in Interlisp’s favor. What should we do?

[?; ?]

[?; ?]

问题的一部分在于 Lisp 机器公司的实力，以及 Common Lisp 小组必须把它们留在阵营之内的需要，这赋予了它们特别强大的权力。对此，早期小组的一位成员这样说：

Part of the problem was the strength of the Lisp machine companies and the need for the Common Lisp Group to keep them within the fold, which bestowed on them a particularly strong brand of power. On this point, one of the people in the early Common Lisp Group put it:

抱歉，但当前版本［草稿］确实给人这样的感觉：“好吧，我们最多能把 Lisp-Machine Lisp 的多大一个子集硬塞进所有人的喉咙，再把它叫作标准？”

Sorry, but the current version [draft] really gives a feeling of ‘well, what’s the largest subset of Lisp-Machine Lisp we can try to force down everyone’s throat, and call a standard?’

[?; ?]

[?; ?]

Lisp 机器阵营有一种很难反驳的论证：他们拥有大型软件系统的经验，而在那个领域，他们自己提出的具体解决方案，据他们说，就是正确的方案。最终结果是 Common Lisp 越长越大。

The Lisp machine folks had a flavor of argument that was hard to contend with, namely that they had experience with large software systems, and in that realm the particular solutions they had come up with were, according to them, the right thing. The net effect was that Common Lisp grew and grew.

人们也许会以为，必须为 Common Lisp 编写编译器的通用机器阵营会反对，但其中声音最强的两个人 Steele 和 Gabriel，正对自己能写出强大编译器、化解 Common Lisp 复杂性的能力充满信心。人们经常听见他们，以及后来的 Moon，说“足够聪明的编译器”能够解决某个问题。不久，核心小组就经常引用这个 SSC 论点。（后来在忠实反对派口中，它成了带有轻微嘲讽意味的说法。）

One would think that the voices of the stock machine crowd, who had to write a compiler for Common Lisp, would have objected, but the two strongest voices—Steele and Gabriel—were feeling their oats over their ability to write a powerful compiler to foil the complexities of Common Lisp. One often heard them, and later Moon, remark that a “sufficiently smart compiler” could solve a particular problem. Pretty soon the core group was quoting this “SSC” argument regularly. (Later, in the mouths of the loyal opposition, it became a term of mild derision.)

核心小组最终成为 CLtL I 的“作者”：Guy Steele、Scott Fahlman、David Moon、Daniel Weinreb 和 Richard Gabriel。他们承担了编写语言规范文档并组织审查的责任，自称“五人执政团”，或更非正式地称为“五人帮”。

The core group eventually became the “authors” of CLtL I: Guy Steele, Scott Fahlman, David Moon, Daniel Weinreb, and Richard Gabriel. This group shouldered the responsibility for producing the language specification document and conducting its review. The self-adopted name for the group was the “Quinquevirate” or, more informally, the “Gang of Five”.

2.10.3 对 Common Lisp 的批评

2.10.3 The Critique of Common Lisp

在1984年的 Lisp 与函数式编程研讨会上，Rod Brooks 和 Gabriel 打破阵营一致，发表了令人震惊的开场论文《对 Common Lisp 的批评》[Brooks, 1984]。这尤其令人震惊，因为 Gabriel 和 Brooks 是一家公司的创始人，而该公司的商业计划正是成为首屈一指的 Common Lisp 公司。Fahlman 听完 Gabriel 的演讲后，称其为背叛。

At the 1984 Symposium on Lisp and Functional Programming, Rod Brooks and Gabriel broke rank and delivered the stunning opening paper, “A Critique of Common Lisp” [Brooks, 1984]. This was all the more stunning because Gabriel and Brooks were founders of a company whose business plan was to become the premier Common Lisp company. Fahlman, on hearing the speech delivered by Gabriel, called it traitorous.

这篇论文只是随后一系列 Common Lisp 批评文章中的第一篇，其中许多文章都引用了它。论文的主要观点揭示了一系列问题，这些问题后来在整个十年间一直困扰 Common Lisp。

This paper was only the first in a string of critiques of Common Lisp, and many of those critiques quoted this paper. The high points of the paper reveal a series of problems that proved to plague Common Lisp throughout the decade.

作者认为，以下各点本应成为设计目标：

The authors believed that the following should have been goals of the design:

概念上的简洁：语言应足够小，使普通程序员能把它记在脑中。编译器的简单性：为语言编写一个能生成高效代码的编译器，应当是一项规模适中的工作。

Intellectual conciseness: A language should be small enough for an average programmer to keep in his head. Compiler Simplicity: It should be a modest job to write a compiler that produces efficient code for the language.

运行效率：目标代码应运行得快。编程的容易程度：编写高效程序应当简单而愉快。

Runtime Efficiency: The object code should run fast. Ease of Programming: It should be simple and pleasant to write efficient programs.

Brooks 和 Gabriel 把这些可能的目标，与 Common Lisp 设计工作中实际发生的事情作了对比：

Brooks and Gabriel contrast these potential goals with what really happened during the Common Lisp design effort:

Common Lisp 小组潜在地相信，人人都会拥有一台大型 Lisp 机器。他们过分指望微码能掩盖类型分派的成本，忽略了不可见指针的成本，而不可见指针似乎又是移位数组等特性必需的组成部分。太多语言成本被“足够聪明的编译器”这一论点轻轻带过。过分强调已有实现，即使那些想法本身效率低下，数组和向量就是例子。过多精力用于过度扩展可移植机制，例如路径名和潜在数字。设计中内置了一种近乎宗教的信念：Lisp 就是系统编程的正确语言。还隐含地相信移植应当不可见，像文本编辑器这样庞大复杂的代码，应该几乎不费力就能从一个 Common Lisp 移到另一个。妥协精神使太多关键事项没有定义。Common Lisp 的通用算术由于所需声明的数量和性质，使通用硬件上的程序员难以编写高效数值代码。算术操作出现了不必要的膨胀。函数调用太昂贵。对计算机规模和能力的预期被夸大。一些好想法被推得过远，例如 FORMAT 和 SETF。本应属于库的东西被纳入语言，例如序列函数。

There was an underlying belief by the Common Lisp Group that everyone would have a big Lisp machine. There was too much hope that microcoding could hide the cost of type dispatch. The cost of invisible pointers, an apparently necessary ingredient for such things as displaced arrays, was ignored. Too many language costs were dismissed with the “a sufficiently smart compiler” argument. There was too much emphasis on existing implementations of inherently inefficient ideas—arrays and vectors are an example. Too much effort was spent overextending transportation mechanisms, such as pathnames and potential numbers. There was a religious belief that Lisp was the correct language for systems programming built into the design. There was an implicit belief that transportation should be invisible, and that large complex pieces of code like text editors should move from Common Lisp to Common Lisp with little or no effort. The spirit of compromise left too many crucial things undefined. Common Lisp generic arithmetic makes it difficult for a stock hardware programmer to write efficient numeric code because of the number and nature of the required declarations. The was an unnecessary explosion of arithmetic operations. Function calling was too expensive. The expected size and power of computers was exaggerated. Some good ideas were taken too far, such as FORMAT and SETF. Things that should have been libraries were included in the language, for example sequence functions.

这一主题在 Common Lisp 历史中反复出现：出现了许多“unCommon Lisp”（非 Common Lisp），这种双关或许连最痴迷陈旧谐音笑话的人也难以忍受。每一种 unCommon Lisp 都宣称，对 Common Lisp 的某些缺点有更好的处理办法。

This theme reappeared in the history of Common Lisp—the emergence of a number of “un- Common” Lisps, straining, perhaps, the tolerance of even the most twisted lovers of overused puns. Each unCommon Lisp proclaimed its better approaches to some of the shortcomings of Common Lisp.

正式或非正式自称“unCommon Lisp”的方言包括 Lisp370、Scheme、EuLisp 和 muLisp。显然，它们试图与 Common Lisp 被认为存在的缺点拉开距离；但不那么显然的是，采用这一说法也证明了 Common Lisp 作为主要 Lisp 方言在表面上和实际上的强势。把一种 Lisp 定义为与另一种方言相对立，就是承认后者的优势地位。（想象福特把 Mustang 宣传为“非 Corvette”。）

Examples of Lisp dialects that officially or unofficially declare themselves “unCommon Lisps” are Lisp370, Scheme, EuLisp, and muLisp. This is clearly an attempt to distance themselves from the perceived shortcomings of Common Lisp, but, less clearly, their use of this term attests to the apparent and real strength of Common Lisp as the primary Lisp dialect. To define a Lisp as standing in contrast to another dialect is to admit the supremacy of that other dialect. (Imagine Ford advertising the Mustang as “the unCorvette”.)

这种行为比任何其他单一现象都更能说明 Lisp 分化的一个关键因素：方言团队之间极端、近乎幼稚的竞争。

More than any other single phenomenon, this behavior demonstrates one of the key ingredients of Lisp diversification: extreme—almost juvenile—rivalry between dialect groups.

我们已经介绍了 Lisp370 和 Scheme。EuLisp 是欧洲对 Common Lisp 的回应，旨在成为欧洲 Lisp 的通用语言。它的主要特征是：采用 Lisp-1，即函数和变量不分命名空间；从底层就集成类似 CLOS 的、基于泛型函数的面向对象系统；内置模块系统；采用分层定义，促进它在小型嵌入式硬件和教学机器上的使用。除此之外，EuLisp 类似 Common Lisp，共同点多于差异。EuLisp 的定义耗时很久：1986年开始，到1990年才有第一个实现（仅解释器）以及近乎完整的语言规范。不过，分层定义、模块系统和从一开始就面向对象，说明 Lisp 世界仍然能够学到新的经验。

We have already seen Lisp370 and Scheme. EuLisp is the European response to Common Lisp, developed as the lingua franca for Lisp in Europe. Its primary characteristics is that it is a Lisp-1 (no separate function and variable namespaces), has a CLOS-style (Common Lisp Object System) generic-function type object-oriented system integrated from the ground up, has a built-in module system, and is defined in layers to promote the use of the Lisp on small, embedded hardware and educational machines. Otherwise, EuLisp is Common-Lisp like, with more commonality than disjointness. The definition of EuLisp took a long time; started in 1986, it wasn’t until 1990 that the first implementation was available (interpreter-only) along with a nearly complete language specification. Nevertheless, the layered definition, the module system, and the object-orientedness from the start demonstrate that new lessons can be learned in the Lisp world.

这是一个复杂时期。显然存在整合，而且第一次是有意识的整合。整合由两个接受群体推动：ARPA 社区带来利他作用，人工智能公司带来商业作用。利他群体认为社区公共利益推动了这项工作，但真正使它起飞的是 Lisp 机器公司的参与。Common Lisp 小组在某种程度上知道，必须争取 Lisp 机器阵营，因此对它们作出了太多妥协。

This is a complex period. Clearly there is consolidation, and for the first time it is a conscious consolidation. The consolidation is driven by two acceptance groups: ARPA community (an altruistic effect) and the AI companies (a commercial effect). The altruistic group feels that community good is driving the effort, while it is really the buy-in of the Lisp machine companies that make it fly. At some level the CLG knows that the Lisp machine people have to be courted, so too many compromises with them are made.

Lisp 机器提供了推动这项工作的声望和吸引力。人工智能团队和 ARPA 正处于上升期，因此工作拥有充足动力。

Lisp machines provide the cachet that drives the effort. The AI groups and ARPA are on the ascendancy, so there is fuel for the effort.

几位新领袖出现了：Steele，学者型、有时天真的领袖；Fahlman，富有号召力，尤其能吸引年轻参与者；Gabriel，自诩政治谋略家，设法让 ARPA 和各团队站到同一阵线；Moon，思想有令人着迷的力量，安静而常带冒犯性，其论证几乎无法反驳；Weinreb，善于思考的倾听者，关注争论的每一方。Jon L 等老领袖略微退居幕后。

Several new leaders emerge: Steele, a scholarly and sometimes na¨ıve leader. Fahlman, a charismatic leader, especially of younger participants. Gabriel, the self-styled political mastermind who maneuvers ARPA and various groups into line. Moon, a seductively powerful thinker, quiet and often insulting, whose arguments are almost impossible to refute. Weinreb, the reflective listener who pays attention to every side of the argument. Old leaders like Jon L fade slightly into the background.

McCarthy 几乎没有表现出兴趣，却始终支付 Gabriel 的报酬。这很符合 McCarthy 的风格：他对这类细节兴趣不大。

McCarthy shows little or no interest, but pays Gabriel all the while. This is typical of McCarthy, whose has little interest in details like this.

新的实现领军人物也出现了：Gabriel、Steele、Brooks、Fahlman。White 则转入 Xerox 和 Interlisp 社区。

There is an emergence of new implementation leaders. Gabriel, Steele, Brooks, Fahlman. White disappears into Xerox and the Interlisp community.

Spice Lisp 成为 Common Lisp 的参考实现，TI、PERQ、Siemens、DEC 等团队用它作为最初 Common Lisp 的基础。Spice Lisp 可以自由获取，为其他 Lisp 方言的免费参考实现开了先河。此外，正在形成的 Common Lisp 有了公有领域参考实现，也促进了采用。

Spice Lisp appeared as a reference implementation for Common Lisp, and several groups—TI, PERQ, Siemens, DEC—use it as the basis of their initial Common Lisps. Spice Lisp is freely available, and it sets the stage for free reference implementations for other dialects of Lisp. Furthermore, the appearance of a public domain reference implementation of the emerging Common Lisp aids its adoption.

由于电子邮件，任何希望参与设计的人都能发声。因此出现了一种促进想法被接受的群体精神：普通人第一次成为设计过程的一部分，而不是某个专家或专家组的旁观者。任何能接入 ARPANET 的人，只要请求加入邮件列表，就能参与。不过，到最后阶段（1983年），五人执政团还是以专家组身份出现，负责引导定义文档的最终起草。

Because of email, everyone who wants a voice in the design has one. Therefore, there is a group spirit that aids adoption of the ideas, because for the first time the common man is part of the design process, not the observer of an expert or group of experts. By merely asking for his or her name to be added to a mailing, anyone with ARPANET access can join the process. Though the Quinquevirate emerges as a expert group at the end (1983) to shepherd the final drafting of the definition.

有些团队没有预见 Common Lisp 的力量。Interlisp 团队仍在向前推进，这也合理，因为它的接受群体与 Symbolics、LMI 的接受群体重叠。Interlisp 的问题在于选错了机器：缓慢的 D 系列和不受欢迎的 VAX。Interlisp 的 VAX 实现始终没有真正起步，部分因为没有完成，主要则因为性能无法接受。当然，问题更多在 VAX 而非 Interlisp：VAX 被设计为运行许多小程序，而不是一两个大程序，因此其页大小要求使用两级页表，因为页表本身也需要分页。

Some groups don’t see the power of Common Lisp coming. The Interlisp group is forging ahead. , and rightly so because their acceptance group overlaps the Symbolics/LMI acceptance groups. The problem for InterLisp is that they have the wrong machines—the slow D-machines and the unpopular Vax. The Vax implementation of Interlisp never really got offthe ground, partly for not being completed but mostly because performance of Interlisp is unacceptable. Of course, the problem lies more with the Vax than with Interlisp: the Vax was designed to run many small programs rather than one or two large ones, so the page size requires a two-level page table because the page table needs to be paged.

当时，负责 ARPA 的 Lisp 事务的项目主管是 Ron Ohlander，他是 Common Lisp 强有力的支持者。他的支持部分源于他认为 Lisp 的命运对人工智能的命运至关重要，部分源于人工智能领域的主要人物告诉他必须关注底层技术，也部分源于 Common Lisp 小组非常重视他及其关切。而且，争论不休的 Lisp 社区恰好在 Ohlander 任内终于团结起来、就标准达成一致，这想必也有好处。

At the time the ARPA program director overseeing ARPA’s Lisp concerns is Ron Ohlander, who is a powerful friend of Common Lisp. His friendliness is due in part to his perception that the fate of Lisp is central to the fate of AI, in part because the principals of AI tell him that he needs to pay attention to the underlying technology, and in part because the Common Lisp Group pays a lot of attention to him and his concerns. In probably doesn’t hurt that it is on Ohlander’s watch that the contentious Lisp community finally gets its act together and agrees on a standard.

2.11 其他通用硬件方言：1980—1984年

2.11 Other Stock Hardware Dialects: 1980–1984

Common Lisp 开发期间，虽然它吸引了大量关注，世界其他部分并没有停步。Portable Standard Lisp 扩展到 VAX、DEC-20、各种 MC68000 机器以及 Cray 1。其 Emode 环境（后来叫 Nmode）吸引了 Hewlett-Packard，后者在 Common Lisp 势力不断增长的背景下将它产品化。

The rest of the world did not stand still while Common Lisp was developed, though Common Lisp was the focus of a lot of attention. Portable Standard Lisp spread to the Vax, DEC-20, a variety of MC68000 machines, and the Cray 1. Its Emode environment (later Nmode) proved appealing to Hewlett-Packard, which “productized” it in the face of a growing Common Lisp presence.

Franz Lisp 被移植到许多系统上，在1985—1986年 Common Lisp 普遍可用之前的那些年里，它成为通用硬件上的主力 Lisp。

Franz Lisp was ported to many systems, and it became the workhorse stock hardware Lisp for the years leading up to the general availability of Common Lisp in 1985–1986.

1980年代初，Interlisp 在其核心接受群体中获得认可。它运行于 PDP-10、Dolphin、Dorado 和 VAX，似乎即将在一种低价 Lisp 机器 Dandelion 上出现；传闻这种机器能以接近 Dolphin 的价格提供接近 Dorado 的性能。

Interlisp gained acceptance from its pure acceptance group during the early 1980’s. It was running on PDP-10’s, Dolphins, Dorados, and Vaxes, and it seemed about to appear on a lowcost Lisp machine called the Dandelion, which was rumored to have Dorado-like performance for a Dolphin-like price.

在市场上，Dolphin 在性能竞争中遭到重创，主要因为它是一台运行 Interlisp 虚拟机的慢速机器。Xerox 的工作集中于移植和性能，对方言或环境的改进关注较少，尽管这方面的工作仍在继续。Interlisp 的主要开发者在做调优。

In the market, the Dolphin was taking a beating in the performance sweepstakes, primarily because it was a slow machine that ran the Interlisp virtual machine. The efforts of Xerox were aimed at porting and performance, with little attention to improving the dialect or the environment, though work continued in this area. The main Interlisp developers were tuning.

Interlisp/VAX 虽然问世，却必须视为失败，原因有三：（1）它兼容的是 Interlisp-10，而不是 Interlisp-D；前者这一家族分支注定随着 PDP-10 的最终消亡而衰落。（2）它只提供停止并复制式垃圾回收器，而这种回收器在 VAX 上性能特别差。（3）当 Lisp 世界的其他部分，包括 Interlisp 世界，都涌向个人 Lisp 机器时，除了少数企业，几乎没有人认真把 VAX 当作 Lisp 平台。

Interlisp/Vax made an appearance, but has to be regarded as a failure with three contributing causes: (1) it provided compatibility with Interlisp-10, the branch of the Interlisp family doomed by the eventual demise of the PDP-10, rather than with Interlisp-D; (2) it provided only a stopand-copy garbage collector, which has particularly bad performance on a Vax; and (3) as the rest of the Lisp world, including the Interlisp world, flocked to personal Lisp machines, the Vax was never taken seriously for Lisp purposes except by a small number of businesses.

在法国，Jérôme Chailloux 及其同事开发了名为 Le Lisp 的新方言。它令人想起 MacLisp，重点是可移植性和效率。之所以必须可移植，是因为欧洲的计算机格局对 Lisp 而言不像美国那样明确。在美国，Lisp 机器占据主导，但在欧洲这些机器不易获得，而且往往贵得难以承受。欧洲研究实验室经常获赠或购入各种从美国角度看来很奇特的机器，因此可移植性必不可少。

In France, J´erˆome Chailloux and his colleagues developed a new dialect of Lisp called Le Lisp. The dialect was reminiscent of MacLisp, and it focused on portability and efficiency. It needed to be portable because the computer situation in Europe was not as clear as it was in the US for Lisp. In the US, Lisp machines were dominant for Lisp, but these machines were not as available and often were prohibitively expensive in Europe. Research labs in Europe were frequently given or acquired a range of peculiar machines (from the US perspective). Therefore, portability was a must.

Vlisp 的经验使 Chailloux 明白，性能和可移植性可以兼得。他的团队扩展了一些 Vlisp 技术，实现了目标。到1984年，这种方言已能在约十种不同机器上运行，性能远优于最可比较的替代品 Franz Lisp。在 VAX 780 上，Le Lisp 的性能大致相当于 Symbolics 3600。

The experience with Vlisp taught Chailloux that performance and portability can go together, and, extending some of the Vlisp techniques, his group was able to achieve their goals. By 1984 their dialect ran on about ten different machines and demonstrated performance very much better than Franz Lisp, the most comparable alternative. On Vax 780’s, Le Lisp performed about as well as Symbolics 3600’s.

此外，Le Lisp 提供了名为 Ceyx 的完整编程环境，包含全套调试辅助工具、全屏多窗口结构编辑器和美化打印器，以及同样名为 Ceyx 的面向对象编程扩展。

In addition, Le Lisp provided a full-fledged programming environment called Ceyx. Ceyx had a full set of debugging aids, a full-screen, multi-window structure editor and pretty printer, and an object-oriented programming extension, also called Ceyx.

重要的是，法国教育部向法国高中提供了这种 Lisp 的一个子集用于教学，培养出一代 Le Lisp 用户。

Of importance is that a subset of this Lisp was provided to French high schools for instruction by the French Education Ministry, providing a generation of Le Lisp users.

Le Lisp 的可移植性很强，有些移植只需要几个人工作一周。该语言基于一种虚拟机，其思想类似于编写 Vlisp 解释器所用的虚拟机；编译器为这台称为 LLM3 的机器生成代码。除机器无关优化外，加载时还会使用窥孔优化器。

Le Lisp was quite portable, with some ports requiring only 1 week of effort by several people. The language was based on a virtual machine similar in philosophy to the one in which the Vlisp interpreter was written, and the compiler generated code for this machine, called LLM3. In addition to machine-independent optimizations, a peephole optimizer at load time was used.

虽然这一时期 Le Lisp 没有进入 Common Lisp 的历史，但到1980年代末的标准化之争中，它的重要性会提升。

Though Le Lisp would not figure into Common Lisp history during this period, in the late 1980’s it would rise in prominence during the standardization wars.

Scheme 社区从少数爱好者发展成一个大得多的群体，其特点是对程序设计语言的数学方面感兴趣。Scheme 体积小、根植于 lambda 演算、语义基础通常简洁，这些特点使它逐渐成为研究和教学的热门工具。特别是在 MIT、莱斯大学和印第安纳大学，形成了强大的 Scheme 支持群体。通常，这些群体由进入这些学校任教的 MIT 毕业生发起。

The Scheme community grew from a few aficionados to a much larger group, characterized by an interest in the mathematical aspects of programming languages. Scheme’s small size, roots in lambda calculus, and generally compact semantic underpinnings began to make it popular as a vehicle for research and teaching. In particular, strong groups of Scheme supporters developed at MIT, Rice, and Indiana University. In general these groups were started by MIT graduates who joined the faculty at these schools.

由于源于 Conniver 和参与者模型，Scheme 原本就有续延，但 CALL-WITH-CURRENT-CONTINUATION 原语进一步推广了这一概念，使人们可以在任意位置捕获当前续延。有了这一原语，就能探索多任务的替代模型，例如印第安纳大学关于 engines 的工作（??? 参见 Haynes）。

Though Scheme had continuations as a result of its roots in Conniver and Actors, the concept was generalized somewhat in the primitive CALL-WITH-CURRENT-CONTINUATION, which provided a means to capture the continuation at any point. With this primitive it was possible to explore such things as alternative models of multitasking, such as exemplified by the work at Indiana Univerity on engines (??? refer to Haynes).

在 MIT，Gerry Sussman 和 Hal Abelson 指导采用 Scheme 教授本科计算机课程。《计算机程序的构造和解释》[?] 成为经典，使 Scheme 在更广泛的社区中声名鹊起。

At MIT, under the guidance of Gerry Sussman and Hal Abelson, Scheme was adopted for teaching undergraduate computing. The book Structure and Interpretation of Computer Programs [?] became a classic and vaulted Scheme to notoriety in a larger community.

出现了几家制作商业 Scheme 实现的公司：R. Kent Dybvig 创办的 Cadence Research Systems 提供 Chez Scheme；Will Clinger、Anne Hartheimer 和 John Ulrich 创办 Semantic Microsystems；Texas Instruments 也推出一种 Scheme。Chez Scheme 运行于各种工作站；Semantic Microsystems 的产品叫 MacScheme，运行于 Macintosh；PC Scheme 则运行于 IBM PC 及其兼容机，包括 TI 制造销售的机型。

Several companies sprang up that made commercial implementations of Scheme: Chez Scheme by Cadence Research Systems was started by R. Kent Dybvig, Semantic Microsystems by Will Clinger, Anne Hartheimer, and John Ulrich, and Texas Instruments fielded a version of Scheme. Chez Scheme ran on various workstations, Semantic Microsystems’ Scheme was called MacScheme and ran on Macintoshes. PC Scheme ran on IBM PC’s and clones such as the one TI built and sold.

最初的《Scheme 修订报告》成为后续 Scheme 定义的范本，一群自行加入、被称为“作者”的人承担了演进 Scheme 的角色。他们采用的规则是：只有全体一致同意，才能增加特性。经过一段相当短的时期，加入 CALL-WITH-CURRENT-CONTINUATION 等特性之后，Scheme 的变化速度因这条规则而放慢。在这个高度重视智识的群体中，只有同侪压力才能说服顽固的作者撤回否决。因此，人们普遍相信，只要某项特性被加入 Scheme，它显然就是正确的东西。例如，直到1991年末，宏才以部分标准化设施的形式加入语言的附录。

The original Revised Report on Scheme was taken as a model for future definitions of Scheme, and a self-selected group of so-called “authors” took on the role of evolving Scheme. The rule they adopted was that features could be added only by unanimous consent. After a fairly short period in which certain features like CALL-WITH-CURRENT-CONTINUATION were added, the rate of change of Scheme slowed down due to this rule. Only peer pressure in a highly intellectual group could convince any recalcitrant author to change his blackball. As a result there is a widely held belief that whenever a feature is added to Scheme, it is clearly the right thing. For example, only in late 1991 were macros added to the language in an appendix—as a partially standardized facility.

后来出现了一系列修订报告，称为《Scheme 的修订、……、修订报告》。1991年末，《Scheme 的修订、修订、修订、修订报告》完成并获批准，人们亲切地称它为 R⁴RS。

There would be a series of Revised Reports, called “The Revised, . . . , Revised Report on Scheme.” In late 1991 “The Revised, Revised, Revised, Revised Report on Scheme” was written and approved; it is affectionately called “R4RS.”

许多后来加入 Common Lisp 小组的人，都曾宣称自己深爱 Scheme，并且并不怎么掩饰地希望某种类似 Scheme 的语言成为下一代 Lisp 标准。然而，到这个十年的后半段，Scheme 和 Common Lisp 社区有时会成为激烈的对手。

Many of those who would later become members of the Common Lisp Group would proclaim a deep-seated love of Scheme and a not-so-secret desire to see something like it become the next Lisp standard. However, the Scheme and Common Lisp communities would become sometimes bitter rivals in the latter part of the decade.

2.11.1 Zetalisp

2.11.1 Zetalisp

Common Lisp 启动时（1981年），MIT Lisp-Machine Lisp 已有很大发展。Common Lisp 小组（见下文）必须应对 Zetalisp 日益增长的影响。

At the time that Common Lisp began (1981) MIT Lisp-machine Lisp had evolved considerably. The Common Lisp Group (see below) needed to address Zetalisp’s growing influence.

选择 Zetalisp 这个名字，是因为它意味着（???）“关于 Lisp 的最后定论”。

The name “Zetalisp” is chosen as (???) “the last word on Lisp.”

Zetalisp 是 Symbolics 版本 Lisp-Machine Lisp 的名称。由于下文将介绍的 Symbolics 第二代 Lisp 机器3600几乎完全用 Lisp 编程，Zetalisp 需要具备此前任何单一 Lisp 都未曾拥有的一整套重要能力。Zetalisp 不仅用于普通编程，操作系统、编辑器、编译器、网络服务器、垃圾回收器和窗口系统也都用它编写。由于早期 Lisp-Machine Lisp 尚不足以胜任这些任务，Zetalisp 被扩展来处理它们。

Zetalisp was the name of the Symbolics version of Lisp-machine Lisp. Because the 3600—the Symbolics second-generation Lisp machine, which is described below—was programmed almost entirely in Lisp, Zetalisp came to require a significant set of capabilities not seen in any single Lisp to this point. Not only was Zetalisp used for ordinary programming, but the operating system, the editor, the compiler, the network server, the garbage collector, and the window system were all programmed in Zetalisp, and because the earlier Lisp-machine Lisp was not quite up to these tasks, Zetalisp was expanded to handle them.

对 Lisp-Machine Lisp 最主要的增补是 Flavors，一种所谓非层次式面向对象语言。我们将在第???节进一步讨论 Flavors；这里先说明，它是基于 Howard Cannon 若干想法开发的、支持多重继承的消息传递系统。把这些想法发展成完整系统的工作主要归功于 David A. Moon，不过 Cannon 也是关键人物。

The primary addition to Lisp-machine Lisp was Flavors, a so-called non-hierarchical objectoriented language, We will talk more about Flavors in the section (???), but for now suffice it to say that it is a multiple inheritance, message-passing system developed from some ideas of Howard Cannon. The development of the ideas into a coherent system was largely due to David A. Moon, though Cannon was a key individual.

Flavors 的特性受 Lisp 机器窗口系统的需求驱动；很长一段时间里，这个窗口系统被认为是唯一一个编程确实需要多重继承的系统实例。

The features of Flavors was driven by the needs of the Lisp machine window system, which, for a long time, was regarded as the only example of a system whose programming required multiple inheritance.

其他值得注意的增补包括 FORMAT、SETF、复杂数组，以及支持可选参数和按关键字命名参数的复杂 lambda 列表。FORMAT 是一种便于生成字符串输出的机制，基本方法是使用预先确定、带占位符的字符串，再用计算出的值或字符串替换占位符；但它后来远比这复杂，因为占位符还包括生成结果列表的迭代原语、单复数词形变化及其他奇特功能。SETF 已在前文讨论。

Other noteworthy additions were FORMAT and SETF, complex arrays, and complex lambdalists for optional and keyword-named arguments. FORMAT is a mechanism for producing string output conveniently by, basically, taking a pre-determined string with placeholders and substituting computed values or strings for those placeholders—though it became much more complex than this because the placeholders included iteration primitives for producing lists of results, plurals, and other such exotica. SETF was discussed earlier.

Zetalisp 获得接受并变得重要的因素之一，是 Lisp 机器本身获得接受，下一节将讨论这一点。由于 Lisp 机器，尤其是 Symbolics Lisp 机器，是商业环境中开展实际 Lisp 工作最受欢迎的平台，人们逐渐形成一种明确的信念，Symbolics 自己也在助长它：Lisp-Machine Lisp，即 Zetalisp，是 Lisp 的主要方言。因此，Symbolics 的人被视为强大的政治力量，也是更广泛 Lisp 标准成功所必需的政治盟友。

One of the factors in the acceptance and importance of Zetalisp was the acceptance of the Lisp machines, which is discussed in the next section. Because Lisp machines—particularly Symbolics Lisp machines—were the most popular vehicles for real Lisp work in a commercial setting, there would grow to be an explicit belief, fostered by Symbolics itself, that Lisp-machine Lisp (Zetalisp) was the primary dialect for Lisp. Therefore, the Symbolics folks were taken very seriously as a strong political force and a required political ally for the success of a wider Lisp standard.

2.11.2 早期 Lisp 机器公司

2.11.2 Early Lisp Machine Companies

主要有五家 Lisp 机器公司：Symbolics；Lisp Machine, Inc.（LMI）；Three Rivers Computer，后来以主要产品之名改称 PERQ；Xerox；以及 Texas Instruments（TI）。

There were five primary Lisp machine companies: Symbolics; Lisp Machine, Inc. (LMI); Three Rivers Computer, later renamed PERQ after its principal product; Xerox; and Texas Instruments (TI).

其中 Symbolics、LMI 和 TI 都以从 MIT 获得许可的基本相同软件作为产品基础。软件包括 Lisp 实现、操作系统、编辑器、窗口系统、网络软件和全部实用工具。协议规定，只要把改进反馈给 MIT，就可以廉价或免费获得软件。

Of these, Symbolics, LMI, and TI all used basically the same software licensed from MIT as the basis of their offerings. The software included the Lisp implementation, the operating system, the editor, the window system, the network software, and all the utilities. There was an arrangement wherein the software would be cheaply (or freely) available as long as improvements are were passed back to MIT.

因此，各公司主要依据硬件性能竞争，其次则是在共同软件基础上的改进反馈回公共源码之前，率先提供这些改进。有些公司还制作专有扩展，例如 Symbolics 的 C 和 FORTRAN 实现。

Therefore, the companies competed primarily on the basis of hardware performance but secondarily on the availability of advances in the common software base before that software passed back to the common source. Some of the companies also produced propriety extensions, such as C and FORTRAN implementations from Symbolics.

Xerox 生产运行 Interlisp-D 的 D 系列机器。

Xerox produced the D-machines, which ran Interlisp-D.

Three Rivers 销售 PERQ，它既可以运行基于 Pascal 的操作系统和语言，也可以运行 Spice Lisp，后来则运行基于 Spice Lisp 的 Common Lisp。

Three Rivers sold a machine, the PERQ, that ran either a Pascal-based operating system and language or Spice Lisp, later Common Lisp based on Spice Lisp.

因此，所有 Lisp 机器公司都是从现有软件起步，而所有源自 MacLisp 的 Lisp 机器公司都从大学获得软件许可。

Thus, all the Lisp-machine companies started out with existing software, and all the MacLispderived Lisp-machine companies licensed their software from a university.

在这些公司中，Symbolics 最成功，衡量标准是 Common Lisp 时代之前结束时的装机数量；其次是 Xerox 和 TI，不过 TI 也可能凭借一两笔大公司的采购而声称拥有最多装机。

Of these companies, Symbolics is most successful (as measured by number of installed machines at the end of the pre-Common-Lisp era), followed by Xerox and TI, though TI possibly can claim the most installed machines on the basis of one or two large company purchases.

Symbolics 在这些公司中最值得关注，因为它对 Common Lisp 的方向产生了极大影响；不过，我们并不想说其他公司没有重大意义。Zetalisp 的流行，或者至少它对 Common Lisp 小组其他成员的明显影响，很大程度上源于3600的流行。

Symbolics is most interesting of these companies because of the extreme influence of Symbolics on the direction of Common Lisp—however, we do not want to claim that the other companies did not have strong significance. The popularity of Zetalisp—or at least its apparent influence on the other people in the Common Lisp group—stemmed largely from the popularity of the 3600.

3600是第二代 Lisp 机器，第一代是名为 LM-2 的 CADR 版本。Symbolics 和 LMI 创业时生产的基本都是 CADR；LMI 的版本叫 LAMBDA。不过，Symbolics 的商业计划是生产快得多的 Lisp 机器，并加入工作站竞赛。

The 3600 was a second-generation Lisp machine, the first being a version of the CADR, called the LM-2. Both Symbolics and LMI started their businesses by producing essentially the CADR; the LMI version was called the LAMBDA. However, Symbolics’s business plan was to produce a much faster Lisp machine and to enter the workstation sweepstakes.

人们有时容易忘记，1980年代初工作站还是稀罕事物，而且计算能力通常弱到许多人并不认真看待。PDP-10 的性能仍远胜工作站，工程师是否会接受工作站、它们是否会形成庞大新市场，都完全不明显。而且，基于 Unix/C 的工作站是否一定会获胜也不明确，因为人们认为推动市场的会更多是应用，而不是软件开发。因此，Symbolics 制定这样的商业计划并不愚蠢。

Sometimes it is easy to forget that, in the early 1980’s, workstations were an oddity, and the workstations were generally so computationally underpowered that many did not take them seriously. The PDP-10 still offered vastly better performance than the workstations, and it simply was not obvious that engineers would ever warm up to them or that they would form a large new market. Furthermore, it was not clear that a Unix-based/C-based workstation was necessarily the winner either, since it was thought that applications would drive the market more than software development. Therefore, it was not foolish for Symbolics to have the business plan it did.

Symbolics 和 LMI 由 MIT 人工智能实验室中的对手创办：Richard Greenblatt 创办 LMI，几乎所有其他人创办 Symbolics，特别是 Moon、Weinreb、Cannon 和 Knight。

Symbolics and LMI were founded by rivals at the MIT AI Lab, with Richard Greenblatt founding LMI and almost everyone else founding Symbolics, in particular Moon, Weinreb, Cannon, and Knight.

Symbolics Lisp 机器和 Zetalisp 获得采用的因素之一，是最早的3600没有垃圾回收器，因此人们没有观察到大地址空间垃圾回收带来的性能代价。3600原计划采用 Baker 风格的增量停止并复制式回收器，但由于地址空间很大，普通程序数天都不会耗尽内存，密集型程序也能运行约8小时。系统提供保存运行映像的设施，基本上就是把停止并复制式垃圾回收做到磁盘上，之后可以恢复映像。因此，程序员不是在运行中回收，而是一直运行到内存耗尽，再启动耗时很长、最多可达数小时的磁盘保存过程，晚饭后或次日再重新启动程序。

One of the factors in the adoption of Symbolics Lisp machines and Zetalisp was the fact that the first 3600’s did not have a garbage collector, which meant that the performance penalty of garbage collecting a large address space was not observed. Originally the 3600 was to have a Baker-style incremental stop-and-copy collector, but because the address space was so large, ordinary programs did not exhaust memory for several days and intensive ones could run for about 8 hours. There was a facility for saving the running image, which basically did a stop-and-copy garbage collection to disk, and this image could be resumed. Therefore, instead of garbage collecting on-the-fly, a programmer would run until memory was exhausted, then he would start up the lengthy (up to several hours) process of disk-saving, and he would restart his program after dinner or the next day.

增量垃圾回收器在首批3600推出数年后才发布，其性能相对较差，可能是分页问题所致。于是 Moon 开发了短生命周期垃圾回收器，类似 Ungar 为 Smalltalk 开发的分代清扫回收器 [?]。在分代清扫中，对象通过停止并复制过程从一代晋升到下一代。经过若干代后，对象晋升为长期存储对象。其思想是：对象往往在创建后不久就成为垃圾，因此只要观察对象年龄并集中处理年轻对象，就能回收大部分垃圾；由于工作集较小，分页性能也较好。

The incremental garbage collector was released several years after the first 3600’s, and it proved to have relatively bad performance, possibly due to paging problems. Instead, Moon developed an ephemeral garbage collector that is similar to the Ungar generation scavenger collector developed for Smalltalk [?]. with generation scavenging, objects are promoted from one generation to the next by a stop-and-copy process. After several generations objects are promoted (tenured) to long-term storage. The idea is that an object will become garbage soon after its creation, so if you can look at the ages of objects and concentrate on only young objects, you will get most of the garbage and because a small working set is maintained paging performance is good.

短生命周期垃圾回收 [Moon, 1984] 与之类似，但维护少数代表各代的分配区域，以及一份内存区域列表，记录哪些区域创建过指向这些分配区域中对象的指针；停止并复制时扫描这些区域，把对象从一代移到另一代。由于 Smalltalk 创建对象的频率低于 Lisp，权衡和数据结构也略有不同。

Ephemeral garbage collection [Moon, 1984] is similar but maintains a few consing areas representing generations and a list of regions of memory where pointers to objects in the consing areas were created, and those regions are scanned in a stop-and-copy operation, moving from one generation to the other. Because objects in Smalltalk are created less frequently than in Lisp, the tradeoffs are a little different and the data structures are different.

短生命周期垃圾回收器证明是有效的。在第一台3600售出数年之后，终于有了有效运转的垃圾回收器。用户花了一些时间适应性能差异，但到那时，3600已经建立起一个处于上升期的接受群体，其地位已经稳固。

The ephemeral garbage collector proved effective, and several years after the first 3600 was sold, an effective garbage collector was operational. It took a while for users to get used to the performance differences, but by then the 3600 had already established an acceptance group that was in the ascendency, and the position of the 3600 was firmly implanted.

PERQ 以一台性能不佳的微码机器进入市场，该机器原先用于文档制作。Scott Fahlman 参与了这家公司；Common Lisp 首次亮相时，Spice Lisp 代码已经几乎符合 Common Lisp，因此 PERQ 成为 Lisp 机器上最早可用的 Common Lisp。

PERQ entered the market with a poorly performing microcoded machine that had been used a document preparation computer. Scott Fahlman was involved with the company, and when Common Lisp made its debut, the Spice Lisp code was a nearly compliant Common Lisp, so PERQ was the first Common Lisp available on a Lisp machine.

最初的 PERQ 及其后续版本，在匹兹堡地区以外始终没有产生多大商业影响，可能是因为性能和性价比相对较差。

The original PERQ and its later versions never made much of a commercial impact outside the Pittsburgh area, probably because its performance and price-performance were relatively poor.

我们已经介绍了 Xerox D 系列机器的早期历史。在 Common Lisp 时代之前，它们在原来的 Interlisp-10 圈子中得到广泛使用。
