We already saw the early history of the Xerox D-machines. Before the Common Lisp era, their use became widespread in former InterLisp-10 circles.

Texas Instruments 在 CLtL1 出版前不久，即1984年初，开始进入 Lisp 机器业务。最初的 Viking 项目使用 Spice Lisp 和 MC68020。后来他们决定走纯 Lisp 机器路线，推出了同样运行 MIT 软件的微码机器 Explorer。再后来，TI 与 LMI 合作交换一些技术，似乎对两家公司的商业命运都没有多少影响，除了给 LMI 注入资金、延长其生存时间。Explorer 的性价比和性能都不错，加上美国国防部对 TI 的高度信任，使 TI 在早期 Common Lisp 时代取得了良好销量。

Texas Instruments began to enter the Lisp machine business just before CLtL1 was published, in early 1984. They began with the Viking project, which was working with Spice Lisp and the MC68020. Later, they decided to go the pure Lisp machine route and introduced the Explorer, which was a microcoded machine that also ran the MIT software. Later, TI joined with LMI to trade some technology, which seemed to have little or no effect on the business fortunes of either company, except to inject capital into LMI, prolonging its existence. The Explorer had good price-performance, and also decent performance, and the high favor in which TI was held by the Department of Defense resulted in good sales for TI during the early Common Lisp era.

2.11.3 走向衰落的 MacLisp

2.11.3 MacLisp on the Decline

虽然 MacLisp 仍被广泛使用，而且还有少量扩展，其开发在1980年代初停止了。此时，MacLisp 运行于 ITS、Multics、TENEX、TOPS-10、TOPS-20 和 WAITS。虽然只适用于 PDP-10 或相关机器，但这些机器包括 PDP-10、DEC-10、DEC-20，以及 Foonly F2 和 F4。

Though still in widespread use, and spreading a bit, the development of MacLisp halted during the early 1980’s. At this point, MacLisp ran on ITS, Multics, Tenex, TOPS-10, TOPS-20, and WAITS. Though only available on PDP-10’s or related machines, these included the PDP-10, the DEC-10, the DEC-20, and the Foonly F2 and F4.

MacLisp 开发经费一直由 Macsyma 团队提供，因为从 MIT 的角度看，MacLisp 的主要客户是 Macsyma 的用户，即 Macsyma 联盟。然而从外部世界看，Macsyma 只是 Lisp 的一个有趣应用，MacLisp 作为人工智能，特别是视觉和机器人领域的研发工具，具有广泛得多的吸引力。不过，这些团队经费并不充裕；而且无论如何，它们都没有理由不接受免费提供的 MacLisp。

Funding for MacLisp development had been provided by the Macsyma Group, because the primary client for MacLisp, from the point of view of MIT, was the people who used Macsyma— the Macsyma Consortium. From the point of view of the rest of the world, however, Macsyma was an interesting application of Lisp, but MacLisp was of much wider appeal as a research and development tool for AI, particularly vision and robotics. However, these groups were not flush with funding, and, in any event, none found any reason to do other than to accept the use of a freely available MacLisp.

MacLisp 的经费被能源部对 VAX 上 NIL 的资助所取代。除民用能源等较温和项目外，能源部还监管核武器研发等事务，因此也是国防经费的另一来源，并资助了 S1 Lisp 等项目。

The funding for MacLisp was supplanted by funding for NIL on the Vax by the Department of Energy. The Department of Energy oversaw such things as research and development of nuclear weapons in addition to its more benign projects such as civilian energy. Therefore, the DOE was an alternative source of defense funding, and it funded such projects as S1 Lisp.

一般来说，每个使用 MacLisp 的地点都有一位本地高手，能处理遇到的大部分问题，必要时咨询 Jon L White。至少有一次，MIT 获得经费进行定制工作。例如，WAITS 上的单段 MacLisp 由斯坦福人工智能实验室出资，Howard Cannon 在现场完成了工作。

In general, each site that used MacLisp had a local wizard who was able to handle most of the problems encountered, possibly by consulting Jon L White. In at least one case, funding was made available to MIT to do some custom work. For instance, the single-segment version of MacLisp on WAITS was paid for by the Stanford AI Lab, and the work was done on site by Howard Cannon.

多年来，MacLisp 承载了各种语言开发和特性，包括 MicroPlanner、Conniver、Scheme、Flavors、Frames、Extends、Qlisp，以及各种视觉处理特性。MacLisp 中最后一项主要研究，是 Martin E. Frost 和 Gabriel 在斯坦福完成的多程序编程环境 [?]。该环境定义了一套协议，使 MacLisp 与获得操作系统支持的斯坦福显示编辑器 E，通过类似邮箱的操作系统机制通信。借助这一机制，底层宿主计算机的分时功能，使任何使用 E 的用户，即使不是进行 Lisp 工作，也能共享编辑代码；而且用于编辑的代码经常直接在操作系统中执行，无需换入或调入代码页。Lisp 程序也可以控制编辑器，因此能够用 Lisp 编写强大的“宏”，而不必使用晦涩的 E 宏语言。这一环境比类似的 Lisp/Emacs 环境早了几年。

MacLisp was the host for a variety of language development and features over the years, including MicroPlanner, Conniver, Scheme, Flavors, Frames, Extends, Qlisp, and various visionprocessing features. The last major piece of research in MacLisp was the multi-program programming environment done by Martin E. Frost and Gabriel at Stanford [?]. This environment defined a protocol that allowed MacLisp and E, the Stanford display editor which had operating-system support, to communicate over a mailbox-style operating system mechanism. With this mechanism, the code devoted to editing was shared by any users using E (even for non-Lisp tasks) by using the timesharing mechanism of the underlying host computer, and frequently code executed for the purpose of editing was executed within the operating system, requiring no code to be swapped in or paged in. It was also possible for Lisp programs to control the editor, so that very powerful “macros” written in Lisp could be used instead of the arcane E macro language. This environment predated similar Lisp/Emacs environments by a few years.

然而，在 Common Lisp 小组成立初期，能源部和 Macsyma 联盟停止了对 VAX 上 NIL 的资助，或许是因为相信 Lisp 机器能很好地运行 Macsyma，也或许是因为相信 Common Lisp 的开发会为 Macsyma 提供共同基础。

However, in the early days of the Common Lisp group, funding for NIL for the Vax by DOE and the Macsyma Consortium was halted, perhaps fueled by the belief that Lisp machines would run Macsyma well, perhaps fueled by the belief that the development of Common Lisp would provide the common base for Macsyma.

能源部停止资助的前后，Symbolics 成立了 Macsyma 团队，销售基于 Lisp 机器的 Macsyma。这个团队直到1980年代末或1990年代初解散之前，一直保持盈利。

Around the same time that DOE funding stopped, Symbolics started a Macsyma Group to sell Lisp-machine-based Macsyma. This group would remain profitable until its dissolution in the late 1980’s or early 1990’s.

NIL 经费停止后，Jon L White 加入 Xerox，从事 Interlisp 工作。很难想象比这更奇怪的情形。首先，White 的性格显然如此具有美国东部气质，加利福尼亚生活方式的整个氛围似乎都过于陌生，难以接受。其次，MacLisp 和 Interlisp 多年来的竞争，似乎本应排除双方合作的可能。

With NIL funding stoppage, Jon L White joined Xerox to work on Interlisp—a stranger situation is difficult to imagine. First, White was apparently so clearly a Easterner in personality that the whole aura of the California lifestyle seems too foreign for him to accept. Second, the rivalry between MacLisp and InterLisp over the years would seem to obviate them working together.

2.12 标准的发展：1984—1992年

2.12 Standards Development: 1984–1992

《Common Lisp：语言》（CLtL1）出版后不久，Lisp 进入了前所未有的流行时期。这种流行很大程度上与人工智能的流行相伴，但不完全如此。让我们看看其中的要素：第一次有了普遍认可的 Lisp 标准，虽然有缺陷；人工智能正在上升，Lisp 是人工智能的语言；工作站市场似乎蓬勃发展，工作站运行 Lisp 的性能已与 Lisp 机器相差不远；风险投资界看到 Sun 等公司的成功，对人工智能前景感到震撼，又因里根任期前半段经济繁荣而拥有大量资金；计算机科学家受到同事在 Sun、Valid 等公司近乎即时成功的激励，成群转为企业家。大众杂志开始刊登 Lisp 文章，对 Common Lisp 的请求不断涌向 CMU 的 Fahlman、斯坦福的 Gabriel 等人；原本只在学术界活动的人，被邀请到产业会议和研讨会上谈论人工智能与 Lisp，并被视为未来趋势的先知。

The period just after the release of Common Lisp: The Language (CLtL1) marked the beginning of an era of unprecedented Lisp popularity. In large part this popularity was coupled with the popularity of AI, but not entirely. Let’s look at the ingredients: For the first time there was a commonly agreed standard for Lisp, albeit a flawed one; AI was on the rise, and Lisp was the language of AI; there appeared to be a burgeoning workstation market, and the performance of the workstations on Lisp was not far offfrom the Lisp machines; the venture capital community was looking at the success of companies like Sun, were awed by the prospects of AI, and had a lot of money as a result of the booming economy in the first half of the Reagan presidency; and computer scientists were turning into entrepreneurs in droves, spurred by the near-instant success of their colleagues at companies like Sun and Valid. Articles about Lisp were being written for popular magazines, requests for Common Lisp were streaming into places like CMU (Fahlman) and Stanford (Gabriel), and otherwise academic-only people were asked to speak at industry conferences and workshops on the topics of AI and Lisp and were regarded as sages of future trends.

产业界对 Lisp 和人工智能产生兴趣的关键动力，是硬件问题似乎已得到控制，接下来要驯服的是软件这头狂暴的野兽。传统方法似乎不足以胜任，而且人们总觉得，新事物、激进事物会比旧的保守事物产生更彻底的影响。这不过是先锋声望的诱惑，却同时吸引了商人和风险投资者。

The key impetus behind the interest by industry in Lisp and AI was that the problems of hardware seemed under control and the raging beast of software was next to be tamed. More traditional methods seemed inadequate, and there was always the feeling that the new thing, the radical thing would have a more thorough effect than the old, conservative thing—this is simply the allure of cachet, and it attracted both businessmen and venture capitalists.

2.12.1 Lisp 公司概览

2.12.1 Overview of Lisp Companies

早在1984年，即 CLtL1 出版之年，就有几家公司成立以商业化 Common Lisp，包括 Franz, Inc.、Gold Hill 和 Lucid, Inc.。其他原本处于 Lisp 边缘的公司，也以 Common Lisp 或正在向它靠拢的 Lisp 加入热潮，包括 Three Rivers（PERQ）和 TI。一些主流计算机制造商进入 Lisp 业务，包括 DEC、HP、Sun、Apollo、Prime 和 IBM。Siemens、Honeywell Bull 等欧洲公司也加入 Common Lisp 行列。老牌参与者，包括 Lisp 机器公司和 Xerox，也开始开发 Common Lisp。来自日本的新参与者京都 Common Lisp（KCL）有些搅局意味：KCL 的编译器把代码编译成 C，再由 C 编译器编译。这种 Lisp 的许可基本免费，Common Lisp 公司突然面对一个出人意料的竞争者。

As early as 1984—the year CLtL1 was published—several companies were founded to commercialize Common Lisp. These included Franz, Inc, Gold Hill, and Lucid, Inc. Other companies on the fringe of Lisp joined the Lisp bandwagon with Common Lisp or with Lisps that were on the road to Common Lisp. These included Three Rivers (PERQ) and TI. Some mainstream computer manufacturers joined in the Lisp business. These included DEC, HP, Sun, Apollo, Prime, and IBM. Some European companies joined the Common Lisp bandwagon, like Siemens and Honeywell Bull. And the old players began work on Common Lisp. These included the Lisp machine companies and Xerox. A new player from Japan—Kyoto Common Lisp (KCL)—provided a bit of a spoiler: KCL has a compiler that compiles to C, which is compiled by the C compiler. This Lisp was licensed essentially free, and the Common Lisp companies suddenly had a surprising competitor.

人们也许认为，免费而质量不错的产品会轻易击败昂贵但质量更好的产品，事实却并非如此；尽管存在零价格竞争者，Common Lisp 公司仍然繁荣。结果证明，更好的性能、更好的质量、开发者跟进标准的承诺，以及更好的服务，比不收钱更重要。

Though one might think a free, good-quality product would easily beat an expensive betterquality product, this proved false, and the Common Lisp companies thrived despite their no-cost competitor. It turned out that better performance, better quality, commitment by developers to moving ahead with the standard, and better service were more important than no price tag.

2.12.2 Common Lisp 公司

2.12.2 Common Lisp Companies

Franz, Inc. 当时已经在销售 Franz Lisp，这是一种类似 MacLisp 的方言，用来移植名为 Vaxsyma 的 Macsyma 版本。在可用的 Common Lisp 出现之前，Franz Lisp 是 VAX 上最流行的 Lisp 方言。Franz 决定进入 Common Lisp 市场，用 Franz Lisp 的销售收入资助开发。主要创始人是 Fritz Kunze、John Foderaro 和 Richard Fateman。Kunze 是加州大学伯克利分校数学系 Fateman 的博士生，离校创办 Franz，证明自己是一位精明务实的商人，能在困难时期凝聚程序员，并在十年末 Lisp 业务衰退时坚持下来。Foderaro 也是 Fateman 的学生，拥有伯克利数学博士学位，是 Franz 各种 Lisp 的主要架构师和实现者。他在公开场合安静而深思熟虑，虽然完全有能力对持续进行的 Common Lisp 工作作出重要贡献，但公开场合的腼腆使他未能发挥主导作用。Fateman 是 MIT 最初的 Macsyma 实现者之一，把 MacLisp/Lisp 的火种带到伯克利，并负责把 Macsyma 移植到 VAX。当时有争议：部分由能源部资助开发的 Macsyma，究竟属于 MIT，还是属于公有领域？Fateman 主张后者，因此组织团队在 VAX 上实现适合支持 Macsyma 的 Lisp，这就是 Franz Lisp。MIT 对他的行为不满。

Franz, Inc., was already in business selling Franz Lisp, the MacLisp-like Lisp dialect used to transport a version of Macsyma called Vaxsyma. Franz Lisp was the most popular dialect of Lisp on the Vax until plausible Common Lisps appeared. Franz decides to go into the Common Lisp market, funding the effort with the proceeds from its Franz Lisp sales. The principal founders of Franz are Fritz Kunze, John Foderaro, and Richard Fateman. Kunze is a PhD student of Fateman’s at the University of California at Berkeley in the mathematics department. Kunze, who left the university to start Franz, proves to be a street-wise businessman who is able to rally his programmers in difficult times and to hang in when the Lisp business fades at the end of the decade. Foderaro is also a former student of Fateman’s with a PhD in mathematics from Berkeley. He is the primary architect and implementor of the various Lisps offered by Franz, Inc. He is a quiet, thoughtful person in public, and though easily capable of making important contributions to the ongoing Common Lisp work, his public shyness prevents him from playing a dominant role. Fateman, one of the original implementors of Macsyma at MIT, carried the MacLisp/Lisp torch to Berkeley, and he was responsible for porting Macsyma to the Vax. There was a controversy about whether Macsyma, developed in part under DOE funding, was the property of MIT or in the public domain. Fateman contended it was in the public domain, and so he brought together a group to put a Lisp on the Vax suitable to support Macsyma—this was Franz Lisp. MIT was displeased at his actions.

Franz 采用直销策略，确定目标客户并直接向他们销售。

Franz adopted a direct-sales strategy, in which the company targeted customers and sold directly to them.

Gold Hill 是 Apiary, Inc. 的一个部门，母公司由 Carl Hewitt 及其学生 Jerry Barber 创办。创办 Apiary/Gold Hill 之前，Barber 在法国 INRIA 待了一年，为 IBM PC 编写了一种类似 MacLisp/Zetalisp 的 Lisp，这项工作部分由 INRIA 资助。他回来时，这种方言已足够接近 Common Lisp，Hewitt 和 Barber 认为，可以借 Common Lisp 的热潮，把现有 Lisp 当作即将成为 Common Lisp 的产品销售。由于 PC 被认为是人工智能的重要机器，这看起来是一份万无一失的商业计划，吸引了许多光鲜的美国东海岸风险投资者。Gold Hill 的 Lisp 并不是 Common Lisp，公司早年因虚假宣传受到批评；但在把它改造成 Common Lisp 的过程中，质量似乎反而下降。同时，“人工智能寒冬”来临，Gold Hill 无法维持原来的规模。风险投资者放弃了它，公司几乎裁掉所有员工，如今仍以两人公司的形式继续经营。

Gold Hill was a division of a parent company named Apiary, Inc., which was founded by Carl Hewitt and his student Jerry Barber. Before founding Apiary/Gold Hill, Barber had spent the year at INRIA in France, where he wrote a MacLisp/Zetalisp-like Lisp for the IBM PC. This work was partly funded by INRIA. When he returned, his dialect was close enough to Common Lisp that Hewitt and Barber thought they could capitalize on the wave of Common interest by selling the existing Lisp as a Lisp about to become Common Lisp. Because the PC was believed to be an important machine for AI, it seemed to be an ironclad business plan, in which a variety of glamorous East-coast venture capitalists invested. Gold Hill’s Lisp was not a Common Lisp and in the early years the company endured some criticism for false advertising, but as the Lisp was transformed into a Common Lisp, its quality apparently dropped. At the same time “AI winter” hit and Gold Hill was not able to survive at the level it once had. It was abandoned by its venture capitalists, laid offjust about all its employees, and continues today as a two-man operation.

“人工智能寒冬”这一说法于1988年首次使用，描述人工智能不幸的商业命运。从1970年代末到1980年代中期，人工智能是计算机产业的重要部分，许多公司依靠当时高科技初创企业可获得的充裕风险资本成立。到1988年，商业分析人士已看清人工智能不会出现爆发式增长，于是商业领域出现了反对人工智能、连带反对 Lisp 的反弹。人工智能公司开始陷入严重财务困难，Lisp 公司也一样。

“AI winter” is the term first used in 1988 to describe the unfortunate commercial fate of AI. From the late 1970’s and until the mid-1980’s, artificial intelligence was an important part of the computer business—many companies were started with the then-abundant venture capital available for high-tech start-ups. By 1988 it became clear to business analysts that AI would not experience meteoric growth, and there was a backlash against AI and, with it, Lisp as a commercial concern. AI companies started to have substantial financial difficulties, and so did the Lisp companies.

最初的 Gold Hill Lisp 是一种采用动态作用域、类似 Lisp-Machine Lisp 的语言，转向 Common Lisp 花了数年。在转换期间，Gold Hill 试图多元化，开发了一个专家系统外壳。它推出时恰逢人工智能寒冬，因此没有像预期那样挽救公司。

The original Gold Hill Lisp was a dynamically scoped Lisp-machine-Lisp-like Lisp. The transition to Common Lisp took a number of years. During the period of switching to Common Lisp, Gold Hill tried to diversify, putting together an expert system shell. This shell was released at about the same time as the AI winter hit, and so it did not rescue the company as it was expected to.

Gold Hill 也采用直销。

Gold Hill sold direct as well.

Lucid, Inc. 由 Gabriel（斯坦福）、Rod Brooks（MIT）、Eric Benson（犹他/PSL）、Scott Fahlman（CMU）及其他几人创办。在风险资本支持下，Lucid 采取了不同于其他 Common Lisp 公司的策略：第一，从零开始编写 Common Lisp 实现；第二，采用 OEM 策略。OEM 路线是与计算机硬件公司达成协议，由后者以自己的品牌推广销售 Lisp，但 Lisp 由外部公司，此处为 Lucid，实现和维护，Lucid 收取许可提成。Lucid 很快与 Sun、Apollo 和 Prime 达成 OEM 协议。之所以能做到，是因为 Lucid 借助创始人的名望，以及从零编写 Common Lisp、因此将成为第一个真正 Common Lisp 的说法。

Lucid, Inc. was founded by Gabriel (Stanford), Rod Brooks (MIT), Eric Benson (Utah/PSL), Scott Fahlman (CMU), and a few others. Backed by venture capital, Lucid adopted a different strategy than the other Common Lisp companies. First, it wrote an implementation of Common Lisp from scratch, and, second, it adopted an OEM strategy. The idea of the OEM route is to make arrangements with computer (hardware) company to market and sell Lisp under its own name. However, the Lisp is implemented and maintained by an outside company, in this case, Lucid, which would collect royalties. Lucid quickly struck OEM deals with Sun, Apollo, and Prime. The reason this was possible was that Lucid traded on the strength of the names of its founders and the fact that it was writing a Common Lisp from scratch and would, therefore, be the first true Common Lisp.

Sun 签约的部分原因，是其一些创始人来自斯坦福，熟悉 Gabriel 及其在 Common Lisp 定义中的作用。

Sun was signed partly because some of the founders of Sun were from Stanford and were familiar with Gabriel and his role in the definition of Common Lisp.

后来 Lucid 移植了自己的 Lisp，并与 IBM、DEC、HP 建立 OEM 关系。虽然每份产品的提成相对较少，OEM 路线使 Lucid 成为通用硬件 Lisp 的主要公司。硬件公司对人工智能和 Lisp 的商机充满热情，因此投入很多，往往支付大笔移植费、固定许可费、维护费以及提成。获得 Sun 这个 OEM 客户是 Lucid 生存的关键，因为 Sun 工作站形成了吸引 Lisp 客户所需的先锋声望。Sun 始终被视为技术前沿，因此对前沿人工智能技术感兴趣的人首先会找 Sun。Sun 还雇用许多工程师进行自己的 Lisp 开发，主要在编程环境方面。

Eventually Lucid ported its Lisp and established OEM arrangements with IBM, DEC, and HP. Though the royalties were relatively small per copy, the OEM route established Lucid as the primary stock-hardware Lisp company. Because the hardware companies were enthusiastic about the business opportunities for AI and Lisp, they invested a lot in the business. Often they would pay a large porting fee, a fixed-price licensing fee, maintenance fees, as well as royalties. Getting Sun as an OEM was the key for Lucid’s survival, because Sun workstations developed the cachet that was needed to attract customers to its Lisp. Sun was always regarded as leading-edge, and so people interested in leading-edge AI technology headed first to Sun. Sun also employed a number of engineers who did their own Lisp development, mostly in the area of programming environments.

人工智能寒冬来临之前，Lucid 开始向其他语言 C/C++ 和编程环境多元化。十年后期，Lucid 试图从 OEM 公司转向直销公司，部分原因是 OEM 公司不再愿意为 Lisp 投入研发和营销资金，因为回报已不如从前，占收入的比例更是明显下降。

Before AI winter hit, Lucid began diversifying into other languages (C/C++) and programming environments. In the latter part of the decade, Lucid began trying to change from an OEM company to a direct sales company, partly because the OEM companies were less interested in spending development and marketing dollars on Lisp when the return was not as high as it once was and certainly not as high a percentage of revenues as before.

2.12.3 其他 Lisp 机器公司

2.12.3 Other Lisp Machine Companies

Three Rivers 制造了第二种 Lisp 机器，性能比上一型号更好，但仍远不及3600，甚至不及较快的基于 MC68020 的 Common Lisp。虽然其 Lisp 已升级为相当完整的 Common Lisp，公司却始终没有成功，并在十年后期倒闭。

Three Rivers made a second Lisp machine, which had better performance than its previous model, but still not nearly as good as the 3600 or even as the faster MC68020-based Common Lisps. Though the Lisp was upgraded to be a fairly complete Common Lisp, this was just never a successful company. In the latter part of the decade it went out of business.

TI 在失败的 Viking 项目之后转为 Lisp 机器公司。TI 雇用了几位优秀人才，包括稍后将在 CLOS 故事中出现的 Patrick Dussud。虽然 TI 与 LMI 有商业协议，Dussud 却与 Symbolics 的 Dave Moon 建立了密切工作关系。通过这种关系，两家公司都改善了 Lisp 软件性能，因此到十年的第三个四分之一阶段，Symbolics 和 TI 成为主要 Lisp 机器供应商。

TI switched to being a Lisp machine company after the ill-fated Viking project. TI hired a couple of good people, including Patrick Dussud (who will appear later in the CLOS story). He developed a close working relationship with Dave Moon, of Symbolics, even though TI had a business arrangement with LMI. Through this relationship, both companies were able to improve the performance of its Lisp software, so that Symbolics and TI were the primary Lisp machine vendors by the third quarter of the decade.

由于与 LMI 的协议，TI 在 Explorer 完成前就能在 LMI 硬件上开发自己的微码。TI 有权使用 LMI 软件，却选择 MIT 软件。LMI 有权转售 Explorer，却没有这样做。

Because of the arrangement with LMI, TI was able to develop its own microcode on LMI hardware before the Explorer was completed. TI had the right to use LMI software, but chose to use the MIT software instead. LMI had the right to remarket the Explorer, but chose not to.

TI 制造了 Explorer I、Explorer II 和 Explorer II+ 三种型号，还根据 DARPA 合同制作了一枚复杂的 Lisp 芯片。Explorer II 和 II+ 都使用这枚芯片。

TI made three models, the Explorer I, the Explorer II, and the Explorer II+. They also made an elaborate Lisp chip under contract to DARPA. The Explorer II and II+ both used this chip.

TI 还制造了名为 Micro Explorer 的板级 Lisp 机器，同样使用这枚 Lisp 芯片。

TI also made a board-level Lisp machine called the Micro Explorer. This machine also used the Lisp chip.

2.12.4 拥有自家 Lisp 的大公司

2.12.4 Big Companies With Their Own Lisps

DEC 和 HP 实现了自己的 Lisp。DEC 从 Spice Lisp 代码起步，HP 从 PSL 起步。两家公司都相信人工智能会起飞，而拥有 Lisp 是在人工智能业务中成功的必要条件。它们通过雇用参与过原实现的学生，以及安排持续咨询，与原实现者建立关系。按其他 Lisp 公司的标准看，DEC 和 HP 的 Lisp 团队都发展出了相当大的业务。1980年代最后四分之一阶段 Lisp 达到顶峰时，按收入衡量，主要参与者是 Symbolics、TI、DEC、HP、Sun 和 Lucid。

DEC and HP implemented their own Lisps. DEC started with the Spice Lisp code and HP with PSL. Each company believed that AI would take offand that having a Lisp was an essential ingredient for success in the AI business. Both DEC and HP made arrangements with the original implementors of those Lisps, both by hiring students who had worked on them and by arranging for on-going consulting. Both DEC and HP grew fairly large businesses out of these Lisp groups, large by the standards of other Lisp companies. At the peak of Lisp in the last quarter of the 1980’s, the main players in the Lisp business by revenue were Symbolics, TI, DEC, HP, Sun, and Lucid.

DEC 和 HP 在 Lisp 产品上投入了大量精力，主要在环境方面，也包括 Lisp 系统本身的性能。

DEC and HP put a lot of effort into their Lisp offerings, primarily in the area of environments but also in the performance of the Lisp system itself.

1980年代最后四分之一阶段，HP 意识到 PSL 不会获胜，需要提供 Common Lisp。他们选择 Lucid 供应，缩减自己的工程团队，转而更重视营销。作出决定时人工智能寒冬几乎已经到来，因此不清楚是否正是对形势的判断迫使他们削减 Lisp 投资。

In the last quarter of the 1980’s, HP realized that PSL was not the winner and they needed to provide a Common Lisp. They chose Lucid to provide it, and they reduced their own engineering staff, choosing to focus more on marketing. Since the AI winter was just about upon them when they made the decision, it is not clear whether their perception of this situation forced them to cut back on their Lisp investment.

1990年代初，在人工智能寒冬中，DEC 也决定放弃自家开发，并同样选择 Lucid。

In the early 1990’s, in the midst of AI winter, DEC also decided to abandon its own efforts and also chose Lucid.

2.12.5 IBM

2.12.5 IBM

IBM 有几个适合 Lisp 的平台：PC、大型机和 RT。最初，IBM 决定只在 RT 上提供 Common Lisp。IBM 资助了一个把 Spice Lisp（Common Lisp）移植到 RT 的试点项目，RT 原计划成为 IBM 首次真正进入工作站市场的产品。Spice Lisp 负责人 Fahlman 也是 Lucid 创始人，借助这一关系，IBM 最终与 Lucid 签约，把其 Lisp 移植到 RT。必须完成移植时，RT 尚无原生窗口系统，虽然后来有了；合同却要求 Lucid 交付完整环境。Lucid 为此投入大量精力，包括编写支撑高层 Lisp 窗口系统的底层窗口系统，但 RT 在商业上失败，主要因为性能太差。

IBM had a number of platforms suitable for Lisp: the PC, the mainframe, and the RT. Of these, IBM initially decided to put a Common Lisp only on the RT. IBM funded a pilot program to put Spice Lisp (Common Lisp) on the RT, which was to be IBM’s first real entry into the workstation market. Fahlman, chief honcho of Spice Lisp, was also a founder of Lucid, and thanks to this connection a contract was eventually written for Lucid to port its Lisp to the RT for IBM. The RT did not have a native window system at the time the port had to be done, though later they did, and the contract required Lucid to deliver a complete environment. A lot of effort was put into providing this environment, along with the low-level window system on which the higher-level Lisp window system was written, but the RT was a commercial flop, primarily because it had such poor performance.

后来 IBM 以性能良好的 RS6000 再次进入工作站市场，Lucid 为它提供 Common Lisp。IBM 最终还与 Lucid 签约，在运行 Unix 版本 AIX 的370和 PS-2 上提供同一种 Lisp。虽然 IBM 统一选择一家 Lisp 供应商看起来合理，但实际上，每个硬件系列都由不同业务部门负责，彼此毫无沟通。因此，某部门选择某供应商虽然有参考意义，却不具有决定性。每个平台的谈判都像是在与不同公司谈判。

Later, IBM reentered the workstation market with its RS6000, which has good performance, and Lucid did the Common Lisp for it. IBM eventually contracted with Lucid to provide the same Lisp on the 370 and PS-2 running AIX, a version of Unix. Though it would seem to make sense that IBM would choose to work with just one company for its Lisp systems, the situation was that different business units were responsible for each hardware family mentioned, and there was no communication within IBM between any of them. Therefore, it was interesting and relevant that one business unit selected one particular vendor, but not compelling. The negotiations for each of these platforms was as if with different companies.

2.12.6 Xerox

2.12.6 Xerox

Xerox 在 Interlisp 之上制作了 Common Lisp 兼容包，但它从未取得真正显著的成功。1980年代末，Xerox 退出 Lisp 业务，并在1989年把 Lisp 软件许可给自己扶植的衍生公司 Envos。

Xerox produced a Common Lisp compatibility package on top of Interlisp. This package was never really a strong success for Xerox, who in the late 1980’s got out of the Lisp business, licensing its Lisp software to a spinoffstarted by Xerox called Envos in 1989.

Envos 推出了真正的 Common Lisp 实现，并销售 Interlisp-D 环境，以及 Notecards、Rooms 等其他 Xerox 软件。Envos 由 Xerox 资助，但成立三年后倒闭。其残余部分成为 Venue 公司，基本上获准继续销售相同软件，却没有直接资金支持，而是通过为 Xerox 的 Lisp 客户提供维护服务获得资金。

Envos put out a real Common Lisp implementation and sold the InterLisp-D environment, plus some other Xerox software such as Notecards and Rooms. Envos was funded by Xerox, but went out of business three years after it was founded. What was left of Envos became the company Venue, which essentially was granted the rights to continue marketing that same software, but without direct funding. The funding was provided by servicing Xerox’s Lisp customer base (maintenance).

2.12.7 京都 Common Lisp

2.12.7 Kyoto Common Lisp

最后一个主要参与者是京都 Common Lisp（KCL）。它由当时还是学生的 Yuasa 和 Hagiya（???）在京都大学开发。他们不属于 Common Lisp 社区，也不属于美国 Lisp 社区，实现依据的是对 CLtL1 的全新阅读。虽然对书中若干内容有有趣的误读，结果却是一个非常忠实的 Common Lisp。

The last major player is Kyoto Common Lisp (KCL). This Lisp was developed at Kyoto University by the then-students Yuasa and Hagiya (???). These students were not part of the Common Lisp community, nor of the US Lisp community, and their implementation was based on a fresh reading of CLtL1. Though they made several interesting misreadings of the book, the result was a very faithful Common Lisp.

此外，他们采用了美国没有使用的实现技术：把 Lisp 代码编译为 C 代码，再交给 C 编译器。KCL 提供支持垃圾回收、输入输出等功能的运行时，但移植到新机器只需几天到几周，取决于该机器的 C 编译器对 K&R C 的忠实程度，以及操作系统相关部分的移植难度。

Furthermore, they used an implementation technique not used in the US: they compiled Lisp code into C code, which was passed through the C compiler. KCL provided a runtime that supported garbage collection, IO, and the like, but porting this Lisp to a new machine took only a few days up to a few weeks, depending on how faithful the C compiler for that machine was to K&R C and how difficult the operating system dependencies were to port.

KCL 在美国免费授权，一家美国 Common Lisp 公司，加利福尼亚的 Ibuki，基本上只是重新包装并提供支持，不过也做一些新开发。

KCL was licensed for free in the US, and one US Common Lisp company—Ibuki in California— basically repackages it and supports it, though they provide some new development.

美国主要 Lisp 实现者从未认真想到，Lisp，乃至 Common Lisp，能这样有效地实现。他们认为编译质量不可能很好，因为执行低层代码生成的编译器看不到原始 Lisp 构造，只能看到自动生成的 C 语句序列。而且，双重编译被认为必然损害编译速度。虽然这种编译方式较慢，但与 Lucid、Franz 等公司普遍缓慢的编译器相比，并没有差多少。

That Lisp, and Common Lisp, could be effectively implemented like this had never seriously occurred to any major US Lisp implementor. It was thought that compilation could not be as good because the compiler that did the low-level code generation would not have the original Lisp construct available, only an automatically generated sequence of C statements. Furthermore, compilation speed was thought need to suffer, because of double compilation. Though sluggish, this mode of compilation is not much worse than the generally sluggish compilers produced by Lucid, Franz, and the others.

因此，这种实现技术不仅没有严重缺点，而且移植优势显著。不过，到1990年代初，KCL 主要仍用于重视零成本的大学，而且它被提升到 CLtL2 或未来 ANSI Common Lisp 语言水平的可能性不大。

So, not only did the implementation technique not have any serious drawbacks, but the porting benefits were significant. Nevertheless, KCL is, in the early 1990’s, used primarily in Universities, where zero cost is important, and there is not much chance that KCL will be brought up to the language level of CLtL2 or ANSI Common Lisp, when it appears.

2.12.8 对性能的执着

2.12.8 Obsession with Performance

1981年，Larry Masinter 和 Dick Gabriel 开始研究如何评估 Lisp 系统的性能。这项工作旨在回应用户开始进行的各种随意基准测试。用户关心这些测试，是想知道哪种实现，甚至哪种方言，性能更好。有时测试者想证明某种方言优于其他方言。但由于 Lisp 语言复杂，实现又涉及许多因素，很容易变成拿苹果与橙子相比。

In 1981, Larry Masinter and Dick Gabriel begin a study of how to evaluate the performance of a Lisp system. This exercise was done to address the variety of casual benchmarks that users had begun to do. Users are interested in such benchmarks because they want to find out which implementation—and perhaps which dialect—has superior performance. Sometimes benchmarkers wish to prove that one dialect is superior to others. But, because the Lisp language is so complex and Lisp implementation so involved, it is easy to end up comparing apples and oranges.

其中一个问题是，Lisp 在统一的语言层面上同时处理原语和抽象。例如，大多数 Lisp 手册以相似方式描述 SETQ 和 APPEND，使人有理由推断两者都耗费单位时间，通常只需一条机器指令。然而，APPEND 是复杂操作，需要从列表开头线性扫描到末尾。不熟悉 Lisp 实现和表示的用户很容易困惑。

Among other things, Lisp treats at a uniform linguistic level both primitives and abstractions. For example, in most Lisp manuals, SETQ and APPEND are described in similar ways, so that it is reasonable to infer that SETQ and APPEND both take unit time, and usually just one machine instruction. However, APPEND is a complicated operation that involves finding the end of a list by linearly scanning it from the beginning. Users not familiar with Lisp implementation and representation will be easily confused.

此外，实现者几乎总能作出各种权衡：用空间换时间，用一类操作的时间换另一类操作的时间，用最坏情况时间换平均情况或最好情况时间，等等。

Furthermore, there are almost always a variety of tradeoffs an implementor can make, trading space for time, time of one category of operation for time of another, worst-case time for averagecase time for best-case time, and so on.

Masinter 和 Gabriel 试图解释他们能想到的所有因素，并在1982年 ACM Lisp 与函数式编程研讨会上发表了这篇论文 [Gabriel, 1982]。

Masinter and Gabriel tried to explain all of the factors they could think of, and they presented this paper at the 1982 ACM Symposium on Lisp and Functional Programming [Gabriel, 1982].

基于这篇论文，Gabriel 获得 ARPA 合同，定义并收集一组基准程序，比较尽可能多的现有实现，并分析测试结果说明了什么。工作于1984年完成，最终出版成书；所谓 Gabriel 基准 [Gabriel, 1985] 成为 Lisp 性能的定义，其社会作用可与 Jack Dongarra 用 LINPACK 衡量超级计算机性能相提并论 [?]。

Based on this paper, Gabriel obtained an ARPA contract to define and gather a series of benchmarks and to compare as many existing implementations as possible, along with an analysis of what the benchmarks showed. The work was completed in 1984, culminating in the publication of the results in a book; the so-called Gabriel benchmarks [Gabriel, 1985] emerge as the definition of Lisp performance, comparable in social function to Jack Dongarra’s LINPACK measurements of supercomputer performance [?].

随着 Common Lisp 和商业 Lisp 实现出现，用户对性能的执着在 Gabriel 基准中找到了方便的出口：它们提供了看似客观的性能度量，因此潜在客户坚持要看这些测试的结果。

With the emergence of Common Lisp and commercial Lisp implementations, the users’ obsession with performance found a convenient outlet in the Gabriel benchmarks—they provided an apparently objective measure of performance, and so prospective customers insisted on seeing the results of the Gabriels.

在贸易展会上，有些公司拒绝展示 Gabriel 性能数字，担心竞争对手利用这些数据。

At trade shows, some companies refused to show their Gabriel performance numbers for fear the competition would take advantage of them.

Gabriel 创办 Lisp 公司，并把性能作为核心销售武器后，局面变得更复杂、更具对抗性。由于基准由 Gabriel 开发，有些人认为这种策略不道德。Franz 把这些基准称为“斯坦福性能评估基准”，而不采用具有刺激性的“Gabriel 基准”名称。

The situation became more complex and confrontational when Gabriel started a Lisp company, which used performance as a central sales weapon. And because the Gabriel benchmarks were developed by Gabriel, some regarded the tactic as unethical. Franz called the benchmarks the “Stanford Performance Evaluation Benchmarks” instead of the inflammatory “Gabriel Benchmarks.”

2.12.9 日本

2.12.9 Japan

这一时期，日本开始重视 Lisp 的使用，一些公司尽管看到通产省明显支持 Prolog，仍资助 Lisp 工作。推动因素一部分是京都 Common Lisp 开始在日本扎根，另一部分是曾在美国读研究生时学习 Lisp 的学生，后来成为日本主要大学的资深教授或日本公司的高级管理者。

During this period Japan awakened to the use of Lisp, and some companies sponsored Lisp work despite the apparent endorsement of Prolog by MITI. In part this was spurred by Kyoto Common Lisp, which began to take hold in Japan. In part, also, this was spurred by former students who learned Lisp in the US as graduate students, students who became senior professors in major Japanese universities or who became senior managers in Japanese companies.

特别值得关注的是 Lisp 与逻辑编程或面向对象编程的各种融合。最好的例子是 Tao [Okuno, 1984]，它融合了三者。NTT（日本电信电话公司）设计制造了名为 Elis 的机器，用来运行 Lisp 和 Tao。这种机器在日本和其他地方都没有流行起来。

Of particular interest are the various blendings of Lisp with logic programming or objectoriented programming. The best example of this was Tao [Okuno, 1984], which is a blending of all three influences. A machine, called Elis, was designed and built by NTT (Nippon Telephone & Telegraph) to run Lisp and Tao. This machine never caught on in Japan or anywhere else.

日本大多数大型计算机公司都实现了 Common Lisp，或从头开始，或以其他 Lisp 为基础，或以 KCL 为基础。ISO 小组启动时，日本已有几个对 Lisp 感兴趣的国家标准组织。其一是 JIS（原文称 Japan Information Systems），相当于美国的 ANSI。JIS 的语言委员会由资深教授主导，Lisp 委员会由 McCarthy 的学生 Takayasu（???）Ito 教授领导，从学术角度关注 Lisp，对 Common Lisp 的重视程度不如 JEIDA。

Most of the large Japanese computer companies implemented Common Lisp either from scratch, from the base of another Lisp, or from KCL. By the time the ISO group started, there were several national standards groups in Japan interested in Lisp. One was JIS (Japan Information Systems), which corresponds to ANSI in the US. Language committees in JIS are dominated by senior professors, and the JIS Lisp committee—headed by Professor Takayasu (???) Ito, a student of McCarthy’s—was interested in Lisp from an academic point of view and did not take Common Lisp as seriously as JEIDA.

JEIDA 是一个松散的企业协会，也由一位教授凝聚起来，但这位教授是年轻的 CLtL1 译者 Masayuki Ida。Ida 所在大学比 Ito 所在大学更小、声望更低，后者是某所国立大学的资深教授；但 Ida 关心 Lisp 及其使用者的商业成功，因此得到产业界尊重。相比之下，Ito 关心适合建立数学理论的优雅语义，而不是语言的实际使用。

JEIDA is a loose association of companies, again unified by a professor, but in this case the professor is the young translator of CLtL1, Masayuki Ida. Ida is from a smaller, less prestigious university than Ito, who is a senior professor at one of the national universities, but Ida was the respect of industry groups because he is concerned with the commercial success of Lisp and those who use it. Ito, on the other hand, is concerned with elegant semantics suitable for building mathematical theories rather than the practical use of the language.

在后面的标准化故事中，我们还会看到这两位人物。

We will see more of these two later in the story of standardization.

2.12.10 DARPA 与 SAIL 邮件列表

2.12.10 DARPA and the SAIL Mailing Lists

1984年 CLtL1 出版后不久，ARPA 改名为 DARPA，即国防高级研究计划局。大约此时，Steve Squires 从 NSA 加入 DARPA，带来一种热情和对政治的高度重视，这在当时所谓“局里”已不多见。他像网中央的蜘蛛，手指感受每根丝线的颤动，推动所有接触网络的人朝他的方向前进。他看到 Common Lisp 完全可以被称为 DARPA 在那个十年中的耀眼成功之一，便迅速巩固这一按事实来看可能相当勉强的说法。也就是说，尽管 DARPA 到此时只发挥了次要作用，他开始采取行动扩大其角色，争取优势。

Right after CLtL1 was published in 1984, ARPA was renamed DARPA, standing for Defense Advanced Research Projects Agency. Around this time, Steve Squires joined DARPA from NSA, and he brought a sort of enthusiasm and a high regard for politics not recently seen at the Office, as it was called. He was like the spider at the center of the web, his fingers feeling every twitch of a strand and urging his direction on all who touched on it. Seeing that Common Lisp could be legitimately called one of the shining successes of DARPA of that decade, he was quick to solidify what could be, based on the facts, a rather tenuous claim. That is, though DARPA played a minor role up to this point, he started actions to increase DARPA’s role and to try to press the advantage.

第一项行动是在加利福尼亚蒙特雷的海军研究生院组织一次 CLtL 出版后的社区会议。虽然那里是 DARPA 经常开会的地点之一，但对通常不怎么出远门的 Common Lisp 小组来说，多少有些异国情调。虽然小组已有自然形成的领袖，DARPA 仍选择自己惯用的一些负责人组织各场会议。会议于1984年末举行。

The first action was to organize a post-CLtL community meeting at the Naval Post-graduate School at Monterey, California. Though the Post-graduate School was one of the regular places DARPA held meetings, it was somewhat of an exotic location for the generally homebound Common Lisp Group. And though there were natural leaders in the Common Lisp Group, DARPA chose some of its regular leaders to organize the sessions. The meeting was held in late 1984.

主要问题是，如何最好地把标准扩展到大家都知道确属 Lisp、却尚未标准化的领域，包括多程序、窗口、错误处理、面向对象编程、子集、图形、外部函数调用、迭代、黄页、蓝页和环境。会议的结果，除了让 Common Lisp 小组困惑 DARPA 究竟在做什么之外，是建立一组邮件列表，每个兴趣领域一个。Gabriel 负责原有列表，所依据的理论是：干杂活的人最终会成为头领；因此他自愿维护这些列表。[?]

The main question regarded how to best extend the standard into areas that everyone knew was really part of Lisp but which were not heretofore standardized. These included: multi-programming, windows, error handling, object-oriented programming, subsets, graphics, foreign function calling, iteration, the yellow pages, the blue pages, and environments. The result of the meeting—besides confusion among the Common Lisp Group about what DARPA was up to—was a set of mailing lists, one for each area of interest. Since Gabriel administered the original lists—on the theory that the person who does the dog work eventually becomes top dog—he volunteered to maintain these lists. [?]

随后一年，这些列表几乎没有活动，因为占参与者大多数的 Lisp 实现者忙于实现 Common Lisp，或努力让自己的公司运转。

The following year saw little activity on these lists, because Lisp implementors, who make up the bulk of the participants, are busy implementing Common Lisp or trying to make their companies go.

2.12.11 X3J13 的开端

2.12.11 The Start of X3J13

一年后的后续会议于1985年12月在马萨诸塞州波士顿举行。一场看似无害的技术会议，被一项震惊的宣布打断。技术议程涉及三个主要问题：面向对象编程、窗口和用户需求。面向对象部分主要介绍 CommonLoops、Object Lisp、HP 面向对象编程 HPOOPS（后来称为 Common Objects）和 New Flavors。窗口部分介绍 Lucid 源于 RT 的窗口系统，以及 Intellicorp 制作的 Common Windows。用户关切部分只是讨论：为什么用户，即人工智能公司，需要的不只是 Lisp，尤其需要面向对象编程和窗口。

In a follow-up meeting one year later—December 1985 in Boston, Massachusetts—an apparently benign technical meeting was interrupted by a shocking announcement. The technical agenda regarded three main issues: object-oriented programming, windows, and the needs of users. The object-oriented programming part of the agenda was dominated by presentations on CommonLoops, Object Lisp, HP Object-oriented programming (HPOOPS, later called Common Objects), and New Flavors. The window part of the agenda was a presentation of Lucid’s RT-originated window system and Common Windows, a window system produced by Intellicorp. The user-concerns part of the meeting was just a discussion of why the users—AI companies—need more than Lisp, particularly object-oriented programming and windows.

重磅消息是，Steve Squires 介绍 Bob Mathis 来谈为 Common Lisp 成立 ANSI 标准小组的必要性。大家听到的是：有人正筹划成立 ISO 的 Lisp 标准小组，而美国唯一能作出的回应，是建立一个能以 ANSI 名义派代表的 Lisp 标准小组。小组被告知，一直有传言说欧洲人不喜欢 Common Lisp，想制定一个非 Common Lisp 标准。这项工作由法国和英国牵头，美国不愿重演 APL 的局面，让“法国佬”拿到召集权。

The bombshell was that Steve Squires introduces Bob Mathis to talk about the need to establish an ANSI standards group for Common Lisp. The news heard was this: There was a plot afoot to start an ISO standards group for Lisp, and the only way that the US could respond was to have a Lisp standards group which sends a representative in the name of ANSI. There were persistent rumblings, the Group was told, that the Europeans dis not like Common Lisp and wished to create a non-Common-Lisp standard. This effort was being lead by the French and British, and the US wanted to avoid the APL by letting the “frogs” get the convenorship.

这句失言尤其有害，因为坐在会场后排的正是 EuLisp 工作负责人 Jérôme Chailloux。他身边还有法国面向对象编程的主要人物 Pierre Cointe，以及其他几位欧洲人。

This slip of the tongue was especially damaging, because sitting in the back of the room was Jerome Chailloux, the leader of the EuLisp effort. Sitting with him are Pierre Cointe, the leading French object-oriented programming guy, and several other Europeans.

DARPA 派 Bob Mathis 来挽救局面，阻止 ISO 最初的推进。他建议成立一个由自己领导的研究小组，判断 Lisp 是否适合标准化，一年后报告。这给美国一年时间成立自己的标准委员会并谋划策略。

Bob Mathis was sent by DARPA to save the day, by heading offthe initial ISO thrust. He did this by recommending a study group that Mathis would head to determine whether Lisp was suitable for standardization. This group was to report in one year. This gave the US a year to start its standards committee and plot its strategy.

Mathis 是 ISO Ada 工作组的召集人，有推动标准流程的经验。Squires 请他帮助初生的 Common Lisp 小组。小组震惊之余，不知道如何回应，只能怀疑威胁的严重性，甚至怀疑威胁是否存在。不过，队伍似乎已经出发，这次会议剩下能做的，只是安排后来成为 X3J13 的首次组织会议，由神秘的 Bob Mathis 掌舵。

Mathis was the convenor for the ISO ADA working group, so he has experience shepherding standards through the process. He was asked by Squires to help the fledgling Common Lisp Group. Stunned, the Group was not sure how to respond, except to wonder at the severity—nay, the existence—of the threat. Nevertheless, it appears the wagon train is leaving, and all that was left to do at this meeting is to schedule the first organizational meeting for what would become X3J13, with the mysterious Bob Mathis at the helm.

Mathis 是老练的政治人物，他争取 Fahlman、Steele 和 Gabriel 帮忙动员大家。

A consummate politician, Mathis enlisted the aid of Fahlman, Steele, and Gabriel in rallying the troops.

DARPA 声称担心欧洲工作的理由是：有一项法律规定，如果某语言有 ISO 标准，美国就必须在国防相关工作中使用它。当然，可以通过例外等许多办法绕过这项法律，但小组被告知，这些机制未必总有效，而且 DARPA 将来未必总有这样一批对 Common Lisp 友好的项目主管。

DARPA’s stated reason for fearing the European effort was that there was a law that says that if there is an ISO standard for a language, the US has to use it in defense-related work. Of course, there are many ways to circumvent this law with exceptions and such things, but possibly, the group was told, these mechanisms would not always work, and perhaps there may not be such a Common-Lisp-friendly set of program managers at DARPA.

最终选择的是 X3 路线，而非 IEEE。X3 是 CBEMA 组织内部一组与计算机和信息处理有关的标准委员会。X3 下属委员会获授权向 ANSI 提交标准草案，随后由 ANSI 执行公开审查并出版标准。这种安排是为了避免技术专家被公司起诉；此前一些使用 COBOL 的公司曾起诉个别技术专家，指责他们制定的 COBOL 标准需要高昂适配成本，危及公司。ANSI 担心，如果专家会因标准工作以个人身份被起诉，就很难找到人来做这项工作。［以下文字非常粗略，尚待充实。］

The route chosen was the X3 route, rather than IEEE. X3 is a set of standards committees having to do with computers and information processing, all within the organization called CBEMA. Committees under X3 are authorized to submit proposed standards to ANSI, which then executes the public review process and publishes the standard. This arrangement is to prevent the technical experts from being sued by companies, as happened when some companies that used COBOL sued the individual technical experts for jeopardizing their companies by creating a COBOL standard that would cost a lot of most to adapt to. ANSI feared it would be hard to find technical experts to do the standards work if they could be sued as individuals for their efforts. [The following text is very sketchy and needs to be filled out.]

• 几个月后，在华盛顿的 SPARC 举行会议，启动 X3 活动。会议讨论标准化目标，最大的议题由 DARPA 的 Squires 和 Scherlis 推动：是否与 Scheme 的工作合并，技术问题围绕宏和 Lisp-1 展开。还讨论了新 X3 小组，即未来 X3J13 的目标。Bobrow 和 Gabriel 说了很多，但这次会议的具体性质不太容易回忆。

• A few months later there is a meeting at SPARC in DC to start offthe X3 activities. At this meeting the goals of standardization are discussed, and the biggest topic, pushed by DARPA in the names of Squires and Scherlis, is whether to merge with the Scheme activities—the technical issues surround macros and being a 1-Lisp. Also the goals of the new X3 group (to be X3J13) were discussed. Bobrow and Gabriel did a lot of the talking, but the nature of this meeting is somehow hard to remember.

• Gabriel 进行了一次愚勇的尝试，接触 Scheme 社区讨论合并，但对方很快退缩。Clinger 是少数同情这一想法的人之一，但他与 Gabriel 联手也不够。大约此时，Scheme 社区开始否认 Scheme 是 Lisp 方言，声称它与 Algol 的联系更密切。

• In a stupidly valiant effort, Gabriel tries to approach the Scheme community about merging, but they back away real fast. Clinger is one of the few who is sympathetic to the idea, but the combination of him and Gabriel is not enough. About this time, the Scheme community starts denying that Scheme is a dialect of Lisp, claiming closer bonds to Algol.

• Gabriel 和 Pitman 在 Clinger、Sussman、Abelson 协助下，写了一份报告，详细讨论宏和 Lisp-1 的技术问题。Kohlbecker 的学位论文 [Kohlbecker, 1986b] 带来希望，报告考虑了若干替代方案，并发表于 LASC [Gabriel, 1988]。合并的想法就此消亡。

• Gabriel and Pitman, with the aid of Clinger and Sussman/Abelson, produce a report detailing the technical issues involved in macros and 1-lisps. There is a thesis by Kohlbecker [Kohlbecker, 1986b] that offers hope, and several alternatives are considered in the paper. This paper is published in LASC [Gabriel, 1988]. The idea for merging dies.

• 1986年夏，L&FP 在 MIT 举行，欧洲与美国 Lisp 社区首次正面交锋。EuLisp 委员会发表一篇 unCommon Lisp 论文，并提出分层 Lisp 定义方案 [Padget, 1986]。在会议设施楼下的一间房里举行的会面中，双方明显互不信任。美国方面表示，成立 X3J13 只是因为担心欧洲人通过成立 ISO 委员会击沉 Common Lisp；欧洲方面则说，成立 ISO 委员会是为了阻止 X3J13 击沉他们的 Lisp 工作。Mathis 一直试图在 ISO 层面阻止小组成立，但事情即将摊牌。

• In summer 1986, L&FP is held at MIT. At this meeting is the first time the European Lisp community and the US Lisp community square off. The EuLisp committee presents an unCommon Lisp paper along with a proposal for a layered Lisp definition [Padget, 1986]. There is a meeting in a downstairs room of the conference facility, where it is clear that neither group trusts the other. The US presents the view that they started X3J13 only because of fear that the Europeans would try to sink Common Lisp by starting an ISO committee, and the Europeans claim they are trying to start an ISO committee to stop X3J13 from sinking their Lisp efforts. Mathis has been trying to fight offan ISO group at that level, but it is about to come to a head.

• 1986年11月，Gabriel 参加 EuLisp 会议。EuLisp 显然是 Le Lisp 的后继，但它是一项欧洲合作，旨在定义一种从小到大各种机器都能运行、具备面向对象设施和模块等功能的分层语言，目标之一是大体兼容 Common Lisp。Chailloux 的立场似乎是尝试搭建通往 Common Lisp 的桥梁。他以观察员身份参加几次 X3J13 会议，并试图与 Gabriel 一起促成妥协。

• In November 1986 Gabriel attends a EuLisp meeting. EuLisp is most clearly a successor to Le Lisp, but it is a European effort to define a layered language that will run on a variety of small to large machines, with object-oriented facilities, modules, and the like. It is a goal to be largely compatible with Common Lisp. Chailloux’s position appears to be to try to build a bridge to Common Lisp. He attends several X3J13 meetings as an observer, and tries to work with Gabriel on forging a compromise.

• X3J13 成立若干工作组，提出大型方案，主题包括迭代、编译、编辑和面向对象编程。DEC 的 Kathy Chapman 被任命为编辑。DEC 选了一个不合适的时机，承诺提供两年的编辑人员。

• X3J13 starts some working groups to make some large proposals. These are: Iteration, compilation, editorial, and object-oriented programming. Kathy Chapman of DEC is named editor. DEC has picked the wrong time to provide an editor for 2 years.

• 四个团队竞争定义 Common Lisp 的面向对象部分：Symbolics 的 New Flavors [Symbolics, 1985]、Xerox 的 CommonLoops [Bobrow, 1986]、LMI 的 Object Lisp [Drescher, 1987]，以及 Common Objects [Kempf, 1987]。经过六个月争斗，以下人员被要求依据 CommonLoops 并吸收少量 New Flavors 编写 CLOS 标准：Moon（Symbolics）、Bobrow（Xerox）、Kiczales（Xerox）、Sonya Keene（Symbolics，撰稿人）、Linda DeMichiel（Lucid）和 Gabriel（Lucid）。最后两人最终编写了规范，但加入小组本来是为了代表中立影响。还有一些知情者非正式参与：Patrick Dussud（TI）、Jim Kempf（Sun）和 Jon L White（Lucid）。

• Four groups vie for defining the object-oriented programming part of Common Lisp: New Flavors (Symbolics) [Symbolics, 1985], CommonLoops (Xerox) [Bobrow, 1986], Object Lisp (LMI) [Drescher, 1987], and Common Objects [Kempf, 1987]. After a 6-month battle the following group is told to go write the standard for CLOS based on CommonLoops and a little New Flavors: Moon (Symbolics), Bobrow (Xerox), Kiczales (Xerox), Sonya Keene (Symbolics, a writer), Linda DeMichiel (Lucid), and Gabriel (Lucid). The last two end up writing the specification, but are in the group to represent a neutral influence. Also, there is a group of informed people who are informally in the group: Patrick Dussud (TI), Jim Kempf (Sun), and Jon L White (Lucid).

• ISO 要求投票成立 WG16 工作组，X3J13 投了赞成票。经过政治运作，法国获得召集权，美国担任编辑。在 X3J13，Mathis 当选主席，Steele 任副主席，Gabriel 任国际代表。Gabriel 和 Clinger 被任命为 ISO Lisp 的美国编辑。首次会议于1987年2月在巴黎举行，之前有一个讨论各种 Lisp 主题的小型研讨会。美国派出大型代表团，有些人由私人公司出资；DARPA 支付 Mathis、Gabriel 和 Clinger 的费用。McCarthy 也出席，以表明美国的认真态度；另有 Masinter、Gregor Kiczales 和 Kent Pitman。美国代表团得到指示，不允许发生任何危及 Common Lisp 在美国地位的事情。整整一天用于讨论名称 ISLISP 和目标，目标包括清晰、简洁、一致、紧凑等（??? 需核对会议记录）。会议决定从 Common Lisp 出发，但删去若干内容，这是法国方案。美国指出，委员会只能标准化已有事物，而欧洲人似乎乐于在委员会里设计新东西。Scheme 被否决为基础，尽管美国给 Scheme 和 Common Lisp 各投半票。日本似乎与美国一致，加拿大也是。欧洲各国团结一致。Christian Queinnec 担任召集人，但有些软弱。BSI 试图通过程序性细节阻止 X3J13，却被否决。

• ISO calls for a vote to start a work group (WG16), and X3J13 votes in favor. Through political maneuvering, France is the convenor and the US is the editor. In X3J13, Mathis is voted chairman, Steele vice chairman, Gabriel in international representative. Gabriel and Clinger are named the US editors for ISO Lisp. The first meeting is in Paris in February 1987. It is preceded by a little workshop on various Lisp topics. The US sends a large delegation, some on the nickel of private companies. DARPA pays for Mathis, Gabriel, and Clinger. Also there are McCarthy—to lend credence to the US seriousness—Masinter, Gregor Kiczales, and Kent Pitman. The US is given instructions to let nothing happen that jeopardizes Common Lisp in the US. A full day is taken up on the name (ISLISP) and goals. The goals include such things as clarity, conciseness, consistency, compactness. (??? Check these in the minutes). It is decided to start with Common Lisp, but to throw out various things. This is the French proposal. The US points out that this committee can only standardize existing things, while the Europeans seem happy to design something in the committee. Scheme is rejected as the basis, though the US casts 1/2 vote for Scheme and 1/2 vote for Common Lisp. Japan seems to vote with US, as does Canada. The Europeans are united. Christian Queinnec is the convenor, and he is a little weak. BSI tries to stop X3J13 through a technicality, but they are rejected.

• X3J13 早期会议讨论目标等问题，Mathis 似乎一心阻止对改变语言的事项进行投票。CLOS 委员会一旦显出进展，就成立了清理委员会，由 Xerox 的 Larry Masinter 负责。在此之前，Fahlman、代表 Tektronix 的 Clinger，以及 INRIA 的 Chailloux 都参加会议。清理委员会成立后不久，他们停止出席。

• The early meetings of X3J13 concern goals and the like, and Mathis appears intent on preventing votes that change the language from taking place. Once the CLOS committee appears to be making progress, a cleanup committee is formed, and Larry Masinter (Xerox) is put in charge of it. Up to this point, Fahlman attended meetings, as does Clinger (representing Tektronics) and Chailloux (INRIA). Soon after the formation of the cleanup committee, these folks stop attending.

• 有了用于修改的清理提案格式之后，Common Lisp 出现大批修改，数以百计，其中许多没有充分理由。特别是采用 LOOP、清理函数类型、采用编译器宏、于1988年采用 CLOS、采用条件系统，以及许许多多其他变化 [Steele, 1990c]。其中值得讨论的有条件、迭代、CLOS、类型，以及修改范围过于宽泛的问题。

• Once there is a cleanup format for changes, there are a raft of changes made to Common Lisp. Hundreds of changes are made, many for no compelling reason. In particular, LOOP is adopted, the function type is cleaned up, compiler macros are adopted, CLOS is adopted (1988), a condition system is adopted, and lots, lots more. [Steele, 1990c] Worth discussing are some of these including conditions, iteration, CLOS, types, and the overbroad range of changes.

• 1989年，人工智能寒冬来临。人工智能公司过度吹嘘能力，付出了代价。LMI 大约在此时倒闭。

• In 1989 AI winter hits. The AI companies have oversold the capabilities of AI, and they pay a price. LMI folds around this time.

• Sun 主导通用硬件市场，迫使 Symbolics 陷入低谷。1985年的 Sun 2 不够用，1986年的 Sun 3 尚可，1988年的 Sun 4 开始相当不错。IBM RT 是巨大失败。HP 试图以 PSL 为基础转向 Common Lisp，但放弃了，改选 Lucid。正如 Brooks 和 Gabriel 预言的那样，Common Lisp 无法胜任交付解决方案，这一点开始变得明显。

• Sun dominates the stock hardware market. They force Symbolics into a slump. In 1985 they had Sun 2’s, which were inadequate, in 1986 they had Sun 3’s, which were OK, and in 1988 they had Sun 4’s, which were beginning to be pretty good. The IBM RT is a huge flop. HP tries to move to Common Lisp based on PSL, but gives up and chooses Lucid instead. It is beginning to be clear that Common Lisp, as predicted by Brooks and Gabriel, cannot hack it is a delivery solution.

• 1989年，通用硬件上出现第一批非侵扰式垃圾回收器，来自 Lucid 和 Franz [Sobalvarro, 1988]。

• In 1989, the first non-intrusive garbage collectors on stock hardware appear (Lucid and Franz) [Sobalvarro, 1988].

• 1990年，TI 退出 Lisp 竞争。Xerox 于1989年放弃 Interlisp 商业业务。IBM 放弃基于 Lisp370 的 Common Lisp，采用 Lucid。1990年 DEC 放弃自家 Lisp，采用 Lucid。显然，人工智能寒冬，以及商业 Lisp 无法解决集成和交付问题，都产生了影响。人们尝试各种方法，如 Lucid 的树摇优化和 Franz 的选择性加载，但这些都失败了。1990年，Gabriel 在 Europal 主题演讲中试图分析失败原因 [?; ?]。

• In 1990 TI drops out of the Lisp game. Xerox drops Interlisp as a commercial venture in 1989. IBM drops its Lisp370-based CL and adopts Lucid. In 1990 DEC drops its Lisp and adopts Lucid. It is clear that the AI winter and inability of the commercial Lisps to solve integration and delivery problems. Various approaches are tried (Treeshaking in Lucid and selective loading in Franz). These are failures. In 1990, Gabriel attempts to analyze the failure in a keynote address at Europal [?; ?].

• 1988年，DARPA 在罗斯林召集 Gabriel、Bobrow、Sussman、Fahlman，可能还有其他几人，再次讨论合并 Scheme 与 Common Lisp。Scherlis 和 Squires 推动此事，再次失败。

• In 1988 DARPA calls a meeting of Gabriel, Bobrow, Sussman, Fahlman, and maybe a few others at DARPA (Rosslyn) to discuss merging Scheme and Common Lisp again. Scherlis and Squires drive this. Another failure.

• Scheme 于1989年开始 IEEE 标准化，1991年完成。首次会议在1989年初冬举行，Gabriel 出席。他反对标准化，或者主张改为加入 X3J13，但 Scheme 对 Common Lisp 的厌恶太强。Scheme 将有两条轨道：IEEE 标准和非正式的 Rⁿ 报告。

• Scheme begins IEEE standardization in 1989 and finishes in 1991. The first meeting is in the early winter of 1989, and Gabriel attends. He argues against standardization or joining X3J13 instead, but Scheme loathing of Common Lisp is too strong. There will be two tracks for Scheme: IEEE standard and informal Rn Reports.

• 在 Scheme 中，卫生宏似乎将进入 Rⁿ Scheme。

• Scheme, hygienic macros appear to make it into the Rn Scheme.

• 1990年，EuLisp 小组形成草案。这是一种分层语言：采用 Lisp-1，具备模块，把类似 CLOS 的面向对象编程分为三个清晰层次，并清理 Common Lisp 的各种错误。这项工作由 Padget 推动，主要由他编写规范。

• In 1990, the EuLisp group produces a draft of EuLisp. This is a layered language that has this: it is a 1-lisp, has modules, CLOS-like OOP layered in three nice layers, and a variety of cleanups of Common Lisp mistakes. This work is driven by Padgett, who primarily writes the spec.

• 到1990年，日本在 ISO 中的声音更强，进程陷入停滞。Gabriel 辞去美国代表团团长职务。Clinger 已于1988年辞职。Kiczales 担任代理代表。

• At ISO, by 1990, Japan has become a stronger voice, and the process is stalled. Gabriel resigns as head of the US delegation. Clinger had resigned in 1988. Kiczales is the acting representative.

• X3J13 最后一次作出决策的会议原定于1989年1月举行，但修改热情不断延续。这就是“人人都必须在上面撒一泡尿”的综合征。

• The last meeting of X3J13 to make decisions is supposed to be January 1989, but fervor to change things drags on. This is the “everyone must piss on it” syndrome.

• 1990年，Pitman 自愿接替1989年退出的 Chapman 担任编辑。1991年，他形成了一份长达1300页的初稿。仍存的 Lisp 公司之一 Ibuki 试图让 X3J13 退让，希望回到 CLtL1。

• In 1990 Pitman volunteers to be the editor taking over for Chapman, who disappeared in 1989. In 1991 he produces a first draft that is 1300 pages long. One of the remaining Lisp companies (Ibuki) tries to get X3J13 to back down, hoping to revert back to CLtL1.

［粗略部分结束。］

[End of sketchy section.]

分析

analysis

［以下文字非常粗略，尚待充实。］

[The following text is very sketchy and needs to be filled out.]

市场力量主导接受群体。Common Lisp 的接受群体于1989年开始衰退，随之出现一阵分化：修改 Common Lisp、增加特性、EuLisp 终于成形（欧洲最先遭遇人工智能寒冬）。接受群体中的一些人因为体积和简单性转向 Scheme，例如 CFI 等扩展语言使用者。

Market forces dominate acceptance groups. The acceptance group for CL goes on the decline in 1989, and a flurry of diversification takes place in terms of changes to CL, additions to CL, EuLisp finally emerging (Europe is hit with AI winter first). Some parts of the acceptance group heads for Scheme because of size and simplicity (e.g. extension language folks like CFI).

Common Lisp 的整合导致 Interlisp 消亡。先是 Lisp 机器，后是 Sun 的先锋声望，推动 Common Lisp 前进。人工智能寒冬之后，规模再次缩小为小型 Lisp 团队，因此我们预期将出现分化时期。

Consolidation of CL causes Interlisp to die. Cachet of Lisp machines first and Suns later pushes CL ahead. The post-AI-winter era has shrunk things down to small Lisp groups again, so we expect a period of diversification.

人工智能公司把寒冬归咎于 Lisp 团队没有提供交付方案，但人工智能公司从未向 Lisp 公司说明需求，因为害怕合作。这是学者转为商人时常见却愚蠢的现象。

AI companies blame AI winter on the Lisp groups who did not provide delivery solutions, but the AI companies never communicated their needs to the Lisp companies, because of fear of collaboration (a common but stupid phenomenon that happens when academics become business people).

CLOS 本身也是一次整合，为原本停滞的 Common Lisp 增添了先锋声望。

CLOS is a consolidation itself, and it has added a cachet to an otherwise stagnant Common Lisp.

Lisp 环境始终没有真正实现标准化，尽管人工智能公司大多提供了一个环境，以及庞大的用户界面和人工智能功能库。［粗略部分结束。］

Environments for Lisp never really were standardized, though the AI companies largely provided an environment plus a big library of UI and AI stuff. [End of sketchy section.]

2.13 表达力

2.13 Expressiveness

事实表明，Lisp 最关心的是表达力，胜过其他一切。观察一下便可发现：只有十分熟悉某个特定 Lisp 实现方式的人，才能写出高效程序。下面这段代码看起来完全不错：

Lisp has proved itself more concerned with expressiveness than anything else. We can see this by observing that only a person well-versed with how a particular Lisp is implemented can write efficient programs. Here is a perfectly nice piece of code:

(defun make-matrix (n m)
  (let ((matrix ()))
    (dotimes (i n matrix)
      (push (make-list m) matrix))))
(defun add-matrix (m1 m2)
  (let ((l1 (length m1))
        (l2 (length m2)))
    (let ((matrix (make-matrix l1 l2)))
      (dotimes (i l1 matrix)
        (dotimes (j l2)
          (setf (nth i (nth j matrix))
                (+ (nth i (nth j m1))
                   (nth i (nth j m2)))))))))

读写矩阵中一个单元的表达式，看起来完全无害，似乎再快不过了。但它很慢，因为 nth 所需的时间与第一个参数的值成正比；本质上，每次调用都会沿列表不断执行 CDR。（有经验的 Lisp 程序员会遍历列表的单元，而不是遍历数字索引，或者干脆使用数组而非列表。）

The expression to read and write a cell in a matrix looks perfectly harmless and fast as anything. But it is slow, because nth takes time proportional to the value of its first argument, essentially CDRing down the list every time it is called. (An experienced Lisp coder would iterate over the cells of a list rather than over numeric indices, or would use arrays instead of lists.)

这里，表达力走偏了。人们往往期望语言中的操作只花很小的单位时间，而不是具有难以计算的复杂成本。因此，恰恰因为 Lisp 的表达力如此强，写出漂亮程序很容易，写出快速程序却很难。

Here expressiveness has gone awry. People tend to expect that operations in the language cost a small unit time, not something complicated to figure out. So, precisely because Lisp is so expressive, it is very hard to write fast programs, though it is easy to write pretty ones.

3 若干具体语言特性的演化

3 Evolution of Some Specific Language Features

本节讨论一些语言特性的演化；它们要么是 Lisp 独有的，要么由 Lisp 以独特方式处理。

In this section we discuss the evolution of some language features that are either unique to Lisp or uniquely handled by Lisp.

3.1 对 NIL（以及 T）的处理

3.1 The Treatment of NIL (and T)

几乎从一开始，Lisp 就用符号 nil 作为标记列表结束的特殊对象，因此它本身也是空列表；同一对象还充当谓词返回的假值。McCarthy 曾评论，这些决定作得“相当轻率”，并且“后来证明很不幸”。此外，最早的实现确立了用零地址表示 NIL 的传统；McCarthy 还说：“除了鼓励不雅的编程方式，对地址0赋予特殊解释，也给此后的所有实现带来了困难。”[McCarthy, 1978]

Almost since the beginning, Lisp has used the symbol nil as the distinguished object that indicates the end of a list (and which is therefore itself the empty list); this same object also serves as the false value returned by predicates. McCarthy has commented that these decisions were made “rather lightheartedly” and “later proved unfortunate.” Furthermore, the earliest implementations established a tradition of using the zero address as the representation of NIL; McCarthy also commented that “besides encouraging pornographic programming, giving a special interpretation to the address 0 has caused difficulties in all subsequent implementations” [McCarthy, 1978].

用地址0表示 nil 的优点是，多数机器都有“为零则跳转”或等价指令，因而能够快速而紧凑地检测列表结尾。但要看实现上的困难，可以考察 PDP-10 架构：它有16个寄存器，或称“累加器”，也能作为内存位置寻址。因此，内存位置0就是寄存器0。由于地址0是 nil，符号的标准表示就要求寄存器0的右半部分包含符号 nil 的属性表，左半部分则包含其他信息的地址，例如名称“nil”的字符串。因此，这一实现传统占用了一个寄存器，而在这种架构中，寄存器是稀缺资源。

The advantage of using address 0 as the representation of nil is that most machines have a “jump if zero” instruction or the equivalent, allowing a quick and compact test for the end of a list. As an example of the implementation difficulties, however, consider the PDP-10 architecture, which had 16 registers, or “accumulators”, which were also addressable as memory locations. Memory location 0 was therefore register 0. Because address 0 was nil, the standard representation for symbols dictated that the right half of register 0 contain the property list for the symbol nil (and the left half contained the address of other information, such as the character string for the name “nil”). The implementation tradition thus resulted in tying up a register in an architecture where registers were a scarce resource.

后来，MacLisp 从 Interlisp 采用了 (car nil) = (cdr nil) = nil 的约定，寄存器0仍被保留；它的两个半字都包含值0，因此 car 和 cdr 操作不必对 nil 作特殊处理。但所有符号操作都必须特殊处理 nil，因为它不再具有与其他符号相同的表示。这给 Guy Steele 带来了一些困难：他必须找出 MacLisp 汇编语言内核中每一处受影响的地方。如今他承认，这其实是个相当好的主意。引入可靠获取属性表的抽象，消除了他最后的反对意见（1975年2月7日，第4项）：

Later, when MacLisp adopted from Interlisp the convention that (car nil) = (cdr nil) = nil, register 0 was still reserved; its two halves contained the value 0 so that the car and cdr operations need not special-case nil. But all operations on symbols had to special-case nil, for it no longer had the same representation as other symbols. This led to some difficulties for Guy Steele, who had to find every place in the assembly-language kernel of MacLisp where this mattered. Nowadays he concedes that it was actually rather a good idea. The introduction of an abstraction for reliably obtaining the property list settled his last objections (item 4 of February 7, 1975):

新增的单参数 SUBR——PLIST——用于取得符号的属性表；它适用于包括 NIL 在内的所有符号，而 CDR 在非 *RSET 模式下［即关闭错误检查时！］只适用于非空符号。新增的双参数 SUBR——SETPLIST——也有类似情况。(SETPLIST X (PLIST X)) 应当等同于不作任何操作。

The new SUBR of one argument, PLIST, is used to retrieve the property list of a symbol; this function works on all symbols including NIL, whereas CDR will work only for non-null symbols in non-*RSET mode [that is, with error checking turned off!]. Similar remarks hold for the new SUBR of two arguments, SETPLIST. (SETPLIST X (PLIST X)) should be a no-op.

如今，一些 Common Lisp 实现使用复杂的偏移数据表示系统，以避免对 cons 单元或符号作特殊处理。每个符号的数据并不从符号指针所指的内存字开始，而是从其后两个字的位置开始。一个 cons 单元由指针所指的字（cdr）及其后的字（car）组成。这样，同一个指针既可表示符号 nil，也可表示空列表 () 这一伪 cons 单元，其 car 和 cdr 都是 nil。

Nowadays some Common Lisp implementations use a complex system of offset data representations to avoid special cases for either conses or symbols; every symbol is represented in such a way that the data for the symbol does not begin at the memory word addressed by a symbol pointer, but two words after the word addressed. A cons cell consists of the addressed word (the cdr) and the word after that (the car). In this way the same pointer serves for both nil the symbol and () the empty list pseudo-cons whose car and cdr are both nil.

快速检测列表结尾存在风险：列表可能是不正规列表，即以一个既非空列表、也非 cons 单元的对象结尾。Interlisp 采取折中办法，让程序员在速度和安全性之间选择 [Teitelman, 1978]：

There is a danger in using a quick test for the end of a list; a list might turn out to be improper, that is, ending in an object that is neither the empty list nor a cons cell. Interlisp split the difference, giving the programmer a choice of speed or safety [Teitelman, 1978]:

虽然多数列表以 NIL 结尾，偶尔也有列表以原子结尾，例如 (A B . C)，更糟的还可能以数字或字符串结尾，这会造成古怪的效果。因此，我们作出以下实现决定：所有遍历列表的函数，例如 member、length、mapc 等，都通过 nlistp 检查终止，而不是使用传统的 null 检查，以防遇到可能造成无限 cdr 循环的数据类型……［原文此处使用斜体。］对于应用需要极高效率的用户，［原脚注：NIL 检查只需一条指令；Interlisp-10 的 nlistp 约需8条，不过两者都只生成一个字的代码。］我们提供了 memb、last、nth、assoc 和 length 的快速版本，它们编译为内联代码，并通过 NIL 检查终止……

Although most lists terminate in NIL, the occasional list that ends in an atom, e.g., (A B . C), or worse, a number or string, could cause bizarre effects. Accordingly, we have made the following implementation decision: All functions that iterate through a list, e.g., member, length, mapc, etc., terminate by an nlistp check, rather than the conventional null-check, as a safety precaution against encountering data types which might cause infinite cdr loops . . . [their italics] For users with an application requiring extreme efficiency, [their footnote: A NIL check can be executed in only one instruction; an nlistp on Interlisp-10 requires about 8, although both generate only one word of code.] we have provided fast versions of memb, last, nth, assoc, and length, which compile open and terminate on NIL checks . . .

早在1964年，Fischer Black 就从编程风格角度评论过 NIL 与 () 的区别 [Black, 1985]。多年来，人们一直讨论是否应当把空列表、假值，以及除此之外并无特别之处的名为“nil”的符号这三种角色分开。在 Scheme 社区中，这类讨论尤其激烈，因为其中许多人重视优雅和清晰。他们认为如下构造：

Fischer Black commented as early as 1964 on the difference between NIL and () as a matter of programming style [Black, 1985]. There has been quite a bit of discussion over the years of whether to tease apart the three roles of the empty list, the false value, and the otherwise uninteresting symbol whose name is “nil”; such discussion was particularly intense in the Scheme community, many of whose constituents are interested in elegance and clarity. They regard such constructions as

(if (car x) (+ (car x) 1))

是拙劣的双关，更喜欢下面这种更明确的写法：

as bad puns, preferring the more explicit

(if (not (null (car x))) (+ (car x) 1))

《Scheme 的修订修订报告》[Clinger, 1985b] 定义了三个不同的值：nil，只是另一个符号；()，空列表；#!false，布尔假值，另有布尔真值 #!true。不过，一项有趣的折中是：语言中所有检测真假的地方，都把 () 和 #!false 视为假，把其他一切对象视为真。报告评论道：

The Revised Revised Report on Scheme [Clinger, 1985b] defined three distinct quantities nil (just another symbol); (), the empty list; and #!false, the boolean false value (along with #!true, the boolean true value). However, in an interesting compromise, all places in the language that tested for true/false values regarded both () and #!false as false and all other objects as true. The report comments:

空列表仅因历史原因才算作假，程序不应依赖这一点，因为未来版本的 Scheme 很可能会取消这种无意义的做法。习惯其他 Lisp 方言的程序员应当注意：Scheme 已经取消了把空列表等同于符号 nil 的荒谬做法。

The empty list counts as false for historical reasons only, and programs should not rely on this because future versions of Scheme will probably do away with this nonsense. Programmers accustomed to other dialects of Lisp should beware that Scheme has already done away with the nonsense that identifies the empty list with the symbol nil.

《算法语言 Scheme 的第三次修订报告》[Rees, 1986] 把 #!false 和 #!true 缩短为 #f 和 #t，并作出类似但更为精炼、也更为文雅的评论：

The Revised3 Report on the Algorithmic Language Scheme [Rees, 1986] shortened #!false and #!true to #f and #t, and made a remark that is similar but more refined (in both senses):

把空列表算作假，是为了兼容作出这一假定的现有程序和实现。习惯其他 Lisp 方言的程序员应当注意，Scheme 将假值和空列表与符号 nil 区分开来。

The empty list counts as false for compatibility with existing programs and implementations that assume this to be the case. Programmers accustomed to other dialects of Lisp should beware that Scheme distinguishes false and the empty list from the symbol nil.

最近获批的 Scheme IEEE 标准规定，#f 和 #t 是标准假值和真值；除 #f 外的所有值都算作真，“包括 #t、空列表、符号、数字、字符串、向量和过程”[IEEE, 1991]。因此，Scheme 社区确实克服了长期传统，把假值、空列表和符号 nil 这三个概念彻底分开。不过，这个问题仍在争论。

The recently approved IEEE standard for Scheme specifies that #f and #t are the standard false and true values, and that all values except #f count as true, “including #t, the empty list, symbols, numbers, strings, vectors, and procedures” [IEEE, 1991]. So the Scheme community has, indeed, overcome long tradition and completely separated the three notions of the false value, the empty list, and the symbol nil. Nevertheless the question continues to be debated.

Common Lisp 的设计也讨论过 nil 问题；至少有一个直接参与贡献的实现 NIL，已经决定空列表 () 不与符号 nil 相同。（当时流传的笑话是：NIL，即 New Implementation of Lisp，卸下了 NIL 作为空列表的职责，好让它腾出身来充当语言名称！）不过，最终还是与过去兼容的愿望占了上风，不管过去的东西多么陈旧杂乱 [Raymond, 1991]。下面这封电子邮件最能表达这种立场：

The question of nil was also debated in the design of Common Lisp, and at least one of the directly contributing implementations, NIL, had already made the decision that the empty list () would not be the same as the symbol nil. (A running joke was that NIL (New Implementation of Lisp) unburdened NIL of its role as the empty list so that it would be free to serve as the name of the language!) Eventually the desire to be compatible with the past, however crufty [Raymond, 1991], carried the day. ; this was best expressed by the following piece of E-mail:

日期：1982年3月14日，星期日，14:40（美国东部标准时间） 发件人：Symbolics 技术人员 回复至：Moon@SCRC-TENEX@MIT-MC 收件人：SU-AI 上的 Common-Lisp 主题：T 和 NIL 问题 很抱歉这封邮件拖了这么久；最近我的时间全被其他项目占满了。我们在内部讨论过 T 和 NIL 的问题。如果是在设计一种全新语言，我们当然会重新考虑这些问题，以及 Lisp 的许多其他瑕疵（或者美人痣）。（不一定会改变，但肯定会重新考虑。）然而，与转换成本相比，现在改变 T 和 NIL 所能带来的好处很小。Symbolics 唯一能够接受的解决办法，就是维持现状。 现状概括如下：NIL 是一个符号、空列表，以及特殊的“假”值。对它应用 SYMBOLP、ATOM 和 LISTP 都为真，CONSP 则为假。NIL 的 CAR、CDR 和 EVAL 都是 NIL。NIL 既不能用作函数，也不能用作变量。NIL 有属性表。T 是一个符号，也是非半谓词所使用的默认“真”值；所谓非半谓词，是指那些在结果为真时不返回“有意义”值的谓词。T 的 EVAL 是 T。T 不能用作变量。T 是 FORMAT 等某些函数识别的关键字。LISTP 的行为是对现状的一项改变，我们［Symbolics］早已同意；如果不是为了等 Common Lisp 出来再作不兼容修改，我们早就实现了。现状是 NIL 有属性表；不过，如果有人强烈认为属性表函数收到 NIL 时应报错，这一点大概仍可商量。CASEQ 和 SELECTQ 中把 T 用作语法关键字的做法，不应带入它们在 Common Lisp 中的替代品 CASE。这源于对 COND 中 T 的约定的误解，肯定无助于语言的可理解性。T 和 NIL 与 Lisp 中数百个其他保留字没有区别，其中几十个作为变量保留，其余大多数作为函数保留。任何程序若想把这些名字用于普通符号，而不是特殊保留符号，都很容易通过包机制做到。可移植包系统应有一个包选项，使保留的 NIL 在需要时打印为“()”，而不是“GLOBAL:NIL”。

Date: Sunday, 14 March 1982 14:40-EST From: Symbolics Technical Staff Reply-to: Moon@SCRC-TENEX@MIT-MC To: Common-Lisp at SU-AI Subject: The T and NIL issues I’m sorry this message has been so long delayed; my time has been completely occupied with other projects recently. We have had some internal discussions about the T and NIL issues. If we were designing a completely new language, we would certainly rethink these, as well as the many other warts (or beauty marks) in Lisp. (We might not necessarily change them, but we would certainly rethink them.) However, the advantages to be gained by changing T and NIL now are quite small compared to the costs of conversion. The only resolution to these issues that Symbolics can accept is to retain the status quo. To summarize the status quo: NIL is a symbol, the empty list, and the distinguished “false” value. SYMBOLP, ATOM, and LISTP are true of it; CONSP is not. CAR, CDR, and EVAL of NIL are NIL. NIL may not be used as a function, nor as a variable. NIL has a property list. T is a symbol and the default “true” value used by predicates that are not semi-predicates (i.e., that don’t return “meaningful” values when they are true.) EVAL of T is T. T may not be used as a variable. T is a keyword recognized by certain functions, such as FORMAT. The behavior of LISTP is a change to the status quo which we [Symbolics] agreed to long ago, and would have implemented long ago if we weren’t waiting for Common Lisp before making any incompatible changes. The status quo is that NIL has a property list; however, this point is probably open to negotiation if anyone feels strongly that the property-list functions should error when given NIL. The use of T as a syntactic keyword in CASEQ and SELECTQ should not be carried over into their Common Lisp replacement, CASE. It is based on a misunderstanding of the convention about T in COND and certainly adds nothing to the understandability of the language. T and NIL are just like the hundreds of other reserved words in Lisp, dozens of which are reserved as variables, most of the rest as functions. Any particular program that wants to use these names for ordinary symbols rather than the special reserved ones can easily do so through the use of packages. There should be a package option in the portable package system by which the reserved NIL can be made to print as “()” rather than “GLOBAL:NIL” when desired.

值得注意的是，Lisp 实现者并未倾向于把 nil 等同于数字0，而不是内部地址0。一个显著例外是 Richard M. Stallman 在1970年代为 PDP-11 编写的 Lisp 系统：其中用数字0而非符号 nil 充当空列表和假值。可以把这与 APL 中用0和1表示假和真 [Iverson, 1962]，或 C 中用0表示假和空指针 [Kernighan, 1978] 的做法相比。这两种语言也引发了与 McCarthy 评论 Lisp 时类似的、关于双关和不良编程习惯的批评。

It is worth noting that Lisp implementors have not been tempted to identify nil with the number 0 (as opposed to the internal address 0), with one notable exception, a Lisp system for the PDP-11 written in the 1970’s by Richard M. Stallman, in which the number 0 rather than the symbol nil was used as the empty list and as false. Compare this to the use of 0 and 1 as false and true in APL [Iverson, 1962], or the use of 0 as false and as the null pointer in C [Kernighan, 1978]. Both of these languages have provoked the same kinds of comments about puns and bad programming practice that McCarthy made about Lisp.

读者可能觉得，本文为这样一个小小的语言设计问题花了太多篇幅。但这里的篇幅，准确反映了多年来 Lisp 社区为此争论实际投入的时间和精力比例。这是表达力与整洁性之间的争论，也是不同清晰观之间的争论。即使 Lisp 不强制强类型，一些程序员仍喜欢在代码中维持类型纪律，写 (if (not (null (car x))) ...)，而不是 (if (car x) ...)。另一些人则认为，这些多余的累赘不但不能提高清晰度，反而会损害它。

This may seem to the reader to be a great deal of discussion to expend in this paper on such a small point of language design. However, the space taken here reflects accurately the proportion of time and energy in debate actually expended on this point by the Lisp community over the years. It is a debate about expressiveness versus cleanliness and about different notions of clarity. Even if Lisp does not enforce strong typing, some programmers prefer to maintain a type discipline in their code, writing (if (not (null (car x))) ...) instead of (if (car x) ...). Others contend that such excess clutter detracts from clarity rather than improving it.

3.2 迭代

3.2 Iteration

按 Lisp 自己的宣传，它历来以条件式和递归定义函数作为表达控制结构的主要手段；但实际上，人们一直反复尝试引入各种语法机制，使迭代更方便。这可能出于模仿 ALGOL 类语言编程风格的愿望，也因为虽然某些编译器有时会优化尾递归调用，但直到1980年代出现优秀的 Scheme 和 Common Lisp 编译器之前，程序员都无法依赖这一点，因此性能是个问题。

While Lisp, according to its P.R., has traditionally used conditionals and recursively defined functions as the principal means of expressing control structure, there have in fact been repeated and continuing attempts to introduce various syntactic devices to make iteration more convenient. This may have been driven by the desire to emulate styles of programming found in ALGOL-like languages and by the fact that, although some compilers would sometimes optimize tail-recursive calls, the programmer could not rely on this until the era of good Scheme and Common Lisp compilers in the 1980’s, so performance was an issue.

最简单的专用迭代构造，也许可以用1969年3月引入 MacLisp 的第一个 do 循环来说明：

Perhaps the simplest special iteration construct is exemplified by the first do loop introduced into MacLisp (in March, 1969):

(do var init step test . body)

(do var init step test . body)

其含义与下列形式相同： (let ((var init)) (block (when test (return)) (progn . body) (setq var step)))

means the same as (let ((var init)) (block (when test (return)) (progn . body) (setq var step)))

因此，下面的 Fortran DO 循环：

Thus the Fortran DO loop

     DO 10 J=1,100
     IF (A(J) .GT. 0) SUM = SUM + A(J)
10 CONTINUE

可以表达为：

could be expressed as

(DO J 1 (+ J 1) (> J 100)
  (WHEN (PLUSP (A J))
    (SETQ TOTAL (+ TOTAL (AREF A J)))))

（当然，Lisp 数组通常从0而不是从1开始索引，因此：

(except, of course, that Lisp arrays are usually 0-origin instead of 1-origin, so

(DO J 0 (+ J 1) (= J 100)
  (WHEN (PLUSP (A J))
    (SETQ TOTAL (+ TOTAL (AREF A J)))))

实际上才是更符合习惯的写法。）

is actually a more idiomatic rendering).

这种“旧式” MacLisp do 循环绝不是最早引入 Lisp 的迭代语法；先介绍它，只因为它最简单。Interlisp CLISP 的迭代语句，是后来不断被重新发明的那种更典型风格的最早实例：

This “old-style” MacLisp do loop was by no means the earliest iteration syntax introduced to Lisp; we mention it first only because it is the simplest. The Interlisp CLISP iterative statements were the earliest examples of the more typical style that has been reinvented ever since:

(FOR J←0 TO 99 SUM (A J) WHEN (PLUSP (A J)))

(FOR J←0 TO 99 SUM (A J) WHEN (PLUSP (A J)))

它把总和作为值返回，而不是通过副作用累积到变量 TOTAL 中。关于 Algol 风格语法的进一步讨论，见第3.7.1节。

This returns the sum as its value rather than accumulating it into the variable TOTAL by side effect. (See section 3.7.1 for further discussion of Algol-style syntax.)

宏或 CLISP 等其他代码转换设施，使这类扩展特别容易实现。Interlisp 的工作是集中开展的，而在其他 Lisp 方言中，包括 MacLisp，经常有本地高手拼凑出花哨的 Lisp 迭代语法。通常，这些语法使用类似 Algol 语言中的伪英语关键字，不过斯坦福的一个版本更多依赖自制键盘提供的扩展 ASCII 字符集。这导致大量彼此接近的语法出现，通常以 FOR 或 LOOP 开头；它们吸引了许多程序员，但随着特性不断堆积，也让另一些人感到反胃。

Macros or other code-transformation facilities such as CLISP make this kind of extension particularly easy. While the effort in Interlisp was centralized, in other Lisp dialects (MacLisp not excepted) there have been repeated instances of one local wizard or another cobbling up some fancy syntax for iterative processes in Lisp. Usually it is characterized by the kind of pseudo- English keywords found in the Algol-like languages, although one version at Stanford relied more on the extended-ASCII character set available on its home-grown keyboards. This led to a proliferation of closely related syntaxes, typically led offby the keyword FOR or LOOP, that have attracted many programmers but turned the stomaches of others as features accreted.

由于人们对迭代语法有如此强烈而不同的审美反应，是否纳入 loop 宏，在 Common Lisp 早期设计中成为一场重大政治争论。Lisp 机器阵营通常支持采用，Scott Fahlman 则坚决反对，Guy Steele 也许以较弱的态度附议。结果是折中：Common Lisp 的第一版定义 [Steele, 1984] 包含一个功能极其有限的 loop 宏，不允许任何特殊关键字，只能表示无限重复执行一串子形式。大家理解，这只是占位符，保留 loop 名称，以便将来扩展为完整迭代语法。ANSI 委员会 X3J13 最终同意并采用了一个稍作清理的 loop 版本 [Steele, 1990c]，以 MIT 和 Lisp 机器所用版本为基础，而它与 Interlisp 的版本也没有太大差别。

Because of these strong and differing aesthetic reactions to iteration syntax, the question of whether to include a loop macro became a major political battle in the early design of Common Lisp, with the Lisp Machine crowd generally in favor of its adoption and Scott Fahlman adamantly opposing it, seconded perhaps more weakly by Guy Steele. The result was a compromise. The first definition of Common Lisp [Steele, 1984] included a loop macro with absolutely minimal functionality: it permitted no special keywords, good only for expressing endless repetition of a sequence of subforms. It was understood to be a placeholder, reserving the name loop for possible extension to some full-blown iteration syntax. ANSI committee X3J13 did eventually agree upon and adopt a slightly cleaned-up version of loop [Steele, 1990c] based on the one used at MIT and on Lisp Machines (which was not very much different from the one in Interlisp).

在此过程中，X3J13 还考虑了期间出现的另两种迭代方法：Richard Waters 提出的 series，以及 Pavel Curtis 和 Crispin Perdue 提出的 generators 和 gatherers [Steele, 1990c; Waters, 1984; Waters, 1989a; Waters, 1989b]。上面的 Fortran DO 循环，用 series 可以写为：

In the process X3J13 also considered two other approaches to iteration that had cropped up in the meantime: series (put forward by Richard Waters) and generators and gatherers (by Pavel Curtis and Crispin Perdue) [Steele, 1990c; Waters, 1984; Waters, 1989a; Waters, 1989b]. The example Fortran DO loop shown above would be rendered using series as

(collect-sum (choose-if #’plusp
                        (#M(lambda (j) (a j))
                         (scan-range :start 0 :below 100)))

这是一种函数式风格，让人想起 APL：

This is a functional style, reminiscent of APL:

+/(T>0)/T←A[ι100]

series 原语及其允许的组合方式，经过巧妙约束，保证它们总能编译为高效的迭代代码，而无需在运行时使用大小无上限的中间数据结构。generator 和 gatherer 是封装 series 的方法，代价是维护运行时状态；它们分别表现为输入流或输出流，通过副作用充当连续值的来源或汇集处。因此：

The definition of the series primitives and their permitted compositions is cleverly constrained so that they can always be compiled into efficient iterative code without the need for unboundedly large intermediate data structures at run time.
