Generators and gatherers are a method of encapsulating series (at the cost of run-time state) so that they look like input or output streams, respectively, that serve as sources or sinks of successive values by side effect. Thus

(generator (choose-if #’plusp
                      (#M(lambda (j) (a j))
                       (scan-range :start 0 :below 100)))

会产生一个对象，把它交给提取函数 next-in 时，就会依次给出 (A 0)、(A 1) 等结果中的正数。这让人想起 Conniver 的可能性列表 [McDermott, 1974]，或 Alphard 的生成器 [Shaw, 1981]，不过我们不知道它们之间有任何直接联系。

produces an object that delivers successive positive numbers resulting from (A 0), (A 1), etc., when given to the extraction function next-in. This reminds one of the possibilities lists of Conniver [McDermott, 1974] or of the generators of Alphard [Shaw, 1981], though we know of no direct connection.

经过大量讨论，X3J13 赞赏 series 和 generator 的发展，但拒绝把它们标准化，希望先让它们接受时间检验。

After much debate, X3J13 applauded the development of series and generators but rejected them for standardization purposes, preferring to subject them first to the test of time.

另一个值得讨论的迭代构造，是1972年3月引入的 MacLisp“新式” do 循环：

One other iteration construct that deserves discussion is the MacLisp “new-style” do loop, introduced in March, 1972:

(do ((var1 init1 step1)
     (var2 init2 step2)
     ...
     (varn initn stepn))
    (test . result)
  . body)

它先对所有 init 形式求值，并把对应的 var 变量绑定到所得值。然后反复执行以下序列：如果 test 形式求值得到真值，就对 result 各形式求值，并返回最后一个的值；否则依次执行 body 各形式，然后重复。

This evaluates all the init forms and binds the corresponding variables var to the resulting values. It then iterates the following sequence: if evaluating the test form produces a true value, evaluate the result forms and return the value of the last one; otherwise execute the body forms in sequence and repeat.

这个构造的妙处，是无需俗气的伪英语关键字，就能初始化和步进多个变量。糟糕之处，是它使用多层括号作分隔，必须写对，否则就得忍受古怪行为；只有死忠 Lisp 爱好者才会喜欢这种语法。

The beauty of this construct is that it allows the initialization and stepping of multiple variables without use of kitschy pseudo-English keywords. The awful part is that it uses multiple levels of parentheses as delimiters and you have to get them right or endure strange behavior; only a diehard Lisper could love such a syntax.

撇开语法争论，值得认识到：在任何程序设计语言中，只步进一个变量的循环都相当无用。几乎总是一个变量生成连续值，另一个变量累积结果。如果循环语法只步进生成变量，就必须通过赋值语句（如 Fortran 示例）或其他副作用“手动”步进累积变量。多变量 do 循环体现了生成与累积之间本质上的对称性，使迭代可以不借助显式副作用来表达：

Arguments over syntax aside, there is something to be said for recognizing that a loop that steps only one variable is pretty useless, in any programming language. It is almost always the case that one variable is used to generate successive values while another is used to accumulate a result. If the loop syntax steps only the generating variable, then the accumulating variable must be stepped “manually” by using assignment statements (as in the Fortran example) or some other side effect. The multiple-variable do loop reflects an essential symmetry between generation and accumulation, allowing iteration to be expressed without explicit side effects:

(define (factorial n)
  (do ((j n (- j 1))
       (f 1 (* j f)))
      ((= j 0) f)))

这种形式的 do 循环具有空循环体、把全部实际工作放在 step 形式中完成，确实并不罕见。

It is indeed not unusual for a do loop of this form to have an empty body, performing all its real work in the step forms.

虽然这个 do 构造可以很直观地翻译为 prog 和 setq，但也有一个清楚而不含副作用的模型：

While there is a pretty obvious translation of this do construct in terms of prog and setq, there is also a perspicuous model free of side effects:

(labels ((the-loop
           (lambda (var1 var2 ... varn)
             (cond (test . result)
                   (t (progn . body)
                      (the-loop step1 step2 ... stepn))))))
  (the-loop init1 init2 ... initn))

事实上，这等价于 Scheme 采用的 do 定义 [IEEE, 1991]。它要求通过绑定而非副作用更新变量，从而解决了一个悬而未决的歧义。因此，整个迭代过程不含副作用。随着 ORBIT [Kranz, 1986] 等优秀 Scheme 编译器，以及优秀 Common Lisp 编译器出现，对这种无副作用转换的结果进行编译，会产生与人们期望从 PROG 和 SETQ 模型得到的完全相同的高效机器代码。

Indeed, this is equivalent to the definition of do adopted by Scheme [IEEE, 1991], which resolves an outstanding ambiguity by requiring that the variables be updated by binding rather than by side effect. Thus the entire iteration process is free of side effects. With the advent of good Scheme compilers such as ORBIT [Kranz, 1986] and good Common Lisp compilers, compiling the result of this side-effect-free translation produces exactly the same efficient machine-language code one would expect from the PROG-and-SETQ model.

3.3 宏

3.3 Macros

宏似乎是 Timothy P. Hart 于1963年在一份简短的 MIT 人工智能备忘录中引入 Lisp 的 [Hart, 1963]。我们在此全文引用［原稿说明：许可申请尚待答复］：

Macros appear to have been introduced into Lisp by Timothy P. Hart in 1963 in a short MIT AI Memo [Hart, 1963], which we quote here in its entirety [request for permission is pending]:

在 LISP 1.5 中，特殊形式用于三个逻辑上不同的目的：a）访问 alist；b）允许函数接受不定数量的参数；c）阻止参数求值。新的 LISP 解释器只需把 alist 设为 SPECIAL 型或 APVAL 型实体，就很容易满足需求 a。用途 b 和 c 则可以通过在 define 中加入 MACRO 指令展开器来替代。我在此提出这样一个展开器。

In LISP 1.5 special forms are used for three logically separate purposes: a) to reach the alist, b) to allow functions to have an indefinite number of arguments, and c) to keep arguments from being evaluated. New LISP interpreters can easily satisfy need (a) by making the alist a SPECIAL- type or APVAL-type entity. Uses (b) and (c) can be replaced by incorporating a MACRO instruction expander in define. I am proposing such an expander.

1. 宏定义的属性表包含指示符 MACRO，其后跟一个单参数函数。参数是以宏名称开头的形式，函数返回值将在所有函数定义中替换原形式。

1. The property list of a macro definition will have the indicator MACRO followed by a function of one argument, a form beginning with the macro’s name, and whose value will replace the original form in all function definitions.

2. 函数 macro[l] 定义宏，正如 define[l] 定义函数一样。

2. The function macro[l] will define macros just as define[l] defines functions.

3. 将修改 define，使它执行宏展开。

3. define will be modified to make macro expansions.

示例：

Examples:

1. 现有 FEXPR csetq 可以用下面的宏定义替代：

1. The existing FEXPR csetq may be replaced by the macro definition:

MACRO ((
(CSETQ (LAMBDA (FORM) (LIST (QUOTE CSET) (LIST (QUOTE QUOTE)
(CADR FORM)) (CADDR FORM))))
))

2. 新宏 stash 将生成 PROG 中常见的形式：

2. A new macro stash will generate the form found frequently in PROG’s:

x := cons[form;x]

使用 stash 宏，就可以不写上面的形式，而写成：

Using the macro stash, one might write instead of the above:

(STASH FORM X)

Stash 可以定义为：

Stash may be defined by:

MACRO ((
(STASH (LAMBDA (FORM) (LIST (QUOTE SETQ) (CADAR FORM)
(LIST (CONS (CADR FORM) (CADAR FORM))) )))
))

3. 新宏可以用已有宏来定义。Enter 是一个宏，用于向存放在程序变量值中的表（点对表）添加新条目。

3. New macros may be defined in terms of old. Enter is a macro for adding a new entry to a table (dotted pairs) stored as the value of a program variable.

enter[form] ≡
            MACRO list[STASH;list[CONS;cadr[form];
caddr[form];cadddr[form]]

顺便说一句，使用宏可以缓解当前的困难：90 LISP 编译器只认识它诞生时已有的那些 fexpr。用于定义宏的函数 macro 很容易定义：

Incidentally, use of macros will alleviate the present difficulty resulting from the 90 LISP compiler’s only knowing about those fexprs in existence at its birth. The macro defining function macro is easily defined:

macro[l] ≡deflist[l;MACRO]

新的 define 稍难一些：

The new define is a little harder:

define[l] ≡deflist[mdef[l];EXPR]
mdef[l] ≡[
    atom[l] →l;
    eq[car[l];QUOTE] →l;
    member[car[l];(LAMBDA LABEL PROG)] →
        cons[car[l];cons[cadr[l];mdef[caddr[l]]]];
    get[car[l];MACRO] →mdef[get[car[l];MACRO][l]];
    T →maplist[l;λ[[j];mdef[car[j]]]]]

4. select 宏说明了如何用宏来允许函数接受任意数量的参数：

4. The macro for select illustrates the use of macros as a means of allowing functions of an arbitrary number of arguments:

select[form] ≡
             MACRO
                     λ[[g];
    list[list[LAMBDA;list[g];cons[COND;
    maplist[cddr[form];λ[[l];
        [null[cdr[l]] →list[T;car[l]];
         T →list[list[EQ;g;caar[l]];cadar[l]]]]]
]];cadr[form]]][gensym[]]

Hart 的提案有几点值得注意。它允许通过任意用户定义函数计算宏展开，而不是像当时许多其他宏处理器那样依赖模板替换。他指出，与 fexpr 不同，编译器无需掌握每个宏的专门知识。宏在函数定义时展开，而不是在解释或编译函数时即时展开。注意文中在 S 表达式与 M 表达式语法之间切换。STASH 宏等价于 Interlisp [Teitelman, 1978] 中的 PUSH 宏；后者经由 Lisp-Machine Lisp，后来进入 Common Lisp。“stash”这个动词在1960年代很常用。mdef 的定义有两个小错误：首先，PROG 处理不正确，因为它没有处理 PROG 形式中的全部语句；其次，没有特殊处理 COND，如果某个变量与宏同名，且该变量被用作 COND 子句的测试部分，就可能出错。（也许这是疏忽，也许 Hart 从未想到有人会品味糟糕到把同一个名字用于两种如此不同的用途。）最后一个例子展示了为临时绑定生成新名称的技巧，以避免对参数形式重复求值。最后，Hart 鼓励用户利用 Lisp 的力量充当它自己的元语言，仅通过一个看似简单的语言改动，就惊人地提升了表达能力。

There are a number of points worth noting about Hart’s proposal. It allows the macro expansion to be computed by an arbitrary user-defined function, rather than relying on substitution into a template, as so many other macro processors of the day did. He noted that macros, unlike fexprs, require no special knowledge for each one on the part of the compiler. Macros are expanded at function definition time, rather than on the fly as a function is interpreted or compiled. Note the switching offbetween S-expression and M-expression syntax. The STASH macro is the equivalent of the PUSH macro found in Interlisp [Teitelman, 1978] and later in Common Lisp by way of Lisp- Machine Lisp; the verb “stash” was commonly used in the 1960’s. There are two minor bugs in the definition of mdef: first, PROG is not properly handled, because it fails to process all the statements in a PROG form; second, COND is not handled specially, which can lead to mistakes if a variable has the same name as a macro and the variable is used as the test part of a COND clause. (Perhaps this was an oversight, or perhaps it never occurred to Hart that anyone would have the bad taste to use a name for two such different purposes.) The last example illustrates the technique of generating a new name for a temporary binding to avoid multiple evaluations of an argument form. Finally, Hart achieved an amazing increase in expressive power with a deceptively simple change to the language, by encouraging the user to exploit the power of Lisp to serve as its own metalanguage.

Hart 的宏语言后来用于 Q-32 的 Lisp 系统 [Saunders, 1985b]。检查编译器代码中的 MDEF 函数 [Saunders, 1985a, p. 311] 可以发现，处理 PROG 语句的错误已修复：mdef[caddr[l]] 被替换为 mdef[cddr[l]]。（事实上，这可能就是 Hart 原本的意思；在 [Hart, 1963] 中，“a”似乎是手写覆盖另一个字母的更正。也许打字员先误打成 mdef[ccddr[l]]，后来又作了错误更正。）遗憾的是，mdef[cddr[l]] 也有自己的问题：LAMBDA 表达式返回其值的变量，或 PROG 开头的标签，可能被错误识别为宏名，从而把 LAMBDA 或 PROG 的主体当作宏调用。真是挑剔，不过如今我们确实会尽力谨慎处理这类事情。

Hart’s macro language was subsequently used in the Lisp system for the Q-32 [Saunders, 1985b]. Inspection of the MDEF function in the compiler code [Saunders, 1985a, p. 311] reveals that the error in processing PROG statements had been repaired: mdef[caddr[l]] was replaced by mdef[cddr[l]]. (In fact, this may be what Hart had originally intended; in [Hart, 1963] the “a” appears to have been written in by hand as a correction over another letter. Perhaps the typist had made the typographical error mdef[ccddr[l]] and subsequently a wrong correction was made.) Unfortunately, the use of mdef[cddr[l]] has its own problems: a variable whose value is returned by a LAMBDA expression or a tag at the head of a PROG might be incorrectly recognized as the name of a macro, thereby treating the body of the LAMBDA or PROG form as a macro call. Picky, picky—but nowadays we do try to be careful about that sort of thing.

MIT PDP-6 Lisp 也出现了类似的计算式宏，但宏调用在编译器或解释器遇到时即时展开。对解释器而言，如果函数调用形式中显式命名的函数，其属性表上有 MACRO 属性，而不是 EXPR、SUBR、LSUBR、FEXPR 或 FSUBR 这些函数指示符之一，那么就把原始宏调用形式作为参数交给该函数定义，并要求它返回另一个形式，以替代该调用进行求值。PDP-6 Lisp 备忘录 [PDP-6 Lisp, 1967] 给出的例子是：

A similar sort of computed macro appeared in the MIT PDP-6 Lisp, but macros calls were expanded on the fly as they were encountered by the compiler or the interpreter. In the case of the interpreter, if an explicitly named function in a function call form turned out to have a MACRO property on its property list (rather than one of the function indicators EXPR, SUBR, LSUBR, FEXPR, or FSUBR) then the function definition was given the original macro call form as an argument and was expected to return another form to be evaluated in place of the call. The example given in the PDP-6 Lisp memo [PDP-6 Lisp, 1967] was

(DEFPROP CONSCONS
         (LAMBDA (A)
                 (COND ((NULL (CDDR A)) (CADR A))
                       ((LIST (QUOTE CONS)
                              (CADR A)
                              (CONS (CAR A)
                                    (CDDR A)))))
         MACRO)

它定义的宏，在效果上等价于 Common Lisp 的 list* 函数。注意这里用 DEFPROP 定义函数，用 QUOTE 而不是单引号字符，并省略了如今几乎普遍惯用的、第二个 COND 子句中的 T。

This defined a macro equivalent in effect to the Common Lisp function list*. Note the use of DEFPROP to define a function, the use of QUOTE rather than a single quote character, and the omission of the now almost universally customary T in the second COND clause.

这种方案的优点是，可以先定义使用宏的函数，稍后再定义或重定义宏；宏调用始终使用最新定义。但缺点是，每次重复执行同一宏调用，解释器都必须重新展开，降低执行速度。MacLisp 用户很快普遍使用一种称为置换宏的机制，它用到工具函数 DISPLACE：

An advantage of this scheme was that one could define some functions that use macros and later define (or redefine) the macro; a macro call would always use the latest macro definition. A drawback, however, was that the interpreter must constantly re-expand the same macro call every time it is repeated, reducing speed of execution. A device called displacing macros soon became common among MacLisp users; it involved the utility function DISPLACE:

(DEFUN DISPLACE (OLD NEW)
  (RPLACA OLD (CAR NEW))
  (RPLACD OLD (CDR NEW))
  OLD)

然后可以写：

One would then write

(DEFPROP CONSCONS
         (LAMBDA (A)
            (DISPLACE A
                 (COND ...)))
         MACRO)

其效果是破坏性地修改宏调用原来的列表结构，用展开结果替换它。

The effect was to destructively alter the original list structure of the macro call so as to replace it with its expansion.

这个过于聪明的技巧也有缺点。第一，如果宏需要展开为原子，例如数字或变量引用，就会失败；宏作者于是学会生成 (PROGN FOO) 而不是 FOO。第二，如果宏调用被置换后又重定义了宏，后续执行该调用时不会使用新定义。第三，代码被修改了：美化打印原本含有宏调用的代码时，显示的是展开结果，而不是原调用。最后这个缺点之所以还能容忍，只因为 MacLisp 环境非常坚定地以文件为基础：置换宏只修改程序在内存中的副本，不影响被视为某个文件中文本的主定义。这类置换宏在 Interlisp 中将无法容忍。

This all-too-clever trick had drawbacks of its own. First, it failed if a macro needed to expand to an atom (such as a number or variable reference); macro writers learned to produce (PROGN FOO) instead of FOO. Second, if a macro were redefined after a call to it had been displaced, subsequent executions of the call would not use the new definition. Third, the code was modified; prettyprinting code that originally contained a macro call would display the expansion, not the original macro call. This last drawback was tolerable only because the MacLisp environment was so firmly file-based: displacing macros modified only the in-core copy of a program; it did not affect the master definition, which was considered to be the text in some file. Displacing macros of this kind would have been intolerable in Interlisp.

约在1978年，Lisp Machine Lisp 改进了置换技术：

Around 1978, Lisp Machine Lisp introduced an improvement to the displacement technique:

(defun displace (old new)
  (rplacd new (list (cons (car old) (cdr old)) new))
  (rplaca old ’si:displaced)
  new)
(defmacro si:displaced (old new) new)

其思路是用列表 (si:displaced macro-call expansion) 置换宏调用。si:displaced 宏透明地返回第二个参数形式，因此一切表现得就像展开结果本身替换了宏调用。展开 si:displaced 调用并非没有成本，但想必比不断重新展开原始宏调用便宜；否则宏作者就不该使用 displace。Lisp 机器的美化打印器识别对 si:displace 的调用，并只打印原始宏调用。

The idea is that the macro call is displaced by a list (si:displaced macro-call expansion). The macro si:displaced transparently returns its second argument form, so everything behaves as if the expansion itself had replaced the macro call. While expanding the call to si:displaced is not free, it is presumably cheaper than continually re-expanding the original macro call (if not, then the macro writer shouldn’t use displace). The Lisp Machine pretty-printer recognizes calls to si:displace and prints only the original macro call.

回到1960年代：MacLisp 很快用 DEFUN 记法替代了 DEFPROP 记法 [White, 1969—1982，Jon L White，1969年3月1日第1项]：

Returning to the 1960’s: The DEFPROP notation was soon replaced by the DEFUN notation in MacLisp [White, 1969–1982, Jon L White, item 1 of March 1, 1969]:

DEFUN 是用于定义函数的 FSUBR。例如：

DEFUN is an FSUBR used to define functions. Examples are

(DEFUN ONECONS (X) (CONS 1 X))

等价于：

which is equivalent to

(DEFPROP ONECONS
         (LAMBDA (X) (CONS 1 X))
 EXPR)

而：

and

(DEFUN SMASH FEXPR (L) (RPLACD L NIL))

［它］等价于：

[which] is equivalent to

(DEFPROP SMASH
         (LAMBDA (L) (RPLACD L NIL))
 FEXPR)

DEFUN 的新颖之处在于，不必过分担心函数定义最末尾的括号配对，因为类型标记若为 EXPR 就可省略，若为其他类型则出现在 DEFUN 列表靠前的位置。此外，也不必直接插入 LAMBDA。

The novel feature of DEFUN is that one need not be so concerned with balancing parentheses at the very end of the function definition, since the type flag may be omitted if it is EXPR, and appears near the front of the DEFUN list if it is some other. Also, the LAMBDA need not be directly inserted.

White 在别处还指出 [?]：

White elsewhere further noted [?]:

［DEFPROP］一直是 MacLisp 中定义函数的通常方法，但实现 DEFUN 是为了更清楚地表明正在执行函数定义，并允许对属性表结构和函数表示进行实验。

[DEFPROP] has been the usual method of defining functions in MacLisp, but DEFUN has been implemented to provide a more clear indication that such a definition is being performed, and to allow experimentation in the structure of p-list and function representation.

DEFUN 本身很容易实现为宏，但实际并非如此；由于使用极为频繁，为了效率、避免分配 cons 单元，它被手写为 FSUBR，并由编译器特殊识别。（Common Lisp 最终规定 defun 应当是宏。）

DEFUN itself might easily have been implemented as a macro, but it was not; because it was used so frequently, for reasons of efficiency (to avoid consing) it was hand-coded as an FSUBR and recognized specially by the compiler. (Common Lisp eventually specified that defun should be a macro.)

BBN Lisp [Teitelman, 1971] 有三种宏：开放式、计算式和替换式，下文会介绍。宏定义以 MACRO 属性存放在宏名的属性表中，属性值的形式决定它属于哪一种。最初，三种宏都只在编译代码中有效。不过，BBN Lisp 后来成为 Interlisp [Teitelman, 1978] 后，加入了名为 MACROTRAN 的 DWIM 技巧，使三种宏也能用于解释代码。如果解释函数调用时发生“未定义函数”错误，DWIM 系统就会介入。MACROTRAN 获得控制权，展开宏，并对展开结果求值。Interlisp 手册明确指出，只有启用 DWIM，解释执行的宏才能工作。与之相比，MIT 的做法是把宏直接内置于解释器和编译器，作为原始语言特性。

BBN Lisp [Teitelman, 1971] had three kinds of macro: open, computed, and substitution (described below). A macro definition was stored in the property list of its name under the MACRO property; the form of the property value determined which of three types of macro it was. Originally all three types were effective only in compiled code. Eventually, however, after BBN Lisp became Interlisp [Teitelman, 1978], a DWIM hack called MACROTRAN was added that made all three types of macro effective in interpreted code. If interpreting a function call resulted in an “undefined function” error, the DWIM system would step in. MACROTRAN would gain control, expand the macro, and evaluate the resulting expansion. The Interlisp manual duly notes that interpreted macros will work only if DWIM is enabled. Contrast this with the MIT approach of building macros directly into the interpreter (as well as the compiler) as a primitive language feature.

BBN Lisp 的开放式宏，只是把宏名替换为 lambda 表达式，使函数以“开放”或内联方式编译。下面是 ABS 的开放式宏定义：

A BBN-Lisp open macro simply caused the macro name to be replaced by a lambda expression, causing the function to be compiled “open” or in-line. Here is an open macro definition for ABS:

(LAMBDA (X) (COND ((GREATERP X 0) X) (T (MINUS X))))

当然，它的形式与函数定义完全相同。

Of course this has exactly the same form as a function definition.

BBN Lisp 的计算式宏类似 MIT PDP-6 Lisp 的宏，但展开函数收到的是宏调用的 CDR，而不是整个宏调用。下面是 LIST 的计算式宏：

A BBN-Lisp computed macro was similar to the kind in MIT PDP-6 Lisp, except that the expander function received the CDR of the macro call rather than the entire macro call. Here is a computed macro for LIST:

(X (LIST (QUOTE CONS)
         (CAR X)
         (AND (CDR X)
              (CONS (QUOTE LIST)
                    (CDR X]

开头的 X 是一个变量名，将绑定到宏调用形式的 CDR。还要注意，定义中使用了闭合超级括号（见第2.3节）。

The leading X is the name of a variable to be bound to the CDR of the macro call form. Note also the use of a closing superbracket in the definition (see section 2.3).

BBN Lisp 的替换式宏由简单模式（参数列表）和替换模板组成；宏调用的子形式会替换模板中对应参数名的出现位置。ABS 的替换式宏如下：

A BBN-Lisp substitution macro consisted of a simple pattern (a parameter list) and a substitution template; subforms of the macro call were substituted for occurrences in the template of corresponding parameter names. A substitution macro for ABS would look like this:

((X) (COND ((GREATERP X 0) X) (T (MINUS X))))

但是，调用 (ABS (FOO Z)) 会展开为：

However, the call (ABS (FOO Z)) would be expanded to

(COND ((GREATERP (FOO Z) 0) (FOO Z)) (T (MINUS (FOO Z))))

这会导致对 (FOO Z) 多次求值；如果它计算代价很高或有副作用，就很不妙。相比之下，使用开放式宏时，调用 (ABS (FOO Z)) 会展开为：

leading to multiple evaluations of (FOO Z), which would be unfortunate if (FOO Z) were an expensive computation or had side effects. By way of contrast, with an open macro the call (ABS (FOO Z)) would be expanded to

((LAMBDA (X) (COND ((GREATERP X 0) X) (T (MINUS X)))) (FOO Z))

这样，(FOO Z) 恰好只求值一次。

which would evaluate (FOO Z) exactly once.

不过，尽管有时需要小心避免重复求值，用模式和模板定义宏的方法仍然十分方便，视觉上也很直观。事实上，模式匹配和模板方法，是1960年代至1970年代人工智能语言发展中普遍关注的主题，见第4节。下文还会回到基于模板的宏。

Despite the care sometimes required to avoid multiple evaluation, however, the pattern/template methodology for defining macros is very convenient and visually appealing. Indeed, pattern matching and template methodologies were a pervasive topic in the development of languages for Artificial Intelligence throughout the 1960’s and 1970’s; see section 4. We will return to the topic of templatebased macros below.

不出所料，Muddle [Galley, 1975] 的宏设施很像 PDP-6 Lisp，但有一点不同：不是以宏形式为单个参数调用宏展开函数，而是把该函数应用于该形式的 CDR。这使 Muddle 复杂的参数列表关键字派上用场，支持某些简单模式匹配，类似 Interlisp 的替换式宏：

Muddle [Galley, 1975], not surprisingly, had a macro facility very much like that of PDP-6 Lisp, with one slight difference. The macro expansion function, rather than being called with the macro form as its argument, was applied to the CDR of the form. This allowed Muddle’s complex argument-list keywords to come into play, allowing certain simple kinds of pattern matching as for Interlisp’s substitution macros:

<DEFMAC INC (ATM "OPTIONAL" (N 1))
        <FORM SET .ATM <FORM + <FORM LVAL .ATM> .N>>>

不过，仍须像计算式宏那样费力构造结果。展开 <INC X> 的结果是 <SET X <+ .X 1>>。注意，Muddle 中的 .X 只是 <LVAL X> 的读取宏缩写，表示 X 的局部值。

It was nevertheless necessary to laboriously construct the result as for a computed macro. The result of expanding <INC X> would be <SET X <+ .X 1>> (note that in Muddle .X is merely a readmacro abbreviation for <LVAL X>, the local value of X).

随着 MacLisp 从 PDP-6 Lisp 发展而来，MacLisp 社区日益多样化，产生了各种定义宏的方法。INC 这样的简单宏，在概念上很容易编写，只是有些繁琐，肯定比 Muddle 或 Interlisp 笨拙：

As MacLisp grew out of PDP-6 Lisp, the MacLisp community diversified, producing a variety of methodologies for defining macros. Simple macros such as INC were conceptually straightforward to write, if a bit cumbersome (certainly more clumsy than in Muddle or Interlisp):

(DEFUN INC MACRO (X)
       (LIST ’SETQ
             (CADR X)
             (LIST ’PLUS
                   (CADR X)
                   (COND ((CDDR X) (CADDR X)) (T 1)))))

注意，由于缺少对参数形式的自动分解，即“解构”，构造结果的代码中出现了许多 CAR、CDR 和 COND。可以用 LET 把解构与构造分开：

Note that the lack of automatic decomposition (“destructuring”) of the argument forms leads to many uses of CAR, CDR, and COND within the code that constructs the result. One can use LET to separate the destructuring from the construction:

(DEFUN INC MACRO (X)
  (LET ((VAR (CADR X))
        (N (COND ((CDDR X) (CADDR X)) (T 1)))
    (LIST ’SETQ VAR (LIST ’PLUS VAR N)))

但是，LET 本身也是各站点先在本地反复发明的宏，在 MacLisp 世界中出现得很晚。据 Lisp Archive 记载，直到1979年，PDP-10 MacLisp 才从 Lisp-Machine Lisp 回头吸收了 LET，同时吸收的还有 DEFMACRO 和 Lisp 机器复杂的 DEFUN 参数语法。在1970年代，能做的最好办法大概就是使用 LAMBDA 表达式：

but LET—itself a macro first invented and reinvented locally at each site—was a late-comer to the MacLisp world; according to Lisp Archive, it was retroactively absorbed into PDP-10 MacLisp from Lisp-Machine Lisp in 1979 at the same time as DEFMACRO and the complex Lisp Machine DEFUN argument syntax. About the best one could do during the 1970’s was to use a LAMBDA expression:

(DEFUN INC MACRO (X)
  ((LAMBDA (VAR N)
     (LIST ’SETQ VAR (LIST ’PLUS VAR N))
(CADR X)
(COND ((CDDR X) (CADDR X)) (T 1))))

许多程序员觉得这种写法实在不讨喜。因此，编写复杂宏是一门相当困难的艺术，高手们发展出各自独立的宏定义风格。下面是 Jon L White 给程序员的一些建议 [White, 1969—1982，1974年5月]：

and many programmers found this none too attractive. As a result, the writing of complex macros was a fairly difficult art, and wizards developed their own separate styles of macro definition. Here is some advice to the programmer from Jon L White [White, 1969–1982, May 1974]:

假设你已经执行：

Suppose you have done

(SETQ BARODD (ARRAY NIL FIXNUM N)
      BAREVEN (ARRAY NIL FIXNUM N))

此时，BARODD 和 BAREVEN 的值都为数组指针……让我们定义两个宏：

Now at this point, both BARODD and BAREVEN hold as value an array pointer. . . . Let us define two macros

(DEFUN MACRO BO (X)
  (SUBST (CADR X) ’INDEX ’(ARRAYCALL FIXNUM BARODD INDEX)))
(DEFUN MACRO BE (X)
  (SUBST (CADR X) ’INDEX ’(ARRAYCALL FIXNUM BAREVEN INDEX)))

然后就可以这样填充 BARODD 和 BAREVEN：

Then we could fill BARODD and BAREVEN as follows:

(DO J 1 (1+ J) (> J N) (STORE (BE (1- J)) (* 2 J)))
(DO J 0 (1+ J) (NOT (< J N)) (STORE (BO J) (1+ (* 2 J))))

诚然，这节省了许多输入。但假如你有一大批这样的数组变量，想用这种 MACRO 缩写，逐个输入宏定义就会成为乏味的重复劳动。请看下面这个用于定义宏的宏，以及它的用法：

Admittedly, this saves a lot of typing. But suppose you have a host of such array variables that you would like to abbreviate with such a MACRO. Typing in all the macro definitions could be tediously repetitive. Consider the following macro-defining macro and some of its uses:

(DEFUN MACRO ABBA (Y)
       (SUBLIS (LIST (CONS ’SHORT (CADR Y))
                     (CONS ’LONG (CADDR Y))
                     (CONS ’TYPE (CADDR Y)))
               ’(DEFUN MACRO SHORT (X)
                  (SUBST (CDR X)
                         ’INDEXLIST
                         ’(ARRAYCALL TYPE LONG . INDEXLIST)))))

现在可以用 ABBA 生成 BE 的宏，不过注意宏的形式稍有不同：宏输出的主体看起来是点列表，而非标准列表。这是为了让不同维数的数组，都能用同一个超级宏定义其缩写。

Now we might use ABBA to produce the macro for BE, but note that the form of the macro is slightly different—the main body of the macro output appears to be a dottedlist rather than a standard list. This is so that arrays of varying numbers of dimensions may have their abbreviations defined by the same super-macro.

(ABBA BO BARODD FIXNUM)

展开为：

expands into

(DEFUN MACRO BO (X)
  (SUBST (CDR X)
         ’INDEXLIST
         ’(ARRAYCALL FIXNUM BARODD . INDEXLIST)))

它随后为 BO 建立适当的宏定义。正如你所料，(BO J) 会展开为：

which then causes the appropriate macro definition for BO. As you would expect, then, (BO J) expands into

(ARRAYCALL FIXNUM BARODD J)

不过，再考虑这样定义的二维散列数组 HASH：

But consider the two-dimensional hash array HASH defined as

(SETQ HASH (ARRAY NIL T 37 37))

那么，(ABBA HA HASH T) 定义 HA，使 (HA 3 (+ N 2)) 展开为 (ARRAYCALL T HASH 3 (+ N 2))。Guy Steele 已经积累了一个包含复杂宏和定义宏之宏的文件，有兴趣的人可以向他请教。

Then (ABBA HA HASH T) defines HA so that (HA 3 (+ N 2)) expands into (ARRAYCALL T HASH 3 (+ N 2)). Guy Steele has accumulated a file of sophisticated macros and macro-defining macros, and the interested may consult with him about them.

要看这种做法多么容易失控，可以考虑一个简单 FOR 循环的宏。典型用法如下：

To see how easily this can get out of hand, consider a macro for a simple FOR loop. A typical use would be this:

(for a 1 100
  (print a)
  (print (* a a)))

它应当展开为：

This should expand to

(do a 1 (+ a 1) (> a 100)
  (print a)
  (print (* a a)))

这是一个微不足道的语法转换，简单但方便：用稍微更通用的“旧式” MacLisp DO 循环，定义类似 Fortran DO 循环的语法，见第3.2节。在1970年代初的 MacLisp 中，可以这样定义：

This is a trivial syntactic transformation, simple but convenient, defining a Fortran–DO-loop–like syntax in terms of the slightly more general “old-style” MacLisp DO loop (see section 3.2). In the MacLisp of the early 1970’s one might define it as follows:

(defun for macro (x)
  (cons ’do
        (cons (cadr x)
              (cons (caddr x)
                    (cons (list ’+ (cadr x) 1)
                          (cons (list ’> (cadr x) (cadddr x))
                                (cddddr x)))))))

为了如此简单的转换，却要写这么多代码。

That’s a lot to write for such a simple transformation.

最终，各 MacLisp 站点的高手发展出几十种相似但不完全兼容的宏定义宏。同一站点同时使用几套这样的包并不罕见，各派追随者使用自家高手开发的那一套。这些包通常包含解构参数形式和构造结果形式的工具。构造结果的工具主要分两类：替换和伪引用。替换技术要求单独列出模板中要被指定值替换的某些符号。伪引用则允许计算替换值的代码直接出现在模板中；之所以称为伪引用，是因为模板外包着一个“就像 quote 一样”的操作符调用，只是模板中有特别标记的位置例外。

Eventually the gurus at various MacLisp sites developed dozens of similar but not quite compatible macro-defining macros. It was not unusual for several such packages to be in use at the same site, with the adherents of each sect using whatever their wizard had developed. Such packages usually included tools for destructuring argument forms and for constructing result forms. The tools for constructing result forms fell into two major categories: substitution and pseudo-quoting. Substitution techniques required separate mention of certain symbols in a template that were to be replaced by specified values. Pseudo-quoting allowed the code to compute a replacement value to occur within the template itself; it was called pseudo-quoting because the template was surrounded by a call to an operator that was “just like quote” except for specially marked places within the template.

Lisp-Machine Lisp 使宏向前迈出了一大步：它把各种宏定义技术整合为两个标准化特性，随后被整个 MacLisp 社区采用，最终进入 Common Lisp。宏定义操作符 DEFMACRO 提供任意深度的列表结构解构；反引号特性提供方便、简洁的伪引用设施。下面是只使用 DEFMACRO 的 FOR 宏定义：

Macros took a major step forward with Lisp-Machine Lisp, which consolidated the various macro-defining techniques into two standardized features that were adopted throughout the MacLisp community and eventually into Common Lisp. The macro-defining operator DEFMACRO provided list-structure destructuring to arbitrary depth; the backquote feature provided a convenient and concise pseudo-quoting facility. Here is the definition of the FOR macro using DEFMACRO alone:

(defmacro for (var lower upper . body)
  (cons ’do
        (cons var
              (cons lower
                    (cons (list ’+ var 1)
                          (cons (list ’> var upper)
                                body))))))

注意，现在可以给原始形式的各部分命名。使用反引号伪引用语法后，就得到十分简洁易读的定义：它复制模板，并把每个逗号标记的位置，填入其后表达式的值。

Notice that we can name the parts of the original form. Using the backquote pseudo-quoting syntax, which makes a copy of a template, filling in each place marked by a comma with the value of the following expression, we get a very concise and easy-to-read definition:

(defmacro for (var lower upper . body)
  ‘(do ,var ,lower (+ ,var 1) (> ,var ,upper) ,@body))

注意，,@ 表示拼接。

Note the use of ,@ to indicate splicing.

反引号语法在嵌套时尤其强大。这主要发生在定义宏的宏中；由于这类代码主要由高手编写，编写和理解嵌套反引号表达式的能力，很快被笼罩上一层神秘色彩。在 Lisp 机器早期，MIT 的 Alan Bawden 尤其以反引号大师著称。

The backquote syntax was particularly powerful when nested. This occurred primarily within macro-defining macros; because such were coded primarily by wizards, the ability to write and interpret nested backquote expressions was soon surrounded by a certain mystique. Alan Bawden of MIT acquired a particular reputation as backquote-meister in the early days of the Lisp Machine.

作为嵌套使用反引号的例子，考虑前面那个宏定义宏 ABBA 的以下版本：

As an example of the nested use of backquote, consider the following version of the macrodefining macro ABBA presented above:

(DEFMACRO ABBA (SHORT LONG TYPE)
  ‘(DEFMACRO ,SHORT (&REST INDEXLIST)
     ‘(ARRAYCALL ,’,TYPE ,’,LONG ,@INDEXLIST)))

这短得多，而且至少对 Bawden 来说，更容易理解。难点在于“逗号、单引号、逗号”这一惯用写法，它说明了为什么除了伪引用，还需要普通引用。这些表达式最容易从内向外理解。内部的反引号表达式：

This is considerably shorter, and (to Bawden, at least) easier to understand. The tricky part is that idiom comma-quote-comma, which illustrates the need to have a plain quote as well as a pseudoquote. These expressions are most easily understood from the inside out. The inner backquote expression

‘(ARRAYCALL ,’,TYPE ,’,LONG ,@INDEXLIST)

等价于：

is equivalent to

(LIST* ’ARRAYCALL ’,TYPE ’,LONG INDEXLIST)

它当然又等同于：

which of course is the same as

(LIST* ’ARRAYCALL (QUOTE ,TYPE) (QUOTE ,LONG) INDEXLIST)

因此：

Therefore

‘(DEFMACRO ,SHORT (&REST INDEXLIST)
   ‘(ARRAYCALL ,’,TYPE ,’,LONG ,@INDEXLIST))

意味着：

means

‘(DEFMACRO ,SHORT (&REST INDEXLIST)
   (LIST* ’ARRAYCALL ’,TYPE ’,LONG INDEXLIST))

它又等同于：

which is the same as

(LIST ’DEFMACRO SHORT ’(&REST INDEXLIST)
      (LIST ’LIST*
            ’’ARRAYCALL
            (LIST ’QUOTE TYPE)
            (LIST ’QUOTE LONG)
            ’INDEXLIST))

所以，ABBA 等同于：

and so ABBA is the same as

(DEFMACRO ABBA (SHORT LONG TYPE)
  (LIST ’DEFMACRO SHORT ’(&REST INDEXLIST)
        (LIST ’LIST*
              ’’ARRAYCALL
              (LIST ’QUOTE TYPE)
              (LIST ’QUOTE LONG)
              ’INDEXLIST)))

但这比使用反引号的版本难读得多。更多嵌套反引号示例，见 [Steele, 1990c] 的附录 C。

but this is much harder to read than the version with backquotes. For more examples of nested backquotes, see Appendix C of [Steele, 1990c].

反引号和 DEFMACRO 带来了巨大变化。以标准形式提供的这次表达能力飞跃，掀起了新一轮语言扩展热潮，因为现在更容易以标准、可移植的方式定义新语言构造，从而共享实验性方言。包括 David Moon 在内的一些人认为，Lisp 作为语言设计者工具箱的成功，很大程度上归功于用户能定义宏：以 Lisp 为处理语言，以列表结构为程序表示，使扩展语言语法和语义变得容易。1980年，Kent Pitman 对使用宏而非 FEXPR 定义新语言语法的优点，作了很好的总结 [Pitman, 1980]。

Backquote and DEFMACRO made a big difference. This leap in expressive power, made available in a standard form, began a new surge of language extension because it was now much easier to define new language constructs in a standard, portable way so that experimental dialects could be shared. Some, including David Moon, have opined that the success of Lisp as a language designer’s kit is largely due to the ability of the user to define macros that use Lisp as the processing language and list structure as the program representation, making it easy to extend the language syntax and semantics. In 1980 Kent Pitman wrote a very good summary of the advantages of macros over FEXPR’s in defining new language syntax [Pitman, 1980].

不过，并非每个宏都容易用这种新格式表达。考虑前面讨论的 INC 宏。截至1978年11月，Lisp 机器 DEFMACRO 的解构能力还不足以处理“可选”参数形式：

Not every macro was easy to express in this new format, however. Consider the INC macro discussed above. As of November 1978, the Lisp Machine DEFMACRO destructuring was not quite rich enough to handle “optional” argument forms:

(DEFUN INC MACRO (VAR . REST)
  ‘(SETQ ,VAR (+ ,VAR ,(IF REST (CAR REST) 1))))

可选部分必须通过显式编程的条件式处理，这里用 IF 表达。IF 本身也是引入 Lisp-Machine Lisp 的宏，可能受 Scheme 影响，展开为等价的 COND 形式。这一不足很快被发现并修复：允许 DEFMACRO 接受与 DEFUN 相同的复杂 lambda 列表语法：

The optional part must be handled with an explicitly programmed conditional (expressed here using IF, which was itself introduced into Lisp-Machine Lisp, probably under the influence of Scheme, as a macro that expanded into an equivalent COND form). This deficiency was soon noticed and quickly remedied by allowing DEFMACRO to accept the same complex lambda-list syntax as DEFUN:

(DEFUN INC MACRO (VAR &OPTIONAL (N 1))
  ‘(SETQ ,VAR (+ ,VAR ,N)))

据 Lisp Archive 记载，这发生在1979年1月；当时 MacLisp 从 Lisp-Machine Lisp 吸收了 DEFMACRO，以及带 & 关键字的 DEFUN。

This occurred in January 1979, according to Lisp Archive, at which time MacLisp absorbed DEFMACRO and DEFUN with &-keywords from Lisp-Machine Lisp.

另一个问题是，这个框架无法捕捉重复语法，也就是可在扩展 BNF 中用 Kleene 星号表达的那类结构，必须显式编程。请看这个简单的 LET 定义：

An additional problem was that repetitive syntax, of the kind that might be expressed in extended BNF with a Kleene star, was not captured by this framework and had to be programmed explicitly. Contemplate this simple definition of LET:

(defmacro let (bindings . body)
  ‘((lambda ,@(mapcar #’car bindings) ,@body)
    ,@(mapcar #’cadr bindings)))

注意这里使用 MAPCAR 迭代处理绑定。Lisp-Machine Lisp 和 Common Lisp 都没有解决这个困难；在那个社区，带 & 关键字的 DEFMACRO 至今仍是最先进的做法。不过，Common Lisp 确实推广了 DEFMACRO，允许这种 lambda 列表递归嵌套。

Note the use of MAPCAR for iterative processing of the bindings. This difficulty was not tackled by Lisp-Machine Lisp or Common Lisp; in that community DEFMACRO with &-keywords is the state of the art today. Common Lisp did, however, generalize DEFMACRO to allow recursive nesting of such lambda-lists.

Lisp 宏理论与实践的进一步发展，主要由关注作用域问题的 Scheme 社区推动。宏充斥着与特殊变量相同的作用域问题和意外名称捕获。从1963年的 Hart 到1980年代中期，Lisp 宏的问题在于：宏调用展开为一个由符号组成的表达式，而这些符号没有附带语义。把展开结果替换回程序时，其含义可能随局部环境而发生相当意外的变化。其他语言的宏也有同样问题，例如 C 预处理器 [Kernighan, 1978; Harbison, 1991]，只要它们通过直接替换文本或词法单元工作。

Further development of the theory and practice of Lisp macros was carried forward primarily by the Scheme community, which was interested in scoping issues. Macros are fraught with the same kinds of scoping problems and accidental name capture that had accompanied special variables. The problem with Lisp macros, from the time of Hart in 1963 to the mid-1980’s, is that a macro call expands into an expression that is composed of symbols that have no attached semantics. When substituted back into the program, a macro expansion could conceivably take on a quite surprising meaning depending on the local environment. (Macros in other languages—the C preprocessor [Kernighan, 1978; Harbison, 1991] is one example—have the same problem if they operate by straight substitution of text or tokens.)

一种实用的规避办法，是宏作者尽量选择用户不太可能碰巧用到的名字：例如 %%foo%% 这样的怪名字，尽管聪明人想到一起的频率高得惊人；或使用 gensym，正如 Hart 在前面的 select 示例中所做；或使用多个 obarray 或包避免名称冲突。但这些技术都不能提供绝对保证。Steele 指出，谨慎使用 thunk，可以证明能消除该问题，但并非适用于所有情况 [Steele, 1978a]。

One practical way to avoid such problems is for the macro writer to try to choose names that the user is unlikely to stumble across, either by picking strange names such as %%foo%% (though it is surprising how often great minds will think alike), or by using gensym (as Hart did in his select example, shown above), or by using multiple obarrays or packages to avoid name clashes. However, none of these techniques provides an iron-glad guarantee. Steele pointed out that careful use of thunks could provably eliminate the problem, though not in all situations [Steele, 1978a].

Scheme 的支持者认为，这些安排要么缺陷太多，要么过于笨拙，不适合“正式”纳入 Scheme。结果是 Scheme 在1980年代分化了。几乎每个实现都有某种宏设施，但没有两种相同。几乎所有人都同意，宏设施在原理和实践中都极有价值，却又看不起每个具体实例，把它当作某种见不得人的家族秘密。要是能找到“正确的做法”就好了！随着制定 Scheme 标准的可能性被反复讨论，这个问题变得更加紧迫。

The proponents of Scheme regarded all of these arrangements as too flawed or too clumsy for “official” adoption into Scheme. The result was that Scheme diversified in the 1980’s. Nearly every implementation had some kind of macro facility but no two were alike. Nearly everyone agreed that macro facilities were invaluable in principle and in practice but looked down upon each particular instance as a sort of shameful family secret. If only The Right Thing could be found! This question became more pressing as the possibility of developing a Scheme standard was bandied about.

1980年代中期，出现了两类新提案：卫生宏和语法闭包。两者都使用特殊的语法环境，确保引用正确匹配定义。一个相关研究方向，则允许程序员通过显式传递和操纵展开器函数来控制展开过程 [Dybvig, 1986]。这些方案都旨在成为 Scheme 的宏设施，因为以前的方法被认为缺陷太深，不适合纳入这样一种其他方面都很优雅的语言。

In the mid-1980’s two new sorts of proposals were put forward: hygienic macros and syntactic closures. Both approaches involve the use of special syntactic environments to ensure that references are properly matched to definitions. A related line of work allows the programmer to control the expansion process by explicitly passing around and manipulating expander functions [Dybvig, 1986]. All of these were intended as macro facilities for Scheme, previous methods being regarded as too deeply flawed for adoption into such an otherwise elegant language.

卫生宏由 Eugene Kohlbecker 于1986年在 Daniel Friedman、Matthias Felleisen 和 Bruce Duba 协助下开发 [Kohlbecker, 1986a]。其思路是给变量的每次出现加上标签，标明它来自原始源代码，还是由宏展开引入；如果发生多次宏展开，标签还必须标明涉及哪一步展开。该技术通过重命名变量，使变量引用不能指向另一步骤引入的绑定。

Hygienic macros were developed in 1986 by Eugene Kohlbecker with assistance from Daniel Friedman, Matthias Felleisen, and Bruce Duba [Kohlbecker, 1986a]. The idea is to label the occurrences of variables with a tag indicating whether it appeared in the original source code or was introduced as a result of macro expansion; if multiple macro expansions occur, the tag must indicate which expansion step was involved. The technique renames variables so that a variable reference cannot refer to a binding introduced at a different step.

Kohlbecker 的博士论文 [Kohlbecker, 1986b] 又推进一步，提出一种用于定义宏的模式匹配和模板替换语言；底层机制自动使用卫生宏展开来避免名称冲突。宏定义语言足以表达许多有用的宏，但不允许执行任意用户指定的 Lisp 代码；人们认为，这一限制是防止破坏卫生保证所必需的。这种小语言本身也很有意思。它不如 DEFMACRO 与反引号各自独立的匹配和替换设施通用，后者还允许在两者之间执行任意计算；但它用类似 BNF 的记法支持可选形式和重复形式，并通过允许多个产生式、使用第一个匹配项来处理可选情况。例如，INC 可以定义为：

Kohlbecker’s Ph.D. dissertation [Kohlbecker, 1986b] carried this a step further by proposing a pattern matching and template substitution language for defining macros; the underlying mechanism automatically used hygienic macro expansion to avoid name clashes. The macro-defining language was rich enough to express a wide variety of useful macros, but provided no facility for the execution of arbitrary user-specified Lisp code; this restriction was thought necessary to avoid subversion of the guarantee of good hygiene. This little language is interesting in its own right. While not as general as the separate matching and substitution facilities of DEFMACRO and backquote (with the opportunity to perform arbitrary computations in between), it does allow for optional and repetitive forms by using a BNF-like notation, and allows for optional situations by permitting multiple productions and using the first one that matches. For example, INC might be defined as

(extend-syntax (inc) ()
  ((inc x) (inc x 1))
  ((inc x n) (setq x (+ x n))))

LET 则定义为：

and LET as

(extend-syntax (let) ()
  ((let ((var value) ...) body ...)
   ((lambda (var ...) body ...) value ...)))

省略号“...”起着某种 Kleene 星号的作用。注意，变量和值的配对如何被隐式解构，并在展开结果中重新排列为两个独立列表。

The ellipsis “...” serves as a kind of Kleene star. Note the way in which variable-value pairs are implicitly destructured and rearranged into two separate lists in the expansion.

传给 extend-syntax 的第一个列表是关键字列表；这些关键字属于宏语法的一部分，不应被标记为可能的变量引用。第二个列表列出可能由宏展开引入、但有意与参数形式交互的变量。例如，考虑用 Scheme 的 call-with-current-continuation 原语，实现归于 Dahl 的 n+½ 循环 [Knuth, 1974] 的一个略作推广的版本：

The first list given to extend-syntax is a list of keywords that are part of the macro syntax and not to be tagged as possible variable references. The second list mentions variables that may be introduced by the macro expansion but are intended to interact with the argument forms. For example, consider an implementation (using the Scheme call-with-current-continuation primitive) of a slight generalization of the n+ 1

它反复执行语句，直到 while 子句（若有）失败，或使用 exit 退出。

2 loop attributed to Dahl [Knuth, 1974]; it executes statements repeatedly until its while clause (if any) fails or until exit is used.

(extend-syntax (loop while repeat) (exit)
  ((loop e1 e2 ... repeat)
   (call/cc (lambda (exit)
              ((label foo
                 (lambda () e1 e2 ... (foo)))))))
  ((loop e1 ... while p e2 ... repeat)
   (call/cc (lambda (exit)
              ((label foo
                 (lambda () e1 ...
                            (unless p (exit #f))
                            e2 ...
                            (foo))))))))

在这个例子中，loop、while 和 repeat 是关键字，不应与可能的变量引用混淆；exit 由宏绑定，但有意供宏调用的参数形式使用。foo 这个名字则不打算用于这种用途，卫生宏展开器会在必要时重命名它，避免名称冲突。注意，要使一个名字可供外部使用，必须刻意操作；默认行为是采取安全做法，因此 extend-syntax 对初学宏作者更容易、更安全。还要注意“e1 e2 ...”这一惯用写法：没有 while 子句时，它要求至少存在一个形式。

In this example loop, while, and repeat are keywords and should not be confused with possible variable references; exit is bound by the macro but is intended for use in the argument forms of the macro call. The name foo is not intended for such use, and the hygienic macro expander will rename it if necessary to avoid name clashes. (Note that you have to try hard to make a name available; the default is to play it safe, which makes extend-syntax easier and safer for novice macro writers to use.) Note the use of the idiom “e1 e2 ...” to require that at least one form is present if there is no while clause.

语法闭包由 Alan Bawden 和 Jonathan Rees 于1988年提出 [Bawden, 1988]。其思想与 Dybvig、Friedman 和 Haynes 的展开器传递技术 [Dybvig, 1986] 很相似，但更通用。语法上下文不是用卫生宏展开中自动管理的标签表示，而是用环境对象表示；可以相对于这样的语法环境“闭合”一段代码，从而让宏作者显式控制符号各次出现之间的对应关系。语法闭包提供强大能力和灵活性，但把正确使用它们的责任交给程序员。

Syntactic closures were proposed in 1988 by Alan Bawden and Jonathan Rees [Bawden, 1988]. Their idea bears a strong resemblance to the expansion-passing technique of Dybvig, Friedman, and Haynes [Dybvig, 1986] but is more general. Syntactic contexts are represented not by the automatically managed tags of hygienic macro expansion but by environment objects; one may “close” a piece of code with respect to such a syntactic environment, thereby giving the macro writer explicit control over the correspondence between one occurrence of a symbol and another. Syntactic closures provide great power and flexibility but put the burden on the programmer to use them properly.

1990年，曾在印第安纳大学工作的 William Clinger 与 Rees 合作，提出一个综合方案，结合卫生宏和语法闭包的优点，而且运行时间从平方级降为线性。他们的技术恰如其分地叫作“能正确工作的宏”[Clinger, 1991]。其关键洞见可类比 lambda 演算中的归约来解释。有时必须用 α 转换重命名 lambda 演算表达式中的变量，使后续 β 归约不会产生名称冲突。这种重命名不能一次完成，必须与 β 归约交错进行，因为 β 归约可能复制一个 lambda 表达式，两份都绑定同名变量，并使一份的绑定与另一份冲突。宏也一样：重命名必须与宏展开交错进行。Clinger 和 Rees 的贡献，是澄清了这个问题，并提供快速、完整的解决方案。

In 1990, William Clinger (who used to be at Indiana University) joined forces with Rees to propose a grand synthesis that combines the benefits of hygienic macros and syntactic closures, with the added advantage of running in linear rather than quadratic time. Their technique is called, appropriately enough, “macros that work” [Clinger, 1991]. The key insight may be explained by analogy to reduction in the lambda calculus. Sometimes the rule of α-conversion must be applied to rename variables in a lambda-calculus expression so that a subsequent β-reduction will not produce a name clash. One cannot do such renaming all at once; it is necessary to intersperse renaming with the β-reductions, because a β-reduction can make two copies of a lambda-expression (hence both bind the same name) and bring the binding of one into conflict with that of the other. The same is true of macros: it is necessary to intersperse renaming with macro expansion. The contribution of Clinger and Rees was to clarify this problem and provide a fast, complete solution.

Scheme 标准 [IEEE, 1991] 在没有宏设施的情况下获通过，因此在这一点上，官方层面仍然一片混乱。宏仍是活跃的研究课题。

The Scheme standard [IEEE, 1991] was adopted without a macro facility, so confusion still officially reigns on this point. Macros remain an active research topic.

为什么宏对 Lisp 程序员如此重要？不仅因为它们提供语法便利，更因为它们是操纵程序的程序，而这一直是 Lisp 社区的核心主题。如果说 FORTRAN 是摆弄数字的语言，C 是摆弄字符和指针的语言，那么 Lisp 就是摆弄程序的语言。它的数据结构适合表示和操纵程序文本。宏是用元语言编写程序的最直接例子。因为 Lisp 是自己的元语言，所以可以把整门程序设计语言的力量都用于转换程序文本。

Why are macros so important to Lisp programmers? Not merely for the syntactic convenience they provide, but because they are programs that manipulate programs, which has always been a central theme in the Lisp community. If FORTRAN is the language that pushes numbers around, and C is the language that pushes characters and pointers around, then Lisp is the language that pushes programs around. Its data structures are useful for representing and manipulating program text. The macro is the most immediate example of a program written in a metalanguage. Because Lisp is its own metalanguage, the power of the entire programming language can be brought to bear on the task of transforming program text.

相比之下，C 预处理器极其贫弱；它的宏语言完全由替换和词法单元拼接组成。虽然有条件指令，也可以有条件地定义宏，但 C 宏不能展开成这样的条件指令。它既没有递归，也没有元递归；也就是说，C 宏既不能调用自身，也不能定义另一个宏。

By comparison, the C preprocessor is completely anemic; the macro language consists entirely of substitution and token concatenation. There are conditionals, and one may conditionally define a macro, but a C macro may not expand into such a conditional. There is neither recursion nor metarecursion, which is to say that a C macro can neither invoke itself nor define another macro.

Lisp 用户觉得这很可笑。他们非常重视把编程过程本身当作讨论对象和计算对象，并坚持为此拥有尽可能好的表达手段。既然可以使用完整的程序设计语言本身，为什么要满足于更少？

Lisp users find this laughable. They are very much concerned with the programming process as an object of discourse and an object of computation, and they insist on having the best possible means of expression for this purpose. Why settle for anything less than the full programming language itself?

3.4 数值功能

3.4 Numerical Facilities

在 Lisp 1.6 以及直到 PDP-6 Lisp 的阶段，多数 Lisp 系统最多只提供单字 fixnum（整数）和单字 flonum（浮点数）。（PDP-1 Lisp [Deutsch, 1985] 只有 fixnum；M-460 Lisp [Hart, 1985] 显然也是如此。7090 上的 Lisp 1.5 有浮点数 [McCarthy, 1962]，Q-32 Lisp [Saunders, 1985b] 和 PDP-6 Lisp [PDP-6 Lisp, 1967] 也有。）

In Lisp 1.6 and through PDP-6 Lisp, most Lisp systems offered at most single-word fixnums (integers) and single-word flonums (floating-point numbers). (PDP-1 Lisp [Deutsch, 1985] had only fixnums; apparently the same is true of the M-460 Lisp [Hart, 1985]. Lisp 1.5 on the 7090 had floating-point [McCarthy, 1962], as did Q-32 Lisp [Saunders, 1985b] and PDP-6 Lisp [PDP-6 Lisp, 1967].)

对于 bignum 的起源，我们仍不太确定。bignum 是一种使用可变存储量表示任意大整数的数据类型，大小受存放它的堆的总容量限制。它似乎大约同时出现在 MacLisp 和斯坦福 Lisp 1.6 中，也可能同时出现在 Standard Lisp 中。REDUCE [Hearn, 1971] 和 MACSYMA [Mathlab Group, 1977] 等符号代数程序需要它。如今，处理 bignum 已成为 Lisp 的鲜明特征，尽管不是绝对要求。Scheme 标准 [IEEE, 1991] 和 Common Lisp [Steele, 1990c] 都要求支持它。通常采用 Knuth 第2卷详述的算法 [Knuth, 1969; Knuth, 1981]。Jon L White 曾撰文介绍一组原语，使大部分 bignum 算术能用 Lisp 高效编写，而不必全部用汇编语言实现 [White, 1986]。

We are still a little uncertain about origin of bignums (a data type that uses a variable amount of storage so as to represent arbitrarily large integer values, subject to the total size of the heap, which is where bignums are stored). They seem to have appeared in MacLisp and Stanford Lisp 1.6 at roughly the same time, and perhaps also in Standard Lisp. They were needed for symbolic algebra programs such as REDUCE [Hearn, 1971] and MACSYMA [Mathlab Group, 1977]. Nowadays the handling of bignums is a distinguishing feature of Lisp, though not an absolute requirement. Both the Scheme Standard [IEEE, 1991] and Common Lisp [Steele, 1990c] require them. Usually the algorithms detailed in Knuth Volume 2 are used [Knuth, 1969; Knuth, 1981]. Jon L White wrote a paper about a set of primitives that allow one to code most of bignum arithmetic efficiently in Lisp, instead of having to code the whole thing in assembly language [White, 1986].

也有关于 BIGFLOAT 算术的文献。它已用于符号代数系统 [Mathlab Group, 1977]，但尚未成为 Lisp 方言的固定组成部分。Lisp 常被用作这类研究的平台，因为有了 bignum，工作就已完成三分之二 [Boehm, 1986; Vuillemin, 1988]。MacLisp 的 HAULONG 和 HAIPART 函数，是为支持 Macsyma 的 bigfloat 算术而引入的；它们后来成为 Common Lisp 的 INTEGER-LENGTH，以及经由 Lisp-Machine Lisp 传入的 LDB。

There is also a literature on BIGFLOAT arithmetic. It has been used in symbolic algebra systems [Mathlab Group, 1977], but has not become a fixture of Lisp dialects. Lisp is often used as a platform for this kind of research because having bignums gets you 2/3 of the way there [Boehm, 1986; Vuillemin, 1988]. The MacLisp functions HAULONG and HAIPART were introduced to support Macsyma’s bigfloat arithmetic; these became the Common Lisp functions INTEGER-LENGTH and (by way of Lisp-Machine Lisp) LDB.

1980年代，Common Lisp 开发者认真处理了引入 IEEE 浮点标准的问题 [IEEE, 1985]。值得注意的是，截至本文写作时，多数其他高级程序设计语言尚未认真处理这一标准。事实上，ANSI X3J3，即 FORTRAN 委员会，拒绝了一项要求这样做的明确请求。

In the 1980’s the developers of Common Lisp grappled with the introduction of the IEEE floating-point standard [IEEE, 1985]. (It is notable that, as of this writing, most other high-level programming languages have not grappled seriously with the IEEE floating-point standard. Indeed, ANSI X3J3 (FORTRAN) rejected an explicit request to do so.)

虽然 Lisp 通常不被视为数值程序设计语言，但有三股强大力量推动它朝这一方向发展：MACSYMA、S-1 项目，以及 Gerald Sussman。

While Lisp is not usually thought of as a numerical programming language, there were three strong influences in that direction: MACSYMA, the S-1 project, and Gerald Sussman.

第一个优秀的数值 Lisp 编译器，是为 MACSYMA 小组开发的 [Golden, 1970; Steele, 1977c; Steele, 1977e]；对他们及其用户而言，数值代码既快速又紧凑十分重要。结果产生了一个可与 DEC PDP-10 FORTRAN 编译器竞争的 Lisp 编译器 [Fateman, 1973]。

The first good numerical Lisp compiler was developed for the MACSYMA group [Golden, 1970; Steele, 1977c; Steele, 1977e]; it was important to them and their users that numerical code be both fast and compact. The result was a Lisp compiler that was competitive with the DEC PDP-10 FORTRAN compiler [Fateman, 1973].

S-1 最初打算做成高速信号处理器。设想的应用之一是潜艇探测，这似乎需要结合数值信号处理和人工智能技术。项目在浮点算术设计上得到 W. Kahan 的建议，因此最终与后来的 IEEE 标准相当接近。改进 MacLisp 编译器技术，使 S-1 Lisp 生成优秀数值代码，看来是合适的做法 [Brooks, 1982b]。S-1 提供四种浮点格式：18、36、72 和144位 [Correll, 1979]。受 S-1 Lisp 影响，Common Lisp 提供了扩展的浮点数据类型系统，以适应这类架构差异。

The S-1 was initially intended to be a fast signal processor. One of the envisioned applications was detection of submarines, which seemed to require a mix of numerical signal processing and artificial intelligence techniques. The project received advice from W. Kahan in the design of its floating-point arithmetic, so it ended up being quite similar to the eventual IEEE standard. It seemed appropriate to refine the techniques of the MacLisp compiler to produce good numerical code in S-1 Lisp [Brooks, 1982b]. The S-1 offered four different floating-point formats (18, 36, 72, and 144 bits) [Correll, 1979]. Influenced by S-1 Lisp, Common Lisp provides an expanded system of floating-point data types to accommodate such architectural variation.

Common Lisp 纳入复数，也是从 S-1 继承而来。¹

The inclusion of complex numbers in Common Lisp was also an inheritance from the S-1. 1

这对 Scott Fahlman 来说是个有些难以接受的点。Guy Steele 为新生的 Common Lisp 实现设计了一套验收测试，成了经常被提起的笑话。测试分三部分。首先输入 T；如果回应 T，就通过第1部分。其次，定义阶乘函数，然后计算：

This was something of a sticking point with Scott Fahlman. A running joke was an acceptance test for nascent Common Lisp implementations developed by Guy Steele. It was in three parts. First you type T; if it responds T, it passes part 1. Second, you define the factorial function and then calculate

(/ (factorial 1000) (factorial 999))

如果回应1000，就通过第2部分。第三，试试 (atanh -2)。如果返回复数，就算通过；若返回正确的复数，还可加分。很长一段时间里，没有任何 Common Lisp 实现能通过第三部分。Steele 曾在展会现场用这套三段测试弄坏过一两个实现。

If it responds 1000, it passes part 2. Third, you try (atanh -2). If it returns a complex number, it passes; extra credit if it returns the correct complex number. It was a long time before any Common Lisp implementation passed the third part. Steele broke an implementation or two on the trade show floor with this three-part test.

Gerald Sussman 和他的学生，包括 Gerald Roylance 与 Matthew Halfant，对数值应用，以及用 Lisp 生成和转换数值程序产生了兴趣 [Sussman, 1988; Roylance, 1988]。Sussman 也在 MIT 花了不少时间教本科生 Lisp。他认为，必须告诉学生10.0除以4.0得2.5，而10除以4却得2，实在荒唐。当然，几乎所有其他程序设计语言都有同样问题，Pascal [Jensen, 1974] 及其派生语言是显著例外；但这不是借口。Lisp 志向更高，几百年的数学惯例，应当比计算机领域几十年的暂时偏离更有分量。在 Sussman 推动下，/ 函数被定义为必要时返回有理数，因此 Common Lisp 中的 (/ 10 4) 得到5/2。（这并不被视为对语言的激进改变。有理数早已用于符号代数系统。Common Lisp 开发者不过是把客户本来就经常需要的功能整合进语言。）

Gerald Sussman and his students (including Gerald Roylance and Matthew Halfant) became interested in numerical applications and in the use of Lisp to generate and transform numerical programs [Sussman, 1988; Roylance, 1988]. Sussman also spent a fair amount of time at MIT teaching Lisp to undergraduates. Sussman thought it was absolutely crazy to have to tell students that the quotient of 10.0 and 4.0 was 2.5 but the quotient of 10 and 4 was 2. Of course, nearly all other programming languages have the same problem (Pascal [Jensen, 1974] and its derivatives being notable exceptions), but that is no excuse; Lisp aspires to better things, and centuries of mathematical precedent should outweigh the few decades of temporary aberration in the field of computers. At Sussman’s urging, the / function was defined to return rationals when necessary, so (/ 10 4) in Common Lisp produces 5/2. (This was not considered a radical change to the language. Rational numbers were already in use in symbolic algebra systems. The developers of Common Lisp were simply integrating into the language functionality frequently required by their clients, anyway.)

¹ 1970年代末，人们曾计划在 PDP-10 MacLisp 中加入双精度和复数，但项目从未完成。Steele 作了设计，随后搁置。Sussman 指导的本科生 Barbara Kerns 后来接手项目。Steele 给了她相关设计文档和背景材料，不巧的是，她第二天就把它们弄丢了。她感到难堪，于是埋头课程学习，接下来四个月一直躲着 Steele。他忙于自己的项目，并没有特别注意她不再出现。第二年，他们结婚了。他们的第一个结婚纪念日，是在斯坦福的一间宿舍里度过的，当时正在为各自在1980年 Lisp 会议上的演讲准备投影片。啊，青春！

1Plans had been laid in the late 1970’s to install double precision and complex numbers in PDP-10 MacLisp, but the project was never completed. Steele did a design for it and then put it aside. An undergraduate advisee of Sussman’s, Barbara Kerns, eventually took on the project. Steele gave her the relevant design documents and background material, and through a stroke of misfortune she lost them the next day. Embarrassed, she plunged into her course work and spent the next four months avoiding Steele. He, buried in his own projects, never particularly noticed her absence. The next year they were married. (They spent their first anniversary in a dormitory room at Stanford, preparing overhead transparencies for their respective talks at the 1980 Lisp Conference. Ah, youth!)

这一切又引发了一场争论，因为在 MacLisp 及其后继方言中，斜杠是字符转义符，而反斜杠是求余运算符。委员会最终决定交换两者角色：斜杠成为普通字母类字符，反斜杠成为字符转义符，从而使除法可以写作“/”而非“//”，也让有理数能用常规记法书写。这还解决了一个当时在 Lisp 社区中鲜为人知、也不受喜爱的操作系统 Unix 带来的问题：它用反斜杠作字符转义符，并在文件名中使用斜杠。不过，相对 MacLisp 和 Zetalisp，这是一项重大的不兼容改动，使 Common Lisp 遭到不少批评。）

All this provoked another debate, for in MacLisp and its descendants the slash was the characterquoter; moreover, backslash was the remainder operator. The committee eventually decided to swap the roles of slash and backslash, so that slash became alphabetic and backslash became the character quoter, thus allowing the division operation to be written “/” instead of “//” and allowing rational numbers to be written in conventional notation. This also solved some problems caused by a then little-known and little-loved (in the Lisp community) operating system called Unix, which used backslash as a character-quoter and slash in file names. However, it was a major incompatible change from MacLisp and Zetalisp, which left Common Lisp open to quite some criticism.)

当然，这让 Common Lisp 缺少了偶尔很有用的截断整数除法。受 S-1 多种舍入模式 [Correll, 1979; Hailpern, 1979] 启发，而这些模式又受 Kahan 影响，Steele 为 Common Lisp 加入四种整数除法：truncate、round、ceiling 和 floor。每种都接受一个或两个参数，并返回商和余数，因而连 Pascal 都超越了。总体而言，Common Lisp 提供的数值原语远比 FORTRAN 丰富，对复数三角函数分支割线等细节的关注，也比 FORTRAN 历来做得更细致。

Of course, this left Common Lisp without a truncating integer division operation, which is occasionally useful. Inspired by the many rounding modes of the S-1 [Correll, 1979; Hailpern, 1979] (which were influenced in turn by Kahan), Steele added four versions of the integer division operation to Common Lisp—truncate, round, ceiling, and floor, each of which accepts either one or two arguments and returns a quotient and remainder—thus bettering even Pascal. Overall, Common Lisp provides a much richer set of numerical primitives, and pays even closer attention to such details as the branch cuts of complex trigonometric functions, than FORTRAN ever has.

浮点数打印存在一个问题。Jon L White 从1970年代初开始，追究了多年。Guy Steele 将方法形式化，他们的解决方案在1980年代初于 Lisp 社区非正式流传，也被至少一个 Pascal 实现采用。Steele 又拖了许多年不断完善，直到1990年才正式发表 [Steele, 1990b]。此后，Kent Dybvig 又进一步改进了算法。一个令人愉快的巧合是，William Clinger 同时着手解决相应的输入问题，并向同一会议提交了解法 [Clinger, 1990]，于是两篇论文一同发表。

There is a problem with printing floating-point numbers. Jon L White pursued the problem for years, beginning in the early 1970’s. Guy Steele formalized the approach and their solution circulated informally in the Lisp community in the early 1980’s and was also used in at least one Pascal implementation. Steele dithered away many more years perfecting it. It was finally published in 1990 [Steele, 1990b]. (Since that time, Kent Dybvig has made further improvements to the algorithm.) By a pleasant coincidence, William Clinger had set out to solve the corresponding input problem and had submitted his solution to the same conference [Clinger, 1990], so they came out together.

顺带一提，Steele 于1974年出于趣味，在 MacLisp 中加入罗马数字输入输出。控制变量 BASE 和 IBASE 原先只能取2至36的整数值，现在也能分别设为 ROMAN。这项特性有点危险：读取如下代码：

On a related note, Steele put Roman numeral I/O into MacLisp in 1974 as a hack. The control variables BASE and IBASE, previously restricted to integer values between 2 and 36, could also independently be set to ROMAN. This feature was a bit dangerous: reading code such as

(do ((i 0 (+ i 1))
     (x ’() (cons (sqrt i) x)))
    ((= i 20) (return x)))

如果此前执行过 (setq ibase ’roman)，就会产生等价于下面的结果：

after (setq ibase ’roman) produced the equivalent of

(do ((1 0 (+ 1 1))
     (10 ’() (cons (sqrt 1) 10)))
    ((= 1 20) (return 10)))

这项特性最初的公告指出，不仅 ROMAN 会启用罗马数字输入输出，还可以把 BASE 或 IBASE 设为 CUNEIFORM，即楔形文字，于是你的 Lisp 就会“卡楔”住，陷入卡死。此后，罗马数字输入输出成了某种传统趣味功能，并进入 Zetalisp 的 FORMAT 函数，后来又进入 Common Lisp。

The initial announcement of this feature noted that not only would the value ROMAN result Roman numeral I/O, but you could set BASE or IBASE to CUNEIFORM, whereupon your Lisp would become wedged. Since that time, Roman numeral I/O has become something of a traditional hack, and became a part of the function FORMAT in Zetalisp and then Common Lisp.

3.5 面向对象程序设计

3.5 Object-Oriented Programming

面向对象编程与 Lisp 最早的结合，是基于消息传递的扩展。例如，Greenblatt 或 Howard Cannon 在1974年或1975年访问 PARC，为 Lisp 机器上的 Lisp 实现了一个简单的消息传递系统。后来 Cannon 加入多重继承，最初的 Flavors 由此诞生 [?]。

The first blending of OOP with Lisp were message-passing-based extensions. For example, Greenblatt or Howard Cannon visited PARC in 1974 or 1975 and implemented a simple messagepassing system for Lisp machine Lisp. Later Canon added multiple inheritance and the first Flavors was born [?].

更早时，Actors [?] 和 Plasma [?] 探索了消息传递与并发。Scheme [Sussman, 1975b] 是一次理解 Actors 的尝试。闭包与参与者被认为是同一种东西。Actors 很可能受到 Simula（Simula I 和 Simula67 [?]）启发。

Earlier, Actors [?] and Plasma [?] explored message-passing and concurrency. Scheme [Sussman, 1975b] was an attempt to understand Actors. Closures and actors were seen as the same. Actors were probably inspired by Simula (Simula I and Simula67 [?]).

Flavors 源自 Smalltalk [?] [?] [?] [?] [?]。

Flavors came from Smalltalk [?] [?] [?] [?] [?].

Flavors [?] [?; ?] 支持多重继承（Smalltalk 曾早期尝试过多重继承，但我记得那是一个以附加库实现的复制系统）。Flavors 在1970年代末引入方法组合，这是定义混入类的自然结果。Flavors 使用自动线性化机制，其他方案则是封装（显式命名）和选择 [?; ?; ?; ?] [?]。LOOPS 是一种基于 Interlisp、源自 Smalltalk 的面向对象系统 [?]，具有单继承和消息传递。

Flavors [?] [?; ?] has multiple inheritance (there was an early attempt at multiple inheritance in Smalltalk, but it was, I think, a copying system, built as an add-on library). Flavors introduced method combination in the late 1970’s, which are a natural result of defining mixins. Flavors uses an automatic linearization mechanism, and the alternatives are encapsulation (explicit naming) and selection [?; ?; ?; ?] [?]. LOOPS is an Interlisp-based OOP derived from Smalltalk [?]. It had single inheritance and message-passing.

CommonLoops [Bobrow, 1986] 衍生自 LOOPS，但略微扩展了类所有权的概念。它也借鉴了 New Flavors [Moon, 1986] [?]。

CommonLoops [Bobrow, 1986] is a derivative of LOOPS, but extending the class-ownership notion a little bit. Also it borrowed from New Flavors [Moon, 1986] [?].

Common Objects 是基于封装的面向对象系统 [?]。其封装特性与 CLU 中的特性有些相似 [?]。

Common Objects is an encapsulation-based OOP system [?]. Its encapsulation features have some similarity to those in CLU [?].

Object Lisp 是一种基于委托的语言 [Drescher, 1987]，参见 [?]、[?; ?]。

Object Lisp is a delegation-based language [Drescher, 1987], [?], [?; ?].

Common Lisp 对象系统有意融合了 CommonLoops 和 New Flavors。它引入真正的泛型函数，并清除了 Smalltalk 对多态面向对象语言影响的残余。[?; ?]、[Steele, 1990c]、[?]。

The Common Lisp Object System is a deliberate blending of CommonLoops and New Flavors. It introduced true generic functions and cleaned up the remnants of Smalltalk-influence on polymorphic object-oriented languages. [?; ?], [Steele, 1990c], [?].

它引入元对象协议，把 CLOS 本身视为一个面向对象程序，可以通过特化来定制或改变语言。[?] [?; ?; ?] [?]

It introduced the metaobject protocol, which treats CLOS itself as an object-oriented program that can be specialized to customize/alter the language. [?] [?; ?; ?] [?]

Scheme 中也有若干面向对象的尝试，包括 SCOOPS [?]、OakLisp [?; ?]，以及 John Ulrich 为 Semantic Microsystems（现称 Lightship）制作的委托语言 [?]。另见 Adams 和 Rees [?]。

In Scheme there were several attempts at object-orientation. These were SCOOPS [?], OakLisp [?; ?], the delegation language done by John Ulrich for Semantic Microsystems (or Lightship as it is now called) [?]. Also see Adams and Rees [?].

3.7 一些值得注意的失败

3.7 Some Notable Failures

尽管 Lisp 随时间不断吸收新特性，既来自其他程序设计语言，也来自 Lisp 社区内部实验，但有些想法虽以各种形式被反复尝试，却由于某种原因始终未能在 Lisp 社区流行。最显著的是 Algol 风格语法、广义多值，以及带变量合一的逻辑编程。

Despite Lisp’s tendency to absorb new features over time, both from other programming languages and from experiments within the Lisp community, there are a few ideas that have been tried repeatedly in various forms but for some reason simply don’t catch on in the Lisp community. Notable among these ideas are Algol-style syntax, generalized multiple values, and logic programming with unification of variables.

3.7.1 Algol 风格语法

3.7.1 Algol-Style Syntax

自 Steve Russell 首次手工编写 EVAL 实现以来，S 表达式就一直是编写程序的标准记法。在过去三十年几乎任何 Lisp 系统中，UNION 函数都可以大致写成下面的形式；它计算两个以元素列表表示的集合的并集：

Ever since Steve Russell first hand-coded an implementation of EVAL, S-expressions have been the standard notation for writing programs. In almost any Lisp system of the last thirty years, one could write the function UNION (which computes the union of two sets represented as lists of elements) in roughly the following form:

(defun union (x y)
(cond ((null x) y)
((member (car x) y) (union (cdr x) y))
(t (cons (car x) (union (cdr x) y)))))

然而，Lisp 设计的最初意图，是用 M 表达式编写程序，S 表达式语法只用于表示数据。用 M 表达式表示的 UNION 函数如下：

The original intention, however, in the design of Lisp was that programs would be written as M-expressions; the S-expression syntax was intended only for representation of data. The UNION function in M-expression notation looks like this:

union[x;y] = [null[x]→y; member[car[x];y]→union[cdr[x];y]; T→cons[car[x];union[cdr[x];y]]]

但正如 McCarthy 所指出的 [McCarthy, 1981]：

But as McCarthy noted [McCarthy, 1981]:

解释器的意外出现，倾向于把语言的形式固定下来……精确定义 M 表达式的项目……既没有最终完成，也没有彻底放弃。它只是退到了遥遥无期的未来；新一代程序员出现了，他们宁愿使用内部记法［即 S 表达式］，也不愿使用任何能够设计出来的类 Fortran 或类 Algol 记法。

The unexpected appearance of an interpreter tended to freeze the form of the language. . . . The project of defining M-expressions precisely. . . was neither finalized nor completely abandoned. It just receded into the indefinite future, and a new generation of programmers appeared who preferred internal notation [i.e., S-expressions] to any Fortran-like or Algol-like notation that could be devised.

但故事并未结束。此后，人们多次尝试为 Lisp 提供类 Algol 语法。一次又一次，某位 Lisp 用户或实现者觉得语言有所欠缺，便提出解决方案，而且往往吸引了相当多的用户；然而，从长远看，没有一种获得普遍接受。

Yet that was not the end of the story. Since that time there have been many other efforts to provide Lisp with an Algol-like syntax. Time and again a Lisp user or implementor has felt a lack in the language and provided a solution—and not infrequently attracted a substantial group of users—and yet in the long run none of these has achieved acceptance.

除 M 表达式外，最早的例子似乎是 Henneman 的 A-language [Henneman, 1985]。Henneman 给出了如下 UNION 定义：

The earliest example of this—after M-expressions, of course—appears to have been Henneman’s A-language [Henneman, 1985]. Henneman gives the following definition of UNION:

(DEFINE UNION (OF AND) (8)
(UNION OF X AND Y) (IF X IS
EMPTY THEN Y ELSE IF FIRST OF
X IS A MEMBER OF Y THEN UNION
OF REST OF X AND Y ELSE
CONNECT FIRST OF X TO BEGIN
UNION OF REST OF X AND Y
END))

定义中的数字8是 UNION 运算符的优先级。注意，BEGIN 和 END 被用作括号式分隔符；之所以需要它们，是因为表示 CONS 的 CONNECT … TO … 运算符具有更高优先级。

The number 8 in this definition is the precedence of the UNION operator; note the use of BEGIN and END as parenthetical delimiters, necessary because the CONNECT . . . TO . . . operator (which means CONS) has higher precedence.

我们觉得奇怪的是，Henneman 费心美化排版了论文中的 M 表达式和 S 表达式，却把所有 A-language 示例都写成连绵不断的整段形式，这种风格常见于其同时代人的 S 表达式。如今，为了清晰，我们会把程序排成下面这样：

We find it curious that Henneman went to the trouble of pretty-printing the M-expressions and S-expressions in his paper but presented all his examples of A-language in the sort of run-on, block-paragraph style often seen in the S-expressions of his contemporaries. Nowadays we would format such a program in this manner for clarity:

(DEFINE UNION (OF AND) (8)
(UNION OF X AND Y)
(IF X IS EMPTY THEN Y
ELSE IF FIRST OF X IS A MEMBER OF Y
THEN UNION OF REST OF X AND Y
ELSE CONNECT FIRST OF X TO
BEGIN UNION OF REST OF X AND Y END
))

这种排版并非前所未闻；当时的 Algol 程序员在发表程序时也采用类似缩进约定。［以下文字非常粗略，尚需充实。］

Such formatting was not unheard of; the Algol programmers of the day used similar indentation conventions in their published programs. [The following text is very sketchy and needs to be filled out.]

Lisp 2.0。［粗略部分结束。］

Lisp 2.0 [End of sketchy section.]

EL1 语言由 Ben Wegbreit 在博士研究中设计 [Wegbreit, 1970]。大体上，可以把它描述为一种具有类 Algol 表层语法和强数据类型的 Lisp。1970年代初，哈佛围绕 EL1 构建了名为 ECL 的完整编程系统 [Wegbreit, 1971; Wegbreit, 1972; Wegbreit, 1974]。EL1 中的 UNION 函数如下：

The EL1 language was designed by Ben Wegbreit as part of his Ph.D. research [Wegbreit, 1970]. It may be loosely characterized as a Lisp with an Algol-like surface syntax and strong data typing. A complete programming system called ECL was built around EL1 at Harvard in the early 1970’s [Wegbreit, 1971; Wegbreit, 1972; Wegbreit, 1974]. The UNION function in EL1 looks like this:

union <- EXPR(x: FORM, y: FORM; FORM)
[) x=NIL => y;
MEMBER(CAR(x), y) => union(CDR(x), y);
CONS(CAR(X), union(CDR(x), y)) (];

注意，x、y 和结果都声明为 FORM 类型，即指向点对的指针。双字符记号 [) 和 (] 相当于 BEGIN 和 END；它们在 Model 33 电传打字机上看起来比这里更顺眼。块内的箭头 => 表示有条件地从块返回一个值，由此形成的记法让人想起 McCarthy 的 M 表达式条件记法。

Note the type declarations of x, y, and the result as type FORM (pointer to dotted pair). The digraphs [) and (] are equivalent to BEGIN and END (they looked better on a Model 33 Teletype than they do here). Within a block the arrow => indicates the conditional return of a value from the block, resulting in a notation reminiscent of McCarthy’s conditional notation for M-expressions.

当时，Lisp 本身在哈佛计算技术研究中心并未广泛使用；EL1 和 PPL（多态程序设计语言，一种更接近 Joss 的交互系统）或许是哈佛当时对 Lisp 的回应。如果 Wegbreit 没有在项目中途离开哈佛加入 Xerox，ECL 也许能存续更久。实际情况是，整个1970年代，哈佛都用 ECL 进行研究和课程教学。

Lisp itself was not widely used at Harvard’s Center for Research in Computing Technology at that time; EL1 and PPL (Polymorphic Programming Language, a somewhat more Joss-like interactive system) may have been Harvard’s answer to Lisp at the time. ECL might have survived longer if Wegbreit had not left Harvard for Xerox in the middle of the project. As it was, ECL was used for research and course work at Harvard throughout the 1970’s.

我们已经讨论过 Teitelman 的 CLISP，即 Conversational Lisp，它是 Interlisp 的一部分 [Teitelman, 1974]。UNION 是 Interlisp 内置函数，但也可以用 CLISP 定义如下：

We have already discussed Teitelman’s CLISP (Conversational Lisp), which was part of Interlisp [Teitelman, 1974]. The function UNION was built into Interlisp, but could have been defined using CLISP in this manner:

DEFINEQ((UNION (LAMBDA (X Y)
(IF ~X THEN Y
ELSEIF X:1 MEMBER Y THEN UNION X::1 Y
ELSE <X:1 !(UNION X::1 Y)>]

在 CLISP 中，~ 是表示 NOT 或 NULL 的一元运算符。X:n 是列表 X 的第 n 个元素，因此 X:1 表示 (CAR X)；类似地，X::1 表示 (CDR X)。CLISP 预定义 MEMBER 为中缀运算符，但 UNION 不是，不过用户可以自行定义。尖括号表示构造列表；其中的 ! 表示拼接，所以 <A !B> 表示 (CONS A B)。最后，用一个 ] 表示所需数量的右括号（此处为4个），虽然不是 CLISP 本身的特性，却符合 Interlisp 的风格。

In CLISP, ~ is a unary operator meaning NOT or NULL. X:n is element n of the list X, so X:1 means (CAR X); similarly X::1 means (CDR X). The function MEMBER is predefined by CLISP to be an infix operator, but UNION is not (though the user may so define it if desired). Angle brackets indicate construction of a list; ! within such a list indicates splicing, so <A !B> means (CONS A B). Finally, the use of a final ] to indicate the necessary number of closing parentheses (four, in this case), while not a feature of CLISP proper, is consistent with the Interlisp style.

MLISP 是 Lisp 的一种类 Algol 语法，最初由 Horace Enea 在 IBM 360 上实现，后来在 PDP-10 的斯坦福 Lisp 1.6 上重新实现 [Smith, 1970]。它提供中缀运算符、用于迭代的复杂 FOR 构造、各种下标记法，例如 A(1,3)（二维数组元素）和 L[1,3,2]（等价于 (cadr (caddr (car L)))）、“向量”运算（MAPCAR 的简洁记法），以及解构赋值。

MLISP was an Algol-like syntax for Lisp, first implemented for the IBM 360 by Horace Enea and then re-implemented for the PDP-10 under Stanford Lisp 1.6 [Smith, 1970]. It provided infix operators; a complex FOR construct for iteration; various subscripting notations such as A(1,3) (element of a two-dimensional array) and L[1,3,2] (equivalent to (cadr (caddr (car L)))); “vector” operations (a concise notation for MAPCAR); and destructuring assignment.

EXPR UNION (X,Y); %MLISP version of UNION
IF ¬X THEN Y ELSE
IF X[1] ϵ Y THEN UNION(X↓1,Y)
ELSE X[1] CONS UNION(X↓1,Y);

Vaughan Pratt 为 Lisp 开发了名为 CGOL 的 Algol 风格记法 [Pratt, 1973]。CGOL 不是把代数语法嵌入 S 表达式，而是使用独立、完整的词法分析器和语法分析器。1970年 Pratt 在斯坦福时，首先为斯坦福 Lisp 1.6 实现了它；当时与 MLISP 项目交换过想法。不久后 Pratt 前往 MIT，又实现了 MacLisp 版本 [Pratt, 1976]。这种分析器的不同版本也用于 IBM 约克敦的符号代数系统 SCRATCHPAD，以及 MIT MAC 项目的 MACSYMA；SCRATCHPAD 的重新实现由同时开发 LISP370 的 Fred Blair 完成，MACSYMA 的版本则由 Michael Genesereth 完成。

Vaughan Pratt developed an Algol-style notation for Lisp called CGOL [Pratt, 1973]. Rather than embedding algebraic syntax within S-expressions, CGOL employed a separate full-blown tokenizer and parser. This was first implemented for Stanford Lisp 1.6 in 1970 when Pratt was at Stanford; at this time there was an exchange of ideas with the MLISP project. After Pratt went to MIT shortly thereafter, he implemented a version for MacLisp [Pratt, 1976]. Versions of this parser were also used in the symbolic algebra systems SCRATCHPAD at IBM Yorktown and MACSYMA at MIT’s Project MAC; Fred Blair, who also developed LISP370, did the reimplementation for SCRATCHPAD, while Michael Genesereth did it for MACSYMA.

下面的 CGOL 版 UNION 把它定义为中缀运算符；数字14和13是语法分析器的左右“结合强度”：

Our CGOL version of the UNION function defines it as an infix operator (the numbers 14 and 13 are left and right “binding powers” for the parser):

define x "UNION" y, 14, 13; if not x then y else if member(car x, y) then cdr x union y else car x . cdr x union y ♦

这里采用的是 MIT 实现的 CGOL 版本，它坚持使用标准 ASCII 字符集。同一个定义若使用斯坦福扩展字符集，就会写成：

Here we have assumed the version of CGOL implemented at MIT, which stuck to the standard ASCII character set; the exact same definition using the Stanford extended character set would be:

define x "∪" y, 14, 13; if ¬x then y else if αx ε y then βx ∪y else αx . βx ∪y ♦

上面两个例子中的“.”都表示 CONS；分隔符 ♦（实际上是 ASCII 的 altmode 字符，如今称为 escape）表示顶层表达式结束。所有一元 Lisp 函数，包括 CAR 和 CDR，在 CGOL 中都是一元运算符。上面的定义依赖这些运算符具有很高优先级，因此 cdr x union y 表示 (cdr x) union y，而不是 cdr (x union y)。我们还仔细选择了 UNION 相对于“.”的结合强度，使最后的表达式解析为 (car x) . ((cdr x) union y)。这未必显然是最佳选择；Henneman 给表示 CONS 的 CONNECT … TO … 赋予了比 UNION 更高的优先级。Pratt 评论道 [Pratt, 1976]：

The “.” represents CONS in both the above examples; the delimiter ♦(actually the ASCII “altmode” character, nowadays called “escape”) indicates the end of a top level expression. All unary Lisp functions are unary operators in CGOL, including CAR and CDR. In the definition above we have relied on the fact that such unary operators have very high precedence, so cdr x union y means (cdr x) union y, not cdr (x union y). We also carefully chose the binding powers for UNION relative to “.” so that the last expression would be parsed as (car x) . ((cdr x) union y)). It is not obvious that this is the best choice; Henneman chose to give CONS (in the form of CONNECT . . . TO . . . ) higher precedence than UNION. Pratt remarked [Pratt, 1976]:

如果你想使用 CGOL 记法，却不想碰结合强度，只需像写 Lisp 那样，给每个 CGOL 表达式都加上括号。不过，即使省略全部括号……通常也不会出错。

If you want to use the CGOL notation but don’t want to have anything to do with binding powers, simply parenthesize every CGOL expression as though you were writing in Lisp. However, if you omit all parentheses . . . you will not often go wrong.

与 Henneman 的评论比较一下 [Henneman, 1985]：

Compare this to Henneman’s remark [Henneman, 1985]:

实际使用中产生错误结果的最大原因，就是给函数赋予了错误的优先级。

The one great cause of most of the incorrect results obtained in practice is an incorrect precedence being assigned to a function.

1970年代，人们设计了多种“人工智能语言”，提供当时认为有助于编写人工智能应用的专用编程构造。其中一些嵌入 Lisp，因此直接继承 Lisp 语法；有时也反过来影响 Lisp 语法，详见第4节。未嵌入 Lisp 的语言通常具有与 Algol 相关的语法，同时保留 Lisp 的其他特性，例如符号数据结构和递归函数。其中包括 POP-2 [Burstall, 1971]、SAIL [Feldman, 1972]，以及基于 Pascal 的 TELOS [Travis, 1977]。

During the 1970’s a number of “AI languages” were designed to provide specific programming constructs then thought to be helpful in writing programs for AI applications. Some of these were embedded within Lisp and therefore simply inherited Lisp syntax (and in some cases influenced Lisp syntax—see section 4 for a discussion of these). Those that were not embedded usually had a syntax related to that of Algol, while including some of the other features of Lisp (such as symbolic data structures and recursive functions). Among these were POP-2 [Burstall, 1971], SAIL [Feldman, 1972], and the Pascal-based TELOS [Travis, 1977].

向 Lisp 引入类 Algol 语法的想法不断出现，而且几乎总会引起巨大争议。一方认为统一使用 S 表达式具有技术优势，并不介意它在数值表达式方面公认相对笨拙；另一方则确信代数语法更简洁、更方便，甚至更自然，尽管所有这些记法都是人为的，“自然”究竟意味着什么也不清楚。

The idea of introducing Algol-like syntax into Lisp keeps popping up and has seldom failed to create enormous controversy between those who find the universal use of S-expressions a technical advantage (and don’t mind the admitted relative clumsiness of S-expressions for numerical expressions) and those who are certain that algebraic syntax is more concise, more convenient, or even more natural (whatever that may mean, considering that all these notations are artificial).

我们猜想，Algol 风格语法未能在整个 Lisp 社区真正流行，原因有二。首先，没有足够多的特殊符号可用。如果讨论领域限于数字或字符，感兴趣的操作数量有限，为每种操作分配一个特殊字符并不难。但 Lisp 的讨论领域丰富得多，Lisp 程序员常把应用看作又一次语言设计练习：通常先设计新数据结构，再设计操作它们的新函数，可能有几十乃至几百个。要发明这么多不同符号实在太难，虽然 APL 社区确实试过。最终，总得退回通用的函数调用记法；只不过 Lisp 程序员不会等到失败才这么做。

We conjecture that Algol-style syntax has not really caught on in the Lisp community as a whole for two reasons. First, there are not enough special symbols to go around. When your domain of discourse is limited to numbers or characters, there are only so many operations of interest, and it is not difficult to assign one special character to each and be done with it. But Lisp has a much richer domain of discourse, and a Lisp programmer often approaches an application as yet another exercise in language design; the style typically involves designing new data structures and new functions to operate on them—perhaps dozens or hundreds—and it’s just too hard to invent that many distinct symbols (though the APL community certainly has tried). Ultimately one must always fall back on a general function-call notation; it’s just that Lisp programmers don’t wait until they fail.

其次，也许更重要的是，Algol 风格语法使程序看起来更不像表示它的数据结构。在一种把操纵程序表示视为核心范式的文化中，若某种记法拉大了程序外观与其数据表示外观之间的距离，就不大可能受到热烈欢迎。这过去是、现在仍是反对把 loop 纳入 Common Lisp 的主要理由之一。

Second, and perhaps more important, Algol-style syntax makes programs look less like the data structures used to represent them. In a culture where the ability to manipulate representations of programs is a central paradigm, a notation that distances the appearance of a program from the appearance of its representation as data is not likely to be warmly received (and this was, and is, one of the principal objections to the inclusion of loop in Common Lisp).

另一方面，恰恰因为 Lisp 让操纵程序表示变得容易，初学者总能轻易试验其他记法。因此，我们预期后代 Lisp 程序员会继续一次又一次地重新发明 Lisp 的 Algol 风格语法；我们也同样确信，经过最初的迷恋之后，他们仍会继续拒绝它。或许，这个过程应被视为 Lisp 黑客的一种成人礼。

On the other hand, precisely because Lisp makes it easy to play with program representations, it is always easy for the novice to experiment with alternative notations. Therefore we expect future generations of Lisp programmers to continue to reinvent Algol-style syntax for Lisp, over and over and over again, and we are equally confident that they will continue, after an initial period of infatuation, to reject it. (Perhaps this process should be regarded as a rite of passage for Lisp hackers.)

3.7.2 广义多值

3.7.2 Generalized Multiple Values

许多 Lisp 扩展者都独立走过下面这条路。有时，希望函数返回不止一项结果。通过全局变量返回部分结果很别扭；明知结果完全可以放在机器寄存器或机器控制栈中，却为它们构造列表，也很低效，还会让系统更接近下一次垃圾回收。有趣的是，应该返回两个结果的典型例子不是符号操作，而是数值操作：整数除法可以方便地同时返回商和余数。其实，对程序员来说，使用两个独立函数也同样方便；但我们很清楚，计算其中一个时，另一个几乎是免费得到的，所以这仍然是效率问题。

Many Lisp extenders have independently gone down the following path. Sometimes it is desirable to return more than one item from a function.
