It is awkward to return some of the results through global variables, and inefficient to cons up a list of the results (pushing the system that much closer to its next garbage collection) when we know perfectly well that they could be returned in machine registers or pushed onto the machine control stack. Curiously, the prototypical example of a function that ought to return two results is not symbolic but numerical: integer division might conveniently return both a quotient and a remainder. (Actually, it would be just as convenient for the programmer to use two separate functions, but we know perfectly well that the computation of one produces the other practically for free—again it is an efficiency issue.)

那么，假设提供某种产生多值的原始机制。一种办法是引入新函数和／或特殊形式。例如，Common Lisp 沿用 Lisp-Machine Lisp，提供名为 VALUES 的原始函数；(values 3 4 5) 的结果就是3、4、5这三个数。特殊形式：

Suppose, then, that some primitive means of producing multiple values is provided. One way is to introduce new functions and/or special forms. Common Lisp, for example, following Lisp- Machine Lisp, has a primitive function called VALUES; the result of (values 3 4 5) is the three numbers 3, 4, and 5. The special form

(multiple-value-bind (p q r) (foo) body) 执行 body，并把变量 p、q、r 局部绑定到形式 (foo) 返回的三个值。

(multiple-value-bind (p q r) (foo) body) executes its body with the variables p, q, and r locally bound to three values returned as the value of the form (foo).

但这些做法都显得临时拼凑、不够优雅。也许多值可以从语言自身的内在结构中自然产生。例如，假设 lambda 表达式的主体隐含 VALUES 构造，返回所有子形式的值，而不是隐含 PROGN、只返回最后一个子形式的值，这就解决了产生多值的问题。再假设重新定义函数调用，使用每个子形式返回的全部值，而不是每个只取一个值，就可以写：

But this is all so ad hoc and inelegant. Perhaps multiple values can be made to emerge from the intrinsic structure of the language itself. Suppose, for example, that the body of a lambda expression were an implicit VALUES construct, returning the values of all its subforms, rather than an implicit PROGN, returning the values of only the last subform? That takes care of producing multiple values. Suppose further that function calls were redefined to use all the values returned by each subform, rather than just one value from each subform? Then one could write

((lambda (quo rem) ...) (/ 44 6))

于是 quo 绑定到商7，rem 绑定到余数2。这就解决了消费多值的问题。一切如此简单整齐！等等，还有两个细节。首先，回到 lambda 表达式，为保持一致，它必须返回所有子形式的全部值，而不是每个子形式只取一个值。因此下面的形式：

thereby binding quo to the quotient 7 and rem to the remainder 2. That takes care of consuming multiple values. All very simple and tidy! Oops, two details to take care of. First, returning to lambda expressions, we see that, for consistency, they need to return all the values of all the subforms, not just one value from each subform. So the form

((lambda (quo rem) (/ quo rem) (/ rem quo)) (/ 44 6))

返回四个值：3、1、0、2。其次，仍然需要按顺序执行赋值等带副作用的形式。简单的办法是让 (setq x 0) 和 (print x) 这样的形式返回零个值，因此：

returns four values: 3, 1, 0, and 2. Second, there is still a need for sequencing forms that have side effects such as assignment. The simple solution is to make such forms as (setq x 0) and (print x) return zero values, so that

((lambda (quo rem) (print quo) rem) (/ 44 6))

在打印商7后，只返回余数2。这样还一石二鸟，消除了交互式顶层因下面这样的语句而连续打印几百行 NIL 的烦恼：

returns only the remainder 2 after printing the quotient 7. (This stone kills another bird at the same time by eliminating the annoyance of an interactive top-level printing hundreds of lines of NIL after NIL just because of

(setq my-array (make-array 1000000))

或其他类似的交互式初始化步骤。）

or some such interactive initialization step.)

这一切都有非常简单而诱人的基于栈的实现。原语只需把所有值依次压栈。开始处理函数调用时，先在栈上放一个标记；所有子形式处理完后，寻找最近的标记，其上所有内容都作为函数参数；不过，转交控制权之前，务必移除或取消标记。另一种策略用浅绑定计数器代替标记，免去搜索，代价是每次压入值时更新计数器。令人满意的是，处理 lambda 表达式主体无需特殊动作。

This all has a very simple and attractive stack-based implementation. Primitives simply push all their values, one after another, onto the stack. At the start of the processing for a function call, place a marker on the stack; after all subforms have been processed, simply search the stack for the most recent marker; everything above it should be used as arguments for the function call—but be sure to remove or cancel the marker before transferring control to the function. (An alternative strategy uses a shallow-bound counter instead of markers; this avoids the search, at the expense of updating the counter as values are pushed.) It is gratifying that no special action is required for processing the bodies of lambda-expressions.

是的，一切非常整齐；可一旦真正使用，就会发现，无论对维护者还是编译器，代码都难懂得多。即使程序员自律，不写下面这样的东西：

Yes, this is all very neat and tidy—and as soon as you try to use it, you find that code becomes much, much harder to understand, both for the maintainer and for the compiler. Even if the programmer has the discipline not to write

(cons (/ 44 6) (setq x 0))

它在把 x 设为0后返回 (7 . 2)，编译器也永远无法确信正在处理的代码里没有潜藏这种恶行。如果不能相当完整地知道每个函数，包括全部用户定义函数，会产生多少个值，编译器就无法验证函数调用是否提供了正确数量的参数。于是，一项重要而实用的 Lisp 编程错误检查，几乎变得不可能。

which returns (7 . 2) after setting x to 0, the compiler can never be sure that no such atrocities lurk in the code it is processing. In the absence of fairly complete information about how many values are produced by each function, including all user-defined functions, a compiler cannot verify that a function call will supply the correct number of arguments. An important practical check for Lisp programming errors is thus made all but impossible.

条件式又带来两个问题。首先，下面的形式应该怎样解释：

Conditionals introduce two further problems. First: what shall be the interpretation of

(if (foo) (bar))

如果 (foo) 返回两个值呢？第二个值应丢弃，还是当第一个值为真时，把它作为返回值？也许应要求谓词恰好返回一个值。好吧，但仍有另一个事实：两个分支可能返回不同数量的值；(if (foo) 3 (/ 44 6)) 可能返回单值3，也可能返回多值7和2。由此立即可知，即使给编译器完整程序，它通常也无法推断每个函数调用返回多少个值；这在形式上是不可判定的。

if (foo) returns two values? Shall the second value be discarded, or treated as the value to be returned if the first value is true? Perhaps the predicate should be required to return exactly one value. Very well, but there remains the fact that the two subforms might return different numbers of values; (if (foo) 3 (/ 44 6)) might return the single value 3 or the multiple values 7 and 2. It follows immediately that no compiler, even if presented with a complete program, can deduce in general how many values are returned by each function call; it is formally undecidable.

这一切似乎完全符合 Lisp 作为弱类型语言的精神：既然函数返回值的类型可能要到运行时才能确定，为什么数量就不行？而且，在效率重要的地方，可以用声明指出值的数量，就像类型声明已经帮助许多 Lisp 编译器那样。然而，事实上，几乎每个走过这条路的人，发展到这里都放弃了，嘟囔着“这条路通向疯狂”，然后掉头回家，不愿陷入泥潭。

Now all this might seem to be entirely in the spirit of Lisp as a weakly typed language; if the types of the values returned by functions may not be determinable until run time, why not their very cardinality? And declarations might indicate the number of values where efficiency is important, just as type declarations already assist many Lisp compilers. Nevertheless, as a matter of fact, nearly everyone who has followed this path has given up at this point in the development, muttering “This way madness lies,” and returned home rather than fall into the tarpit.

我们自己也曾独立沿着这条思路探索，并与不少同样做过的人交谈。不过，能引用的已发表资料很少，恰恰因为大多数人在发表之前，最终就认定这不是好主意。这并不表示它确实不好，也不表示某种变体无法消除缺点；我们这里只想强调，许多独立研究者的思路如此相似。在最终放弃之前确实产生实现的工作中，最值得注意的是 SEUS 和 POP-2 [Burstall, 1971]。SEUS 的设计者和实现者 Richard Weyhrauch、Carolyn Talcott、David Posner、Ralph Goren、William Scherlis、Len Bosack 和 Gabriel 从未发表成果，尽管其语言设计新颖，拥有基于编译器和微码的快速实现，以及完整编程环境。POP-2 的设计者把它看作人工智能语言，而非 Lisp 变体，是1960年代末至1970年代初出现的众多此类语言之一；它在欧洲颇受欢迎，并被用于实现逻辑编程语言 POPLOG [Mellish, 1984]。

We ourselves have independently followed this line of thought and have had conversations with quite a few other people who have also done so. There are few published sources we can cite, however, precisely because most of them eventually judged it a bad idea before publishing anything about it. (This is not to say that it actually is a bad idea, or that some variation cannot eliminate its disadvantages; here we wish merely to emphasize the similarity of thinking among many independent researchers.) Among the most notable efforts that did produce actual implementations before eventual abandonment are SEUS and POP-2 [Burstall, 1971]. The designers and implementors of SEUS (Richard Weyhrauch, Carolyn Talcott, David Posner, Ralph Goren, William Scherlis, Len Bosack, and Gabriel) never published their results, although it was a novel language design, and had a fast, compilerand microcode-based implementation, complete with programming environment. POP-2 was regarded by its designers as an AI language, one of the many produced in the late 1960’s and early 1970’s, rather than as a variant of Lisp; it enjoyed quite some popularity in Europe and was used to implement the logic programming language POPLOG [Mellish, 1984].

3.7.3 逻辑编程与合一

3.7.3 Logic Programming and Unification

从1970年代到1980年代，人们多次尝试把也许最重要的两个人工智能语言家族 Lisp 和 Prolog 的优点整合到一种语言中。这类工作尤其是日本第五代项目软件部分的特色。例如 Robinson 的 LOGLISP [Robinson, 1982]、TAO 项目 [Takeuchi, 1983; Okuno, 1984] 和 TABLOG [Malachi, 1984]。也有人尝试整合函数式编程与 Prolog。所有这些都应与仅把 Lisp 当作方便的 Prolog 实现语言区分开来，后者的例子包括 Komorowski 的 QLOG [Komorowski, 1982]，以及 Kahn 和 Carlsson 的工作 [Kahn, 1984]。

During the 1970’s and on into the 1980’s there have been a number of attempts to integrate the advantages of the two perhaps foremost AI programming language families, Lisp and Prolog, into a single language. Such efforts were particularly a feature of the software side of the Japanese Fifth Generation project. Examples of this are Robinson’s LOGLISP [Robinson, 1982], the TAO project [Takeuchi, 1983; Okuno, 1984], and TABLOG [Malachi, 1984]. There have also been related attempts to integrate functional programming and Prolog. (All these should be contrasted with the use of Lisp as a convenient language for implementing Prolog, as exemplified by Komorowski’s QLOG [Komorowski, 1982] and the work of Kahn and Carlsson [Kahn, 1984].)

我们猜想，这种想法未能在 Lisp 社区流行，原因在于合一，即 Prolog 使用的变量匹配过程。确实，很容易设计一种具有许多 Lisp 特性、却在过程调用时使用合一的语言。问题是，合一与 lambda 绑定的本质差别足够大，使得结果不再有 Lisp 的感觉。普通 Lisp 程序员会觉得它是 Prolog 的扩展，而不是 Lisp 的扩展；不能如此大幅度改动过程调用这样根本的东西。另一方面，可以保持 Lisp 过程调用不变，把合一作为独立设施显式调用。但这样，它就只是另一个 Lisp 库例程，结果完全没有 Prolog 的感觉。

We conjecture that this idea has not caught on in the Lisp community because of unification, the variable-matching process used in Prolog. Indeed one can easily design a language that has many of the features of Lisp but uses unification during procedure calls. The problem is that unification is sufficiently different in nature from lambda-binding that the resulting language doesn’t really feel like Lisp any more. To the average Lisp programmer, it feels like an extension of Prolog but not an extension of Lisp; you just can’t mess around that much with something as fundamental as procedure calls. On the other hand, one can leave Lisp procedure calls as they are and provide unification as a separate facility that can be explicitly invoked. But then it is just another Lisp library routine, and the result doesn’t feel at all like Prolog.

3.8 其他主题

3.8 Other Topics

其他值得讨论的主题包括：

Other topics we might usefully address are:

• 作为表达手段的续延。

• continuations as an expressive device

• 具体化与反射。

• reification and reflection

• 垃圾回收。重点包括标记／清扫、复制、对引用计数的尝试、Baker 的实时方法 [Baker, 1978]、Bishop 处理超大地址空间的提议、White 的“别做垃圾回收！”建议 [White, 1980]、Moon 的短生代垃圾回收 [Moon, 1984]；Brooks 关于通用硬件上垃圾回收的工作；还应提到 Cohen 在 Computing Surveys 上的论文。

• garbage collection (high points include mark/sweep, copying, flirtations with reference counting, Baker’s real-time methods [Baker, 1978], Bishop’s proposal to handle a very large address space, White’s suggestion “don’t do it!” [White, 1980], Moon’s ephemeral GC [Moon, 1984]; Brooks on GC for stock hardware; also mention Cohen’s paper in Computing Surveys)

• 高效管理栈的各种技术：意大利面栈 [Bobrow, 1973]、[Steele, 1977a]、McDermott 的想法 [McDermott, 1980]、Steele 和 Sussman 的惰性寄存器保存 [Steele, 1980]、数值的栈分配 [Steele, 1977e] [Brooks, 1982b]、Stallman 的幽灵栈、Appel 关于垃圾回收比栈分配更快的论断（Info. Proc. Letters 25，275–279），以及 [Clinger, 1988]、[Bartley, 1986]、[Hieb, 1990]。

• various techniques for managing stacks efficiently: spaghetti stacks [Bobrow, 1973], [Steele, 1977a], McDermott’s ideas [McDermott, 1980], Steele and Sussman on lazy register saving [Steele, 1980], stack allocation of numbers [Steele, 1977e] [Brooks, 1982b], Stallman’s phantom stacks, Appel’s claim (Info. Proc. Letters 25, 275–279) that GC is faster than stack allocation, also [Clinger, 1988], [Bartley, 1986] [Hieb, 1990]

• 记录结构、DEFSTRUCT、Interlisp 的 RECORD 包。

• record structures, DEFSTRUCT, Interlisp RECORD package

• 从 oblist 到 obarray，再到包、模块的发展……

• development of oblist to obarray to packages to modules . . .

• 哈希表：速度；在把对象视为不透明实体的同时建立对象间关系，创建指针不必修改任一对象；属性表使用的减少；不会阻止对象被回收的弱哈希链接，因此具有弱链接的哈希表可以充当缓存。

• Hash tables: speed, creating relationships between objects while also regarding them as opaque (don’t have to modify either of them to create a pointer), decreased use of property lists, weak hash links that don’t prevent an object from being reclaimed (hence a hash table with weak links serves as a cache)

• 讨论编程环境策略：基于文件与基于内存；美化打印；基于 Lisp 的编辑器，特别是结构编辑，与 EMACS 的比较；自动美化打印对编程风格的影响；采用一整套工具；使工具成为可能的语言特性和钩子，如 trace、advice、inspect、evalhook 等。

• Talk about programming environment strategies: File-based versus memory-based; Prettyprinting; Lisp-based editor, esp. structure editing, vs. EMACS; Influence of mechanical pretty-printing on programming style; Buying into a set of tools; Languages features and hooks that make tools possible: Trace, advice, inspect, evalhook, etc.

• 闭包，动态闭包和词法闭包，可追溯到 Moses 1970年的工作。

• Closures (dynamic and lexical—trace back to Moses 1970)

• 非局部退出：errset/err、catch、call/cc、unwind-protect、Interlisp 所有的相关机制、跳到外层 PROG 的 GO、词法作用域的 GO 和 RETURN（Common Lisp），以及续延作为通用语言特性及其对实现的影响。

• Nonlocal exits: errset/err, catch, call/cc, unwind-protect, whatever Interlisp had, GO to an outer PROG, lexically scoped GO an RETURN (Common Lisp), continuations as a general language feature and implications for implementation

4 作为语言实验室的 Lisp

4 Lisp as a Language Laboratory

与围绕大多数其他程序设计语言形成的文化相比，Lisp 文化有个有趣特点：玩具方言也颇受尊重。纵观历史，Lisp 一直被用作语言实验室。给 Lisp 加几个新函数，让它们看起来像系统自带设施，轻而易举。有了宏、CLISP 或等价机制，添加新的控制结构或其他语法构造也相当容易。若这些还不够，用 Lisp 为一种新方言编写完整解释器，也只需半小时。要体会这有多惊人，不妨设想：手上有一个可工作的 C、Fortran、Pascal、PL/I、BASIC 或 APL 系统，可以编写并运行该语言的任何程序，却拿不到编译器或解释器源代码，然后尝试完成以下三道练习：

An interesting aspect of the Lisp culture, in contrast to those surrounding most other programming languages, is that toy dialects are regarded with a fair amount of respect. Lisp has been used throughout its history as a language laboratory. It is trivial to add a few new functions to Lisp in such a way that they look like system-provided facilities. Given macros, CLISP, or the equivalent, it is pretty easy to add new control structures or other syntactic constructs. If that fails, it is the work of only half an hour to write, in Lisp, a complete interpreter for a new dialect. To see how amazing this is, imagine starting with a working C, Fortran, Pascal, PL/I, BASIC, or APL system—you can write and run any program in that language, but have no access to source code for the compiler or interpreter—and then tackling these three exercises:

1. 给语言添加一个新算术运算符，类似 Knuth 的 Metafont 语言中的勾股加法 [Knuth, 1986]：a++b 计算 √(a² + b²)。

1. Add a new arithmetic operator to the language similar to the one for Pythagorean addition in Knuth’s Metafont language [Knuth, 1986]: a++b computes √(a² + b²).

扩展后的新运算符，在语法上应与语言的加减运算符类似。对 C，可以使用 +++ 或 @ 代替 ++；对 APL，可使用惯常的那些难看的叠印符号之一，例如 [+]。

The language is to be augmented in such a way that the new operator is syntactically similar to the language operators for addition and subtraction. (For C, you may use +++ or @ rather than ++; for APL, use one of the customary awful overstrikes such as [+].)

2. 给语言添加 case 语句。如果它已有 case，就添加名为 switch 的语句，行为与原有 case 相同，但选中分支执行完后，控制会继续落入后续分支；必须使用专门的 break 语句，或从最后一个分支落出，才能结束 switch 的执行。

2. Add a case statement to the language. (If it already has a case statement, then add a statement called switch that is just like the case statement already in the language, except that when the selected branch has been executed control falls through to succeeding branches; a special break statement, or dropping out of the last branch, must be used to terminate execution of the switch statement.)

3. 给语言添加完整的词法作用域函数闭包。

3. Add full lexically scoped functional closures to the language.

如果没有编译器或解释器源代码，三个项目实际上都要求几乎从头再来。这正是我们想说明的一点。但即使有 Fortran 编译器或 APL 解释器源代码，这三道练习也比在 Lisp 中困难得多。Lisp 对第一题的回答只有一行，下面用 Common Lisp 表示：

Without source code for the compiler or interpreter, all three projects require one practically to start over from scratch. That is part of our point. But even given source code for a Fortran compiler or APL interpreter, all three exercises are much more difficult than in Lisp. The Lisp answer to the first one is a one-liner (shown here in Common Lisp):

(defun ++ (x y) (+ (sqrt x) (sqrt y)))

Lisp 不把中缀运算符等特殊语法保留给内置操作，所以对调用者而言，用户定义函数与系统定义函数看起来完全一样。

Lisp does not reserve special syntax (such as infix operators) for use by built-in operations, so user-defined functions look just like system-defined functions to the caller.

第二题需要大约十几行代码，仍使用 Common Lisp，并采用宏一节所述的反引号语法：

The second requires about a dozen lines of code (again, in Common Lisp, using backquote syntax as described in the discussion of macros):

(defmacro switch (value &rest body)
(let* ((newbody (mapcar #’(lambda (clause)
‘(,(gensym) ,@(rest clause)))
body))
(switcher (mapcar #’(lambda (clause newclause)
‘(,(first clause) (go ,(first newclause))))
body newbody)))
‘(block switch
(tagbody (case ,value ,@switcher)
(break)
,@(apply #’nconc newbody))))
(defmacro break () ’(return-from switch))

这里使用两个宏，一个用于 switch，一个用于 break，它们共同使下面的语句：

Here we use two macros, one for switch and one for break, which together cause the statement

(switch n
(0 (princ "none") (break))
(1 (princ "one "))
(2 (princ "too "))
(3 (princ "many")))

它只会打印 many、too many、one too many、none，或者什么也不打印，展开成：

(which always prints either many, too many, one too many, none, or nothing) to expand into

(block switch
(tagbody (case n
(0 (go G0042))
(1 (go G0043)
(2 (go G0044))
(3 (go G0045)))
(return-from switch)
G0042 (princ "none")
(return-from switch)
G0043 (princ "one ")
G0044 (princ "too ")
G0045 (princ "many")))

译注：原稿的 ++ 示例代码计算的是 √x + √y，与上一页定义的勾股加法 √(x² + y²) 不一致；此处保留原稿代码，不作静默修改。

这与 C 编译器生成的代码并无太大差别。

which is not unlike the code that would be produced by a C compiler.

关于用约100行代码解决第三题的解释器示例，参见 [Steele, 1978c; Steele, 1978b]。

For examples of interpreters that solve the third problem in about 100 lines of code, see [Steele, 1978c; Steele, 1978b].

Lisp 有着丰富的扩展实验传统，从“只加一个新特性”到把 Lisp 作为实现语言、发明全新语言，无所不包。1960年代末至1970年代初，MIT 尤其热衷这类活动，斯坦福大学、卡内基梅隆大学和印第安纳大学等机构也如此。当时，人们惯于提出一种新语言作为范例，把一套编程风格思想具体化。Lisp 社区之外也一样，同期大量类 Algol、尤其受 Pascal 启发的语言就是证明。但 Lisp 既便于以很小开销试验小想法，也适合开展需要许多人月的大规模改造。

There is a rich tradition of experimenting with augmentations of Lisp, ranging from “let’s add just one new feature” to inventing completely new languages using Lisp as an implementation language. This activity was carried on particularly intensively at MIT during the late 1960’s and early 1970’s but also at other institutions such as Stanford University, Carnegie-Mellon University, and Indiana University. At that time it was customary to make a set of ideas about programming style concrete by putting forth a new programming language as an exemplar. (This was true outside the Lisp community as well; witness the proliferation of Algol-like, and particularly Pascal-inspired, languages around the same time period. But Lisp made it convenient to try out little ideas with a small amount of overhead, as well as tackling grand revampings requiring many man-months of effort.)

最早的 Lisp 基础语言之一是 METEOR [Bobrow, 1985]，它是采用 Lisp 语法的 COMIT 版本，注意这个双关语。COMIT [MIT RLE, 1962a; MIT RLE, 1962b; Yngve, 1972] 是模式匹配语言，反复用一组规则匹配平坦、线性的符号记号工作区中的内容；它是 SNOBOL 的先驱，也是 OPS5 [Forgy, 1977] 等基于规则语言的祖先。METEOR 嵌入运行于 IBM 7090 的 MIT Lisp 1.5 系统。其 Lisp 代码不到300张80列卡片，其中有些留白较多。相比之下，据 Yngve 说，7090 的 COMIT 解释器实现占约10,000个内存字。假设这相当于约10,000行汇编代码，那么它就是 Lisp 作为高级语言、用于其他语言原型设计之有效性的早期例证。

One of the earliest Lisp-based languages was METEOR [Bobrow, 1985], a version of COMIT (note the pun) with Lisp syntax. COMIT [MIT RLE, 1962a; MIT RLE, 1962b; Yngve, 1972] was a pattern-matching language that repeatedly matched a set of rules against the contents of a flat, linear workspace of symbolic tokens; it was a precursor of SNOBOL and an ancestor of such rule-based languages as OPS5 [Forgy, 1977]. METEOR was embedded within the MIT Lisp 1.5 system that ran on the IBM 7090. The Lisp code for METEOR is a little under 300 80-column cards (some with more whitespace than others). By contrast, the 7090 implementation of the COMIT interpreter occupied about 10,000 words or memory, according to Yngve; assuming this reflects about 10,000 lines of assembly language code, we can view this as an early example of the effectiveness of LISP as a high-level language for prototyping other languages.

另一种早期基于 Lisp 的模式匹配语言是 CONVERT [Guzman, 1966]。METEOR 基本只是把 COMIT 直接实现为 Lisp 数据结构；CONVERT 则把 COMIT 的模式匹配与 Lisp 的递归数据结构结合起来，允许将递归定义的模式匹配到任意 Lisp 数据结构。

Another early LISP-based pattern-matching language was CONVERT [Guzman, 1966]. Whereas METEOR was pretty much a straight implementation of COMIT represented as Lisp data structures, CONVERT merged the pattern-matching features of COMIT with the recursive data structures of Lisp, allowing the matching of recursively defined patterns to arbitrary Lisp data structures.

Carl Hewitt 设计了一种极具雄心、用于定理证明的类 Lisp 语言 Planner [Hewitt, 1972]。其主要贡献是推进了模式导向调用，并把自动回溯用作目标导向搜索的实现机制。它从未按最初设想完整实现，却推动了 Lisp 历史上另外三项重要发展：Micro-Planner、Muddle 和 Conniver。

Carl Hewitt designed an extremely ambitious Lisp-like language for theorem-proving called Planner [Hewitt, 1972]. Its primary contributions consisted of advances in pattern-directed invocation and the use of automatic backtracking as an implementation mechanism for goal-directed search. It was never completely implemented as originally envisioned, but it spurred three other important developments in the history of Lisp: Micro-Planner, Muddle, and Conniver.

Gerald Jay Sussman、Drew McDermott 和 Eugene Charniak 实现了 Planner 的一个子集 Micro-Planner [Sussman, 1971]，嵌入后来成为 MacLisp 的 MIT PDP-6 Lisp 系统。实际实现的语言语义并未完全形式化。实现技术相当临时拼凑，在某些复杂情况下无法正确工作；匹配器设计为匹配两个可能都含变量的模式，却没有使用完整合一算法。很久以后，Sussman 了解 Prolog 后对 Guy Steele 说，Prolog 看来是 Micro-Planner 的第一个正确实现。

Gerald Jay Sussman, Drew McDermott, and Eugene Charniak implemented a subset of Planner called Micro-Planner [Sussman, 1971], which was embedded within the MIT PDP-6 Lisp system that eventually became MacLisp. The semantics of the language as implemented were not completely formalized. The implementation techniques were rather ad hoc and did not work correctly in certain complicated cases; the matcher was designed to match two patterns, each of which might contain variables, but did not use a complete unification algorithm. (Much later, Sussman, on learning about Prolog, remarked to Guy Steele that Prolog appeared to be the first correct implementation of Micro-Planner.)

POP-2 中也实现过一个 Planner 版本 [Davies, 1984]。

A version of Planner was also implemented in POP-2 [Davies, 1984].

Muddle 语言，后来称 MDL，是 Lisp 的扩展版本，在某些方面也是竞争者，由 MIT 动态建模小组设计和使用。该小组独立于 MIT 人工智能实验室，但同处科技广场545号大楼。这项工作于1970年底由 Gerald Jay Sussman、Carl Hewitt、Chris Reeve 和 David Cressey 开始，后来 Bruce Daniels、Greg Pfister 和 Stu Galley 加入。其设计目标是“……作为 Lisp 的后继者、动态建模系统的候选载体，以及实现 Planner-70 的可能基础”[Galley, 1975]。Muddle 与 Lisp 的竞争，以及 Sussman 脚踏两边阵营的事实，在一定程度上促进了相互启发。Muddle 的 I/O、中断处理和多道程序设计，即协程，远比当时的 MacLisp 先进。它的垃圾回收器比 PDP-10 MacLisp 曾有的任何版本都复杂，应用子程序库也更大，尤其是图形方面。当时有些 Lisp 拥护者会反驳，说 Muddle 只用于编写子程序库，根本没有主程序！但实际上，确有一些大型应用用 Muddle 编写。Muddle 引入 lambda 列表语法标记 OPTIONAL、REST 和 AUX，后来被 Conniver、Lisp Machine Lisp 和 Common Lisp 采用。

The language Muddle (later MDL) was an extended version of Lisp and in some ways a competitor, designed and used by the Dynamic Modeling Group at MIT, which was separate from the MIT AI Laboratory but in the same building at 545 Technology Square. This effort was begun in late 1970 by Gerald Jay Sussman, Carl Hewitt, Chris Reeve, and David Cressey, later joined by Bruce Daniels, Greg Pfister, and Stu Galley. It was designed “. . . as a successor to Lisp, a candidate vehicle for the Dynamic Modeling System, and a possible base for implementation of Planner-70.” [Galley, 1975] To some extent the competition between Muddle and Lisp, and the fact that Sussman had a foot in each camp, resulted in cross-fertilization. The I/O, interrupt handling, and multiprogramming (that is, coroutining) facilities of Muddle were much more advanced than those of MacLisp at the time. Muddle had a more complex garbage collector than PDP-10 MacLisp ever had, as well as a larger library of application subroutines, especially for graphics. (Some Lisp partisans at the time would reply that Muddle was used entirely to code libraries of subroutines but no main programs! But in fact some substantial applications were coded in Muddle.) Muddle introduced the lambda-list syntax markers OPTIONAL, REST, and AUX that were later adopted by Conniver, Lisp Machine Lisp, and Common Lisp.

1972年，Drew McDermott 和 Gerald Jay Sussman 针对他们认为 Micro-Planner 存在的局限，尤其是控制结构的局限，设计了 Conniver。在经典论文《为何“谋划”优于“规划”》[Sussman, 1972b; Sussman, 1972a] 中，他们认为，自动嵌套回溯不过是表达一组用于穷举搜索的 FORALL 循环的一种过分复杂的方法：

The language Conniver was designed by Drew McDermott and Gerald Jay Sussman in 1972 in reaction to perceived limitations of Micro-Planner and in particular of its control structure. In the classic paper Why Conniving Is Better Than Planning [Sussman, 1972b; Sussman, 1972a], they argued that automatic nested backtracking was merely an overly complicated way to express a set of FORALL loops used to perform exhaustive search:

我们认为，作为 Planner 骨架的回溯控制结构，对解决问题而言，与其说是帮助，不如说是障碍。尤其是，自动回溯鼓励低效算法，向用户隐瞒实际发生的事情，并用名字听起来强大、实际力量却只是表面的原语误导用户。

It is our contention that the backtrack control structure that is the backbone of Planner is more of a hindrance in the solution of problems than a help. In particular, automatic backtracking encourages inefficient algorithms, conceals what is happening from the user, and misleads him with primitives having powerful names whose power is only superficial.

Conniver 的设计把控制流非常明确地交到程序员手中。其模型是协程的极端泛化：只有一个活动控制点，却可以有任意多个逻辑线程，并提供原语，把活动控制点显式地从一个线程转移到另一个。设计深受 Daniel Bobrow 和 Ben Wegbreit 提出的“意大利面栈”模型影响 [Bobrow, 1973]，该模型已在 BBN-Lisp，后来称 Interlisp，中实现。与意大利面栈一样，Conniver 区分数据环境和控制环境，并允许对两者分别创建闭包。后来 Scheme 的工作表明，在解释类 Lisp 语言时，数据环境与控制环境的角色并不对称 [Steele, 1977a]。Conniver 与意大利面栈的差别主要来自实现考虑。Conniver 的重点是通用性和易实现性；它用 Lisp 编写，以 Lisp 列表结构表示控制环境和数据环境，让 Lisp 垃圾回收器回收被放弃的环境。意大利面栈的实现则涉及 Lisp 系统最底层的结构修改。它尽可能允许栈式分配和释放，以解决效率问题。原则是按使用付费，不用就不付费：不创建闭包的程序，不应承担控制环境和数据环境堆管理的开销。［以下文字非常粗略，尚需充实。］

The design of Conniver put the flow of control very explicitly in the hands of the programmer. The model was an extreme generalization of coroutines; there was only one active locus of control, but arbitrarily many logical threads and primitives for explicitly transferring the active locus from one to another. This design was strongly influenced by the “spaghetti stack” model introduced by Daniel Bobrow and Ben Wegbreit [Bobrow, 1973] and implemented in BBN-Lisp (later to be known as Interlisp). Like spaghetti stacks, Conniver provided separate notions of a data environment and a control environment and the possibility or creating closures over either. (Later work with the Scheme language brought out the point that data environments and control environments do not play symmetrical roles in the interpretation of Lisp-like languages [Steele, 1977a].) Conniver differed from spaghetti stacks in ways stemming primarily from implementation considerations. The main point of Conniver was generality and ease of implementation; it was written in Lisp and represented control and data environments as Lisp list structures, allowing the Lisp garbage collector to handle reclamation of abandoned environments. The implementation of spaghetti stacks, on the other hand, involved structural changes to a Lisp system at the lowest level. It addressed efficiency issues by allowing stack-like allocation and deallocation behavior wherever possible. The policy was pay as you go but don’t pay if you don’t use it: programs that do not create closures should not pay for the overhead of heap management of control and data environments. [The following text is very sketchy and needs to be filled out.]

这里需要讨论 MIT 之外的模式匹配和回溯语言，例如 MLISP2 [Smith, 1973]、LISP70 [Tesler, 1973]、QA4、SAIL 的 LEAP 扩展 [Swinehart, 1972; Feldman, 1972]，以及 PLITS。需要查阅1977年在罗切斯特召开的 AIPL 会议资料。［粗略部分结束。］

Need to talk here about pattern-matching and backtracking languages outside MIT, such as MLISP2 [Smith, 1973], LISP70 [Tesler, 1973], QA4, the LEAP extensions to the SAIL language [Swinehart, 1972; Feldman, 1972], and PLITS. Need to check 1977 AIPL Conference in Rochester. [End of sketchy section.]

大约此时，Carl Hewitt 和学生开始发展参与者计算模型，其中每个计算实体，无论程序还是数据，都是一个参与者，即能够接收消息并作出反应的代理。Conniver 揭示的那些幕后活动，在此模型中变得更加显式：一切都是消息传递，一切都基于续延运行。Hewitt 和学生 Brian Smith 对当时多个研究小组的互动评论如下 [?]：

At about this time Carl Hewitt and his students began to develop the actor model of computation, in which every computational entity, whether program or data, is an actor: an agent that can receive and react to messages. The under-the-table activity brought out by Conniver was made even more explicit in this model; everything was message-passing, everything ran on continuations. Hewitt and his student Brian Smith commented on the interaction of a number of research groups at the time [?]:

PLANNER 的早期工作在 MIT 完成，并发表于 IJCAI-69 [Hewitt, 1969]。1970年，一群感兴趣的研究者，包括 Peter Deutsch、Richard Fikes、Carl Hewitt、Jeff Rulifson、Alan Kay、Jim Moore、Nils Nilsson 和 Richard Waldinger，聚集在 Pajaro Dunes，交流笔记和概念。……1972年11月，Alan Kay 在 MIT 的研讨会上强调，应使用数据结构的内涵式定义，并向它们传递消息。Landin、Evans 和 Reynolds 的 lambda 演算语言中的“过程式数据结构”已经有限地采用了这种方法，SIMULA-67 则广泛采用。他认为，只有数据类型本身才真正“知道”如何实现某项操作。此前我们自己的研究也曾关注过程式数据结构。……但我们误以为，尽管它们有一些优点，效率却太低，无法实际使用。Kay 的演讲引起了共鸣……我们立即看出如何运用他的想法……把知识的过程式嵌入原则扩展到数据结构。实际上，每种数据结构都变成一个小规划，规定收到每类请求时应做什么。……Kay 提出一种名为 SMALLTALK 的语言，使用面向记号流的解释器实现这些想法。……［当时，］Peter Bishop 和 Carl Hewitt 正试图为多年来困扰类 PLANNER 问题求解系统的控制结构问题找到通用解法。Sussman 提出围绕“可能性列表”的方案，但我们认为它有严重弱点。……仅仅用 try-next 查看内容，就可能造成不良的全局副作用……［这］使 Conniver 程序难以调试和理解。SMALLTALK 的记号流与 Conniver 的可能性列表具有同样的副作用问题。演讲后，Hewitt 向 Kay 指出了面向记号流解释器方案中的控制结构问题。到1972年12月，我们成功地把 SMALLTALK 和 SIMULA-67 的消息机制，Krutar、Balzer 和 Mitchell 的端口机制，以及先前 PLANNER-71 的 CALL 语句，推广为通用通信机制。我们的推广解决了 Hewitt 向 Kay 指出的 SMALLTALK 设计中的控制结构问题。我们把参与者传输通信原语开发为一种新的、独立于语言和机器的行为计算模型的一部分。参与者计算模型及其衍生发展的形成，是我们在这一研究领域的主要原创贡献。……以下是参与者计算模型发展的主要影响因素：

The early work on PLANNER was done at MIT and published in IJCAI-69 [Hewitt, 1969]. In 1970 a group of interested researchers (including Peter Deutsch, Richard Fikes, Carl Hewitt, JeffRulifson, Alan Kay, Jim Moore, Nils Nilsson, and Richard Waldinger) gathered at Pajaro Dunes to compare notes and concepts. . . . In November 1972, Alan Kay gave a seminar at MIT in which he emphasized the importance of using intentional definitions of data structures and of passing messages to them such as was done to a limited extent for the “procedural data structures” in the lambda calculus languages of Landin, Evans, and Reynolds and extensively in SIMULA- 67. His argument was that only the data type itself really “knows” how to implement any given operation. We had previously given some attention to procedural data structures in our own research. . . .However, we were under the misconception that procedural data structures were too inefficient for practical use although they had certain advantages. Kay’s lecture struck a responsive note . . . We immediately saw how to use his idea . . . to extend the principle of procedural embedding of knowledge to data structures. In effect each type of data structure becomes a little plan of what to do for each kind of request that it receives. . . . Kay proposed a language called SMALLTALK with a token stream oriented interpreter to implement these ideas. . . . [At that time,] Peter Bishop and Carl Hewitt were working to try to obtain a general solution to the control structure problems which had continued to plague PLANNER- like problem solving systems for some years. Sussman had proposed a solution oriented around “possibility lists” which we felt had very serious weaknesses. . . . Simply looking at their contents using try-next can cause unfortunate global side-effects . . . [which] make Conniver programs hard to debug and understand. The token streams of SMALLTALK have the same side-effect problem as the possibility lists of Conniver. After the lecture, Hewitt pointed out to Kay the control structure problems involved in his scheme for a token stream oriented interpreter. By December 1972, we succeeded in generalizing the message mechanism of SMALLTALK and SIMULA-67; the port mechanism of Krutar, Balzer, and Mitchell; and the previous CALL statement of PLANNER-71 to a universal communication mechanism. Our generalization solved the control structure problems that Hewitt pointed out to Kay in the design of SMALLTALK. We developed the actor transmission communication primitive as part of a new language-independent, machine-independent, behavioral model of computation. The development of the actor model of computation and its ramifications is our principal original contribution to this area of research. . . . The following were the main influences on the development of the actor model of computation:

• ［Alan］Kay 建议把过程式嵌入扩展到数据结构；其背景是，我们此前试图推广 Church、Landin、Evans 和 Reynolds 关于“函数式数据结构”的工作。

• The suggestion by [Alan] Kay that procedural embedding be extended to cover data structures in the context of our previous attempts to generalize the work by Church, Landin, Evans, and Reynolds on “functional data structures.”

• 我们此前试图整理和推广以下研究者关于协程控制结构的工作： Landin、Mitchell、Krutar、Balzer、Reynolds、Bobrow–Wegbreit 和 Sussman。

• The context of our previous attempts to clean up and generalize the work on coroutine control structures of Landin, Mitchell, Krutar, Balzer, Reynolds, Bobrow- Wegbreit, and Sussman.

• Seymour Papert 在 LOGO 中用“小人”比喻计算的影响。

• The influence of Seymour Papert’s “little man” metaphor for computation in LOGO.

• 基于能力的保护方案的局限和复杂性。每次参与者传输实际上都是一次跨域调用，在参与者机器上高效地提供内在保护。

• The limitations and complexities of capability-based protection schemes. Every actor transmission is in effect an inter-domain call efficiently providing an intrinsic protection on actor machines.

• 开发前几代 PLANNER 的经验。Julian Davies 在爱丁堡大学用 POP-2 实现了几乎整个 PLANNER-71 语言，以及一些扩展。

• The experience developing previous generations of PLANNER. Essentially the whole PLANNER-71 language (together with some extensions) was implemented by Julian Davies in POP-2 at the University of Edinburgh.

从参与者计算模型看，控制结构只是一种传递消息的模式。……参与者控制结构相对 Conniver 有以下优势：

In terms of the actor model of computation, control structure is simply a pattern of passing messages. . . . Actor control structure has the following advantages over that of Conniver:

• Conniver 控制结构方法的一个严重问题是，程序员，无论人还是机器，都必须用活动记录、可能性链接等低层数据结构思考。参与者方法则让程序员用所编程领域中自然出现的对象行为来思考。……

• A serious problem with the Conniver approach to control structure is that the programmer (whether human or machine) must think in terms of low level data structures such as activation records or possibility links. The actor approach allows the programmer to think in terms of the behavior of objects that naturally occur in the domain being programmed. . . .

• 参与者传输完全没有副作用。……

• Actor transmission is entirely free of side-effects. . . .

• Conniver 的控制机制违反模块化原则。……Dijkstra 曾说，goto 的使用与结构不良的程序相关。我们赞同这个判断，但认为原因在于 goto 不是足够强大的原语。goto 的问题是，不能在把控制转交目标时，同时发送一条消息。……

• The control mechanisms of Conniver violate principles of modularity. . . . Dijkstra has remarked that the use of the goto is associated with badly structured programs. We concur in this judgement but feel that the reason is that the goto is not a sufficiently powerful primitive. The problem with the goto is that a message cannot be sent along with control to the target. . . .

• 由于控制结构过于原始，Conniver 程序难写也难调试。……Conniver 程序容易陷入无限循环，而原因对程序员并不明显。

• Because of its primitive control structures, Conniver programs are difficult to write and debug. . . . Conniver programs are prone to going into infinite loops for no reason that is very apparent to the programmer.

尽管如此，在提高可轻松执行的目标导向计算的通用性方面，Conniver 相比 Micro-Planner 仍是重大进步。但这种通用性的提高，是以降低问题求解语言的层次为代价的。它迫使用户用“可能性列表”和“a 链接”等低层实现术语思考。我们建议改变问题求解范式，把它看作由通过消息传递相互通信的个体组成的社会。

Nevertheless Conniver represents a substantial advance over Micro-Planner in increasing the generality of goal-oriented computations that can be easily performed. However, this increase in generality comes at the price of lowering the level of the language of problem solving. It forces users to think in low level implementation terms such as “possibility lists” and “a-links.” We propose a shift in the paradigm of problem solving to be one of a society of individuals communicating by passing messages.

我们长篇引用 Smith 和 Hewitt，有三个原因：他们的比较分析十分明确；这段话展示了人工智能、Lisp 和其他程序设计语言社区中流传的不同思想之间的众多联系；而且，思想演化到这个特定节点时完成了一次提炼，很快又迅速而有力地反馈到 Lisp 自身的演化。

We have quoted Smith and Hewitt at length for three reasons: because their comparative analysis is very explicit; because the passage illustrates the many connections among different ideas floating around in the AI, Lisp, and other programming language communities; and because this particular point in the evolution of ideas represented a distillation that soon fed back quickly and powerfully into the evolution of Lisp itself.

Hewitt 和学生，尤其是 Howie Shrobe、Brian Smith、Todd Matson、Roger Hale、Peter Bishop、Marilyn McLennan、Russ Atkinson、Mike Freiling、Ken Kahn、Keith Nishihara、Kathy Van Sant、Aki Yonizawa、Benjamin Kuipers、Richard Stieger 和 Irene Greif，在 MacLisp 中开发并实现了一种新语言，把参与者计算模型具体化。它最初称 Planner-73，后来改名 PLASMA，即 PLAnner-like System Modeled on Actors，基于参与者建模的类 Planner 系统 [?; Hewitt, 1975]。

Hewitt and his students (notably Howie Shrobe, Brian Smith, Todd Matson, Roger Hale, Peter Bishop, Marilyn McLennan, Russ Atkinson, Mike Freiling, Ken Kahn, Keith Nishihara, Kathy Van Sant, Aki Yonizawa, Benjamin Kuipers, Richard Stieger, and Irene Greif) developed and implemented in MacLisp a new language to make concrete the actor model of computation. This language was first called Planner-73 but the name was later changed to PLASMA (PLAnner-like System Modeled on Actors) [?; Hewitt, 1975].

PLASMA 的语法明显类似 Lisp，却使用多种圆括号和方括号，Muddle 也如此，还有许多其他特殊字符。可以合理推测，Hewitt 受到了当时新出现的 Knight 键盘和 Xerox Graphics Printer（XGP）打印机的吸引。Xerox Graphics Printer 是最早的激光打印机，分辨率每英寸200点。键盘由 MIT 人工智能实验室的 Tom Knight 设计，相当于 MIT 版的斯坦福人工智能实验室多年前开发的扩展 ASCII 键盘。与斯坦福键盘一样，Knight 键盘有 Control 和 Meta 键，很快被用于 EMACS 文本编辑器命令集的开发，还具有 α、β 和 ≡ 等特殊图形字符。每英寸200点的 XGP 使这些字符能够打印。PLASMA 中的递归阶乘函数如下：

While the syntax of PLASMA was recognizably Lisp-like, it made use of several kinds of parentheses and brackets (as did Muddle) as well as many other special characters. It is reasonable to assume that Hewitt was tempted by the possibilities of the then newly available Knight keyboard and Xerox Graphics Printer (XGP) printer. (The Xerox Graphics Printer was the first of the laser printers, 200 dots per inch. The keyboards, designed by Tom Knight of the MIT AI Lab, were the MIT equivalent of the extended-ASCII keyboards developed years earlier at the Stanford AI Laboratory. Like the Stanford keyboards, Knight keyboards had Control and Meta keys (which were soon pressed into service in the development of the command set for the EMACS text editor) and a set of graphics that included such exotic characters as α, β, and ≡. The XGP, at 200 dots per inch, made possible the printing of such exotic characters.) The recursive factorial function looked like this in PLASMA:

[factorial ---
(cases
(---> [0] 1)
(---> [=n] (n * (factorial (n - 1)))))]

注意中缀算术运算符的使用。这不仅是巧妙的语法，也是巧妙的语义；(n - 1) 真正的含义是，把包含减法运算符和数字1的消息，发送给由 n 命名的数／参与者／对象。

Note the use of infix arithmetic operators. This was not merely clever syntax, but clever semantics; (n - 1) really meant that a message containing the subtraction operator and the number 1 was to be sent to the number/actor/object named by n.

可以说，1970年代 MIT 的 Lisp 发展走了两条不同道路。第一条以 MacLisp 为主力工具，用汇编语言实现，以追求最高效率和最紧凑代码，服务人工智能实验室和 MACSYMA 小组。第二条则是 Hewitt 及其学生与 Sussman 及其学生之间长期的对话、竞争和争论，双方都从外界吸收思想，也向外传播思想。第二条道路的特点是追求“正确的东西”，每套新思想都通过一种新语言来展示，通常为快速原型和实验而构建于 MacLisp 或 Muddle 等类 Lisp 语言之上。

One may argue that Lisp development at MIT took two distinct paths during the 1970’s. In the first path, MacLisp was the workhorse tool, coded in assembly language for maximum efficiency and compactness, serving the needs of the AI Laboratory and the MACSYMA group. The second path consisted of an extended dialogue/competition/argument between Hewitt (and his students) and Sussman (and his students), with both sides drawing in ideas from the rest of the world and spinning some offas well. This second path was characterized by a quest for “the right thing” where each new set of ideas was exemplified in the form of a new language, usually implemented on top of a Lisp-like language (MacLisp or Muddle) for the sake of rapid prototyping and experimentation.

Hewitt 与 Sussman 对话的下一轮，当然就是第2.8节讨论的 Scheme。回头看，这一发展似乎结束了对话，也许因为它让整个探索过程绕了一圈，回到原点。他们从 Lisp 出发，试图阐明搜索、控制结构和计算模型问题，最后又回到熟悉的老 Lisp，但有一个不同之处：需要词法作用域，简言之就是闭包，才能使 Lisp 不仅在语法上，也在语义上兼容 lambda 演算，从而把它牢牢连接到数理逻辑的各种发展，并为 Lisp 社区与函数式编程的发展互动铺路。

The next round in the Hewitt/Sussman dialogue was, of course, Scheme (as discussed in section 2.8); in hindsight, we observe that this development seems to have ended the dialogue, perhaps because it brought the entire path of exploration full circle. Starting from Lisp, they sought to explicate issues of search, of control structures, of models of computation, and finally came back simply to good old Lisp, but with a difference: lexical scoping—closures, in short—were needed to make Lisp compatible with the lambda calculus not merely in syntax but also in semantics, thereby connecting it firmly with various developments in mathematical logic and paving the way for the Lisp community to interact with developments in functional programming.

这种连接的一个结果是，近年来，在 ACM 年度程序设计语言原理会议上，不作进一步解释就把 Scheme 当作讨论框架，已经完全正当。Hewitt 指出，参与者模型能够捕捉 lambda 演算的关键方面；Scheme 则表明，lambda 演算能够捕捉参与者模型几乎全部关键方面，仅副作用和同步除外。

(One consequence of this hookup is that it has for some years now become quite respectable to assume Scheme without further explanation as a framework for discussion at the annual ACM Principles of Programming Languages conference!) Hewitt had noted that the actor model could capture the salient aspects of the lambda calculus; Scheme demonstrated that the lambda calculus captured nearly all salient aspects (excepting only side effects and synchronization) of the actor model.

在这一新视角下，Sussman 和 Steele 开始深入研究类 Lisp 语言和基于参与者的语言的语义。一旦接受维护词法环境和闭包的开销，Scheme 甚至比 Lisp 1.5 简单得多，其完整 Lisp 解释器可以写在一张纸上，或两个各30行的屏幕中。这使语言与实现思想的实验极其迅速；一度，他们每周测试和测量多达十个新解释器。部分结果汇总于《解释器的艺术》[Steele, 1978b]。一个特别关注点是按名调用与按值调用参数的比较；这方面，他们受到印第安纳大学工作的影响，相关讨论见《CONS 不应对其参数求值》[Friedman, November]。

Sussman and Steele began to look fairly intensely at the semantics of Lisp-like as well as actorbased languages in this new light. Scheme was so much simpler even than Lisp 1.5, once one accepted the overheads of maintaining lexical environments and closures, that one could write a complete interpreter for it in Lisp on a single sheet of paper (or in two 30-line screenfuls). This allowed for extremely rapid experimentation with language and implementation ideas; at one point Sussman and Steele were testing and measuring as many as ten new interpreters a week. Some of their results were summarized in The Art of the Interpreter [Steele, 1978b]. A particular point of interest was comparison of call-by-name and call-by-value parameters; in this they were influenced by work at Indiana University discussed in the paper CONS Should Not Evaluate Its Arguments [Friedman, November].

Scheme 不仅自身易于快速变异，也成为快速构建其他语言原型的实现基础。理论研究者形式化描述语言含义的一种常用技术是指称语义，它用构造的各部分及其关系来描述其含义，lambda 演算则是这种记法的黏合剂。Scheme 实际上在可用编程环境中提供了简单、实用的 lambda 演算实现，因此可以把指称语义直接转写成 Scheme，得到新语言的可工作解释器。

Besides being itself susceptible to rapid mutation, Scheme has also served as an implementation base for rapid prototyping of yet other languages. One popular technique among theoreticians for formally describing the meaning of a language is to give a denotational semantics, which describes the meaning of each construct in terms of its parts and their relationships; lambda calculus is the glue of this notation. Because Scheme in effect provides a simple and practical implementation of lambda calculus in a usable programming environment, a denotational semantics can be transcribed straightforwardly into Scheme to produce a working interpreter for the new language.

Scheme 表明，设计恰当的 Lisp 能提供管理控制结构和消息传递所需的全部灵活性，虽然未必提供全部语法，但它没有解决基于 Lisp 的人工智能语言开发的另一个目标：自动管理目标导向搜索或定理证明。Scheme 之后，Sussman 和学生沿此方向开发了几种新的 Lisp 基础语言，包括基于约束的系统 [Sussman, 1975a; Stallman, 1976; Steele, 1979; Kleer, 1978b]，以及基于非单调逻辑的真值维护系统 [Kleer, 1978a; McAllester, 1978]。依赖导向回溯消除了按时间顺序回溯所造成的“巨大 FORALL 循环嵌套”效应。随着时间推移，这条研究路线越来越像数据库设计问题，而非语言设计问题，尚未反馈到 Lisp 主流演化中。

While Scheme showed that a properly designed Lisp gave one all the flexibility (if not all the syntax) one needed in managing control structure and message-passing, it did not solve the other goal of the development of Lisp-based AI languages: the automatic management of goal-directed search or of theorem proving. After Scheme, a few new Lisp-based languages were developed in this direction by Sussman and his students, including constraint-based systems [Sussman, 1975a; Stallman, 1976; Steele, 1979; Kleer, 1978b] and truth maintenance systems [Kleer, 1978a; McAllester, 1978] based on non-monotonic logic. The technique of dependency-directed backtracking eliminated the “giant nest of FORALL loops” effect of chronological backtracking. Over time this line of research became more of a database design problem than a language design problem, and has not yet resulted in feedback to the mainstream evolution of Lisp.

不过，其他地方仍在继续开发人工智能应用语言，Lisp 也一直是实现它们的首选。1980年代初，C 曾一度成为首选替代方案，尤其在效率至关重要的场合。Lisp 实现技术，特别是编译和垃圾回收的改进，使这一钟摆又略微摆了回来。

Development of languages for artificial intelligence applications continued at other sites, however, and Lisp has remained the vehicle of choice for implementing them. During the early 1980’s C became the alternative of choice for a while, especially where efficiency was a major concern. Improvements in Lisp implementation techniques, particularly in compilation and garbage collection, have swung that particular pendulum back a bit.

1980年代初人工智能热潮中，“专家系统”是流行词，通常指用表面上与 METEOR 或 CONVERT 差别不大的语言编写的基于规则系统。OPS5 是当时较著名的规则语言之一，XCON 是它最突出的成功应用案例。XCON 是卡内基梅隆大学为 DEC 开发的、用于配置 VAX 安装系统的专家系统。OPS5 最初用 Lisp 实现，后来为提高效率，用 BLISS [Wulf, 1971] 重写。BLISS 是 CMU 开发、DEC 支持的系统实现语言，其语义层次大致与 C 相同。

During the AI boom of the early 1980’s, “expert systems” was the buzzword; this was usually understood to mean rule-based systems written in languages superficially not very different from METEOR or CONVERT. OPS5 was one of the better-known rule-based languages of this period, and XCON (an expert system for configuring VAX installations, developed by Carnegie-Mellon University for Digital Equipment Corporation) was its premier application success story. OPS5 was first implemented in Lisp; later it was recoded for efficiency in BLISS [Wulf, 1971] (a CMU- developed and DEC-supported systems implementation language at about the same semantic level as C).

人工智能语言的另一重要类别是基于框架的语言；一个好例子是用 Interlisp 实现的 KRL，即知识表示语言。

Another important category of AI languages was frame-based; a good example was KRL (Knowledge Representation Language), which was implemented in Interlisp.

Lisp 的另一条实验路线是并行性。早期发展虽包含中断处理和多道程序设计设施，但真正的多处理，只有在适当硬件出现后才得以发展，其中一些硬件专为此构建。S1 Lisp [Brooks, 1982a] 设计为使用 S1 系统的多个处理器，但与 S1 Lisp 的许多其他特性一样，这部分从未真正工作起来。最重要的早期“真正”并行 Lisp 实现包括 Multilisp、Qlisp 和 Butterfly PSL。

Another line of experimentation in Lisp is in the area of parallelism. While early developments included facilities for interrupt handling and multiprogramming, true multiprocessing evolved only with the availability of appropriate hardware facilities (in some cases built for the purpose). S1 Lisp [Brooks, 1982a] was designed to use the multiple processors of an S1 system, but (like so many other features of S1 Lisp) that part never really worked. Some of the most important early “real” parallel Lisp implementations were Multilisp, Qlisp, and Butterfly PSL.

Multilisp [Halstead, 1984; Halstead, 1985] 是 MIT 的 Bert Halstead 和学生的工作。它基于 Scheme，主要依赖 future 概念，类似洗衣取件凭证，承诺计算完成后交付一个值。Multilisp 还提供 PCALL 构造，本质上是先并发且完整地求出各参数，再调用函数。因此 PCALL 为 future 的使用提供了某种结构化纪律，足以满足许多用途。Multilisp 运行于 Concert 多处理机，它由32个 Motorola 68000 处理器组成。其后继 MultiScheme 后来在 BBN Butterfly 上实现 [Miller, 1987]。

Multilisp [Halstead, 1984; Halstead, 1985] was the work of Bert Halstead and his students at MIT. Based on Scheme, it relied primarily on the notion of a future, which is a sort of laundry ticket, a promise to deliver a value later once it has been computed. Multilisp also provided a PCALL construct, essentially a function call that evaluates the arguments concurrently (and completely) before invoking the function. PCALL thus provides a certain structured discipline for the use of futures that is adequate for many purposes. Multilisp ran on the Concert multiprocessor, a collection of 32 Motorola 68000 processors. MultiScheme, a descendant of Multilisp, was later implemented for the BBN Butterfly [Miller, 1987].

Butterfly PSL [Swanson, 1988] 是 Portable Standard Lisp [Griss, 1982] 在 BBN Butterfly 上的实现。它也完全依靠 future 来派生并行进程。

Butterfly PSL [Swanson, 1988] was an implementation of Portable Standard Lisp [Griss, 1982] on the BBN Butterfly. It also relied entirely on futures for the spawning of parallel processes.

Qlisp [Gabriel, 1984; Goldman, 1988] 由斯坦福的 Richard Gabriel 和 John McCarthy 开发。它用一组并行控制结构扩展 Common Lisp，这些结构与已有 Common Lisp 控制构造相对应，此处原文有 parallel 的双关，尤其是 QLET、QLAMBDA 和 QCATCH。计算模型包含全局进程队列，以及派生进程、控制其交互和资源消耗的手段。例如，QLAMBDA 可产生三种函数：LAMBDA 所产生的普通函数；创建时派生独立进程的急切函数；调用时派生独立进程的延迟函数。Qlisp 在 Alliant FX8 上实现，是第一个经过编译的并行 Lisp 实现。

Qlisp [Gabriel, 1984; Goldman, 1988] was developed by Richard Gabriel and John McCarthy at Stanford. It extended Common Lisp with a number of parallel control structures that parallel (pun intended) existing Common Lisp control constructs, notably QLET, QLAMBDA, and QCATCH. The computational model involved a global queue of processes and a means of spawning processes and controlling their interaction and resource consumption. For example, QLAMBDA could produce three kinds of functions: normal ones, as produced by LAMBDA; eager ones, which would spawn a separate process when created; and delayed ones, which would spawn a separate process when invoked. Qlisp was implemented on the Alliant FX8 and was the first compiled parallel Lisp implementation.

Connection Machine Lisp [Steele, 1986] 是 Common Lisp 方言，增加了名为 xapping 的新数据结构，用于支持细粒度数据并行。实现上，xapping 是数组、哈希表和关联表的奇特混合；语义上，它是有序索引–值对的集合。语言原语面向并发处理一个 xapping 的全部值，并按关联索引匹配不同 xapping 中的值。其思想是，把索引当作虚拟处理器标签。如果存储管理系统工作得当，相同索引的值实际上会位于同一个物理处理器中，只有不同索引值之间的交互才会引发处理器间通信。Connection Machine Lisp 编程相当愉快，有点像符号版 APL，同时具有 Lisp 在表示方面的许多优势；但实践中，所需存储管理很难有效实现。Skef Wholey 曾在 CMU 跟随 Scott Fahlman 从事 Spice Lisp，他为 Connection Machine 制作的原型在 Thinking Machines 用作研究工具一段时间，却从未作为产品发布。它能很好地运行简单程序，但 xapping 嵌套时，原型存储分配器表现不佳。Wholey 返回 CMU 完成博士学位后，项目被搁置。Sabot 的 Paralation Lisp [Sabot, 1988] 部分就是针对 Connection Machine Lisp 中通信控制问题而设计的。Connection Machine Lisp 的主要贡献，是以类 Scheme 解释器作为阐释载体，探究为 FP [Backus, 1978] 等函数式语言赋予显式并行操作解释所带来的部分理论和实践后果。Steele 后来进一步探索嵌套并行的后果，并展示了另一个能够检测并行程序竞态条件的类 Scheme 解释器 [Steele, 1990a]。

Connection Machine Lisp [Steele, 1986] was a dialect of Common Lisp extended with a new data structure, the xapping intended to support fine-grain data parallelism. A xapping was implementationally a strange hybrid of array, hash table, and association list; semantically it is a set of ordered index-value pairs. The primitives of the language are geared toward processing all the values of a xapping concurrently, matching up values from different xappings by their associated indexes. The idea was that indexes are labels for virtual processors. If the storage management system does its job, values with the same index will in fact reside in the same physical processor, and interprocessor communication will occur only as a consequence of interactions of values with different indexes. While Connection Machine Lisp was reasonably pleasant to program in—sort of symbolic version of APL with many of the representational advantages of Lisp—in practice the required storage management was very difficult to implement effectively. A prototype implementation by Skef Wholey (who had earlier worked on Spice Lisp under Scott Fahlman at CMU) for Connection Machine was used as a research tool at Thinking Machines for a while but never released as a product. It worked well for simple programs but the prototype storage allocator did not behave well when xappings were nested. It was left on the back burner when Wholey returned to CMU to complete his doctorate. Sabot’s Paralation Lisp [Sabot, 1988] was designed in part as a reaction to the problems of controlling communication in Connection Machine Lisp. The primary contribution of Connection Machine Lisp was to work out, using a Scheme-like interpreter as an expository vehicle, some of the theoretical and pragmatic consequences of providing a functional language such as FP [Backus, 1978] with an explicitly parallel operational interpretation. Steele later explored the further consequences of nested parallelism and exhibited yet another Scheme-like interpreter that could detect race conditions in parallel programs [Steele, 1990a].

概括起来，Lisp 是出色的语言实验室，有两个原因。第一，可以选取仅含十几个原语的极小子集，却仍明显属于类 Lisp 语言。把这样的小语言连同选定变体引导到新平台上非常容易；若前景不错，再补齐长长的便利设施清单。第二，在已有 Lisp 实现中引导这种新方言尤其容易，一小时甚至更短就能完成。即使宿主实现与新方言在根本方面不同，它仍能提供算术、I/O 等原始操作，而且本身就是一种编写语言解释器非常方便的编程语言。如果能够接受通用的、面向列表结构的语法，就可以尽情重新编写语义。等语义正确之后，有的是时间重新进行工程实现；如果非要如此，还可以在前面加一个语法分析器。

To recapitulate: Lisp is an excellent laboratory for language experimentation for two reasons. First, one can choose a very small subset, with only a dozen primitives or so, that is still recognizably a member of the class of Lisp-like languages. It is very easy to bootstrap such a small language, with variations of choice, on a new platform. If it looks promising, one can flesh out the long laundry list of amenities later. Second, it is particularly easy—the work of an hour or less—to bootstrap such a new dialect within an existing Lisp implementation. Even if the host implementation differs in fundamental ways from the new dialect, it can provide primitive operations such as arithmetic and I/O as well as being a programming language that is just plain convenient for writing language interpreters. If you can live with the generic, list-structure-oriented syntax, you can have a field day reprogramming the semantics. After you get that right there is time enough to re-engineer it and, if you must, slap a parser on the front.

5 为什么 Lisp 如此多样

5 Why Lisp is Diverse

在这部 Lisp 演化史中，我们看到，Lisp 的历史似乎比使用更广泛的语言更曲折、更复杂。几乎每个小研究组都有自己的 Lisp 版本；语言概念有多少种变化，似乎就有多少种 Lisp。自然要问：Lisp 有什么特殊或不同之处，能够解释这种现象？

In this history of the evolution of Lisp we have seen that Lisp seems to have a more elaborate and complex history than languages with wider usage. It would seem that almost every little research group has its own version of Lisp, and there would appear to be as many Lisps as variations on language concepts. It is natural to ask what is so special or different about Lisp that explains it.

基本原因有五个：理论基础、可塑性、交互式与增量式特性、操作系统设施，以及选择从事 Lisp 工作的人。

There are five basic reasons: its theoretical foundations, its malleability, its interactive and incremental nature, its operating system facilities, and the people who choose to work on it.

理论基础。Lisp 建立在递归函数理论和可计算性理论之上。其最纯粹的形式适合数学推理和证明。因此，许多偏重理论的研究者采用 Lisp 或类 Lisp 语言表达思想、开展工作。于是，我们看到许多面向 Lisp 的论文，解释新的或已有的语言构造、证明程序性质，并探索证明技术。

Its theoretical foundations. Lisp was founded on the footing of recursive function theory and the theory of computability. Its purest form is useful for mathematical reasoning and proof. Therefore, many theoretically minded researchers have adopted Lisp or Lisp-like languages in which to express their ideas and to do their work. We thus see many Lisp-oriented papers with new language constructs explained, existing constructs explained, properties of programs proved, and proof techniques explored.

结果是，Lisp 和类 Lisp 语言始终处于语言基础研究的前沿。较重实践的理论研究者，也常用 Lisp 实现自己的想法。

The upshot is that Lisp and Lisp-like languages are always in the forefront of basic language research. And it is common for more practically minded theoretical researchers to also implement their ideas in Lisp.

可塑性。用 Lisp 试验新语言特性很容易，因为可以扩展 Lisp，使用户无法区分扩展与基础语言。这主要通过宏实现；自1963年起，宏就是 Lisp 的一部分 [Hart, 1963]。Lisp 宏使用 Lisp 作为计算引擎来计算展开结果，事实证明，它比其他语言的字符串处理机制更能有效扩展语言。此外，Lisp 社区对这类基于宏的扩展的接受程度，也是其他语言社区所没有的。

Its malleability. It is easy with Lisp to experiment with new language features, because it is possible to extend Lisp in such a way that the extensions are indistinguishable to users from the base language. Primarily this is accomplished through the use of macros, which have been part of Lisp since 1963 [Hart, 1963]. Lisp macros, with their use of Lisp as a computation engine to compute expansions, have proved to be a more effective way to extend a language than the stringprocessing mechanisms of other languages. Furthermore, such macro-based extensions are accepted within the Lisp community in a way that is not found in other language communities.

较新的 Lisp 方言还提供了扩展类型系统的机制，让人们能够试验新数据类型。当然，其他语言也有这种机制；但在 Lisp 中，数据类型机制与强大的宏设施及语言的函数式特性结合，使全新的计算范式能够在 Lisp 中构建。例如，我们已看到数据驱动范式 [Sussman, 1971]、可能世界范式 [McDermott, 1974] 和面向对象范式 [Moon, 1986] [Bobrow, 1986] 在 Lisp 中实现，而且 Lisp 与这些新范式之间的接缝基本不可见。

Furthermore, more recent Lisp dialects have provided mechanisms to extend the type system. This enables people to experiment with new data types. Of course, other languages have had this mechanism, but in Lisp the data typing mechanism combines with the powerful macro facility and the functional nature of the language to allow entirely new computing paradigms to be built in Lisp. For example, we have seen data-driven paradigms [Sussman, 1971], possible-worlds paradigms [McDermott, 1974], and object-oriented paradigms [Moon, 1986] [Bobrow, 1986] implemented in Lisp in such a way that the seams between Lisp and these new paradigms are essentially invisible.

交互式与增量式特性。在 Lisp 中探索编程问题的解法很容易，因为可以轻松实现部分解法、测试、修改、改变设计并调试改动，无需漫长的编辑、编译、链接周期。因此，Lisp 适合快速原型，也适合在解决方案不完整、甚至可能无法完整制定时构建大型程序。所以，人们常用 Lisp 探索那些用其他语言会令人望而却步的领域。

Its interactive and incremental nature. It is easy to explore the solutions to programming problems in Lisp, because it is easy to implement part of a solution, test it, modify it, change design, and debug the changes. There is no lengthy edit-compile-link cycle. Because of this, Lisp is useful for rapid prototyping and for constructing very large programs in the face of an incomplete—and possibly impossible to complete—plan of attack. Therefore, Lisp has often been used for exploring territory that is too imposing with other languages.

这一特点使 Lisp 对富于冒险和开拓精神的人很有吸引力。

This characteristic of Lisp makes it attractive to the adventuresome and pioneering.

操作系统设施。许多 Lisp 实现提供类似操作系统的设施：命令处理器、自动存储管理、文件管理、显示设施，包括窗口、图形和鼠标、多任务、编译器、增量式重链接器／加载器、符号调试器、性能监控，有时还有多处理，即并行编程。

Its operating system facilities. Many Lisp implementations provide facilities reminiscent of operating systems: a command processor, an automatic storage management facility, file management, display (windows, graphics, mouse) facilities, multitasking, a compiler, an incremental (re)linker/loader, a symbolic debugger, performance monitoring, and sometimes multiprocessing (parallel programming).

这意味着，可以用 Lisp 研究操作系统，并提供完整运行环境。结合交互式与增量式特性，还能编写复杂的文本编辑器，取代宿主计算机的原生操作系统。因此，Lisp 系统可以提供一种运行环境，在各种互不兼容的平台之间具有很强的可移植性。

This means that it is possible to do operating system research in Lisp and to provide a complete operating environment. Combined with its interactive and incremental nature, it is possible to write sophisticated text editors, and to supplant the native operating system of the host computer. Thus, a Lisp system can provide an operating environment that provides strong portability across a wide variety of incompatible platforms.

这使 Lisp 成为研究者喜爱的载体，从而进一步促进其多样化。

This makes Lisp an attractive vehicle for researchers and thereby further diversifies Lisp.

使用它的人。当然，语言不会自行多样化，是人使语言多样化。上述四个因素只是吸引人们使用 Lisp，并为他们提供试验 Lisp 的设施。如果被吸引来的人对探索新的语言方案没有兴趣，Lisp 就不会如此多样。因此，Lisp 必定具有某种吸引冒险者的特质。

Its people. Of course, languages do not diversify themselves, people diversify languages. The above four factors merely serve to attract people to Lisp, and they provide facilities for them to experiment with Lisp. If the people attracted to Lisp were not interested in exploring new language alternatives, then Lisp would not have been diversified, so there must be something about Lisp that attracts adventuresome people.

Lisp 的用途之一，是作为人工智能的语言。人工智能是计算机科学的一个分支，致力于探索最困难、最奇特的编程任务：模仿或理解认知与智能。被人工智能吸引的人通常富于创造力和胆识，语言设计者与实现者也往往如此，他们自己常常就是人工智能研究者或曾经的人工智能研究者。

Lisp is the language of artificial intelligence, among other things. And AI is a branch of computer science that is directed towards exploring the most difficult and exotic of all programming tasks: mimicking or understanding cognition and intelligence. The people who are attracted to AI are generally creative and bold, and the language designers and implementors follow in this mold, often being AI researchers or former AI researchers themselves.

Lisp 具有这套独特特征，是因为人工智能早期进展需要这些特性或类似特性。直到人工智能成为商业关注对象，人工智能公司才转向 Lisp 以外的语言。

Lisp provides its peculiar set of characteristics because those features—or ones like them—were required for the early advances of AI. Only when AI was the subject of commercial concerns did AI companies turn to other languages than Lisp.

另一个吸引力是，Lisp 是专家的语言。这里的意思是，Lisp 并非为了让缺乏经验的程序员编写健壮、可靠的软件而设计。因此，它很少进行编译期类型检查，模块系统很少，语言内置的安全约束或纪律也很少。它是一种无政府主义的语言，而大多数其他语言则是“法西斯式”的，借用黑客们的说法 [Raymond, 1991]。

Another attraction is that Lisp is a language of experts, which for our purposes means that Lisp is not a language designed for inexpert programmers to code robust reliable software. Therefore, there is little compile-time type checking, there are few module systems, there is little safety or discipline built into the language. It is an anarchic language, while most other languages are “fascist” (as hackers would have it [Raymond, 1991]).

关键在于，Lisp 吸引某一类人；这类人对以特定方式编程、探索新的语言设计，以及展示或发挥创造力感兴趣。

The key point is that a certain sort of person is attracted to Lisp, and that person is interested in programming a certain way, in exploring new language designs, and in demonstrating or exercising creativity.

6 大整合

6 The Great Consolidation

我们本想探索、却没有探索的一个现象，是1980年代的大整合。纵观程序设计语言史，1960年代和1970年代显然出现了大量语言，其中许多由彼此隔离的极小团队开发。这符合我们的多样化模型：当不存在足够庞大而有影响力的接受群体，且语言开发者彼此隔离时，多样化就会发生。

A phenomenon that we would have liked to have explored but didn’t was the great consolidation of the ’80’s. Looking at the overall history of programming languages, it seems clear that during the 1960’s and 1970’s there were a profusion of programming languages developed, many of them by very small isolated groups. This fits our model of diversification—diversification happens when there is no sufficiently large and powerful acceptance group and when language developers are isolated.

但到了1980年代，几乎所有语言发展都在整合。1980年代是乏味的。

But in the 1980’s, almost all language development was consolidation—the ’80’s were boring.

对此有几种可能解释，每种都涉及一种可能的接受群体；当然，它们可以共同起作用。一种解释是，网络，尤其 ARPANet，变得成熟，并向更多人开放。这形成了庞大的接受群体，同时推动可移植性和标准化。

There are some possible explanations for this, each concerning a possible acceptance group, and, of course, the explanations combine. One explanation is that the network—the ARPANet in particular—solidified and was accessible to many more people. This created a large acceptance group and a coincident push for portability and standardization.

第二种解释是，1980年代个人计算机市场走向成熟，形成商业接受群体，也再次推动可移植性。

A second explanation is that the 1980’s saw the solidification of the PC marketplace, which provides a commercial acceptance group and, again, a push for portability.

第三种解释是，工作站市场开始发展。它既是商业接受群体，也是庞大的、基于网络且面向用户的接受群体；它吸走了许多原本从事多样化探索的人才，使他们转而追逐名利。

A third explanation is that the workstation marketplace, which is both a commercial acceptance group and a large network-based, user-oriented acceptance group, began to develop and drained offa lot of diversification talent in pursuit of fame and fortune.

这种解释的一个变体是，商业语言最好能提供指导和纪律，帮助天赋不那么高的程序员开发健壮、可靠、可维护的软件。而对1980年代的多样化探索者来说，考虑到1960年代和1970年代为实现这些目标而形成的技术和实践已根深蒂固，这类语言实在不太有趣。

A variation on this explanation is that commercial languages are best when they provide guidance and discipline to help the not-so-talented programmer to develop robust, reliable, maintainable software, and such languages are just not very interesting for diversifiers in the 1980’s, given the well-entrenched techniques and practices developed in the ’60’s and ’70’s for achieving those goals.

第四种解释是，相比探索单个概念或特性，设计完整语言变得太难，或不再时髦。在1960年代和1970年代，一个人或小团队还可能设计并实现性能足以实用的语言。到了1980年代，商业压力迫使商业支持的语言不断提高性能，开发一种哪怕能拥有接受群体的语言，都已成为过于艰巨的任务，更不用说拥有足够庞大或有影响力的群体，让语言进入整合阶段。

A fourth explanation is that designing complete languages rather than exploring individual concepts or features became too hard to do or too unfashionable. In the ’60’s and ’70’s, it was possible for a single person or a small group to design and implement a language with sufficient performance to be useful. In the 1980’s, because commercial pressure forced performance improvements in commercially supported languages, it became too much of a task to produce a language that would even have an acceptance group, let alone one large or powerful enough to enter the language into a consolidation phase.

在1980年代，设计和实现语言，作为获得终身教职、名声或晋升的途径，也许实在太困难，所以人们倾向于放弃这项活动。

Designing and implementing languages in the 1980’s is perhaps too difficult a means to achieve tenure or fame or a promotion, and so the tendency is to abandon the activity.

其他一些人是这样说的：

Here are how some others have put it:

LISP 与众不同，因为它明显偏离了此前开发的每一种其他类型的程序设计语言。……LISP 的理论概念及其含义，远远超出了它的实际用途。

LISP is unusual, in the sense that it clearly deviates from every other type of programming language that has ever been developed. . . . The theoretical concepts and implications of LISP far transcend its practical usage.

署名：Jean E. Sammet [?, p. 406]

—Jean E. Sammet [?, p. 406]

这是类 Lisp 语言的一大优势：构造复合表达式的方式极少，几乎没有语法结构。……过不了多久，我们就会忘掉语言的语法细节，因为根本没什么细节，转而处理真正的问题。

This is one of the great advantage of Lisp-like languages: They have very few ways of forming compound expressions, and almost no syntactic structure. . . . After a short time we forget about syntactic details of the language (because there are none) and get on with the real issues.

署名：Abelson 和 Sussman [?, p. xvii]

—Abelson and Sussman[?, p. xvii]

语法糖会导致分号癌。

Syntactic sugar causes cancer of the semicolon.

署名：Alan Perlis

—Alan Perlis

我喜欢 Lisp，是因为你能感觉到比特从脚趾间穿过。

What I like about Lisp is that you can feel the bits between your toes.

署名：Drew McDermott [?]

—Drew McDermott [?]

Lisp 的语法和语义如此简单，以至于语法分析可以当作基础任务。因此，语法分析技术在 Lisp 程序中几乎不起作用，构建语言处理器也很少妨碍大型 Lisp 系统的增长和变化速度。

Lisp has such a simple syntax and semantics that parsing can be treated as an elementary task. Thus parsing technology plays almost no role in Lisp programs, and the construction of language processors is rarely an impediment to the rate of growth and change of large Lisp systems.

署名：Alan Perlis，为 [?] 所作序言

–Alan Perlis (forward to [?])

Pascal 适合建造金字塔：由大军推动沉重石块堆砌而成、宏伟壮观却静止不动的结构。Lisp 适合构建生命体。……

Pascal is for building pyramids—imposing, breathtaking, static structures built by armies pushing heavy blocks into place. Lisp is for building organisms. . . .

署名：Alan Perlis，为 [?] 所作序言

—Alan Perlis (forward to [?])

对喜爱自由风格与灵活性的人而言，Lisp 是首选媒介。

Lisp is the medium of choice for people who enjoy free style and flexibility.

署名：Gerald Jay Sussman，《The Little Lisper》导言，第 ix 页

—Gerald Jay Sussman (intro to The Little Lisper, p. ix)

嘿，Quux：别再折腾这篇论文了，咱们去折腾 Lisp 吧！

Hey, Quux: Let’s quit hacking this paper and hack Lisp instead!

署名：rpg，最后一次编辑时

—rpg (the final edit)

[Fateman, 1973] Fateman, Richard J. Reply to an editorial. ACM SIGSAM Bulletin, 25, pp. 9–11, March 1973. This reports the results of a test in which a compiled MacLisp floating-point program was faster than equivalent Fortran code. The numerical portion of the code was identical and MacLisp used a faster subroutine-call protocol.
